## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:03:37 AM

Saya akan susun sebagai peta praktis untuk microservice Java/Jersey: dari kontrak OpenAPI → generated client/server stubs → runtime HTTP client → resilience/observability/testing. Saya juga akan pisahkan mana yang “wajib onboarding cepat” vs “lanjutan produksi”.

## Introduction

Inti topiknya: **HTTP communication antar microservice Java/Jersey dengan kontrak sebagai sumber kebenaran utama**, bukan kode implementation sebagai sumber kebenaran. Dalam pendekatan ini, **OpenAPI spec** menjadi boundary formal: mendefinisikan endpoint, method, request/response schema, status code, error model, security scheme, dan compatibility rule. OpenAPI sendiri didefinisikan sebagai interface description untuk HTTP APIs agar manusia dan komputer bisa memahami service tanpa membaca source code atau traffic langsung. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html))

Untuk stack Anda, peta praktisnya berada di antara empat dunia:

1. **Provider side**: Jersey/JAX-RS resource yang expose API.
2. **Contract side**: OpenAPI YAML/JSON sebagai artefak governance.
3. **Consumer side**: generated client / handwritten client memakai Jersey Client atau OpenFeign.
4. **Runtime side**: timeout, retry, circuit breaker, auth, tracing, logging, compatibility, dan testing.

Jersey relevan karena ia adalah implementasi Jakarta REST/JAX-RS untuk service dan client development, sedangkan Jakarta REST menyediakan annotation model seperti `@Path`, `@GET`, `@POST`, `@PUT`, `@DELETE`, `@PATCH`, dan parameter binding untuk membangun RESTful web service di Java. ([projects.eclipse.org](https://projects.eclipse.org/projects/ee4j.jersey)) ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html)) OpenAPI Generator relevan karena Java generator-nya mendukung pembuatan Java client library, termasuk library seperti Jersey dan OpenFeign. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/)) OpenFeign sendiri memosisikan dirinya sebagai Java-to-HTTP client binder yang memproses annotation menjadi request template, dengan decoder dan error handling yang bisa dikustomisasi. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openfeign/feign))

## Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill / domain | Fokus yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting di microservice Jersey/JAX-RS |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Contract-first mental model | Treat OpenAPI sebagai source of truth; implementasi mengikuti kontrak, bukan sebaliknya | Mengurangi mismatch antar service, mempercepat onboarding consumer, dan membuat integrasi bisa dites sebelum service selesai |
| 2 | OpenAPI structure | `openapi`, `info`, `servers`, `paths`, `operationId`, `parameters`, `requestBody`, `responses`, `components/schemas` | Ini fondasi agar generator, mock server, dokumentasi, dan contract test bekerja konsisten |
| 3 | Resource modeling | Bedakan resource, command, query, sub-resource, collection, dan action endpoint | Menghindari API yang terlalu RPC-like tanpa alasan jelas |
| 4 | Operation design | Naming `operationId`, idempotency, HTTP method semantics, status code matrix | `operationId` buruk akan menghasilkan nama method client yang buruk dan menyulitkan refactor |
| 5 | Schema design | DTO schema, enum, nullable, required, format, nested object, polymorphism | Schema adalah kontrak serialisasi; perubahan kecil bisa breaking untuk generated client |
| 6 | Error contract | Standard error body, error code, message, field violations, correlation ID | Consumer tidak boleh parsing string error bebas; error harus bisa diproses secara deterministik |
| 7 | Validation contract | Bean Validation alignment: required, min/max, pattern, enum, length | Kontrak harus sinkron dengan validasi runtime di Jersey service |
| 8 | API compatibility | Backward-compatible vs breaking change; additive field, optional field, removed field, enum expansion | Wajib untuk release microservice secara independen |
| 9 | API versioning | URI version, header version, media type version, semantic versioning spec artifact | Menentukan bagaimana consumer lama dan baru berjalan paralel |
| 10 | OpenAPI Generator CLI/Maven | Generate client saat build; pisahkan generated source dari handwritten code | OpenAPI Generator menyediakan Maven plugin dan Java generator untuk client SDK generation. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/plugins/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 11 | Generator config | `generatorName`, `library`, `apiPackage`, `modelPackage`, `invokerPackage`, `dateLibrary`, `serializationLibrary` | Config menentukan style client, dependency, dan package boundary |
| 12 | Jersey Client generated SDK | Client berbasis JAX-RS/Jersey untuk komunikasi HTTP dari service ke service | Cocok bila aplikasi sudah memakai Jersey/JAX-RS dan ingin mental model seragam |
| 13 | OpenFeign client | Declarative HTTP client berbasis interface/annotation, decoder, encoder, error decoder | Cocok untuk client yang ingin API call terlihat seperti method interface |
| 14 | Plain OpenFeign vs Spring Cloud OpenFeign | Bedakan OpenFeign core dari integrasi Spring Cloud | Dalam aplikasi Jersey non-Spring, biasanya gunakan OpenFeign core atau wrapper sendiri via HK2/CDI |
| 15 | Client abstraction layer | Jangan panggil generated client langsung dari business service; bungkus dalam adapter/gateway | Memudahkan fallback, observability, testing, dan migrasi Jersey Client ↔ Feign |
| 16 | DTO boundary | Bedakan generated DTO, internal domain model, persistence model MyBatis, dan API response model | Menghindari schema eksternal bocor ke domain dan database |
| 17 | Serialization | JSON-B/Jackson/FasterXML alignment, date/time format, enum naming, unknown fields | Mismatch serializer adalah sumber bug paling umum di generated client |
| 18 | Timeout model | Connect timeout, read timeout, write/request timeout, total deadline | Tanpa timeout eksplisit, satu downstream lambat bisa menghabiskan thread service |
| 19 | Retry policy | Retry hanya untuk operasi aman/idempotent; gunakan backoff dan jitter | Retry sembarangan bisa menggandakan command, payment, order, atau workflow transition |
| 20 | Idempotency | Idempotency key, request deduplication, safe re-submit, command result lookup | Penting untuk POST/command endpoint yang bisa dipanggil ulang karena timeout |
| 21 | Circuit breaker / bulkhead | Fail fast, isolate dependency, limit concurrent downstream calls | Mencegah cascading failure antar microservice |
| 22 | Fallback strategy | Fallback data, cached response, degraded mode, atau explicit failure | Tidak semua API boleh fallback; command API biasanya lebih aman fail explicitly |
| 23 | Auth propagation | OAuth2/JWT, service token, mTLS, API key, token exchange | Client harus tahu header/security scheme dari kontrak dan runtime policy |
| 24 | Header governance | `Authorization`, `X-Correlation-Id`, `traceparent`, `Idempotency-Key`, tenant/user context | Header adalah bagian kontrak operasional, bukan sekadar detail transport |
| 25 | Observability | Log request metadata, metrics per downstream, tracing span, error classification | Memudahkan RCA saat “service A gagal karena service B lambat” |
| 26 | Contract testing | Validate provider response terhadap OpenAPI; validate consumer expectation terhadap mock | Mengurangi integrasi rusak setelah deploy |
| 27 | Mock server workflow | Generate mock dari OpenAPI untuk consumer dev dan test | Consumer bisa develop sebelum provider selesai |
| 28 | API diff / breaking change check | CI check untuk membandingkan spec lama vs baru | Harus menjadi gate sebelum merge agar kontrak tidak berubah diam-diam |
| 29 | Generated code ownership | Tentukan apakah generated code di-commit atau generated saat build | Commit generated code memudahkan review; generate saat build mengurangi noise-pilih konsisten |
| 30 | Template customization | Custom OpenAPI Generator templates untuk logging, retry hooks, base client, package style | OpenAPI Generator mendukung template dan library-specific override untuk Java generator. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/templating/)) |
| 31 | Multi-environment endpoint | `servers`, base URL config, Kubernetes DNS, ingress, on-prem/AWS/Azure endpoint | Generated client tidak boleh hardcode URL environment |
| 32 | Kubernetes communication | Service DNS, ingress, mesh/mTLS, network policy, readiness dependency | HTTP client config harus cocok dengan runtime deployment |
| 33 | Large payload handling | Streaming download/upload, pagination, compression, memory limit | Generated client sering default ke in-memory model; perlu dicek untuk payload besar |
| 34 | Pagination contract | Page/size, cursor, sort, filtering, total count semantics | Consumer perlu kontrak stabil agar tidak salah interpretasi result set |
| 35 | Concurrency semantics | ETag, `If-Match`, optimistic locking, conflict response `409` | Penting untuk update resource yang bisa diedit banyak actor |
| 36 | Workflow integration | HTTP call dari/ke Camunda delegate, external task, worker, atau orchestration service | Timeout dan retry harus sinkron dengan retry policy workflow engine |
| 37 | Database boundary | Jangan expose MyBatis entity atau PL/pgSQL result shape langsung sebagai API contract | Database refactor tidak boleh otomatis menjadi breaking API change |
| 38 | Testing pyramid | Unit test adapter, generated client smoke test, contract test, integration test dengan WireMock/mock server | Menjaga confidence tanpa semua test harus full environment |
| 39 | Failure modeling | Timeout, 4xx, 5xx, malformed response, incompatible schema, partial outage | Senior engineer perlu mendesain behavior saat dependency tidak reliable |
| 40 | Governance | API review checklist, naming convention, status code policy, deprecation policy | Contract-first hanya efektif kalau ada governance ringan tapi konsisten |

---

## Peta Praktis Berdasarkan Tool

| Tool / pendekatan | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko / perhatian |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Jersey Client handwritten** | Client kecil, endpoint sedikit, butuh kontrol penuh | Natural untuk ekosistem JAX-RS/Jersey | Boilerplate mudah menyebar; raw response handling bisa tidak konsisten |
| **Jersey Client generated by OpenAPI Generator** | Banyak endpoint, ingin SDK stabil berbasis kontrak | Kontrak → client otomatis; cocok untuk standardisasi | Generated code perlu governance, versioning, dan wrapper adapter |
| **OpenFeign handwritten** | Interface-style client, readability tinggi | Method call terasa deklaratif; decoder/error decoder mudah dipusatkan | Perlu setup manual di non-Spring app; jangan samakan dengan Spring Cloud OpenFeign |
| **OpenFeign generated by OpenAPI Generator** | Consumer ingin contract-first + declarative client | Mengurangi handwritten code dan menjaga sinkron dengan spec | Template/config harus dikunci agar hasil generate stabil |
| **OpenAPI-only + custom adapter** | Sistem enterprise yang butuh boundary kuat | Generated code tersembunyi di bawah adapter internal | Lebih banyak lapisan, tapi lebih aman untuk long-term maintainability |

---

## Contract-first Flow yang Direkomendasikan

```text
1. Design OpenAPI spec
   ↓
2. Review contract: naming, schema, status code, error model, security
   ↓
3. Generate server interface / DTO / client SDK
   ↓
4. Implement Jersey resource against generated or reviewed contract
   ↓
5. Consumer uses generated client behind adapter/gateway
   ↓
6. Contract test + mock server + integration test
   ↓
7. CI checks OpenAPI diff for breaking changes
   ↓
8. Release spec artifact + client artifact + service implementation
```

---

## Invariant yang Perlu Dipegang

| Invariant | Makna praktis |
|---|---|
| Contract bukan dokumentasi pasif | OpenAPI harus dipakai untuk generate, test, mock, dan review |
| Generated client bukan domain layer | Generated model jangan langsung menjadi domain object internal |
| Timeout wajib eksplisit | Tidak boleh mengandalkan default HTTP client |
| Retry harus sadar idempotency | Retry POST command tanpa idempotency key adalah bug desain |
| Error response harus stabil | Consumer butuh error code dan structure, bukan pesan bebas |
| Breaking change harus terdeteksi CI | Jangan tunggu integration environment rusak |
| Header operasional adalah kontrak | Trace, auth, tenant, correlation, idempotency harus distandardisasi |
| Client call harus observable | Setiap downstream call perlu metric, log, dan trace span |
| Base URL harus environment-driven | Jangan hardcode endpoint di generated client |
| Adapter wajib untuk service penting | Business service tidak ideal bergantung langsung ke generated class |

---

## Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target praktis |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | HTTP semantics + REST/JAX-RS dasar | Paham method, status code, resource, header, content negotiation |
| 2 | OpenAPI 3.x contract design | Bisa menulis spec untuk endpoint nyata lengkap dengan schema dan error |
| 3 | OpenAPI Generator + Maven | Bisa generate Java client dengan package/config yang rapi |
| 4 | Jersey Client | Bisa membuat client manual, timeout, filter, auth header, JSON mapping |
| 5 | OpenFeign core | Bisa membuat declarative client, encoder/decoder, error decoder |
| 6 | Adapter pattern untuk downstream client | Generated client dibungkus dalam `OrderServiceClient`, `CustomerGateway`, dll |
| 7 | Resilience | Timeout, retry, circuit breaker, idempotency, fallback |
| 8 | Contract testing | Mock server, schema validation, provider/consumer compatibility |
| 9 | Observability | Trace, metric, structured log per downstream dependency |
| 10 | CI/CD governance | Spec lint, breaking change diff, generated artifact versioning |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:05:18 AM

Kita mulai dari **Seri 1/16**. Fokus seri ini adalah mental model komunikasi HTTP antar microservice: bagaimana request berjalan, di mana kontrak berperan, dan failure apa saja yang harus dipikirkan sejak awal.

# Seri 1/16 - Mental Model HTTP Communication antar Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membangun fondasi sebelum masuk ke OpenAPI Generator, Jersey Client, atau OpenFeign.

Masalah utamanya bukan “bagaimana call endpoint lain dari Java”, tetapi:

> **Bagaimana mendesain komunikasi HTTP antar microservice agar kontraknya jelas, failure-nya terprediksi, dan implementasinya tidak menyebar liar ke business logic.**

Dalam contract-first approach, OpenAPI menjadi **interface description** untuk HTTP API sehingga consumer dapat memahami kemampuan service tanpa harus membaca source code, dokumentasi tambahan, atau mengintip traffic runtime. Ini selaras dengan tujuan OpenAPI Specification: mendeskripsikan HTTP API secara language-agnostic dan machine-readable. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS, provider side biasanya dibangun dengan annotation seperti `@Path`, `@GET`, `@POST`, `@PUT`, `@DELETE`, `@PATCH`, dan sejenisnya; behavior resource ditentukan oleh HTTP method yang ditangani. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Di sisi consumer, Jakarta REST menyediakan Client API untuk mengakses REST resource dari aplikasi Java lain. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest-client/rest-client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Besar

```text id="8hu4h9"
Consumer Service
  |
  | 1. Business use case butuh data / command dari service lain
  v
Client Adapter / Gateway
  |
  | 2. Translate domain intent menjadi HTTP request
  v
Generated / Handwritten HTTP Client
  |
  | 3. Encode URL, method, headers, body, auth, timeout
  v
Network / Kubernetes / Ingress / Service Mesh
  |
  | 4. DNS, routing, TLS, connection pool, latency, failure
  v
Provider Service - Jersey/JAX-RS Resource
  |
  | 5. Validate request, execute use case, return response
  v
Provider Response
  |
  | 6. Decode success/error response
  v
Client Adapter / Gateway
  |
  | 7. Map external result/error into internal domain result
  v
Consumer Business Logic
```

Hal penting: **HTTP call bukan sekadar method call jarak jauh**. Ia melibatkan network, serialization, compatibility, timeout, retry, observability, security, dan ownership kontrak.

Kesalahan umum senior engineer baru saat onboarding adalah melihat client call seperti ini:

```java
customerClient.getCustomer(customerId)
```

Padahal realitas produksinya lebih dekat ke ini:

```text id="onw157"
getCustomer(customerId)
  may fail because:
  - DNS/service discovery gagal
  - target pod tidak ready
  - TLS/auth gagal
  - timeout
  - response schema berubah
  - error body tidak sesuai kontrak
  - downstream lambat
  - downstream partial outage
  - retry menggandakan command
  - correlation ID hilang
  - generated client tidak compatible dengan versi API
```

---

## 3. Core Concepts

| Konsep | Makna praktis | Risiko kalau diabaikan |
|---|---|---|
| **Provider** | Service yang expose HTTP API | API berubah tanpa sadar dan merusak consumer |
| **Consumer** | Service yang memanggil API service lain | Terlalu bergantung pada detail internal provider |
| **Contract** | OpenAPI spec: path, method, schema, status code, error, auth | Dokumentasi tidak sinkron dengan implementasi |
| **Client Adapter / Gateway** | Lapisan internal yang membungkus generated/manual client | Business logic tercemar detail HTTP |
| **Generated Client** | Client SDK hasil OpenAPI Generator | Bisa bocor ke domain layer jika tidak dibatasi |
| **Transport Failure** | Masalah network, DNS, TLS, timeout, connection reset | Error dianggap business error |
| **Application Failure** | `4xx`, `5xx`, validation error, conflict, not found | Semua error diperlakukan sama |
| **Compatibility** | Kemampuan provider berubah tanpa mematahkan consumer lama | Deploy independen antar microservice gagal |
| **Observability** | Log, metric, trace untuk downstream call | RCA sulit saat latency/error terjadi |
| **Resilience** | Timeout, retry, circuit breaker, bulkhead, fallback | Satu dependency rusak bisa menjatuhkan service lain |

---

## 4. Contract-first vs Code-first

```text id="twpngl"
CODE-FIRST
Jersey Resource Code
  -> generate OpenAPI from annotations
  -> consumer reads/generated client
  -> risk: contract follows implementation accidentally

CONTRACT-FIRST
OpenAPI Contract
  -> review contract
  -> generate server/client artifacts
  -> provider implements contract
  -> consumer integrates against contract
```

| Aspek | Code-first | Contract-first |
|---|---|---|
| Sumber kebenaran | Implementasi Java/Jersey | OpenAPI spec |
| Cocok untuk | Internal API kecil, tim tunggal, iterasi cepat | Multi-service, multi-team, API governance |
| Risiko | Annotation tidak lengkap, error/status code tidak terdokumentasi | Perlu disiplin review spec |
| Kekuatan | Cepat mulai | Stabil untuk integrasi |
| Dalam enterprise microservice | Bisa dipakai, tapi rawan drift | Lebih aman untuk dependency antar tim |

Untuk konteks Anda sebagai senior engineer di sistem enterprise, **contract-first lebih defensible** karena API menjadi artefak eksplisit yang bisa direview, dites, di-versioning, dan dipakai sebagai input code generation.

---

## 5. Tiga Jenis HTTP Communication

### A. Query Call

Consumer meminta data dari provider.

```text id="k129hx"
Order Service -> Customer Service
GET /customers/{customerId}
```

Karakteristik:

| Properti | Nilai umum |
|---|---|
| HTTP method | `GET` |
| Side effect | Tidak ada |
| Retry | Relatif aman, jika timeout/read failure |
| Idempotency key | Biasanya tidak perlu |
| Cache | Mungkin bisa |
| Failure handling | Bisa fallback/cache/degraded response |

Contoh use case:

```text id="l2stkn"
Order Service butuh customer profile
untuk menampilkan detail order.
```

---

### B. Command Call

Consumer meminta provider melakukan perubahan state.

```text id="xqixha"
Order Service -> Payment Service
POST /payments
```

Karakteristik:

| Properti | Nilai umum |
|---|---|
| HTTP method | `POST`, `PUT`, `PATCH`, `DELETE` |
| Side effect | Ada |
| Retry | Berbahaya jika tidak idempotent |
| Idempotency key | Sangat disarankan untuk command sensitif |
| Cache | Tidak |
| Failure handling | Harus jelas: unknown, accepted, rejected, duplicate |

Contoh use case:

```text id="6f96ld"
Order Service meminta Payment Service
membuat transaksi pembayaran.
```

Bahaya utama: consumer menerima timeout, lalu retry, padahal provider sebenarnya sudah memproses command pertama.

---

### C. Workflow / Orchestration Call

HTTP call terjadi sebagai bagian dari proses panjang, misalnya Camunda delegate, worker, atau orchestrator service.

```text id="mya4lz"
Camunda Process
  -> Java Delegate / External Worker
      -> call Customer Service
      -> call Risk Service
      -> call Notification Service
```

Karakteristik:

| Properti | Nilai umum |
|---|---|
| Latency tolerance | Lebih panjang, tapi tetap harus bounded |
| Retry | Harus sinkron dengan retry policy workflow |
| State | Ada process instance / business key |
| Failure | Bisa menjadi BPMN error, incident, retry, compensation |
| Observability | Harus bawa process instance id / business key / correlation id |

Di sini, HTTP timeout dan retry tidak boleh didesain terpisah dari Camunda retry cycle. Kalau tidak, retry bisa terjadi di dua level: client retry dan workflow retry.

---

## 6. Layering yang Sehat di Java/Jersey Microservice

```text id="sf7ebc"
JAX-RS Resource
  |
  v
Application Service / Use Case
  |
  v
Domain Service
  |
  v
Downstream Gateway Interface
  |
  v
HTTP Client Adapter
  |
  v
Generated Jersey Client / OpenFeign Client / Manual Jersey Client
```

### Contoh struktur package

```text id="s9u9ka"
com.company.order
  ├── api
  │   └── OrderResource.java
  ├── application
  │   └── SubmitOrderUseCase.java
  ├── domain
  │   └── Order.java
  ├── infrastructure
  │   ├── customer
  │   │   ├── CustomerGateway.java
  │   │   ├── CustomerHttpGateway.java
  │   │   └── generated
  │   │       ├── api
  │   │       ├── model
  │   │       └── invoker
  │   └── payment
  │       ├── PaymentGateway.java
  │       ├── PaymentHttpGateway.java
  │       └── generated
  └── persistence
      └── mybatis
```

### Prinsip penting

| Prinsip | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Business logic tidak tahu HTTP detail | Use case memanggil `CustomerGateway`, bukan `ApiClient` langsung |
| Generated code dikurung | Jangan sebar generated DTO ke seluruh aplikasi |
| Error external diterjemahkan | `404 customer` bisa menjadi `CustomerNotFound`, bukan raw HTTP exception |
| Config runtime dipusatkan | Base URL, timeout, auth, retry tidak hardcode di use case |
| Observability masuk adapter | Log/metric/trace downstream call ada di satu tempat |

---

## 7. Peran Jersey Client, OpenAPI Generator, dan OpenFeign

| Komponen | Peran | Kapan dipakai |
|---|---|---|
| **Jersey/JAX-RS Resource** | Provider HTTP endpoint | Saat service expose REST API |
| **OpenAPI spec** | Kontrak API | Sebelum provider/consumer implementasi |
| **OpenAPI Generator** | Generate Java client/server/model dari spec | Saat ingin contract-first automation |
| **Jersey Client** | Programmatic HTTP client berbasis Jakarta REST/JAX-RS | Cocok jika stack utama sudah Jersey/JAX-RS |
| **OpenFeign** | Declarative HTTP client berbasis interface | Cocok jika ingin client interface ringkas |
| **Client Adapter** | Boundary antara business logic dan HTTP client | Hampir selalu diperlukan untuk service serius |

OpenAPI Generator Java generator mendukung pembuatan Java client library dan mencantumkan beberapa HTTP library seperti Jersey, Retrofit, dan OpenFeign. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jersey sendiri menyediakan API untuk menyederhanakan development RESTful service dan client. ([Jersey](https://jersey.github.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Feign berfokus membuat Java HTTP client lebih mudah melalui decoder dan error handling yang dapat dikustomisasi. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openfeign/feign?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. HTTP Call Lifecycle

```text id="0gpm7v"
1. Build request
   - method
   - URL
   - path param
   - query param
   - headers
   - body

2. Serialize request
   - DTO -> JSON
   - date/time format
   - enum format
   - nullable handling

3. Send over network
   - DNS
   - connection pool
   - TLS
   - proxy/ingress/service mesh
   - timeout

4. Provider receives request
   - routing
   - auth
   - validation
   - use case execution

5. Provider returns response
   - status code
   - headers
   - response body / error body

6. Consumer decodes response
   - JSON -> DTO
   - error mapping
   - exception mapping

7. Adapter maps external result
   - external DTO -> internal model
   - HTTP error -> domain/application error
```

Failure bisa terjadi di setiap tahap. Karena itu, mental model yang benar bukan:

```text id="24eig4"
call service -> get result
```

Melainkan:

```text id="e57q19"
prepare request
  -> maybe fail locally
send request
  -> maybe fail in transport
provider executes
  -> maybe succeed, fail, or unknown
decode response
  -> maybe fail due to schema mismatch
map result
  -> maybe fail due to semantic mismatch
```

---

## 9. Failure Taxonomy

| Failure class | Contoh | Biasanya ditangani di mana | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Local configuration failure | Base URL kosong, invalid timeout, missing token provider | Startup validation / config layer | Fail fast saat service start |
| Serialization failure | DTO tidak bisa jadi JSON, enum tidak dikenal | Client adapter / serializer config | Bisa menandakan contract drift |
| DNS / routing failure | Kubernetes service name salah, network policy block | HTTP client / platform | Jangan retry terlalu agresif |
| Connect timeout | Tidak bisa connect ke host/port | HTTP client | Biasanya dependency unavailable |
| Read timeout | Provider lambat memberi response | HTTP client | Result command bisa unknown |
| TLS/auth failure | Certificate invalid, token expired | Security/client layer | Jangan treat sebagai business failure |
| `400 Bad Request` | Request consumer invalid | Adapter/application | Biasanya bug consumer atau contract mismatch |
| `401/403` | Unauthorized/forbidden | Security boundary | Bisa token propagation issue |
| `404 Not Found` | Resource tidak ditemukan | Domain mapping | Bisa valid business outcome |
| `409 Conflict` | Version conflict, duplicate command, state conflict | Domain/application | Penting untuk concurrency control |
| `422 Unprocessable Entity` | Valid secara syntax, invalid secara business validation | Application mapping | Berguna untuk validation detail |
| `429 Too Many Requests` | Rate limited | Resilience layer | Honor retry-after jika ada |
| `500` | Provider internal error | Adapter/resilience | Jangan expose mentah ke caller |
| `502/503/504` | Gateway/upstream unavailable/timeout | Platform/resilience | Umum di Kubernetes/ingress/service mesh |
| Schema mismatch | Field berubah, type berubah, error body beda | Decoder/contract test | Ini alasan contract testing penting |

---

## 10. Timeout Mental Model

Minimal pikirkan tiga timeout:

| Timeout | Arti | Contoh akibat jika salah |
|---|---|---|
| Connect timeout | Maksimum waktu membuka koneksi | Terlalu panjang membuat thread tertahan saat dependency mati |
| Read/request timeout | Maksimum waktu menunggu response | Terlalu panjang memperbesar latency cascading |
| Overall deadline | Batas total use case | HTTP retry bisa melebihi SLA request utama |

Rule praktis:

```text id="oaa61x"
caller deadline
  > downstream timeout
    > per-attempt timeout
      > connect timeout
```

Contoh:

```text id="nnpf16"
API utama punya SLA internal 2 detik
  -> downstream customer lookup maksimal 500 ms
  -> retry maksimal 1x
  -> connect timeout 100 ms
  -> read timeout 300 ms
```

Jangan membiarkan default timeout menjadi kebijakan implisit. Di production, default sering tidak sesuai dengan kebutuhan service.

---

## 11. Retry Mental Model

Retry bukan “obat universal”. Retry hanya aman jika operasi aman untuk diulang atau memiliki mekanisme idempotency.

| Jenis call | Retry? | Syarat |
|---|---:|---|
| `GET /customers/{id}` | Biasanya boleh | Timeout/network error, small bounded retry |
| `POST /payments` | Berbahaya | Harus ada idempotency key / command id |
| `PUT /resources/{id}` | Lebih aman | Jika request merepresentasikan target state |
| `PATCH /resources/{id}` | Hati-hati | Bergantung semantic patch |
| `DELETE /resources/{id}` | Bisa aman, bisa tidak | Harus jelas apakah idempotent |
| Workflow external call | Hati-hati | Sinkron dengan retry Camunda/process engine |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="400xpc"
retry all 5xx and timeout 3 times
untuk semua endpoint
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="7k2nhv"
retry policy per operation:
  - query: bounded retry with backoff
  - command: retry only with idempotency key
  - workflow: coordinate with process retry
```

---

## 12. Status Code Mental Model

| Status | Interpretasi praktis |
|---|---|
| `200 OK` | Request sukses, response body biasanya ada |
| `201 Created` | Resource berhasil dibuat |
| `202 Accepted` | Command diterima tapi proses belum selesai |
| `204 No Content` | Sukses tanpa body |
| `400 Bad Request` | Request invalid secara syntax/contract |
| `401 Unauthorized` | Caller belum authenticated |
| `403 Forbidden` | Caller authenticated tapi tidak berhak |
| `404 Not Found` | Resource tidak ditemukan |
| `409 Conflict` | State conflict, duplicate, optimistic locking issue |
| `422 Unprocessable Entity` | Business/validation error pada payload yang syntactically valid |
| `429 Too Many Requests` | Rate limit |
| `500 Internal Server Error` | Bug/error internal provider |
| `502 Bad Gateway` | Gateway/proxy gagal ke upstream |
| `503 Service Unavailable` | Provider tidak tersedia |
| `504 Gateway Timeout` | Gateway timeout menunggu upstream |

Yang penting bukan sekadar hafal status code, tetapi membuat **status code matrix per operation** di OpenAPI.

Contoh:

```text id="fb5mxu"
POST /orders
  201 -> order created
  400 -> malformed request
  401 -> missing/invalid auth
  403 -> not allowed to create order
  409 -> duplicate client request id
  422 -> business validation failed
  500 -> unexpected provider error
  503 -> dependency unavailable
```

---

## 13. Error Contract Mental Model

Jangan membiarkan setiap service membuat format error sendiri.

Buruk:

```json id="1j7opf"
{
  "message": "Something went wrong"
}
```

Lebih baik:

```json id="0tgt7w"
{
  "errorCode": "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND",
  "message": "Customer was not found.",
  "correlationId": "8d2c9b1a",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "customerId",
      "reason": "No customer exists for the given id."
    }
  ]
}
```

Untuk enterprise microservice, error contract minimal perlu:

| Field | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| `errorCode` | Machine-readable error |
| `message` | Human-readable explanation |
| `correlationId` | Trace debugging |
| `details` | Field-level atau business-level details |
| `timestamp` | Optional, membantu audit/log |
| `path` | Optional, endpoint yang gagal |

---

## 14. Data Mapping Boundary

Dalam stack Anda, boundary data biasanya seperti ini:

```text id="xm8ed9"
External JSON
  -> generated DTO
    -> adapter mapping
      -> internal domain/application model
        -> MyBatis persistence model / SQL parameter
          -> PostgreSQL row / function result
```

Jangan disingkat menjadi:

```text id="8n2lzq"
External JSON
  -> generated DTO
    -> MyBatis mapper
```

Kenapa?

| Masalah | Dampak |
|---|---|
| DTO eksternal dipakai langsung ke persistence | Perubahan API memaksa perubahan DB mapping |
| Generated class tersebar ke domain | Refactor contract menjadi breaking internal code |
| Field eksternal dianggap domain truth | Business invariant melemah |
| Nullability external tidak dikendalikan | NPE atau invalid domain state |
| Enum external langsung dipakai internal | Enum expansion dari provider bisa merusak consumer |

---

## 15. Observability Mental Model

Setiap downstream HTTP call harus bisa menjawab:

```text id="0g5s2j"
Siapa memanggil siapa?
Endpoint apa?
Berapa latency-nya?
Status code apa?
Error class apa?
Correlation ID apa?
Trace ID apa?
Retry berapa kali?
Timeout atau provider error?
```

Minimal data observability:

| Data | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Downstream service | `customer-service` |
| Operation | `GET /customers/{customerId}` atau `getCustomerById` |
| Status class | `2xx`, `4xx`, `5xx`, `timeout`, `connect_error` |
| Latency | `127ms` |
| Attempt count | `1`, `2`, `3` |
| Correlation ID | Dari inbound request diteruskan |
| Trace context | `traceparent` / OpenTelemetry context |
| Error code | Dari error response provider |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="rlts6w"
log.error("Failed calling customer service", e)
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="4q5cq5"
downstream=customer-service
operation=getCustomerById
status=504
latencyMs=501
attempt=2
correlationId=...
errorClass=DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT
```

---

## 16. Security Context Propagation

HTTP communication antar microservice biasanya membawa salah satu atau kombinasi berikut:

| Model | Makna |
|---|---|
| End-user token propagation | Token user diteruskan ke downstream |
| Service-to-service token | Service punya credential sendiri |
| Token exchange | Token user ditukar menjadi token scoped untuk downstream |
| mTLS | Identitas service dibuktikan melalui certificate |
| API key/internal secret | Sederhana, tapi kurang ideal untuk sistem besar |
| Tenant/user context header | Context authorization/audit diteruskan eksplisit |

Pertanyaan desain penting:

```text id="evfyyb"
Apakah downstream harus tahu user asli,
atau cukup tahu service caller?
```

Untuk regulatory/case-management system, biasanya audit trail memerlukan minimal:

```text id="9yw06b"
actor user id
service caller id
tenant/org id
correlation id
business transaction id
```

---

## 17. Contract-first Checklist untuk HTTP Call

Sebelum implement client call, pastikan ini ada:

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Operation jelas | Ini query, command, atau workflow call? |
| Endpoint jelas | Path dan method sudah benar? |
| Request schema jelas | Required/optional/nullability sudah eksplisit? |
| Response schema jelas | Success body dan empty body jelas? |
| Error schema jelas | Semua error penting punya code dan body? |
| Status code matrix jelas | `400`, `401`, `403`, `404`, `409`, `422`, `500`, `503` perlu atau tidak? |
| Timeout jelas | Connect/read/deadline sudah ditentukan? |
| Retry jelas | Aman diulang atau perlu idempotency key? |
| Auth jelas | Token/header/security scheme sudah disepakati? |
| Observability jelas | Correlation ID, trace, metrics, logs tersedia? |
| Versioning jelas | Perubahan nanti akan compatible atau breaking? |
| Adapter jelas | Generated client tidak langsung dipakai business logic? |

---

## 18. Contoh Mini Design

Misal `Order Service` perlu memanggil `Customer Service`.

### Naive design

```java id="g4ypja"
CustomerDto customer = customerApi.getCustomer(customerId);
```

Masalah:

| Masalah | Kenapa buruk |
|---|---|
| Tidak jelas timeout | Bisa menggantung lama |
| Tidak jelas error mapping | `404` bisa menjadi exception generic |
| Tidak jelas observability | Sulit trace dependency latency |
| Generated DTO bocor | Domain order bergantung pada external DTO |
| Tidak jelas retry | Timeout bisa langsung gagal atau retry sembarangan |

### Better design

```java id="h31vda"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

```java id="mr6ssr"
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {
    private final GeneratedCustomerApi api;
    private final CustomerMapper mapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy policy;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId) {
        // apply timeout/retry/trace/log/error mapping here
        // map generated DTO into internal model
        return Optional.empty();
    }
}
```

Business use case cukup tahu:

```java id="m9t4a4"
Optional<CustomerProfile> customer =
    customerGateway.findCustomerById(order.customerId());
```

---

## 19. Practical Skill Map Seri 1

| Skill | Level onboarding | Yang harus bisa Anda lakukan |
|---|---|---|
| Membaca API dependency graph | Wajib | Tahu service ini call siapa dan dipanggil siapa |
| Mengklasifikasi call | Wajib | Bedakan query, command, workflow |
| Membaca OpenAPI sebagai kontrak | Wajib | Pahami path, schema, response, error |
| Membaca generated client | Wajib | Tahu class API, model, invoker, config |
| Membaca Jersey Client config | Wajib | Cari timeout, base URL, filter, auth |
| Membaca Feign config | Wajib | Cari encoder, decoder, error decoder, retryer |
| Menilai retry safety | Wajib | Tahu operasi mana yang boleh retry |
| Menilai timeout safety | Wajib | Tahu call mana yang bisa menggantung |
| Menilai error mapping | Wajib | Tahu 4xx/5xx dipetakan ke apa |
| Menilai observability | Wajib | Tahu apakah downstream call terlihat di logs/metrics/traces |
| Membaca CI contract check | Lanjutan | Tahu apakah breaking change dideteksi |
| Mendesain API compatibility | Lanjutan | Tahu perubahan mana yang aman dan tidak |

---

## 20. Red Flags Saat Review Codebase Baru

Cari tanda-tanda ini di repository:

| Red flag | Dampak |
|---|---|
| `new Client()` tersebar di banyak class | Config HTTP tidak konsisten |
| Base URL hardcoded | Sulit deploy multi-env |
| Timeout tidak eksplisit | Risiko thread exhaustion |
| Retry global untuk semua method | Risiko duplicate command |
| Generated DTO masuk domain/persistence | Coupling tinggi |
| Catch `Exception` lalu return null | Failure hilang |
| Semua non-2xx dianggap sama | Business error dan system error tercampur |
| Tidak ada correlation ID propagation | Sulit debugging |
| Error body tidak distandardisasi | Consumer parsing brittle |
| OpenAPI tidak dipakai di CI | Contract drift |
| Consumer bergantung pada undocumented field | Breaking change tersembunyi |
| Test hanya mock Java interface | Tidak memvalidasi HTTP contract nyata |

---

## 21. Pertanyaan Onboarding yang Harus Anda Tanyakan

Saat masuk ke tim baru, tanyakan ini:

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| API ownership | Siapa owner kontrak OpenAPI tiap service? |
| Spec location | OpenAPI spec disimpan di repo service, repo terpisah, atau registry? |
| Generation | Client digenerate saat build atau di-commit? |
| Versioning | Bagaimana versi API dan versi client artifact dikelola? |
| Compatibility | Apakah ada API diff check di CI? |
| Runtime | Library utama: Jersey Client, OpenFeign, Apache HttpClient, OkHttp, atau campuran? |
| Timeout | Apakah ada default timeout standard perusahaan? |
| Retry | Apakah retry per endpoint atau global? |
| Idempotency | Command endpoint memakai idempotency key? |
| Auth | Token user diteruskan atau service token? |
| Observability | Apakah downstream call punya metrics/tracing? |
| Testing | Ada contract test, mock server, WireMock, integration test? |
| Kubernetes | Service discovery pakai DNS internal, ingress, service mesh, atau API gateway? |

---

## 22. Ringkasan Mental Model

```text id="noz28t"
HTTP microservice communication =
  contract
  + generated/manual client
  + adapter boundary
  + serialization
  + timeout
  + retry/idempotency
  + auth propagation
  + error mapping
  + observability
  + compatibility governance
```

Kalau hanya fokus pada “cara call endpoint”, desain akan rapuh.

Kalau fokus pada **contract + failure + boundary**, komunikasi antar microservice menjadi lebih stabil, bisa dites, dan lebih mudah dioperasikan.

7/13/2026, 7:07:27 AM

# Seri 2/16 - Contract-first Approach dengan OpenAPI

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan utama:

> **Bagaimana menjadikan OpenAPI sebagai source of truth untuk komunikasi HTTP antar microservice, bukan sekadar dokumentasi tambahan?**

Dalam contract-first approach, urutannya bukan:

```text
Tulis Jersey Resource
  -> generate dokumentasi
  -> consumer menyesuaikan
```

Tetapi:

```text
Desain OpenAPI contract
  -> review kontrak
  -> generate client/server artifact
  -> provider implement
  -> consumer integrate
  -> CI menjaga compatibility
```

OpenAPI Specification mendefinisikan standar formal dan language-agnostic untuk mendeskripsikan HTTP APIs, sehingga manusia maupun komputer dapat memahami kemampuan service tanpa perlu akses ke source code, dokumentasi tambahan, atau inspeksi traffic. Versi official terbaru yang saya temukan saat pengecekan adalah **OpenAPI Specification v3.2.0**, diterbitkan **19 September 2025**. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Contract-first

```text
Business Capability
  |
  v
API Contract Design
  |
  v
OpenAPI YAML/JSON
  |
  +--> Human review
  +--> Client SDK generation
  +--> Server stub/interface generation
  +--> Mock server
  +--> Contract testing
  +--> API documentation
  +--> CI breaking-change check
  +--> Governance / API catalog
```

Kontrak yang baik tidak hanya menjawab:

```text
Endpoint-nya apa?
```

Tetapi juga:

```text
Apa resource/command/query-nya?
Request body-nya apa?
Response body-nya apa?
Error body-nya apa?
Status code apa saja yang valid?
Auth scheme apa?
Header operasional apa?
Field mana required?
Field mana nullable?
Perubahan apa yang breaking?
Bagaimana consumer harus handle failure?
```

---

## 3. Kenapa Contract-first Penting di Microservice

| Masalah umum | Contract-first membantu dengan |
|---|---|
| Consumer menebak payload dari contoh response | Schema formal |
| Dokumentasi berbeda dari implementasi | Spec dipakai untuk generate/test |
| Provider deploy change yang merusak consumer | API diff dan compatibility gate |
| Setiap service punya error format sendiri | Reusable error schema |
| Client HTTP ditulis manual dan tidak konsisten | Generated client SDK |
| Consumer tidak tahu semua status code | Response matrix di OpenAPI |
| Testing baru bisa dilakukan setelah provider selesai | Mock server dari kontrak |
| Onboarding engineer lambat | Satu artefak kontrak yang bisa dibaca lintas tim |
| Integrasi antar tim sering friction | Contract review sebelum implementation |

OpenAPI Initiative juga menekankan bahwa OpenAPI memungkinkan pemahaman API, generasi client code, pembuatan test, dan penerapan design standard. ([OpenAPI Initiative](https://www.openapis.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Contract-first Lifecycle

```text
1. Domain/API discovery
   - use case
   - actor
   - resource
   - command/query
   - ownership

2. Contract drafting
   - OpenAPI paths
   - schemas
   - responses
   - errors
   - security
   - examples

3. Contract review
   - API naming
   - compatibility
   - error semantics
   - validation
   - observability headers

4. Artifact generation
   - Java client
   - DTO/model
   - server interface/stub
   - documentation
   - mock server

5. Implementation
   - Jersey/JAX-RS resource
   - validation
   - mapper
   - service layer
   - exception mapper

6. Consumer integration
   - generated client
   - adapter/gateway
   - timeout/retry/auth
   - error mapping

7. Verification
   - unit test
   - contract test
   - schema validation
   - integration test

8. Release governance
   - versioning
   - API diff
   - deprecation
   - client artifact release
```

---

## 5. Source of Truth: Apa yang Harus Ada di OpenAPI

Minimal kontrak harus memuat:

| Elemen | Harus menjelaskan | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `info` | Nama API, versi, owner/description | `Order API`, `1.4.0` |
| `servers` | Base URL logical/environment | dev, staging, prod |
| `paths` | Endpoint dan method | `GET /orders/{orderId}` |
| `operationId` | Nama operasi stabil | `getOrderById` |
| `parameters` | Path/query/header parameter | `orderId`, `page`, `sort` |
| `requestBody` | Payload untuk command | `CreateOrderRequest` |
| `responses` | Status code dan schema | `200`, `400`, `404`, `409`, `500` |
| `components.schemas` | DTO reusable | `OrderResponse`, `ProblemDetail` |
| `components.securitySchemes` | Auth model | bearer JWT, mTLS, API key |
| `examples` | Contoh request/response | valid dan invalid example |
| `tags` | Grouping operation | `Orders`, `Customers` |

---

## 6. Contoh OpenAPI Contract Kecil

Contoh endpoint query:

```yaml
openapi: 3.1.0
info:
  title: Customer API
  version: 1.0.0

paths:
  /customers/{customerId}:
    get:
      tags:
        - Customers
      operationId: getCustomerById
      summary: Get customer by id
      parameters:
        - name: customerId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            minLength: 1
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Customer found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/CustomerResponse'
        '400':
          description: Invalid request
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '404':
          description: Customer not found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '500':
          description: Unexpected server error
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

components:
  schemas:
    CustomerResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - customerId
        - displayName
        - status
      properties:
        customerId:
          type: string
        displayName:
          type: string
        status:
          type: string
          enum:
            - ACTIVE
            - SUSPENDED
            - CLOSED

    ErrorResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - errorCode
        - message
        - correlationId
      properties:
        errorCode:
          type: string
        message:
          type: string
        correlationId:
          type: string
        details:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorDetail'

    ErrorDetail:
      type: object
      properties:
        field:
          type: string
        reason:
          type: string
```

Yang penting dari contoh ini bukan YAML-nya, tetapi kontraknya eksplisit:

```text
GET /customers/{customerId}
  200 -> CustomerResponse
  400 -> ErrorResponse
  404 -> ErrorResponse
  500 -> ErrorResponse
```

Consumer tidak perlu menebak response shape.

---

## 7. Contract-first di Jersey/JAX-RS Application

Di provider side, implementasi Jersey/JAX-RS sebaiknya mengikuti kontrak.

```text
OpenAPI Contract
  |
  v
Generated / reviewed interface or DTO
  |
  v
Jersey Resource
  |
  v
Application Service
  |
  v
Domain / Persistence
```

Contoh resource manual yang mengikuti kontrak:

```java
@Path("/customers")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CustomerResource {

    private final CustomerApplicationService customerService;

    public CustomerResource(CustomerApplicationService customerService) {
        this.customerService = customerService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{customerId}")
    public Response getCustomerById(@PathParam("customerId") String customerId) {
        return customerService.findCustomer(customerId)
                .map(customer -> Response.ok(CustomerResponse.from(customer)).build())
                .orElseGet(() -> Response.status(Response.Status.NOT_FOUND)
                        .entity(ErrorResponse.of(
                                "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND",
                                "Customer was not found."
                        ))
                        .build());
    }
}
```

Masalah yang harus Anda cek:

| Area | Cek |
|---|---|
| Status code | Apakah resource mengembalikan status sesuai OpenAPI? |
| Response body | Apakah body sesuai schema? |
| Error response | Apakah semua error memakai model standard? |
| Validation | Apakah `required`, `minLength`, `pattern`, enum sesuai runtime validation? |
| Content type | Apakah `application/json` konsisten? |
| Exception mapper | Apakah exception tidak bocor sebagai HTML/plain text? |

---

## 8. Contract-first di Consumer Side

Consumer sebaiknya tidak langsung mengikat business logic ke generated client.

```text
Generated CustomerApi
  |
  v
CustomerHttpGateway
  |
  v
CustomerGateway interface
  |
  v
Order Use Case
```

Contoh:

```java
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(String customerId);
}
```

```java
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomerApi customerApi;

    public CustomerHttpGateway(CustomerApi customerApi) {
        this.customerApi = customerApi;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(String customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response = customerApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
            return Optional.of(map(response));
        } catch (ApiException e) {
            if (e.getCode() == 404) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            throw new DownstreamServiceException("CUSTOMER_SERVICE_FAILED", e);
        }
    }

    private CustomerProfile map(CustomerResponse response) {
        return new CustomerProfile(
                response.getCustomerId(),
                response.getDisplayName(),
                response.getStatus()
        );
    }
}
```

Perhatikan boundary-nya:

| Layer | Boleh tahu generated client? |
|---|---:|
| HTTP adapter | Ya |
| Application service | Sebaiknya tidak |
| Domain model | Tidak |
| MyBatis mapper | Tidak |
| PL/pgSQL boundary | Tidak |
| Camunda process variable model | Sebaiknya tidak langsung |

---

## 9. OpenAPI Generator dalam Contract-first

OpenAPI Generator dapat menghasilkan client libraries, server stubs, documentation, dan configuration dari OpenAPI document. Dokumentasi Maven plugin-nya menunjukkan plugin `openapi-generator-maven-plugin` dengan goal `generate`, dan default phase-nya adalah `generate-sources`. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/plugins/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh struktur Maven sederhana:

```xml
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.openapitools</groupId>
    <artifactId>openapi-generator-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>7.23.0</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>generate-customer-client</id>
            <goals>
                <goal>generate</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <inputSpec>${project.basedir}/src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml</inputSpec>
                <generatorName>java</generatorName>
                <library>jersey3</library>
                <apiPackage>com.company.customer.client.api</apiPackage>
                <modelPackage>com.company.customer.client.model</modelPackage>
                <invokerPackage>com.company.customer.client.invoker</invokerPackage>
                <generateApiTests>false</generateApiTests>
                <generateModelTests>false</generateModelTests>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

OpenAPI Generator Java generator mendukung banyak opsi konfigurasi untuk generated Java client, termasuk pengaturan package, enum naming, dynamic operations, publication of input spec, dan lain-lain. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Catatan praktis: versi plugin di atas mengikuti dokumentasi yang saya temukan saat pengecekan; di repo perusahaan Anda, tetap ikuti versi yang sudah distandardisasi oleh platform/build engineering.

---

## 10. Generated Artifact yang Umum

```text
target/generated-sources/openapi
  ├── src/main/java
  │   └── com/company/customer/client
  │       ├── api
  │       │   └── CustomersApi.java
  │       ├── model
  │       │   ├── CustomerResponse.java
  │       │   └── ErrorResponse.java
  │       └── invoker
  │           ├── ApiClient.java
  │           ├── ApiException.java
  │           └── Configuration.java
```

| Artifact | Fungsi | Boleh dipakai langsung? |
|---|---|---:|
| `ApiClient` | Runtime HTTP client config | Hanya di infrastructure |
| `CustomersApi` | Method per operation | Hanya di adapter |
| `CustomerResponse` | Generated DTO | Jangan bocor ke domain |
| `ErrorResponse` | Generated error DTO | Map ke internal exception/result |
| `ApiException` | HTTP/client exception | Jangan bocor ke application service |
| Generated tests | Smoke/example test | Bisa dimatikan jika tidak berguna |

---

## 11. Contract-first dengan Jersey Client vs OpenFeign

| Aspek | Jersey Client generated | OpenFeign generated/manual |
|---|---|---|
| Style | Programmatic / JAX-RS ecosystem | Declarative interface |
| Cocok jika | Service sudah dominan Jersey/JAX-RS | Ingin client ringkas seperti method interface |
| Config penting | `ClientConfig`, filters, connector, timeout | Encoder, decoder, error decoder, retryer |
| Risiko | Boilerplate atau generated invoker kompleks | Magic annotation/decoder jika tidak distandardisasi |
| Boundary terbaik | Tetap bungkus dalam adapter | Tetap bungkus dalam adapter |

Feign memproses annotation menjadi request template dan menyediakan mekanisme decoder serta error handling yang dapat dikustomisasi. ([GitHub](https://github.com/OpenAPITools/openapi-generator/releases?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Contract Review Checklist

Sebelum OpenAPI spec dianggap siap, review ini:

| Review area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Resource design | Apakah path merepresentasikan resource/action dengan jelas? |
| Operation naming | Apakah `operationId` unik dan stabil? |
| Method semantics | Apakah `GET/POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE` tepat? |
| Request schema | Apakah required/optional/nullable jelas? |
| Response schema | Apakah success response lengkap? |
| Error response | Apakah error format standard? |
| Status code matrix | Apakah semua status penting didefinisikan? |
| Pagination | Jika list endpoint, apakah pagination jelas? |
| Sorting/filtering | Apakah query semantics eksplisit? |
| Idempotency | Jika command sensitif, apakah ada idempotency key? |
| Auth | Apakah security scheme jelas? |
| Headers | Apakah correlation/trace/tenant header disepakati? |
| Compatibility | Apakah perubahan dari versi sebelumnya aman? |
| Examples | Apakah ada contoh valid dan invalid? |
| Generator impact | Apakah nama schema/operation menghasilkan Java class/method yang baik? |

---

## 13. Breaking Change Mental Model

Contract-first tidak berguna kalau perubahan kontrak tidak dijaga.

| Perubahan | Umumnya compatible? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Menambah optional response field | Ya | Consumer lama mengabaikan field baru |
| Menambah required request field | Tidak | Consumer lama tidak mengirim field |
| Menghapus response field | Tidak | Consumer lama mungkin bergantung |
| Mengubah type field | Tidak | Decoder bisa gagal |
| Rename field | Tidak | Sama seperti remove + add |
| Menambah enum value | Hati-hati | Banyak generated client tidak siap enum unknown |
| Menghapus enum value | Tidak | Consumer mungkin masih mengirim/menerima |
| Menambah endpoint baru | Ya | Tidak memengaruhi consumer lama |
| Mengubah status code success | Hati-hati | Consumer mungkin hanya handle `200` |
| Mengubah error body | Tidak | Error decoder consumer bisa rusak |
| Menambah optional query param | Ya | Jika default behavior tidak berubah |
| Mengubah default behavior | Hati-hati | Bisa semantic breaking |

---

## 14. Contract-first CI Pipeline

```text
Pull Request
  |
  v
OpenAPI lint
  |
  v
Schema validation
  |
  v
Breaking-change diff against main/released spec
  |
  v
Generate client/server code
  |
  v
Compile generated code
  |
  v
Run unit + contract tests
  |
  v
Publish spec/client artifact
```

Minimal pipeline yang sehat:

| Step | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Validate OpenAPI | Spec syntactically valid |
| Lint naming convention | Path, schema, operationId konsisten |
| Diff spec | Deteksi breaking change |
| Generate code | Pastikan spec bisa menjadi Java client |
| Compile generated code | Deteksi naming/type issue |
| Run mock/contract test | Pastikan implementasi sesuai spec |
| Publish artifact | Consumer memakai versi yang jelas |

---

## 15. API Contract sebagai Release Artifact

Jangan hanya treat OpenAPI sebagai file di repo. Idealnya ia menjadi artifact yang bisa dirilis.

```text
customer-api
  version 1.2.0
  ├── customer-api.yaml
  ├── customer-java-client-1.2.0.jar
  ├── generated docs
  └── changelog
```

| Artifact | Consumer memakai untuk |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI YAML | Review, mock, test, generate sendiri |
| Java client JAR | Integrasi langsung |
| Docs | Human reference |
| Changelog | Menilai perubahan |
| Compatibility report | Release confidence |

---

## 16. Naming Rules yang Praktis

### Path

```text
Good:
GET /customers/{customerId}
GET /customers/{customerId}/contacts
POST /orders
POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel

Avoid:
GET /getCustomer
POST /doCancelOrder
POST /process
```

### Operation ID

```text
Good:
getCustomerById
listCustomerContacts
createOrder
cancelOrder

Avoid:
get
postOrder
process
execute
operationUsingGET
```

### Schema

```text
Good:
CreateOrderRequest
OrderResponse
CancelOrderRequest
ErrorResponse
ValidationErrorDetail

Avoid:
OrderDto
Response
Request
Data
Payload
```

Kenapa penting: generator akan memakai nama schema dan operation untuk menghasilkan class/method Java. Nama yang buruk di OpenAPI akan menjadi API client yang buruk.

---

## 17. Contract-first dan Error Model

Sebaiknya setiap API punya reusable error schema.

```yaml
components:
  schemas:
    ErrorResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - errorCode
        - message
        - correlationId
      properties:
        errorCode:
          type: string
          example: CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND
        message:
          type: string
          example: Customer was not found.
        correlationId:
          type: string
          example: 8d2c9b1a7f6e4d1b
        details:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorDetail'

    ErrorDetail:
      type: object
      properties:
        field:
          type: string
          example: customerId
        reason:
          type: string
          example: must not be blank
```

Status code matrix:

```yaml
responses:
  '400':
    description: Invalid request
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
  '404':
    description: Customer not found
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
  '409':
    description: State conflict
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
  '500':
    description: Unexpected server error
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
```

---

## 18. Contract-first dan Idempotency

Untuk command endpoint yang bisa dipanggil ulang akibat timeout/retry, idempotency perlu menjadi bagian kontrak.

Contoh:

```yaml
/postings:
  post:
    operationId: createPosting
    parameters:
      - name: Idempotency-Key
        in: header
        required: true
        schema:
          type: string
          minLength: 16
          maxLength: 128
    requestBody:
      required: true
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreatePostingRequest'
    responses:
      '201':
        description: Posting created
      '409':
        description: Duplicate or conflicting request
```

Interpretasi:

```text
Consumer boleh retry POST
hanya karena request membawa Idempotency-Key
dan provider punya deduplication semantics.
```

---

## 19. Contract-first dan Observability Headers

Header operasional yang sering perlu distandardisasi:

| Header | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `Authorization` | Auth bearer token |
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Korelasi log antar service |
| `traceparent` | Distributed tracing W3C/OpenTelemetry |
| `Idempotency-Key` | Dedup command retry |
| `X-Tenant-Id` | Multi-tenant routing/audit |
| `X-User-Id` | Actor context jika policy mengizinkan |
| `X-Request-Source` | Identitas caller/service |

Jangan semua header dimasukkan sembarangan ke setiap endpoint. Bedakan:

```text
Security headers
Tracing headers
Business context headers
Idempotency headers
```

---

## 20. Anti-pattern Contract-first

| Anti-pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Spec dibuat setelah code selesai | Tetap code-first, hanya dokumentasi |
| OpenAPI tidak dipakai untuk generate/test | Spec mudah drift |
| Generated client dipakai langsung di use case | Coupling tinggi |
| Error response tidak distandardisasi | Consumer sulit handle error |
| Semua response `200` dengan `success=false` | HTTP semantics hilang |
| `operationId` auto-generated buruk | Client SDK tidak nyaman |
| Satu schema `GenericResponse` untuk semua | Type safety hilang |
| Field required tidak jelas | Consumer/provider beda asumsi |
| Enum dianggap selalu closed | Consumer rusak saat enum baru muncul |
| Tidak ada API diff di CI | Breaking change lolos |
| OpenAPI spec tidak punya owner | Governance tidak jalan |
| Versioning hanya di service image tag | API contract tidak terlacak |

---

## 21. Cara Membaca OpenAPI Saat Onboarding

Saat masuk repo baru, baca spec dengan urutan ini:

```text
1. info.title + info.version
2. servers
3. tags
4. paths by business capability
5. operationId
6. requestBody
7. responses
8. components.schemas
9. components.securitySchemes
10. shared error model
11. examples
12. vendor extensions / company conventions
```

Pertanyaan yang harus Anda jawab setelah membaca:

| Pertanyaan | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| Service ini expose capability apa? | Menentukan domain boundary |
| Endpoint mana query vs command? | Menentukan retry/idempotency |
| Error standard atau custom? | Menentukan error decoder |
| Ada versioning? | Menentukan compatibility strategy |
| Ada generated client artifact? | Menentukan cara consume |
| Ada breaking-change history? | Menilai governance maturity |
| Ada auth scheme jelas? | Menentukan integration security |
| Ada pagination/filtering convention? | Penting untuk list endpoint |
| Schema mirip DB table atau domain API? | Menilai coupling API-DB |

---

## 22. Practical Contract-first Template

Untuk endpoint baru, isi dulu template ini sebelum coding:

```text
Capability:
Owner service:
Consumer service(s):
Operation type: Query / Command / Workflow callback

HTTP method:
Path:
operationId:

Request:
  path params:
  query params:
  headers:
  body:

Success responses:
  200/201/202/204:

Error responses:
  400:
  401:
  403:
  404:
  409:
  422:
  429:
  500:
  503:

Idempotency:
Timeout expectation:
Retry policy:
Auth scheme:
Observability headers:
Compatibility notes:
Examples:
```

Kalau template ini belum bisa diisi, biasanya API design belum matang.

---

## 23. Decision Rules

| Situasi | Rule |
|---|---|
| Consumer butuh data | Mulai dari `GET` query endpoint |
| Consumer memerintahkan perubahan state | Gunakan command endpoint, pikirkan idempotency |
| Proses lama/asynchronous | Pertimbangkan `202 Accepted` + status resource/callback/event |
| Response bisa besar | Desain pagination/streaming sejak awal |
| Data bisa berubah bersamaan | Gunakan `409`, ETag, atau optimistic locking |
| Error perlu diproses mesin | Gunakan stable `errorCode` |
| API dipakai banyak service | Wajib generated client + CI diff |
| Endpoint hanya internal satu service pair | Tetap pakai kontrak, governance bisa lebih ringan |
| Contract berubah breaking | Naikkan versi, deprecate, jangan diam-diam replace |
| Butuh compatibility kuat | Hindari rename/remove/type change |

---

## 24. Ringkasan

Contract-first approach berarti:

```text
OpenAPI bukan catatan setelah coding.
OpenAPI adalah desain, kontrak, input generator, input test, dan artefak release.
```

Dalam Java/Jersey microservice, ini membantu menjaga boundary antara:

```text
API contract
  != Jersey resource implementation
  != generated client
  != domain model
  != MyBatis persistence model
```

Kunci senior-level-nya adalah memastikan kontrak mencakup:

```text
schema
status code
error model
auth
headers
compatibility
idempotency
observability
release governance
```
