## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:10:08 AM

# Seri 3/16 - OpenAPI Structure: Paths, Operations, Schemas, Responses

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada kemampuan praktis membaca dan menulis struktur OpenAPI.

Targetnya:

```text id="6n44gu"
Anda bisa membuka file openapi.yaml
lalu langsung memahami:
  - service ini expose endpoint apa
  - endpoint mana query vs command
  - request body-nya apa
  - response sukses dan error-nya apa
  - schema mana reusable
  - bagian mana berisiko breaking change
  - bagian mana memengaruhi generated Java client
```

OpenAPI adalah standard language-agnostic untuk mendeskripsikan HTTP API agar manusia dan komputer dapat memahami kemampuan service tanpa harus membaca source code, dokumentasi tambahan, atau traffic runtime. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Struktur Besar OpenAPI

File OpenAPI biasanya berbentuk YAML atau JSON.

```yaml id="eo0fkm"
openapi: 3.1.0

info:
  title: Order API
  version: 1.0.0

servers:
  - url: https://api.example.com

tags:
  - name: Orders

paths:
  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      operationId: getOrderById
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Order found

components:
  schemas:
    OrderResponse:
      type: object
      properties:
        orderId:
          type: string
```

Mental model-nya:

```text id="x6ras0"
OpenAPI document
  ├── metadata
  │   ├── openapi
  │   ├── info
  │   ├── servers
  │   └── tags
  │
  ├── API surface
  │   └── paths
  │       └── path item
  │           └── operation
  │               ├── parameters
  │               ├── requestBody
  │               └── responses
  │
  └── reusable components
      ├── schemas
      ├── responses
      ├── parameters
      ├── requestBodies
      ├── headers
      ├── examples
      └── securitySchemes
```

---

## 3. Bagian Metadata

| Bagian | Fungsi | Catatan praktis |
|---|---|---|
| `openapi` | Versi OpenAPI yang dipakai | Misalnya `3.0.3`, `3.1.0`, atau `3.2.0` |
| `info.title` | Nama API | Harus jelas: `Customer API`, bukan `Service API` |
| `info.version` | Versi kontrak API | Jangan disamakan mentah-mentah dengan image version |
| `info.description` | Penjelasan capability | Berguna saat API catalog/onboarding |
| `servers` | Base URL logical/environment | Jangan hardcode environment tunggal jika multi-env |
| `tags` | Grouping operation | Memengaruhi grouping docs dan generated client |

Catatan penting: saat saya cek sumber official, versi OpenAPI terbaru yang tersedia adalah **OpenAPI Specification v3.2.0**, diterbitkan 19 September 2025. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Dalam codebase perusahaan, Anda tetap harus mengikuti versi yang sudah distandardisasi oleh platform team, karena generator, linter, dan toolchain belum tentu langsung memakai versi terbaru.

---

## 4. `paths`: Permukaan API

`paths` adalah daftar endpoint relatif yang diekspos API. Dalam dokumentasi Swagger/OpenAPI, path merepresentasikan endpoint/resource seperti `/users`, sedangkan operation adalah HTTP method pada path tersebut seperti `GET`, `POST`, atau `DELETE`. ([Swagger](https://swagger.io/docs/specification/v3_0/paths-and-operations/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```yaml id="k23s37"
paths:
  /orders:
    get:
      operationId: listOrders
    post:
      operationId: createOrder

  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      operationId: getOrderById
    patch:
      operationId: updateOrder
    delete:
      operationId: deleteOrder
```

Mental model:

```text id="f7kdzs"
/orders
  GET  -> query collection
  POST -> create command

/orders/{orderId}
  GET    -> query single resource
  PATCH  -> partial update command
  DELETE -> delete/cancel command
```

Dalam Jersey/JAX-RS provider, konsep ini akan dipetakan ke annotation seperti `@Path`, `@GET`, `@POST`, `@PUT`, `@DELETE`, dan `@HEAD`; Jakarta REST memang menyediakan request method designator untuk HTTP method umum tersebut. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Path Design Rules

| Rule | Contoh baik | Hindari |
|---|---|---|
| Gunakan noun/resource | `/orders/{orderId}` | `/getOrder` |
| Collection plural | `/customers` | `/customerList` |
| Sub-resource jelas | `/orders/{orderId}/items` | `/getOrderItems` |
| Command action boleh jika benar-benar aksi domain | `/orders/{orderId}/cancel` | `/processOrder` |
| Path parameter bernama jelas | `{customerId}` | `{id}` jika ambigu |
| Jangan masukkan environment | `/orders` | `/prod/orders` |
| Jangan masukkan implementation detail | `/orders` | `/mybatis/orders` |
| Jangan expose table name mentah | `/orders` | `/tbl_order_hdr` |

Untuk enterprise system, command action seperti ini masih wajar:

```text id="64e88a"
POST /cases/{caseId}/assign
POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel
POST /work-items/{workItemId}/complete
```

Yang perlu dihindari adalah path generik:

```text id="94mycb"
POST /execute
POST /process
POST /do-action
```

Karena tidak memberi semantic contract yang jelas.

---

## 6. Operation Object

Operation adalah detail satu endpoint + method.

```yaml id="gek5z1"
paths:
  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      tags:
        - Orders
      operationId: getOrderById
      summary: Get order by id
      description: Returns the order identified by orderId.
      parameters:
        - name: orderId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Order found
```

| Field | Fungsi | Dampak ke Java |
|---|---|---|
| `tags` | Grouping operation | Bisa memengaruhi class API generated |
| `operationId` | Nama operasi unik | Sering menjadi nama method Java |
| `summary` | Ringkasan pendek | Untuk docs |
| `description` | Detail behavior | Untuk reviewer dan consumer |
| `parameters` | Path/query/header/cookie params | Menjadi method parameter |
| `requestBody` | Body request | Menjadi argument object |
| `responses` | Status code + body | Menentukan decode behavior |
| `security` | Security requirement per operation | Menentukan auth expectation |

OpenAPI Generator Java generator menyediakan banyak opsi yang memengaruhi output Java, termasuk package, enum naming, parameter uniqueness, dan publikasi input spec. Karena itu, nama `operationId`, schema, dan parameter harus dirancang dengan Java output dalam pikiran. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. `operationId`: Kecil tapi Sangat Penting

`operationId` sering menjadi nama method generated client.

Buruk:

```yaml id="xz3qot"
operationId: get
```

Akan menghasilkan method yang ambigu:

```java id="ej81qc"
api.get(...)
```

Lebih baik:

```yaml id="tcindh"
operationId: getOrderById
```

Generated client menjadi lebih readable:

```java id="hshvng"
ordersApi.getOrderById(orderId);
```

Rules:

| Rule | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Harus unik satu dokumen | `getOrderById`, `createOrder` |
| Gunakan verb + domain object | `listOrders`, `cancelOrder` |
| Jangan terlalu generik | Hindari `execute`, `process`, `submit` tanpa konteks |
| Stabil antar versi | Jangan rename tanpa alasan kuat |
| Pikirkan Java method name | Hindari karakter/nama aneh |

---

## 8. Parameters

OpenAPI parameter bisa berada di:

```text id="cxpngh"
path
query
header
cookie
```

Contoh:

```yaml id="1mqw7v"
parameters:
  - name: orderId
    in: path
    required: true
    schema:
      type: string
      minLength: 1

  - name: includeItems
    in: query
    required: false
    schema:
      type: boolean
      default: false

  - name: X-Correlation-Id
    in: header
    required: false
    schema:
      type: string
```

Mapping ke Jersey/JAX-RS:

```java id="vv1ddq"
@GET
@Path("/orders/{orderId}")
public Response getOrderById(
        @PathParam("orderId") String orderId,
        @QueryParam("includeItems") boolean includeItems,
        @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-Id") String correlationId
) {
    ...
}
```

---

## 9. Parameter Rules

| Parameter type | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Path parameter | Identitas resource | Biasanya required |
| Query parameter | Filtering, pagination, sorting, flags | Jangan pakai untuk payload kompleks |
| Header parameter | Auth, tracing, tenant, idempotency, conditional request | Jangan sembarang jadikan business field |
| Cookie parameter | Session/browser style | Jarang untuk internal microservice |

Contoh yang baik:

```text id="y5xaj1"
GET /orders?status=OPEN&page=0&size=50
GET /orders/{orderId}
POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel
Header: Idempotency-Key
```

Contoh yang buruk:

```text id="0r449a"
GET /orders?payload={large-json}
POST /orders/{orderId}?newStatus=CANCELLED&reason=...
Header: X-Order-Status
```

---

## 10. `requestBody`

`requestBody` dipakai untuk operasi yang menerima payload, biasanya `POST`, `PUT`, atau `PATCH`.

```yaml id="fd7d41"
paths:
  /orders:
    post:
      operationId: createOrder
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreateOrderRequest'
      responses:
        '201':
          description: Order created
```

Schema:

```yaml id="zrd5ic"
components:
  schemas:
    CreateOrderRequest:
      type: object
      required:
        - customerId
        - items
      properties:
        customerId:
          type: string
        items:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreateOrderItemRequest'
```

Rule penting:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Request command pakai object eksplisit | `CreateOrderRequest`, bukan `Order` |
| Jangan reuse response sebagai request | Request dan response punya lifecycle berbeda |
| Required field harus minimal tapi jelas | Jangan semua field optional tanpa alasan |
| Gunakan schema reusable | Agar generator menghasilkan DTO yang konsisten |
| Beri example | Memudahkan consumer dan mock |

---

## 11. `responses`

`responses` mendefinisikan semua kemungkinan status code yang valid untuk operation.

```yaml id="mk70lm"
responses:
  '201':
    description: Order created
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreateOrderResponse'

  '400':
    description: Invalid request
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

  '409':
    description: Duplicate or conflicting order
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

  '500':
    description: Unexpected server error
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
```

Mental model response:

```text id="37mo8t"
Operation
  ├── success responses
  │   ├── 200 OK
  │   ├── 201 Created
  │   ├── 202 Accepted
  │   └── 204 No Content
  │
  └── error responses
      ├── 400 invalid request
      ├── 401 unauthenticated
      ├── 403 unauthorized
      ├── 404 not found
      ├── 409 conflict
      ├── 422 business validation
      ├── 429 rate limit
      ├── 500 unexpected
      └── 503 unavailable
```

---

## 12. Status Code Design Matrix

| Operation type | Common success | Common client errors | Common system errors |
|---|---|---|---|
| Get by id | `200`, `404` | `400`, `401`, `403` | `500`, `503`, `504` |
| List/search | `200` | `400`, `401`, `403` | `500`, `503`, `504` |
| Create | `201`, `202` | `400`, `401`, `403`, `409`, `422` | `500`, `503`, `504` |
| Replace | `200`, `204` | `400`, `401`, `403`, `404`, `409`, `422` | `500`, `503`, `504` |
| Patch | `200`, `204` | `400`, `401`, `403`, `404`, `409`, `422` | `500`, `503`, `504` |
| Delete/cancel | `200`, `202`, `204` | `400`, `401`, `403`, `404`, `409` | `500`, `503`, `504` |
| Workflow callback | `200`, `202`, `204` | `400`, `401`, `403`, `409`, `422` | `500`, `503`, `504` |

Jangan semua error dijadikan `500`. Kalau business state conflict terjadi, biasanya lebih baik `409`. Kalau field invalid, biasanya `400` atau `422`, tergantung convention perusahaan.

---

## 13. `components.schemas`

`components.schemas` adalah tempat definisi model reusable.

```yaml id="iy75mr"
components:
  schemas:
    OrderResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - orderId
        - status
        - createdAt
      properties:
        orderId:
          type: string
        status:
          type: string
          enum:
            - DRAFT
            - SUBMITTED
            - CANCELLED
        createdAt:
          type: string
          format: date-time
```

Schema ini biasanya menghasilkan Java class:

```java id="7kx4gv"
public class OrderResponse {
    private String orderId;
    private OrderStatus status;
    private OffsetDateTime createdAt;
}
```

Tergantung generator config, type seperti `date-time` bisa menjadi `OffsetDateTime`, `LocalDateTime`, `Date`, atau type lain.

---

## 14. Schema Design Rules

| Rule | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Request dan response dipisah | `CreateOrderRequest` vs `OrderResponse` |
| Nama schema spesifik | `OrderSummaryResponse`, `OrderDetailResponse` |
| Hindari `Dto` generik | `OrderDto` kurang jelas lifecycle-nya |
| Required eksplisit | Jangan biarkan semua nullable |
| Enum hati-hati | Tambah enum value bisa merusak consumer lama |
| Gunakan `format` untuk date/time | `date`, `date-time`, `uuid` |
| Jangan expose DB shape | API schema bukan table schema |
| Jangan terlalu nested | Schema terlalu dalam sulit evolve |
| Tambahkan example | Mempercepat pemahaman |
| Stabilkan field names | Rename field adalah breaking change |

---

## 15. Required, Optional, Nullable

Ini sumber bug besar.

```yaml id="6mh75x"
CustomerResponse:
  type: object
  required:
    - customerId
    - displayName
  properties:
    customerId:
      type: string
    displayName:
      type: string
    email:
      type:
        - string
        - "null"
```

Interpretasi:

| Field | Required? | Nullable? | Makna |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `customerId` | Ya | Tidak | Harus selalu ada dan bukan null |
| `displayName` | Ya | Tidak | Harus selalu ada dan bukan null |
| `email` | Tidak | Ya | Boleh absent atau null, tergantung convention |

Design rule:

```text id="xgg7b7"
required != non-null dalam semua versi/tooling
nullable semantics harus dites terhadap generator dan serializer yang dipakai
```

Untuk Java, ini berpengaruh ke:

```text id="nkbswv"
String field nullability
Optional usage
Bean Validation
JSON-B/Jackson behavior
generated client validation
```

---

## 16. Enum

```yaml id="say0bj"
OrderStatus:
  type: string
  enum:
    - DRAFT
    - SUBMITTED
    - CANCELLED
```

Risiko enum:

```text id="kqxkh6"
Provider menambah:
  - EXPIRED

Consumer lama menerima EXPIRED,
generated enum tidak kenal,
deserialization bisa gagal.
```

Mitigasi:

| Strategi | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Unknown enum handling | Generated client/serializer disiapkan untuk nilai tak dikenal |
| String-based internal mapping | Map external enum ke internal enum dengan fallback |
| Compatibility review | Penambahan enum value dianggap potentially breaking |
| Explicit docs | Jelaskan apakah enum closed atau evolvable |

OpenAPI Generator Java generator memiliki opsi terkait enum naming, misalnya `enumPropertyNaming`, yang dapat memengaruhi hasil generated enum Java. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 17. Reusable Error Schema

```yaml id="cpsf66"
components:
  schemas:
    ErrorResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - errorCode
        - message
        - correlationId
      properties:
        errorCode:
          type: string
          example: ORDER_NOT_FOUND
        message:
          type: string
          example: Order was not found.
        correlationId:
          type: string
          example: 01HVX9E1P4M8S7
        details:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorDetail'

    ErrorDetail:
      type: object
      properties:
        field:
          type: string
          example: orderId
        reason:
          type: string
          example: must not be blank
```

Gunakan di semua error response:

```yaml id="xs9yzi"
'404':
  description: Order not found
  content:
    application/json:
      schema:
        $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
```

Rule:

```text id="a56we9"
Error response harus machine-readable.
Jangan paksa consumer parsing message text.
```

---

## 18. Reusable Parameters

Daripada mengulang parameter pagination di semua endpoint:

```yaml id="tus3nv"
components:
  parameters:
    PageParam:
      name: page
      in: query
      required: false
      schema:
        type: integer
        minimum: 0
        default: 0

    SizeParam:
      name: size
      in: query
      required: false
      schema:
        type: integer
        minimum: 1
        maximum: 200
        default: 50

    CorrelationIdHeader:
      name: X-Correlation-Id
      in: header
      required: false
      schema:
        type: string
```

Pemakaian:

```yaml id="x1tq0w"
parameters:
  - $ref: '#/components/parameters/PageParam'
  - $ref: '#/components/parameters/SizeParam'
  - $ref: '#/components/parameters/CorrelationIdHeader'
```

Manfaat:

| Manfaat | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Konsisten | Semua endpoint pakai rule pagination sama |
| Mudah berubah | Ubah default/max di satu tempat |
| Mudah review | Governance lebih sederhana |
| Generated client lebih stabil | Parameter naming konsisten |

---

## 19. Reusable Responses

```yaml id="4nkz8g"
components:
  responses:
    BadRequest:
      description: Invalid request
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

    Unauthorized:
      description: Authentication is required

    Forbidden:
      description: Caller is not allowed to access this resource

    InternalServerError:
      description: Unexpected server error
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
```

Pemakaian:

```yaml id="cb1nzw"
responses:
  '400':
    $ref: '#/components/responses/BadRequest'
  '401':
    $ref: '#/components/responses/Unauthorized'
  '403':
    $ref: '#/components/responses/Forbidden'
  '500':
    $ref: '#/components/responses/InternalServerError'
```

Catatan: reusable response bagus untuk standard error, tetapi response bisnis seperti `409 DuplicateOrder` sering perlu description spesifik per operation.

---

## 20. Security Schemes

Contoh bearer JWT:

```yaml id="hsynci"
components:
  securitySchemes:
    bearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT

security:
  - bearerAuth: []
```

Per-operation override:

```yaml id="v0307a"
paths:
  /health:
    get:
      operationId: healthCheck
      security: []
      responses:
        '200':
          description: OK
```

Rule:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Global security untuk default | Jika hampir semua endpoint butuh auth |
| Override untuk public endpoint | Misalnya health, readiness, public metadata |
| Jangan hanya tulis di docs bebas | Security harus machine-readable |
| Object-level auth tetap di implementation | OpenAPI security scheme belum cukup untuk ABAC/RBAC kompleks |

---

## 21. Content Types

```yaml id="dm3jum"
content:
  application/json:
    schema:
      $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderResponse'
```

Bisa juga:

```yaml id="ea8ebm"
content:
  application/pdf:
    schema:
      type: string
      format: binary

  multipart/form-data:
    schema:
      type: object
      properties:
        file:
          type: string
          format: binary
```

Untuk microservice internal, biasanya mayoritas:

```text id="j2pvos"
application/json
```

Tapi perlu perhatian khusus untuk:

| Use case | Content type |
|---|---|
| JSON API | `application/json` |
| File upload | `multipart/form-data` |
| File download | `application/octet-stream`, `application/pdf` |
| Problem details | `application/problem+json` jika memakai RFC 9457 style |
| Streaming | Bergantung design, bisa chunked/binary/event-stream |

---

## 22. Pagination Schema

Contoh list endpoint:

```yaml id="3fqz70"
paths:
  /orders:
    get:
      operationId: listOrders
      parameters:
        - $ref: '#/components/parameters/PageParam'
        - $ref: '#/components/parameters/SizeParam'
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Orders page
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderPageResponse'
```

Schema:

```yaml id="ww6rku"
OrderPageResponse:
  type: object
  required:
    - items
    - page
    - size
    - totalElements
  properties:
    items:
      type: array
      items:
        $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderSummaryResponse'
    page:
      type: integer
      minimum: 0
    size:
      type: integer
      minimum: 1
    totalElements:
      type: integer
      minimum: 0
```

Rule:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Standardisasi pagination | Jangan tiap endpoint beda |
| Batasi `size` maksimum | Lindungi DB dan memory |
| Bedakan offset vs cursor | Cursor lebih baik untuk data besar/berubah cepat |
| Dokumentasikan sort/filter | Jangan implicit |
| Jangan return array mentah untuk list besar | Sulit tambah metadata |

---

## 23. Examples

```yaml id="qco7nj"
examples:
  activeCustomer:
    summary: Active customer
    value:
      customerId: CUST-001
      displayName: PT Nusantara
      status: ACTIVE
```

Atau inline:

```yaml id="fk7yxv"
content:
  application/json:
    schema:
      $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
    example:
      errorCode: ORDER_NOT_FOUND
      message: Order was not found.
      correlationId: 01HVX9E1P4M8S7
```

Manfaat examples:

| Manfaat | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Human review lebih cepat | Reviewer melihat bentuk nyata payload |
| Mock server lebih berguna | Response contoh bisa dipakai |
| Test case lebih jelas | Valid/invalid scenario eksplisit |
| Onboarding lebih cepat | Engineer baru tidak perlu menebak |

---

## 24. Full Mini Contract Example

```yaml id="i733ru"
openapi: 3.1.0

info:
  title: Order API
  version: 1.0.0
  description: API for order query and command operations.

servers:
  - url: https://orders.internal.example.com

tags:
  - name: Orders

paths:
  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      tags:
        - Orders
      operationId: getOrderById
      summary: Get order by id
      parameters:
        - name: orderId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            minLength: 1
        - $ref: '#/components/parameters/CorrelationIdHeader'
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Order found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderResponse'
        '400':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/BadRequest'
        '404':
          description: Order not found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '500':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/InternalServerError'

  /orders:
    post:
      tags:
        - Orders
      operationId: createOrder
      summary: Create order
      parameters:
        - $ref: '#/components/parameters/CorrelationIdHeader'
        - $ref: '#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKeyHeader'
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreateOrderRequest'
      responses:
        '201':
          description: Order created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreateOrderResponse'
        '400':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/BadRequest'
        '409':
          description: Duplicate or conflicting order request
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '422':
          description: Business validation failed
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '500':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/InternalServerError'

components:
  parameters:
    CorrelationIdHeader:
      name: X-Correlation-Id
      in: header
      required: false
      schema:
        type: string

    IdempotencyKeyHeader:
      name: Idempotency-Key
      in: header
      required: true
      schema:
        type: string
        minLength: 16
        maxLength: 128

  responses:
    BadRequest:
      description: Invalid request
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

    InternalServerError:
      description: Unexpected server error
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

  schemas:
    CreateOrderRequest:
      type: object
      required:
        - customerId
        - items
      properties:
        customerId:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
        items:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreateOrderItemRequest'

    CreateOrderItemRequest:
      type: object
      required:
        - productId
        - quantity
      properties:
        productId:
          type: string
          minLength: 1
        quantity:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1

    CreateOrderResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - orderId
        - status
      properties:
        orderId:
          type: string
        status:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderStatus'

    OrderResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - orderId
        - customerId
        - status
        - createdAt
      properties:
        orderId:
          type: string
        customerId:
          type: string
        status:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderStatus'
        createdAt:
          type: string
          format: date-time

    OrderStatus:
      type: string
      enum:
        - DRAFT
        - SUBMITTED
        - CANCELLED

    ErrorResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - errorCode
        - message
        - correlationId
      properties:
        errorCode:
          type: string
        message:
          type: string
        correlationId:
          type: string
        details:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorDetail'

    ErrorDetail:
      type: object
      properties:
        field:
          type: string
        reason:
          type: string
```

---

## 25. Mapping OpenAPI ke Jersey Resource

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="cxajxf"
paths:
  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      operationId: getOrderById
```

Jersey/JAX-RS:

```java id="9de26c"
@Path("/orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class OrderResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{orderId}")
    public Response getOrderById(@PathParam("orderId") String orderId) {
        ...
    }
}
```

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="ob74w1"
parameters:
  - name: orderId
    in: path
    required: true
```

Jersey:

```java id="dsmwew"
@PathParam("orderId") String orderId
```

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="kbxwj1"
requestBody:
  required: true
```

Jersey:

```java id="rp8w4n"
public Response createOrder(CreateOrderRequest request) {
    ...
}
```

---

## 26. Mapping OpenAPI ke Generated Java Client

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="qaovw1"
operationId: getOrderById
```

Generated client kira-kira:

```java id="3uh7bb"
ordersApi.getOrderById(orderId);
```

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="s7t9mx"
CreateOrderRequest:
  type: object
```

Generated model kira-kira:

```java id="8kltug"
CreateOrderRequest request = new CreateOrderRequest()
    .customerId("CUST-001")
    .items(items);
```

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="sk9cc2"
responses:
  '404':
    schema:
      $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
```

Generated behavior bergantung library/config:

```text id="jdpezz"
- bisa throw ApiException
- bisa expose status code
- bisa expose raw response body
- bisa decode error body jika dikustomisasi
```

Karena itu, jangan langsung biarkan generated exception bocor ke application layer.

---

## 27. Review Checklist untuk OpenAPI Structure

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Metadata | `title`, `version`, `description` jelas |
| Servers | Tidak mengunci satu environment secara buruk |
| Tags | Grouping masuk akal |
| Paths | Resource naming konsisten |
| OperationId | Unik, stabil, Java-friendly |
| Parameters | Path/query/header tepat tempatnya |
| Request body | Command request pakai schema eksplisit |
| Responses | Success dan error matrix lengkap |
| Error schema | Standard dan reusable |
| Schemas | Request/response dipisah |
| Required | Field wajib benar-benar required |
| Nullable | Nullability eksplisit dan dites |
| Enum | Risiko unknown value dipikirkan |
| Pagination | Standard dan bounded |
| Security | Machine-readable, bukan hanya deskripsi |
| Examples | Ada contoh payload penting |
| Components | Reuse tidak berlebihan tapi konsisten |

---

## 28. Red Flags di File OpenAPI

| Red flag | Dampak |
|---|---|
| `operationId: get`, `post`, `execute` | Generated client buruk |
| Schema bernama `Response`, `Request`, `Data` | Tidak jelas domain/lifecycle |
| Semua response hanya `200` | HTTP semantics hilang |
| Error tidak punya schema | Consumer tidak bisa handle deterministik |
| Request dan response reuse object sama | Evolusi API sulit |
| Field semua optional | Validasi kontrak lemah |
| Enum tanpa strategy unknown value | Consumer lama bisa rusak |
| Tidak ada `409` untuk state-changing operation | Conflict tidak termodelkan |
| Tidak ada pagination limit | Risiko query besar |
| Header trace/correlation tidak distandardisasi | Debugging sulit |
| Path seperti `/process` | Semantik API tidak jelas |
| Response list array mentah | Sulit tambah metadata |
| OpenAPI valid tapi generated client gagal compile | Naming/schema tidak generator-friendly |

---

## 29. Ringkasan

Struktur OpenAPI yang perlu Anda kuasai:

```text id="jxj6uf"
openapi/info/servers/tags
  -> metadata dan ownership

paths
  -> endpoint surface

operation
  -> satu HTTP method pada satu path

parameters
  -> path/query/header/cookie input

requestBody
  -> payload command

responses
  -> status code + body contract

components.schemas
  -> reusable model

components.responses/parameters/securitySchemes
  -> reusable governance building blocks
```

Untuk Java/Jersey microservice, dampaknya langsung ke:

```text id="33rhe1"
Jersey Resource
Generated Java Client
Generated DTO
Error Mapper
Validation
Contract Testing
CI Compatibility Check
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:14:49 AM

# Seri 4/16 - API Design Rules: Resource, Command, Query, Status Code

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus ke pertanyaan desain:

> **Bagaimana mendesain endpoint HTTP antar microservice agar jelas secara semantik, aman untuk contract-first, mudah di-generate menjadi Java client, dan tidak menimbulkan ambiguity saat production failure?**

OpenAPI membedakan **paths** sebagai endpoint/resource yang diekspos API dan **operations** sebagai HTTP methods pada path tersebut, seperti `GET`, `POST`, atau `DELETE`. Ini penting karena desain path + method akan menjadi kontrak utama antara provider dan consumer. ([Swagger](https://swagger.io/docs/specification/v3_0/paths-and-operations/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Di sisi HTTP, RFC 9110 mendefinisikan semantics HTTP, termasuk method, status code, request/response behavior, dan arsitektur umum HTTP sebagai application-level protocol. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9110?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Di sisi Jersey/JAX-RS, annotation seperti `@GET`, `@POST`, `@PUT`, `@DELETE`, dan `@HEAD` adalah request method designator yang menentukan method Java mana yang memproses HTTP request tertentu. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model: API Bukan Sekadar Endpoint

Jangan mulai dari:

```text id="16k9wy"
Butuh fungsi getCustomer()
Maka bikin:
GET /getCustomer
```

Mulai dari:

```text id="iydsks"
Apa resource/domain object-nya?
Apa intent caller?
Apakah caller membaca state atau mengubah state?
Apakah operasi idempotent?
Apa response sukses?
Apa failure yang valid?
Apa kontrak error-nya?
Apa konsekuensi retry?
```

Diagram mental:

```text id="sdfm43"
Business Intent
  |
  v
Operation Type
  ├── Query
  ├── Command
  └── Workflow / async transition
  |
  v
HTTP Design
  ├── Method
  ├── Path
  ├── Request schema
  ├── Response schema
  ├── Status code matrix
  └── Error contract
  |
  v
Generated Client / Jersey Resource / Runtime Policy
```

---

## 3. Tiga Kategori Operasi Utama

| Kategori | Tujuan | HTTP umum | Retry safety | Contoh |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Resource Query** | Membaca state | `GET` | Relatif aman | `GET /customers/{customerId}` |
| **Collection Query** | Search/list/filter | `GET` atau kadang `POST` untuk search kompleks | Relatif aman jika read-only | `GET /orders?status=OPEN` |
| **Command** | Mengubah state | `POST`, `PUT`, `PATCH`, `DELETE` | Harus hati-hati | `POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel` |
| **Workflow Transition** | Menggerakkan state machine/proses | Biasanya `POST` | Harus idempotent-aware | `POST /cases/{caseId}/assign` |
| **Async Command** | Request diterima, hasil belakangan | `POST` + `202` | Perlu tracking/idempotency | `POST /exports` |
| **Callback/Webhook** | Provider memanggil balik consumer | `POST` | Perlu signature/idempotency | `POST /callbacks/payment-status` |

---

## 4. Query Design Rules

Query adalah operasi baca. Secara desain, query tidak boleh mengubah state bisnis.

### Contoh baik

```text id="iu1btq"
GET /customers/{customerId}
GET /orders/{orderId}
GET /orders?customerId=C001&status=OPEN&page=0&size=50
GET /cases/{caseId}/tasks
```

### Contoh buruk

```text id="qqraei"
GET /getCustomer
GET /orders/searchByStatus
GET /orders/{orderId}/markAsRead
GET /cases/{caseId}/complete
```

Masalah contoh buruk: `GET` dipakai untuk command atau path terlalu RPC-like.

### Rules

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| `GET` harus read-only | Jangan ubah business state |
| Path merepresentasikan resource | `/customers/{customerId}`, bukan `/getCustomer` |
| Query parameter untuk filter/sort/page | Jangan taruh filter sederhana di path |
| Response list jangan array mentah untuk data besar | Gunakan wrapper dengan pagination metadata |
| Query harus punya bounded result | Selalu pikirkan `size` maksimum |
| Search kompleks boleh pakai `POST /search` | Jika filter terlalu besar/kompleks untuk query string |

---

## 5. Resource Query Pattern

```text id="q9uv9n"
GET /orders/{orderId}
```

OpenAPI sketch:

```yaml id="p1ufpg"
paths:
  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      operationId: getOrderById
      parameters:
        - name: orderId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            minLength: 1
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Order found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderResponse'
        '404':
          description: Order not found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
```

Generated client expectation:

```java id="qd35c7"
OrderResponse order = ordersApi.getOrderById(orderId);
```

Adapter expectation:

```java id="tlii1b"
Optional<OrderProfile> findOrderById(OrderId orderId);
```

Jangan expose generated `OrderResponse` langsung ke domain/use-case layer.

---

## 6. Collection Query Pattern

```text id="3claoe"
GET /orders?status=OPEN&page=0&size=50
```

Response lebih baik:

```json id="4uwv6k"
{
  "items": [
    {
      "orderId": "ORD-001",
      "status": "OPEN"
    }
  ],
  "page": 0,
  "size": 50,
  "totalElements": 250
}
```

Bukan:

```json id="ufq3ky"
[
  {
    "orderId": "ORD-001",
    "status": "OPEN"
  }
]
```

Kenapa wrapper lebih baik?

| Alasan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Evolvable | Bisa tambah `nextCursor`, `totalPages`, `links` |
| Aman untuk UI/API consumer | Consumer tahu pagination metadata |
| Lebih jelas kontraknya | List result bukan sekadar array |
| Bisa support cursor nanti | Tanpa breaking change besar |

---

## 7. Search Endpoint: `GET` vs `POST`

Biasanya:

```text id="7u6ujj"
GET /orders?status=OPEN&customerId=C001
```

Tetapi untuk search kompleks:

```text id="psqa99"
POST /orders/search
```

Dengan body:

```json id="ys6r1z"
{
  "criteria": {
    "customerIds": ["C001", "C002"],
    "statuses": ["OPEN", "PENDING_REVIEW"],
    "createdFrom": "2026-01-01",
    "createdTo": "2026-01-31"
  },
  "page": {
    "size": 50,
    "cursor": "eyJvZmZzZXQiOjUwfQ=="
  }
}
```

Decision rule:

| Kondisi | Pilihan |
|---|---|
| Filter sederhana, bookmarkable, pendek | `GET` dengan query params |
| Filter kompleks/nested/banyak nilai | `POST /resource/search` |
| Search tidak mengubah state | Tetap treat sebagai query walau method `POST` |
| Search mahal | Tambahkan limit, timeout expectation, dan possibly async export |

Catatan penting: `POST /search` adalah kompromi teknis, bukan command bisnis. Maka retry/failure handling-nya tetap seperti query selama provider menjamin tidak ada side effect bisnis.

---

## 8. Command Design Rules

Command mengubah state. Di sinilah banyak bug produksi terjadi.

### Contoh baik

```text id="89hcty"
POST /orders
POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel
POST /cases/{caseId}/assign
POST /work-items/{workItemId}/complete
PUT /customers/{customerId}
PATCH /customers/{customerId}
DELETE /draft-orders/{orderId}
```

### Contoh buruk

```text id="1crvyz"
GET /cancelOrder
POST /process
POST /execute
POST /updateStatus
POST /orders/action
```

### Rules

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Command harus jelas intent-nya | `cancelOrder`, `assignCase`, `completeTask` |
| Jangan pakai `GET` untuk command | Proxy/cache/retry bisa berbahaya |
| Command sensitif perlu idempotency | Terutama create/payment/submission/workflow transition |
| Response harus menjelaskan hasil | Created, accepted, completed, conflict, rejected |
| Conflict harus termodelkan | Pakai `409` untuk duplicate/state conflict |
| Validation harus jelas | `400` untuk request invalid, `422` untuk business validation jika dipakai |
| Retry policy harus per-operation | Jangan global retry semua command |

---

## 9. Create Command Pattern

```text id="a2g9zj"
POST /orders
```

Request:

```json id="vcf2sf"
{
  "customerId": "CUST-001",
  "items": [
    {
      "productId": "PROD-001",
      "quantity": 2
    }
  ]
}
```

Response:

```text id="9gdeqj"
201 Created
Location: /orders/ORD-001
```

Body:

```json id="qwgnhk"
{
  "orderId": "ORD-001",
  "status": "DRAFT"
}
```

Status matrix:

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `201` | Resource created |
| `400` | Request syntactically invalid |
| `401` | Missing/invalid authentication |
| `403` | Caller not allowed |
| `409` | Duplicate idempotency key / duplicate business key / conflict |
| `422` | Business validation failed |
| `500` | Unexpected provider error |
| `503` | Dependency unavailable |

Design note: `Location` header sangat berguna untuk created resource, tetapi tetap boleh return body ringkas agar consumer tidak perlu langsung query ulang.

---

## 10. Update Pattern: `PUT` vs `PATCH`

### `PUT`: replace target state

```text id="yr5lju"
PUT /customers/{customerId}
```

Makna praktis:

```text id="acav7p"
Client mengirim representasi lengkap target resource.
Server mengganti state resource dengan representasi itu.
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="tw5moj"
Update profile lengkap
Replace configuration
Upsert resource dengan known id, jika convention mengizinkan
```

### `PATCH`: partial update

```text id="4l59mq"
PATCH /customers/{customerId}
```

Makna praktis:

```text id="66ik66"
Client hanya mengirim field yang ingin diubah.
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="vvy4ro"
Update email
Update display name
Update small subset of mutable fields
```

Risk area:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Nullable ambiguity | Field absent vs field null harus jelas |
| Concurrency | Butuh version/ETag agar tidak lost update |
| Validation partial | Required full object tidak sama dengan required patch |
| Audit | Harus tahu field mana berubah |

---

## 11. Action Command Pattern

Untuk domain action yang bukan CRUD murni:

```text id="m3sxdh"
POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel
POST /cases/{caseId}/assign
POST /tasks/{taskId}/complete
POST /documents/{documentId}/approve
```

Kenapa `POST`?

```text id="uttpyb"
Karena operasi ini membuat transition / side effect,
bukan sekadar mengganti representasi resource biasa.
```

Request:

```json id="v6k43s"
{
  "reasonCode": "CUSTOMER_REQUEST",
  "comment": "Customer requested cancellation."
}
```

Response options:

| Status | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| `200` | Action selesai dan return state terbaru |
| `202` | Action diterima tapi proses async |
| `204` | Action selesai tanpa body |
| `400` | Request invalid |
| `404` | Resource target tidak ada |
| `409` | State tidak memungkinkan action |
| `422` | Business rule menolak action |

Contoh `409`:

```json id="38s0ol"
{
  "errorCode": "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED",
  "message": "Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABC"
}
```

---

## 12. Workflow Transition Pattern

Sangat relevan untuk Camunda/case management/regulatory lifecycle.

Contoh:

```text id="7ev0oq"
POST /cases/{caseId}/submit
POST /cases/{caseId}/assign
POST /cases/{caseId}/escalate
POST /cases/{caseId}/close
POST /work-items/{workItemId}/complete
```

State machine view:

```text id="o41laj"
DRAFT
  --submit--> SUBMITTED
  --assign--> IN_REVIEW
  --escalate--> ESCALATED
  --close--> CLOSED
```

Status code interpretation:

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `200` | Transition completed and current state returned |
| `202` | Transition accepted for async processing |
| `204` | Transition completed with no body |
| `404` | Case/work item not found |
| `409` | Transition invalid from current state |
| `422` | Transition valid syntactically but business precondition failed |

Rule penting:

```text id="xa6n0n"
Invalid state transition biasanya bukan 500.
Biasanya 409 Conflict.
```

Contoh:

```text id="hsj10p"
POST /cases/C-001/close
Current state = DRAFT
```

Response:

```text id="3ejst6"
409 Conflict
```

Body:

```json id="odj0xk"
{
  "errorCode": "INVALID_CASE_STATE_TRANSITION",
  "message": "Case cannot be closed from DRAFT state.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABC"
}
```

---

## 13. Async Command Pattern

Jika proses bisa lama:

```text id="jds0e5"
POST /reports/export
```

Response:

```text id="mkpkv4"
202 Accepted
Location: /reports/export-jobs/JOB-001
```

Body:

```json id="3lj47z"
{
  "jobId": "JOB-001",
  "status": "ACCEPTED"
}
```

Follow-up:

```text id="pekmnw"
GET /reports/export-jobs/JOB-001
```

Response:

```json id="wdqe6u"
{
  "jobId": "JOB-001",
  "status": "COMPLETED",
  "downloadUrl": "/reports/export-jobs/JOB-001/file"
}
```

Status lifecycle:

```text id="kmkgpz"
ACCEPTED -> RUNNING -> COMPLETED
                    -> FAILED
                    -> CANCELLED
```

Use this when:

| Kondisi | Kenapa async |
|---|---|
| Operation > request timeout budget | Jangan tahan HTTP thread |
| Butuh batch/export besar | Lebih aman pakai job resource |
| Bergantung downstream lambat | Bisa retry internal |
| Butuh progress tracking | Job state bisa di-query |
| Butuh audit lifecycle | Status job menjadi audit artifact |

---

## 14. Callback/Webhook Pattern

Untuk komunikasi balik:

```text id="8a8jnd"
POST /callbacks/payment-status
POST /callbacks/document-signed
POST /callbacks/workflow-completed
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Callback harus idempotent | Provider callback bisa retry |
| Signature/security wajib | Jangan percaya callback bebas |
| Event id wajib | Deduplication dan audit |
| Timestamp wajib | Replay protection |
| Response sederhana | Biasanya `202` atau `204` |
| Jangan expose internal exception | Callback sender hanya perlu tahu accepted/rejected |

Request:

```json id="kd2zd9"
{
  "eventId": "EVT-001",
  "eventType": "PAYMENT_COMPLETED",
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-13T10:15:30Z",
  "paymentId": "PAY-001",
  "orderId": "ORD-001"
}
```

Status code:

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `202` | Event accepted for processing |
| `204` | Event processed/no body |
| `400` | Invalid payload |
| `401/403` | Invalid signature/auth |
| `409` | Duplicate/conflicting event |
| `500/503` | Receiver temporarily failed |

---

## 15. HTTP Method Decision Table

| Intent | Recommended method | Path style | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Get one resource | `GET` | `/resources/{id}` | Return `200` or `404` |
| List resources | `GET` | `/resources` | Use pagination |
| Search simple | `GET` | `/resources?filter=x` | Query params |
| Search complex | `POST` | `/resources/search` | Read-only despite POST |
| Create resource | `POST` | `/resources` | Return `201` |
| Replace resource | `PUT` | `/resources/{id}` | Full representation |
| Partial update | `PATCH` | `/resources/{id}` | Define null/absent semantics |
| Delete resource | `DELETE` | `/resources/{id}` | Return `204` or `200` |
| Domain action | `POST` | `/resources/{id}/action` | e.g. cancel, approve |
| Async job create | `POST` | `/jobs` or `/exports` | Return `202` + job resource |
| Callback receive | `POST` | `/callbacks/event-type` | Idempotent receiver |

---

## 16. Status Code Design Rules

OpenAPI v3.2.0 menyatakan HTTP status code digunakan untuk menunjukkan status operasi yang dieksekusi, dan status code sebaiknya dipilih dari status code yang terdaftar di IANA Status Code Registry. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Success

| Status | Gunakan untuk |
|---|---|
| `200 OK` | Success dengan body |
| `201 Created` | Resource baru dibuat |
| `202 Accepted` | Request diterima, processing async/belum selesai |
| `204 No Content` | Success tanpa body |

### Client/business error

| Status | Gunakan untuk |
|---|---|
| `400 Bad Request` | Request malformed / contract invalid |
| `401 Unauthorized` | Belum authenticated / token invalid |
| `403 Forbidden` | Authenticated tapi tidak berhak |
| `404 Not Found` | Resource tidak ditemukan |
| `409 Conflict` | State conflict, duplicate, optimistic locking |
| `422 Unprocessable Content` | Business validation gagal pada payload yang syntactically valid |
| `429 Too Many Requests` | Rate limited |

### Server/platform error

| Status | Gunakan untuk |
|---|---|
| `500 Internal Server Error` | Unexpected bug/error provider |
| `502 Bad Gateway` | Gateway/proxy gagal dari upstream |
| `503 Service Unavailable` | Service/dependency unavailable |
| `504 Gateway Timeout` | Gateway timeout menunggu upstream |

MDN juga mengelompokkan HTTP status code ke lima kelas: informational `100-199`, successful `200-299`, redirection `300-399`, client error `400-499`, dan server error `500-599`. ([MDN Web Docs](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Reference/Status?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 17. Status Code Matrix per Operation

### `GET /orders/{orderId}`

| Status | Meaning | Consumer handling |
|---|---|---|
| `200` | Order found | Decode `OrderResponse` |
| `400` | Invalid `orderId` | Treat as caller bug/request bug |
| `401` | Missing/invalid auth | Refresh/re-auth/fail security |
| `403` | Not allowed | Fail authorization |
| `404` | Order not found | Map to `Optional.empty()` or domain not found |
| `500` | Provider bug | Downstream failure |
| `503` | Provider unavailable | Retry if policy allows |

### `POST /orders`

| Status | Meaning | Consumer handling |
|---|---|---|
| `201` | Order created | Return created order id |
| `400` | Invalid request shape | Caller bug/validation |
| `401` | Missing/invalid auth | Security failure |
| `403` | Not allowed | Authorization failure |
| `409` | Duplicate/conflict | Map to duplicate/conflict outcome |
| `422` | Business validation failed | Return business validation result |
| `500` | Unexpected provider error | Downstream failure |
| `503` | Dependency unavailable | Retry only if idempotency safe |

### `POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel`

| Status | Meaning | Consumer handling |
|---|---|---|
| `200` | Cancelled and body returned | Update local state/view |
| `204` | Cancelled, no body | Treat success |
| `400` | Invalid cancel request | Caller bug |
| `404` | Order not found | Domain not found |
| `409` | Cannot cancel from current state | Business conflict |
| `422` | Cancel precondition failed | Business rejection |
| `500/503` | Provider/system failure | Retry only if idempotent |

---

## 18. `400` vs `422` vs `409`

Ini sering membingungkan.

| Status | Pakai saat | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `400` | Request tidak valid secara kontrak/syntax | `customerId` missing, invalid JSON, invalid enum |
| `422` | Request valid secara bentuk, tapi gagal business validation | customer tidak eligible, amount melebihi policy |
| `409` | Ada konflik dengan current state/resource version/duplicate | order sudah shipped, version mismatch, duplicate idempotency key |

Contoh:

```text id="fiucvq"
POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel
```

| Situasi | Status |
|---|---|
| Body JSON invalid | `400` |
| `reasonCode` tidak dikenal | `400` atau `422`, tergantung convention |
| Order tidak ada | `404` |
| Order sudah shipped | `409` |
| Customer account frozen sehingga cancel butuh approval | `422` |
| Database down | `503` atau `500`, tergantung source |

Rule praktis:

```text id="6scbxt"
400 = caller mengirim request yang tidak sesuai kontrak
409 = request bentrok dengan state saat ini
422 = request dipahami, tapi business rule menolak
```

---

## 19. Idempotency Design

Idempotency berarti operasi bisa diulang tanpa menggandakan efek bisnis.

### Query

```text id="b8vy0b"
GET /customers/{id}
```

Biasanya idempotent/read-only.

### Create command tanpa idempotency

```text id="uqc1ce"
POST /payments
```

Bahaya:

```text id="2n2u5c"
client timeout
  -> provider sebenarnya berhasil create payment
  -> client retry
  -> payment kedua tercipta
```

### Create command dengan idempotency

Header:

```text id="d2vn28"
Idempotency-Key: 01J2ABC-CLIENT-REQ-001
```

Provider menyimpan hasil request pertama. Retry dengan key sama mengembalikan hasil yang sama atau conflict jika payload berbeda.

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="zplgla"
parameters:
  - name: Idempotency-Key
    in: header
    required: true
    schema:
      type: string
      minLength: 16
      maxLength: 128
```

Rule:

| Operation | Idempotency need |
|---|---|
| `GET` | Tidak perlu |
| `PUT` | Biasanya by nature jika full replace |
| `PATCH` | Bergantung patch semantics |
| `DELETE` | Biasanya bisa dibuat idempotent |
| `POST create` | Perlu jika retry mungkin |
| `POST action` | Perlu untuk action sensitif |
| Callback | Wajib event id/dedup |

---

## 20. Retry Design Berdasarkan API Semantics

| Operation | Retry policy |
|---|---|
| Query `GET` | Boleh bounded retry untuk timeout/503 |
| Search `POST /search` | Boleh retry jika read-only |
| Create `POST /orders` | Retry hanya dengan idempotency key |
| Payment/financial command | Sangat hati-hati, wajib idempotency |
| Workflow transition | Retry harus sinkron dengan workflow engine |
| Callback receive | Sender boleh retry; receiver harus dedup |
| Delete | Boleh jika delete idempotent dan hasil `404` dipahami |
| Patch | Hati-hati, terutama jika operation increment/append |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="v035rv"
Retry semua 5xx tiga kali.
```

Lebih aman:

```text id="pdb2zl"
Retry policy per operation:
  query:
    maxAttempts=2
    retryOn=timeout, 503, 504

  command with idempotency:
    maxAttempts=2
    retryOn=connect_timeout, 503

  command without idempotency:
    maxAttempts=1
```

---

## 21. Response Body Design

### Query response

```json id="d5gq30"
{
  "orderId": "ORD-001",
  "status": "OPEN",
  "createdAt": "2026-07-13T10:00:00Z"
}
```

### Create response

```json id="occy26"
{
  "orderId": "ORD-001",
  "status": "DRAFT"
}
```

### Command action response

Option A - return state:

```json id="xvzvl8"
{
  "orderId": "ORD-001",
  "status": "CANCELLED",
  "cancelledAt": "2026-07-13T10:05:00Z"
}
```

Option B - return no content:

```text id="dbkyw4"
204 No Content
```

Option C - async accepted:

```json id="3f96lc"
{
  "commandId": "CMD-001",
  "status": "ACCEPTED"
}
```

Decision:

| Situation | Response |
|---|---|
| Consumer needs updated state immediately | `200` with state |
| Consumer only needs success/failure | `204` |
| New resource created | `201` with id and `Location` |
| Processing async | `202` with job/command id |

---

## 22. Error Body Design

Standard error:

```json id="pih1jb"
{
  "errorCode": "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED",
  "message": "Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABC",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "orderId",
      "reason": "Current status is SHIPPED"
    }
  ]
}
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| `errorCode` stable | Consumer logic jangan parsing `message` |
| `message` human-readable | Untuk log/UI internal |
| `correlationId` wajib | Debugging cross-service |
| `details` structured | Validasi field/business condition |
| Jangan leak stack trace | Security dan noise |
| Error schema reusable | Semua service konsisten |

---

## 23. Naming Rules

### Path naming

| Good | Avoid |
|---|---|
| `/orders/{orderId}` | `/getOrderById` |
| `/orders/{orderId}/items` | `/orderItemsByOrder` |
| `/orders/{orderId}/cancel` | `/cancelOrder` |
| `/cases/{caseId}/assignments` | `/caseAssign` |
| `/work-items/{workItemId}/complete` | `/doComplete` |

### Operation ID naming

| Intent | Good |
|---|---|
| Get one | `getOrderById` |
| List | `listOrders` |
| Search | `searchOrders` |
| Create | `createOrder` |
| Replace | `replaceCustomer` |
| Patch | `updateCustomerPartial` atau `patchCustomer` |
| Cancel | `cancelOrder` |
| Assign | `assignCase` |
| Complete | `completeWorkItem` |

### Schema naming

| Type | Good |
|---|---|
| Create request | `CreateOrderRequest` |
| Create response | `CreateOrderResponse` |
| Detail response | `OrderResponse` / `OrderDetailResponse` |
| Summary response | `OrderSummaryResponse` |
| Action request | `CancelOrderRequest` |
| Error | `ErrorResponse`, `ErrorDetail` |
| Page | `OrderPageResponse` |

---

## 24. API Design untuk Generated Client

Karena contract-first akan menghasilkan Java client, desain OpenAPI harus Java-friendly.

| OpenAPI choice | Dampak ke generated Java |
|---|---|
| `operationId: getOrderById` | Method `getOrderById(...)` |
| Schema `CreateOrderRequest` | Class `CreateOrderRequest` |
| Generic schema `Response` | Class ambiguous `Response` |
| Path param `{id}` di banyak resource | Parameter `id`, kurang jelas |
| Enum value aneh | Enum Java bisa ugly |
| Deep nested anonymous schema | Generated inner/auto names buruk |
| Required/nullable tidak jelas | Null-safety buruk |
| Response status tidak lengkap | Error handling generated client lemah |

Rule:

```text id="npy3d7"
Desain OpenAPI sambil membayangkan hasil generated Java-nya.
```

---

## 25. Jersey Resource Mapping

OpenAPI:

```text id="ez71k1"
POST /orders/{orderId}/cancel
```

Jersey:

```java id="r2z6qw"
@Path("/orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class OrderResource {

    @POST
    @Path("/{orderId}/cancel")
    public Response cancelOrder(
            @PathParam("orderId") String orderId,
            CancelOrderRequest request
    ) {
        CancelOrderResult result = cancelOrderUseCase.cancel(orderId, request);

        return switch (result.status()) {
            case CANCELLED -> Response.ok(toResponse(result)).build();
            case NOT_FOUND -> Response.status(Response.Status.NOT_FOUND)
                    .entity(error("ORDER_NOT_FOUND"))
                    .build();
            case INVALID_STATE -> Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
                    .entity(error("ORDER_CANNOT_BE_CANCELLED"))
                    .build();
        };
    }
}
```

Yang perlu dicek:

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Method annotation | Sesuai OpenAPI method? |
| Path | Sama persis dengan kontrak? |
| Status code | Semua branch sesuai matrix? |
| Error body | Konsisten schema? |
| Validation | Request validation sesuai schema? |
| Content type | JSON response selalu benar? |

---

## 26. Design Review Checklist

Sebelum endpoint disetujui:

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Intent | Ini query, command, workflow transition, atau callback? |
| Method | HTTP method sesuai semantics? |
| Path | Resource/action jelas dan tidak RPC-generic? |
| Idempotency | Jika command, aman di-retry atau butuh key? |
| Status code | Success dan failure matrix lengkap? |
| Error code | Error penting punya stable `errorCode`? |
| Request schema | Required/optional/nullability jelas? |
| Response schema | Consumer mendapat data yang diperlukan? |
| Pagination | List/search bounded? |
| Concurrency | Butuh version, ETag, `If-Match`, atau `409`? |
| Auth | Security scheme dan context jelas? |
| Observability | Correlation/trace header jelas? |
| Generated client | Nama method/class bagus? |
| Compatibility | Perubahan di masa depan bisa additive? |

---

## 27. Red Flags

| Red flag | Kenapa berbahaya |
|---|---|
| `GET` mengubah state | Bisa di-cache/retry secara salah |
| Semua command pakai `/process` | Semantics tidak jelas |
| Semua response `200` | Client tidak bisa bedakan success/error HTTP |
| Error body hanya string | Consumer tidak bisa handle deterministik |
| Tidak ada `409` untuk state transition | Conflict jadi 500/400 ambigu |
| Create command tanpa idempotency | Retry bisa duplicate |
| List endpoint tanpa pagination | Risiko load besar |
| Generated schema bernama `Data` | Client code buruk |
| `PATCH` tanpa null/absent rule | Partial update ambigu |
| No contract for auth headers | Runtime integration gagal |
| OperationId auto-generated | Generated client sulit dipakai |
| Domain action dipaksa CRUD | API tidak mencerminkan lifecycle bisnis |
| DB table shape diekspos | Database refactor jadi breaking API |

---

## 28. Mini Decision Tree

```text id="109q4l"
Apakah operasi membaca data saja?
  ├─ Ya:
  │   ├─ Filter sederhana? -> GET /resources?filter=x
  │   ├─ Filter kompleks? -> POST /resources/search
  │   └─ Single resource? -> GET /resources/{id}
  │
  └─ Tidak:
      Apakah membuat resource baru?
        ├─ Ya -> POST /resources, return 201
        └─ Tidak:
            Apakah mengganti full resource?
              ├─ Ya -> PUT /resources/{id}
              └─ Tidak:
                  Apakah update sebagian field?
                    ├─ Ya -> PATCH /resources/{id}
                    └─ Tidak:
                        Apakah domain action/transition?
                          ├─ Ya -> POST /resources/{id}/action
                          └─ Apakah proses lama?
                              ├─ Ya -> POST /jobs, return 202
                              └─ Review ulang intent API
```

---

## 29. Ringkasan

API design rules untuk contract-first microservice:

```text id="9v9eaf"
Query:
  GET, read-only, bounded, paginated, safe retry

Command:
  POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE, explicit state change,
  idempotency-aware, conflict-aware

Workflow transition:
  POST /resource/{id}/action,
  model state conflict with 409,
  sync with process engine retry

Async:
  return 202 + job/command resource

Error:
  stable machine-readable errorCode

Status:
  use HTTP semantics, not always 200
```

Kunci senior-level-nya:

> **Desain endpoint bukan hanya agar bisa dipanggil, tetapi agar aman saat timeout, retry, partial failure, schema evolution, dan multi-service release.**

---
