## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:20:11 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 5/16**. Seri ini akan fokus ke error contract: bagaimana provider Jersey menghasilkan error yang stabil, dan bagaimana consumer/generated client memetakannya tanpa parsing string bebas.

# Seri 5/16 - Error Contract, Validation Contract, dan Problem Details

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini menjawab:

> **Bagaimana mendesain error response dan validation response yang stabil, machine-readable, konsisten antar service, dan aman diproses oleh generated Jersey Client / OpenFeign client?**

Dalam HTTP microservice, error tidak boleh dianggap “exception detail yang kebetulan dikirim ke caller”. Error adalah bagian dari kontrak.

Provider harus bisa menjawab:

```text id="8yyzn6"
Saat request gagal:
  status code apa?
  error body seperti apa?
  errorCode apa?
  field mana yang invalid?
  business rule apa yang gagal?
  correlationId apa?
  apakah error boleh di-retry?
```

Consumer harus bisa menjawab:

```text id="6bq9mq"
Saat menerima non-2xx:
  ini validation error, business error, auth error, conflict, atau system failure?
  apakah bisa map ke Optional.empty?
  apakah harus lempar exception?
  apakah boleh retry?
  apakah perlu incident/log warning/error?
```

Problem Details for HTTP APIs didefinisikan oleh **RFC 9457** sebagai format machine-readable untuk membawa detail error HTTP response dan menggantikan RFC 7807. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9457/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Error Contract

```text id="gedy6b"
Exception / Failure
  |
  v
Classify failure
  ├── request contract error
  ├── authentication / authorization error
  ├── resource not found
  ├── state conflict
  ├── business validation error
  ├── downstream/platform failure
  └── unexpected bug
  |
  v
Map to HTTP status code
  |
  v
Map to stable error body
  |
  v
Consumer decodes error
  |
  v
Consumer maps to domain/application result
```

Kesalahan umum:

```text id="vwwr46"
throw exception
  -> framework default error
  -> random JSON/plain text/HTML
  -> consumer parse message
  -> production integration brittle
```

Desain yang benar:

```text id="tryldg"
domain/application failure
  -> explicit exception/result
  -> ExceptionMapper
  -> standardized ErrorResponse / ProblemDetail
  -> generated client receives predictable error body
  -> adapter maps deterministically
```

Jakarta REST menyediakan `ExceptionMapper<E extends Throwable>` sebagai kontrak provider untuk memetakan Java exception menjadi HTTP `Response`, dan provider mapper harus diregistrasikan secara programmatic atau diberi `@Provider` agar ditemukan runtime. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/ext/exceptionmapper?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Dua Pilihan Error Format

Secara praktis ada dua pendekatan:

| Pendekatan | Format | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| **Custom enterprise error** | `ErrorResponse { errorCode, message, correlationId, details }` | Organisasi sudah punya standard error sendiri |
| **Problem Details** | RFC 9457 fields: `type`, `title`, `status`, `detail`, `instance`, plus extension fields | Ingin mengikuti standard HTTP API error modern |

Keduanya valid. Yang buruk adalah setiap service membuat format sendiri.

---

## 4. Custom Enterprise Error Model

Contoh:

```json id="w8vv2e"
{
  "errorCode": "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED",
  "message": "Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "orderId",
      "reason": "Current status is SHIPPED"
    }
  ]
}
```

Schema OpenAPI:

```yaml id="zo9q7j"
components:
  schemas:
    ErrorResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - errorCode
        - message
        - correlationId
      properties:
        errorCode:
          type: string
          description: Stable machine-readable error code.
          example: ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED
        message:
          type: string
          description: Human-readable explanation.
          example: Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.
        correlationId:
          type: string
          description: Correlation id for tracing the request across services.
          example: 01J2ABCDEF
        details:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorDetail'

    ErrorDetail:
      type: object
      properties:
        field:
          type: string
          example: orderId
        reason:
          type: string
          example: Current status is SHIPPED
        code:
          type: string
          example: INVALID_STATE
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Mudah dipahami tim internal | Field langsung sesuai kebutuhan enterprise |
| Cocok untuk generated Java DTO | `ErrorResponse`, `ErrorDetail` sederhana |
| Bisa dipakai lintas service | Standard internal |
| Bisa membawa correlation ID eksplisit | Praktis untuk observability |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Bukan standard web umum | Tool eksternal mungkin tidak otomatis mengenali |
| Perlu governance sendiri | Field dan semantics harus dijaga |
| Bisa berkembang liar | Kalau tidak ada standard error code registry |

---

## 5. Problem Details Model

RFC 9457 mendefinisikan problem detail untuk membawa detail machine-readable dari error HTTP response sehingga API tidak perlu membuat format error baru sendiri. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9457.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```json id="h8h4ry"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/order-already-shipped",
  "title": "Order already shipped",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.",
  "instance": "/orders/ORD-001/cancel",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "errorCode": "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED",
  "invalidParams": [
    {
      "name": "orderId",
      "reason": "Current status is SHIPPED"
    }
  ]
}
```

Standard fields:

| Field | Makna |
|---|---|
| `type` | URI yang mengidentifikasi problem type |
| `title` | Ringkasan human-readable untuk problem type |
| `status` | HTTP status code |
| `detail` | Penjelasan spesifik instance error |
| `instance` | URI/reference untuk occurrence error tertentu |

Extension fields yang umum:

| Field | Makna |
|---|---|
| `errorCode` | Stable internal code |
| `correlationId` | Trace/debugging |
| `invalidParams` | Field validation details |
| `retryable` | Opsional, apakah error layak retry |
| `service` | Opsional, service yang menghasilkan error |

Schema OpenAPI:

```yaml id="kyd19k"
components:
  schemas:
    ProblemDetail:
      type: object
      required:
        - type
        - title
        - status
        - correlationId
        - errorCode
      properties:
        type:
          type: string
          format: uri
          example: https://api.company.internal/problems/order-already-shipped
        title:
          type: string
          example: Order already shipped
        status:
          type: integer
          minimum: 400
          maximum: 599
          example: 409
        detail:
          type: string
          example: Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.
        instance:
          type: string
          example: /orders/ORD-001/cancel
        correlationId:
          type: string
          example: 01J2ABCDEF
        errorCode:
          type: string
          example: ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED
        invalidParams:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/InvalidParam'

    InvalidParam:
      type: object
      required:
        - name
        - reason
      properties:
        name:
          type: string
          example: orderId
        reason:
          type: string
          example: Current status is SHIPPED
        code:
          type: string
          example: INVALID_STATE
```

---

## 6. Mana yang Sebaiknya Dipakai?

Untuk enterprise Java microservice, saya biasanya rekomendasikan salah satu dari dua ini:

### Opsi A - Custom standard internal

```json id="k53mwn"
{
  "errorCode": "...",
  "message": "...",
  "correlationId": "...",
  "details": [...]
}
```

Cocok jika perusahaan sudah punya style guide dan banyak legacy consumer.

### Opsi B - Problem Details + extension

```json id="wv14ag"
{
  "type": "...",
  "title": "...",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "...",
  "correlationId": "...",
  "errorCode": "...",
  "invalidParams": [...]
}
```

Cocok jika ingin standard modern, extensible, dan lebih defensible untuk API governance.

### Jangan lakukan ini

```json id="sfskjc"
{
  "success": false,
  "message": "Failed"
}
```

Atau:

```text id="g3svxg"
500 Internal Server Error
Content-Type: text/html
<body>Stacktrace...</body>
```

---

## 7. Error Classification

| Class | HTTP status | Error code contoh | Retry? |
|---|---:|---|---:|
| Malformed JSON | `400` | `MALFORMED_JSON` | Tidak |
| Missing required field | `400` | `REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED` | Tidak |
| Invalid path/query param | `400` | `INVALID_PARAMETER` | Tidak |
| Unauthenticated | `401` | `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED` | Tidak, kecuali refresh token |
| Forbidden | `403` | `ACCESS_DENIED` | Tidak |
| Resource not found | `404` | `ORDER_NOT_FOUND` | Tidak |
| Duplicate idempotency key conflict | `409` | `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` | Tidak |
| Invalid state transition | `409` | `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` | Tidak |
| Business rule failed | `422` | `BUSINESS_VALIDATION_FAILED` | Tidak |
| Rate limited | `429` | `RATE_LIMITED` | Nanti, honor `Retry-After` |
| Downstream unavailable | `503` | `DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE` | Mungkin |
| Gateway timeout | `504` | `DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT` | Mungkin |
| Unexpected bug | `500` | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Mungkin, bounded |
| Maintenance | `503` | `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` | Mungkin nanti |

Kunci: **status code dan errorCode harus saling menguatkan**, bukan saling bertentangan.

---

## 8. Validation Contract

Validation contract adalah aturan formal untuk request.

Ada dua level:

```text id="y9a5ky"
Request shape validation
  - JSON valid?
  - required field ada?
  - type benar?
  - string length?
  - enum valid?
  - number range?

Business validation
  - customer eligible?
  - order can be cancelled?
  - case can be assigned to this user?
  - workflow state allows transition?
```

Mapping umum:

| Error | Status |
|---|---:|
| Invalid JSON | `400` |
| Missing required field | `400` |
| Invalid enum/type/format | `400` |
| Bean Validation constraint gagal | Biasanya `400` |
| Business rule gagal | `422` atau `409`, tergantung penyebab |
| State conflict/current state tidak cocok | `409` |

Jakarta REST 3.1 specification menyebut implementasi JAX-RS harus menyediakan default exception mapper untuk `jakarta.validation.ValidationException`, jadi validasi bisa otomatis menjadi HTTP response jika tidak dioverride; untuk kontrak enterprise, biasanya tetap lebih baik membuat mapper sendiri agar body konsisten. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-3.1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. Field Validation Error

Contoh request:

```json id="hi6szn"
{
  "customerId": "",
  "items": []
}
```

Response custom:

```json id="r4en1f"
{
  "errorCode": "REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "message": "Request validation failed.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "customerId",
      "reason": "must not be blank",
      "code": "NotBlank"
    },
    {
      "field": "items",
      "reason": "must contain at least 1 item",
      "code": "Size"
    }
  ]
}
```

Problem Details variant:

```json id="wd5y1s"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/request-validation-failed",
  "title": "Request validation failed",
  "status": 400,
  "detail": "One or more request fields are invalid.",
  "instance": "/orders",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "errorCode": "REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "invalidParams": [
    {
      "name": "customerId",
      "reason": "must not be blank",
      "code": "NotBlank"
    },
    {
      "name": "items",
      "reason": "must contain at least 1 item",
      "code": "Size"
    }
  ]
}
```

Rules:

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Field path harus stabil | Consumer/UI bisa highlight field |
| Constraint code lebih baik daripada free text saja | Bisa diterjemahkan/diproses |
| Jangan leak Java class name internal | `NotBlank` boleh, `com.company.foo.ValidatorImpl` tidak |
| Semua validation error pakai schema sama | Consumer handling sederhana |
| Jangan return error pertama saja | Return multiple violations jika aman |

---

## 10. Business Validation Error

Contoh:

```text id="0sqwvo"
POST /orders
```

Request valid secara bentuk, tapi customer tidak boleh membuat order karena statusnya suspended.

Response:

```text id="mc9s4e"
422 Unprocessable Content
```

Body:

```json id="dwi7ej"
{
  "errorCode": "CUSTOMER_NOT_ELIGIBLE",
  "message": "Customer is not eligible to create an order.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "customerId",
      "reason": "Customer status is SUSPENDED",
      "code": "CUSTOMER_SUSPENDED"
    }
  ]
}
```

Bedakan dari:

```text id="ji1l03"
404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND
```

dan:

```text id="g3e6qk"
409 ORDER_ALREADY_EXISTS
```

Business validation bukan semua dijadikan `400`.

---

## 11. Conflict Error

Conflict berarti request bertentangan dengan current state/resource version.

Contoh state machine:

```text id="p3x8rd"
Order status = SHIPPED
Request = cancel order
```

Response:

```text id="e42jib"
409 Conflict
```

Body:

```json id="nv8hie"
{
  "errorCode": "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED",
  "message": "Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "orderId",
      "reason": "Current status is SHIPPED",
      "code": "INVALID_STATE"
    }
  ]
}
```

Conflict juga cocok untuk:

| Case | Error code |
|---|---|
| Optimistic locking version mismatch | `RESOURCE_VERSION_CONFLICT` |
| Duplicate business key | `DUPLICATE_ORDER_REFERENCE` |
| Idempotency key reused with different payload | `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` |
| Workflow transition invalid from current state | `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` |

---

## 12. Error Code Registry

Jangan biarkan errorCode tumbuh liar.

Minimal registry:

```text id="95r9b3"
COMMON
  REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED
  MALFORMED_JSON
  AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED
  ACCESS_DENIED
  RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND
  INTERNAL_ERROR
  SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
  DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT

ORDER
  ORDER_NOT_FOUND
  ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED
  ORDER_ALREADY_CANCELLED
  ORDER_CREATION_NOT_ALLOWED

CUSTOMER
  CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND
  CUSTOMER_NOT_ELIGIBLE
  CUSTOMER_SUSPENDED
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| `errorCode` uppercase snake case | Stabil dan mudah dicari |
| Jangan rename errorCode sembarangan | Itu breaking untuk consumer |
| Jangan encode dynamic data di errorCode | Pakai details untuk data |
| Error code harus punya owner | Domain team bertanggung jawab |
| Error code harus terdokumentasi | OpenAPI examples atau registry file |
| Common error dipakai lintas service | Konsistensi consumer |

Anti-pattern:

```json id="8vym5w"
{
  "errorCode": "Order ORD-001 cannot be cancelled at 2026-07-13"
}
```

Lebih baik:

```json id="cm01i6"
{
  "errorCode": "ORDER_CANNOT_BE_CANCELLED",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "orderId",
      "reason": "ORD-001"
    }
  ]
}
```

---

## 13. OpenAPI Responses untuk Error

Jangan hanya tulis success response.

```yaml id="eapbs4"
paths:
  /orders/{orderId}/cancel:
    post:
      operationId: cancelOrder
      parameters:
        - name: orderId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/CancelOrderRequest'
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Order cancelled
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderResponse'
        '400':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/BadRequest'
        '401':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/Unauthorized'
        '403':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/Forbidden'
        '404':
          description: Order not found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '409':
          description: Order cannot be cancelled from current state
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '422':
          description: Business validation failed
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '500':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/InternalServerError'
        '503':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/ServiceUnavailable'
```

Swagger/OpenAPI documentation notes that each operation must define at least one response, usually a successful response, and responses are identified by HTTP status code plus returned data in body/headers. ([Swagger](https://swagger.io/docs/specification/v3_0/describing-responses/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) For contract-first production systems, “at least one response” is not enough; define the important failure responses too.

---

## 14. Reusable Error Responses

```yaml id="h1t5jg"
components:
  responses:
    BadRequest:
      description: Invalid request.
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

    Unauthorized:
      description: Authentication is required.
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

    Forbidden:
      description: Caller is not allowed to perform this operation.
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

    InternalServerError:
      description: Unexpected server error.
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'

    ServiceUnavailable:
      description: Service is temporarily unavailable.
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
```

Gunakan reusable untuk common errors, tapi tetap beri description spesifik untuk domain errors seperti `409`.

---

## 15. Jersey ExceptionMapper Design

Struktur yang sehat:

```text id="mwmwrl"
Domain/Application exception
  |
  v
ExceptionMapper
  |
  v
ErrorResponseFactory
  |
  v
HTTP Response
```

Contoh domain exception:

```java id="2l1axj"
public final class OrderAlreadyShippedException extends RuntimeException {
    private final String orderId;

    public OrderAlreadyShippedException(String orderId) {
        super("Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.");
        this.orderId = orderId;
    }

    public String orderId() {
        return orderId;
    }
}
```

Error model:

```java id="em5qb3"
public record ErrorResponse(
        String errorCode,
        String message,
        String correlationId,
        List<ErrorDetail> details
) {}

public record ErrorDetail(
        String field,
        String reason,
        String code
) {}
```

Exception mapper:

```java id="vr60mo"
@Provider
public final class OrderAlreadyShippedExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<OrderAlreadyShippedException> {

    @Context
    private HttpHeaders headers;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(OrderAlreadyShippedException exception) {
        String correlationId = resolveCorrelationId(headers);

        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED",
                "Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.",
                correlationId,
                List.of(new ErrorDetail(
                        "orderId",
                        exception.orderId(),
                        "INVALID_STATE"
                ))
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .entity(body)
                .build();
    }

    private String resolveCorrelationId(HttpHeaders headers) {
        String value = headers.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        return value != null && !value.isBlank()
                ? value
                : UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    }
}
```

---

## 16. Centralized Error Factory

Kalau setiap mapper membangun response sendiri, format bisa drift. Lebih baik pusatkan.

```java id="lxalkf"
public final class ErrorResponseFactory {

    public ErrorResponse validationFailed(
            String correlationId,
            List<ErrorDetail> details
    ) {
        return new ErrorResponse(
                "REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED",
                "Request validation failed.",
                correlationId,
                details
        );
    }

    public ErrorResponse conflict(
            String errorCode,
            String message,
            String correlationId,
            List<ErrorDetail> details
    ) {
        return new ErrorResponse(
                errorCode,
                message,
                correlationId,
                details
        );
    }

    public ErrorResponse internalError(String correlationId) {
        return new ErrorResponse(
                "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "An unexpected error occurred.",
                correlationId,
                List.of()
        );
    }
}
```

Keuntungan:

| Keuntungan | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Format konsisten | Semua mapper sama |
| Logging policy terpusat | Internal error bisa log stacktrace |
| Security lebih aman | Tidak leak exception message |
| Testing mudah | Assert factory output |
| OpenAPI alignment mudah | Satu model error |

---

## 17. Bean Validation Mapper

Contoh request DTO:

```java id="ovrjgp"
public record CreateOrderRequest(
        @NotBlank String customerId,
        @NotEmpty List<CreateOrderItemRequest> items
) {}
```

Mapper untuk `ConstraintViolationException`:

```java id="zoqgxs"
@Provider
public final class ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<ConstraintViolationException> {

    private final ErrorResponseFactory errorFactory = new ErrorResponseFactory();

    @Context
    private HttpHeaders headers;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        String correlationId = resolveCorrelationId(headers);

        List<ErrorDetail> details = exception.getConstraintViolations()
                .stream()
                .map(violation -> new ErrorDetail(
                        violation.getPropertyPath().toString(),
                        violation.getMessage(),
                        violation.getConstraintDescriptor()
                                .getAnnotation()
                                .annotationType()
                                .getSimpleName()
                ))
                .toList();

        ErrorResponse body = errorFactory.validationFailed(correlationId, details);

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .entity(body)
                .build();
    }

    private String resolveCorrelationId(HttpHeaders headers) {
        String value = headers.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        return value != null && !value.isBlank()
                ? value
                : UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    }
}
```

Caution:

```text id="k06l9v"
propertyPath dari Bean Validation kadang tidak user-friendly.
Untuk API publik/internal enterprise,
sering perlu normalize menjadi JSON field path.
```

---

## 18. JSON Parse / Mapping Error

Request invalid JSON harus menjadi `400`, bukan `500`.

Contoh:

```json id="9mrcf9"
{
  "customerId": "C001",
  "items": [
}
```

Response:

```json id="rmz2i2"
{
  "errorCode": "MALFORMED_JSON",
  "message": "Request body is not valid JSON.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "details": []
}
```

Jika enum invalid:

```json id="2sjyzl"
{
  "status": "UNKNOWN_STATUS"
}
```

Response bisa:

```json id="3yhf3a"
{
  "errorCode": "REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "message": "Request validation failed.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "status",
      "reason": "Unsupported value: UNKNOWN_STATUS",
      "code": "INVALID_ENUM_VALUE"
    }
  ]
}
```

Red flag:

```text id="bmc8xx"
Invalid JSON menghasilkan HTML error page.
```

Ini berarti exception mapper / message body reader error handling belum distandardisasi.

---

## 19. Generic Throwable Mapper

Anda tetap butuh fallback mapper, tapi hati-hati.

```java id="ej559s"
@Provider
public final class UnhandledExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    private static final Logger log =
            LoggerFactory.getLogger(UnhandledExceptionMapper.class);

    @Context
    private HttpHeaders headers;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        String correlationId = resolveCorrelationId(headers);

        log.error("Unhandled exception. correlationId={}", correlationId, exception);

        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "An unexpected error occurred.",
                correlationId,
                List.of()
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .entity(body)
                .build();
    }

    private String resolveCorrelationId(HttpHeaders headers) {
        String value = headers.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        return value != null && !value.isBlank()
                ? value
                : UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    }
}
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Log stacktrace server-side | Untuk debugging |
| Jangan return stacktrace ke client | Security |
| Jangan return raw exception message | Bisa leak SQL/internal info |
| Pakai stable `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Consumer handling konsisten |
| Sertakan correlation ID | Client bisa report ke support |

---

## 20. Provider Error Mapping Table

| Exception / failure | HTTP | errorCode |
|---|---:|---|
| `JsonParseException` / invalid JSON | `400` | `MALFORMED_JSON` |
| `ConstraintViolationException` | `400` | `REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| `AuthenticationException` | `401` | `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED` |
| `AccessDeniedException` | `403` | `ACCESS_DENIED` |
| `EntityNotFoundException` | `404` | `{DOMAIN}_NOT_FOUND` |
| `OptimisticLockException` | `409` | `RESOURCE_VERSION_CONFLICT` |
| Invalid state transition | `409` | `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` |
| Duplicate business key | `409` | `DUPLICATE_RESOURCE` |
| Business rule violation | `422` | domain-specific code |
| Rate limit exceeded | `429` | `RATE_LIMITED` |
| Downstream timeout | `504` or `503` | `DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT` |
| Downstream unavailable | `503` | `DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE` |
| Unhandled exception | `500` | `INTERNAL_ERROR` |

---

## 21. Consumer-side Error Handling

Generated client biasanya memberi Anda salah satu bentuk ini:

```text id="plkc06"
- throws ApiException with status code + response body
- returns response wrapper with status code
- throws FeignException
- uses custom ErrorDecoder / ResponseExceptionMapper
```

Yang buruk:

```java id="afig45"
try {
    return customerApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
} catch (Exception e) {
    return null;
}
```

Yang lebih baik:

```java id="io96g0"
public Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(String customerId) {
    try {
        CustomerResponse response = customerApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
        return Optional.of(map(response));
    } catch (ApiException e) {
        ErrorResponse error = decodeErrorBody(e);

        if (e.getCode() == 404 &&
                "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND".equals(error.errorCode())) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        if (e.getCode() == 403) {
            throw new DownstreamAccessDeniedException(error);
        }

        if (e.getCode() >= 500) {
            throw new DownstreamSystemException("CUSTOMER_SERVICE_FAILED", e);
        }

        throw new DownstreamBusinessException(error);
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="6s73eu"
Consumer adapter maps HTTP/provider error
into internal application/domain result.
Business layer does not parse HTTP directly.
```

---

## 22. OpenFeign ErrorDecoder

Untuk OpenFeign, gunakan `ErrorDecoder`.

```java id="560f8w"
public final class CustomerErrorDecoder implements ErrorDecoder {

    private final ErrorDecoder defaultDecoder = new Default();

    @Override
    public Exception decode(String methodKey, Response response) {
        ErrorResponse error = tryDecodeError(response);

        return switch (response.status()) {
            case 404 -> new CustomerNotFoundException(error);
            case 409 -> new DownstreamConflictException(error);
            case 422 -> new DownstreamBusinessValidationException(error);
            case 503, 504 -> new DownstreamUnavailableException(error);
            default -> defaultDecoder.decode(methodKey, response);
        };
    }

    private ErrorResponse tryDecodeError(Response response) {
        // Decode JSON body safely.
        // If decoding fails, return synthetic ErrorResponse.
        return new ErrorResponse(
                "UNKNOWN_DOWNSTREAM_ERROR",
                "Unable to decode downstream error response.",
                "unknown",
                List.of()
        );
    }
}
```

Feign mendukung kustomisasi decoder dan error handling pada client HTTP berbasis interface, sehingga error contract bisa dipusatkan di client adapter/config, bukan tersebar di business logic. ([Swagger](https://swagger.io/blog/problem-details-rfc9457-doing-api-errors-well/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 23. Jersey Client Error Handling

Dengan generated Jersey Client, behavior tergantung library/config OpenAPI Generator. Pola umum:

```java id="w1yqgt"
try {
    OrderResponse response = ordersApi.getOrderById(orderId);
    return map(response);
} catch (ApiException e) {
    int status = e.getCode();
    String body = e.getResponseBody();

    ErrorResponse error = errorDecoder.decode(body);

    throw mapDownstreamError(status, error, e);
}
```

Jika memakai Jersey Client manual:

```java id="6nl1xp"
Response response = client
        .target(baseUrl)
        .path("/orders/{orderId}")
        .resolveTemplate("orderId", orderId)
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .get();

if (response.getStatus() == 200) {
    return response.readEntity(OrderResponse.class);
}

ErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ErrorResponse.class);
throw mapError(response.getStatus(), error);
```

Important:

| Concern | Rule |
|---|---|
| Error body might be empty | Decoder harus tahan |
| Error body might not be JSON | Fallback error |
| Status and body might disagree | Status code tetap source utama |
| Response stream can be read once | Decode dengan hati-hati |
| 404 might be domain result | Jangan selalu exception fatal |
| 5xx should not become business error | Map ke downstream system failure |

---

## 24. Error Handling di Adapter Pattern

```text id="hztej4"
Generated Client Exception
  |
  v
HTTP Client Adapter
  |
  +--> 404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND -> Optional.empty()
  +--> 409 ORDER_CONFLICT -> DomainConflict
  +--> 422 BUSINESS_VALIDATION_FAILED -> BusinessValidationResult
  +--> 401/403 -> Security exception
  +--> 5xx/timeout -> DownstreamUnavailable
```

Adapter interface:

```java id="6o2cam"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

Adapter implementation:

```java id="eeqc1o"
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomerApi customerApi;
    private final DownstreamErrorMapper errorMapper;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    customerApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(toProfile(response));
        } catch (ApiException e) {
            throw errorMapper.mapOrReturnNotFound(e)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("unreachable"));
        }
    }
}
```

Pola lebih bersih bisa memakai sealed result type.

```java id="jakvd8"
public sealed interface CustomerLookupResult {
    record Found(CustomerProfile customer) implements CustomerLookupResult {}
    record NotFound(CustomerId customerId) implements CustomerLookupResult {}
    record Failed(String reason) implements CustomerLookupResult {}
}
```

---

## 25. Error Observability

Setiap error response harus punya correlation ID.

Request masuk:

```text id="9jsu6p"
X-Correlation-Id: 01J2ABCDEF
```

Response error:

```json id="07y8zd"
{
  "errorCode": "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED",
  "message": "Order cannot be cancelled because it has already been shipped.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "details": []
}
```

Log provider:

```text id="95z16r"
level=WARN
correlationId=01J2ABCDEF
operation=cancelOrder
status=409
errorCode=ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED
orderId=ORD-001
```

Log consumer:

```text id="x5nqe2"
level=WARN
correlationId=01J2ABCDEF
downstream=order-service
operation=cancelOrder
status=409
errorCode=ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED
latencyMs=84
```

Rules:

| Error class | Log level umum |
|---|---|
| `400` validation from external caller | WARN/INFO, tergantung volume |
| `404` expected lookup miss | INFO/DEBUG |
| `409` business conflict | WARN/INFO |
| `422` business validation | INFO/WARN |
| `401/403` | WARN/security event |
| `500` | ERROR |
| `503/504` | WARN/ERROR tergantung impact |

---

## 26. Security Rules untuk Error

| Jangan | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Return stacktrace | Bocor internal implementation |
| Return SQL query/error mentah | Security risk |
| Return class/package name internal | Bocor architecture |
| Return token/auth detail | Security risk |
| Bedakan terlalu detail resource existence untuk unauthorized caller | Bisa enumeration risk |
| Pakai exception message mentah | Tidak stabil dan bisa leak |

Contoh buruk:

```json id="b4hjvw"
{
  "message": "org.postgresql.util.PSQLException: relation tbl_order_secret does not exist"
}
```

Contoh baik:

```json id="o6sfkv"
{
  "errorCode": "INTERNAL_ERROR",
  "message": "An unexpected error occurred.",
  "correlationId": "01J2ABCDEF",
  "details": []
}
```

Detail internal hanya di log server.

---

## 27. Contract Testing untuk Error

Jangan hanya test success response.

Minimal test:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Missing required field | `400 REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| Invalid enum | `400 REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| Resource not found | `404 {DOMAIN}_NOT_FOUND` |
| Invalid state transition | `409 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` |
| Business rule failed | `422 domain error` |
| Unauthorized | `401 AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED` |
| Forbidden | `403 ACCESS_DENIED` |
| Unexpected exception | `500 INTERNAL_ERROR`, no stacktrace |
| Downstream timeout | `503/504 DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT` |
| Error body schema | Matches OpenAPI `ErrorResponse` |

Test shape:

```text id="7x2eay"
Given invalid request
When POST /orders
Then status = 400
And response.contentType = application/json
And body.errorCode = REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED
And body.correlationId exists
And body.details contains customerId
And body has no stacktrace
```

---

## 28. Error Contract Compatibility

| Change | Breaking? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Add optional field to error response | Usually no | Consumer lama ignore |
| Remove `errorCode` | Yes | Core contract broken |
| Rename `errorCode` value | Yes | Consumer logic broken |
| Change HTTP status for same error | Often yes | Consumer routing broken |
| Change `details.field` path format | Potentially yes | UI/consumer mapping broken |
| Add new error code | Usually okay | Consumer should handle unknown |
| Change message text | Usually okay | Jika consumer tidak parsing message |
| Change schema type | Yes | Generated client decode broken |
| Remove correlationId | Yes operationally | Debugging broken |

Rule:

```text id="etweo9"
errorCode and HTTP status are part of public contract.
Treat them like API fields.
```

---

## 29. Error Contract Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Format | Custom enterprise error atau Problem Details dipilih jelas |
| Schema | Error schema ada di OpenAPI |
| Status matrix | Semua operation punya error responses penting |
| Error code | Stable, documented, tidak dynamic |
| Validation | Field-level details tersedia |
| Business validation | Dibedakan dari request shape validation |
| Conflict | `409` dipakai untuk state/version conflict |
| ExceptionMapper | Provider menghasilkan body konsisten |
| Generic mapper | Tidak leak stacktrace |
| Consumer decoder | Non-2xx diproses deterministik |
| Adapter | Error HTTP tidak bocor ke domain/use-case |
| Observability | correlationId masuk response dan log |
| Security | Error tidak membocorkan SQL/token/internal class |
| Compatibility | Perubahan error diperlakukan sebagai contract change |
| Testing | Success dan error path sama-sama dites |

---

## 30. Red Flags

| Red flag | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Semua error `500` | Consumer tidak bisa membedakan business vs system failure |
| Semua error `200` dengan `success=false` | HTTP semantics hilang |
| Error body hanya string | Tidak machine-readable |
| Consumer parsing `message` | Sangat brittle |
| Exception mapper tidak ada | Framework default response bisa inconsistent |
| Stacktrace dikirim ke caller | Security risk |
| `errorCode` berisi data dynamic | Tidak stabil |
| `404` selalu fatal exception | Query not found tidak bisa jadi normal outcome |
| `409` tidak dipakai untuk state conflict | Workflow/state machine error ambigu |
| Validation error hanya error pertama | UX/integrasi buruk |
| Error schema tidak di OpenAPI | Generated client tidak tahu bentuk error |
| Feign/Jersey error decoder tersebar | Handling tidak konsisten |
| `catch Exception return null` | Failure hilang dan debugging sulit |

---

## 31. Ringkasan

Error contract yang sehat:

```text id="yeudmq"
HTTP status code
  + stable errorCode
  + structured error body
  + field/business details
  + correlationId
  + ExceptionMapper provider-side
  + ErrorDecoder consumer-side
  + OpenAPI response schema
  + contract tests
```

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:

```text id="q5g4e8"
Provider:
  exception -> ExceptionMapper -> ErrorResponse/ProblemDetail

Consumer:
  ApiException/FeignException -> ErrorDecoder -> internal exception/result

Business layer:
  tidak tahu raw HTTP error detail
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Error bukan detail implementasi. Error adalah bagian dari API contract dan harus didesain, di-versioning, diobservasi, dan dites seperti success response.**

---

# Seri 6/16 - API Versioning, Compatibility, Deprecation, Breaking Change

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus ke masalah yang sering muncul setelah microservice mulai banyak consumer:

> **Bagaimana mengubah API contract tanpa merusak consumer lama, generated client, workflow integration, dan release independen antar microservice?**

Dalam contract-first approach, versi API bukan hanya angka di `info.version`. Versi API adalah janji compatibility antara provider dan consumer.

OpenAPI sendiri memakai skema versioning `major.minor.patch`; bagian `major.minor` menunjukkan feature set OpenAPI, sedangkan patch dipakai untuk koreksi/klarifikasi specification. Artinya, versi specification seperti OpenAPI `3.1.x` atau `3.2.x` berbeda dari versi kontrak API bisnis Anda. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Versioning

```text id="tv5gt7"
Service implementation version
  != API contract version
  != Java client artifact version
  != Docker image version
  != database schema version
  != Camunda process definition version
```

Contoh:

```text id="ndew8d"
order-service image:          2026.07.13-1452
Order API contract:           1.4.0
order-api-java-client.jar:    1.4.0
Database schema:              202607131200_add_order_status
Camunda process version:      order-fulfillment:v17
```

Kenapa perlu dipisah?

| Version | Apa yang berubah | Consumer peduli? |
|---|---|---:|
| Service image version | Bugfix/internal implementation | Tidak selalu |
| API contract version | Endpoint/schema/status/error behavior | Ya |
| Java client artifact version | Generated API/model classes | Ya |
| DB schema version | Table/index/procedure | Tidak langsung, kecuali bocor ke API |
| Process definition version | Workflow state/transition | Ya jika API workflow berubah |

---

## 3. Compatibility First Principle

Prinsip utamanya:

```text id="82cjdn"
Provider boleh berubah lebih cepat daripada consumer,
selama perubahan itu backward-compatible terhadap kontrak yang sudah dirilis.
```

Backward-compatible artinya consumer lama tetap bisa:

```text id="hs0lhd"
compile
start
call endpoint
decode response
handle errors
preserve business semantics
```

Bukan hanya “endpoint masih return 200”.

---

## 4. Semantic Versioning untuk API Contract

Semantic Versioning mendefinisikan pola `MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH`, dan aturan dasarnya adalah: public API harus jelas; `MAJOR` naik untuk incompatible API changes, `MINOR` naik untuk functionality baru yang backward-compatible, dan `PATCH` naik untuk bug fix backward-compatible. ([Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk contract-first HTTP API:

```text id="8ymw3v"
MAJOR:
  breaking change terhadap consumer

MINOR:
  additive backward-compatible change

PATCH:
  clarification, documentation, bugfix,
  tanpa perubahan kontrak yang terlihat consumer
```

Contoh:

| Change | Versi |
|---|---|
| Tambah optional response field | `1.3.0 -> 1.4.0` |
| Tambah endpoint baru | `1.3.0 -> 1.4.0` |
| Perbaiki typo description tanpa ubah schema | `1.3.0 -> 1.3.1` |
| Rename field response | `1.x.x -> 2.0.0` |
| Hapus enum value | `1.x.x -> 2.0.0` |
| Ubah status `404` menjadi `200` empty body | `1.x.x -> 2.0.0` |

Catatan: SemVer membantu komunikasi, tetapi tidak otomatis menjamin semantic compatibility. Anda tetap butuh API diff, contract test, dan review manusia.

---

## 5. Jenis Perubahan API

```text id="2g4vfi"
API change
  ├── Additive compatible
  ├── Potentially compatible
  ├── Breaking structural
  ├── Breaking semantic
  └── Operational breaking
```

| Jenis | Contoh | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Additive compatible | Tambah optional field response | Rendah |
| Potentially compatible | Tambah enum value | Sedang/tinggi untuk generated Java enum |
| Breaking structural | Rename/remove field | Tinggi |
| Breaking semantic | Field sama, makna berubah | Sangat tinggi |
| Operational breaking | Timeout/latency/auth berubah | Tinggi, walau schema sama |

---

## 6. Compatibility Matrix

| Perubahan | Compatible? | Kenapa |
|---|---:|---|
| Tambah endpoint baru | Ya | Consumer lama tidak terpengaruh |
| Tambah optional query parameter | Ya | Jika default behavior tetap sama |
| Tambah optional request field | Ya | Provider tetap menerima request lama |
| Tambah optional response field | Ya | Consumer lama idealnya ignore unknown field |
| Tambah required response field | Biasanya ya | Tapi consumer lama belum tentu peduli |
| Tambah required request field | Tidak | Consumer lama tidak mengirim |
| Hapus request field optional | Hati-hati | Consumer lama mungkin masih mengirim |
| Hapus response field | Tidak | Consumer lama mungkin membaca |
| Rename field | Tidak | Sama seperti remove + add |
| Ubah type field | Tidak | Decoder generated client bisa gagal |
| Ubah `string` format date | Tidak / hati-hati | Parsing bisa rusak |
| Tambah enum value | Hati-hati | Generated enum lama bisa gagal deserialize |
| Hapus enum value | Tidak | Consumer mungkin masih mengirim/menerima |
| Ubah HTTP status code | Hati-hati / sering breaking | Consumer branch logic berubah |
| Ubah errorCode | Tidak | Consumer logic bisa rusak |
| Ubah message text | Biasanya ya | Jika consumer tidak parsing message |
| Ubah pagination default | Hati-hati | Bisa semantic/operational breaking |
| Turunkan max page size | Hati-hati | Consumer yang request size besar bisa gagal |
| Wajibkan auth baru | Tidak untuk consumer lama | Operational breaking |
| Tambah rate limit ketat | Operational breaking | Bisa merusak production behavior |
| Perbesar latency signifikan | Operational breaking | Timeout consumer bisa kena |

---

## 7. Breaking Change yang Sering Tidak Disadari

Banyak breaking change tidak terlihat sebagai “hapus endpoint”.

| Hidden breaking change | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Semantic change | `status=ACTIVE` dulu berarti “bisa order”, sekarang hanya “account exists” |
| Validation tightening | Field `referenceNo` dulu optional, sekarang wajib |
| Enum expansion | Tambah `PENDING_REVIEW`, consumer lama gagal deserialize |
| Error status berubah | Not found dulu `404`, sekarang `200` dengan `null` |
| Error code rename | `ORDER_NOT_FOUND` jadi `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND` |
| Nullability berubah | Field dulu selalu ada, sekarang bisa null |
| Sort default berubah | List endpoint menghasilkan urutan berbeda |
| Pagination max berubah | Consumer request `size=500`, sekarang max `100` |
| Date timezone berubah | `LocalDateTime` berubah menjadi UTC offset |
| Numeric precision berubah | `double` menjadi decimal string |
| Auth context berubah | Downstream sekarang butuh user token, bukan service token |
| Timeout behavior berubah | Endpoint async jadi sync atau sebaliknya |

Rule senior-level:

```text id="evh7vv"
Breaking change bukan hanya perubahan schema.
Breaking change adalah perubahan apa pun yang dapat membuat consumer lama gagal compile, gagal runtime, salah decode, atau salah mengambil keputusan bisnis.
```

---

## 8. Version Placement Options

Ada beberapa cara menempatkan versi API.

### A. URI versioning

```text id="2in74o"
/v1/orders
/v2/orders
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Mudah dilihat | Versi jelas di URL |
| Mudah routing | Gateway/Ingress gampang |
| Mudah support paralel | `/v1` dan `/v2` coexist |
| Cocok internal enterprise | Operasional sederhana |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| URL berubah | Consumer perlu migrasi endpoint |
| Bisa terlalu kasar | Semua endpoint terlihat satu versi |
| Resource URI kurang “pure” | Tapi biasanya pragmatis |

### B. Header versioning

```text id="bp7vgf"
GET /orders
API-Version: 1
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| URL tetap bersih | Version ada di header |
| Bisa granular | Gateway bisa route berdasarkan header |
| Cocok untuk advanced API gateway | Lebih fleksibel |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Kurang terlihat | Debug via browser/curl lebih mudah lupa |
| Cache/routing lebih kompleks | Perlu vary/header policy |
| Client generation perlu disiplin | Header harus masuk contract |

### C. Media type versioning

```text id="10o72a"
Accept: application/vnd.company.order-v1+json
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Semantically dekat content negotiation | Versi representasi |
| Cocok public API tertentu | Lebih formal |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Lebih sulit operasional | Banyak tooling internal kurang nyaman |
| Consumer onboarding lebih berat | Perlu paham media type |
| Generated client bisa lebih rumit | Config content type harus rapi |

### D. Spec/artifact version only

```text id="zg8mka"
Path tetap:
/orders

Contract artifact:
order-api.yaml version 1.4.0
order-api-client-1.4.0.jar
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Cocok jika semua change backward-compatible | Tidak perlu URL version |
| Release ringan | Provider evolve secara additive |
| Good for internal microservices | Jika governance kuat |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Breaking change sulit coexist | Butuh path/header baru |
| Consumer harus tracking artifact | Perlu dependency management |
| Runtime tidak selalu tahu versi | Observability perlu tambahan |

---

## 9. Rekomendasi Praktis untuk Internal Microservices

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS enterprise internal microservice, rekomendasi pragmatis:

```text id="a7oqs3"
Gunakan artifact SemVer untuk contract/client:
  order-api-client:1.4.0

Gunakan backward-compatible evolution selama mungkin:
  tambah optional fields/endpoints

Gunakan URI versioning hanya saat benar-benar perlu breaking runtime coexistence:
  /v1/orders
  /v2/orders
```

Jangan membuat `/v2` untuk setiap perubahan kecil. Itu akan menciptakan version sprawl.

Decision rule:

| Situasi | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Tambah endpoint/field optional | Tetap versi path sama, bump MINOR |
| Perbaiki description/example | Bump PATCH |
| Ubah behavior breaking pada endpoint lama | Buat endpoint/contract v2 |
| Consumer lama dan baru harus coexist | URI/header version |
| Hanya internal generated client berubah non-breaking | Artifact MINOR/PATCH |
| Banyak endpoint berubah secara domain besar | Major API version |

---

## 10. Generated Java Client Compatibility

Dalam contract-first Java, compatibility juga berarti generated client aman.

OpenAPI Generator Java generator memiliki opsi yang memengaruhi output seperti `modelPackage`, `apiPackage`, enum naming, nullable handling, dan pilihan library. Perubahan spec kecil bisa mengubah nama class/method generated client jika `operationId`, schema name, atau parameter berubah. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| OpenAPI change | Generated Java impact |
|---|---|
| Rename `operationId` | Method name berubah |
| Rename schema | Class name berubah |
| Rename property | Getter/setter berubah |
| Change type | Java field type berubah |
| Change enum value | Enum constant berubah |
| Add enum value | Generated enum baru, consumer lama belum tahu |
| Add required request field | Constructor/builder/validation bisa berubah |
| Change package config | Import semua consumer berubah |
| Change library `jersey3` → `feign` | Runtime client API berubah total |

Rule:

```text id="c3m9c2"
Stabilkan operationId, schema name, property name, enum value.
Untuk generated client, nama adalah kontrak.
```

---

## 11. Provider Compatibility vs Consumer Compatibility

### Provider compatibility

Provider baru masih menerima request dari consumer lama.

Contoh compatible:

```yaml id="k9h0z2"
CreateOrderRequest:
  type: object
  required:
    - customerId
  properties:
    customerId:
      type: string
    priority:
      type: string
      enum:
        - NORMAL
        - HIGH
      default: NORMAL
```

Consumer lama tidak mengirim `priority`, provider pakai default.

### Consumer compatibility

Consumer lama masih bisa decode response provider baru.

Contoh relatif compatible:

```yaml id="dod6rq"
OrderResponse:
  type: object
  required:
    - orderId
    - status
  properties:
    orderId:
      type: string
    status:
      type: string
    priority:
      type: string
```

Consumer lama mengabaikan `priority`.

Tapi ini hanya aman jika JSON decoder configured untuk ignore unknown fields. Jika generated client/serializer fail on unknown, additive response field pun bisa breaking secara runtime.

---

## 12. Schema Evolution Rules

### Request schema

| Change | Rule |
|---|---|
| Tambah optional request field | Aman |
| Tambah required request field | Breaking |
| Hapus request field | Hati-hati; consumer lama mungkin masih kirim |
| Ubah optional menjadi required | Breaking |
| Ubah required menjadi optional | Biasanya compatible |
| Perketat constraint | Potentially breaking |
| Longgarkan constraint | Biasanya compatible |
| Ubah type | Breaking |
| Rename field | Breaking |

### Response schema

| Change | Rule |
|---|---|
| Tambah optional response field | Biasanya compatible |
| Hapus response field | Breaking |
| Rename response field | Breaking |
| Ubah type | Breaking |
| Ubah non-null menjadi nullable | Potentially breaking |
| Ubah nullable menjadi non-null | Biasanya compatible, jika semantic tetap |
| Tambah enum value | Potentially breaking |
| Hapus enum value | Breaking/potentially breaking |
| Ubah meaning field | Breaking semantic |

### Error schema

| Change | Rule |
|---|---|
| Tambah optional field | Compatible |
| Hapus `errorCode` | Breaking |
| Rename errorCode | Breaking |
| Ubah status code | Breaking/potentially breaking |
| Ubah details structure | Potentially breaking |
| Ubah message | Usually compatible |

---

## 13. Enum Evolution

Enum adalah salah satu sumber breaking change terbesar pada generated Java client.

Contoh:

```yaml id="lr4zvd"
OrderStatus:
  type: string
  enum:
    - DRAFT
    - SUBMITTED
    - CANCELLED
```

Provider menambah:

```yaml id="3x08kr"
    - PENDING_REVIEW
```

Consumer lama bisa mengalami:

```text id="eac8rr"
JSON decode error
unknown enum constant
switch expression tidak handle value baru
business logic default salah
```

Strategi:

| Strategi | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Treat enum expansion as risky | Jangan otomatis anggap compatible |
| Gunakan unknown fallback | `UNKNOWN` atau raw string mapping |
| Adapter mapping | External enum → internal enum with fallback |
| Jangan switch exhaustive tanpa default | Terutama untuk external enum |
| Dokumentasikan enum sebagai evolvable/closed | Contract harus jelas |

Java adapter contoh:

```java id="6hw8xb"
public OrderStatus mapExternalStatus(String externalStatus) {
    return switch (externalStatus) {
        case "DRAFT" -> OrderStatus.DRAFT;
        case "SUBMITTED" -> OrderStatus.SUBMITTED;
        case "CANCELLED" -> OrderStatus.CANCELLED;
        default -> OrderStatus.UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL_STATUS;
    };
}
```

Jika generated client memaksa enum type, pertimbangkan config generator/serializer agar unknown tidak langsung mematikan consumer, atau map lebih awal di adapter.

---

## 14. Nullability Evolution

Nullability bisa lebih berbahaya dari penambahan field.

Contoh lama:

```json id="23528s"
{
  "customerId": "C001",
  "email": "a@example.com"
}
```

Contoh baru:

```json id="k1dgvw"
{
  "customerId": "C001",
  "email": null
}
```

Risiko:

```java id="iv4kcg"
response.getEmail().toLowerCase(); // NPE
```

Rules:

| Change | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Optional absent → nullable null | Consumer bisa NPE |
| Non-null → nullable | Breaking potential |
| Nullable → non-null | Biasanya aman |
| Required field → optional absent | Breaking potential |
| Optional field → required but always present | Usually okay, but generated model changes |

OpenAPI harus eksplisit soal required dan nullable. Tetapi behavior generator untuk nullable berbeda-beda, jadi wajib dites.

---

## 15. Status Code Evolution

Mengubah status code bisa breaking walau response body sama.

Contoh lama:

```text id="69udld"
GET /customers/C404
-> 404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND
```

Consumer adapter:

```java id="nfqew4"
if (e.getCode() == 404) {
    return Optional.empty();
}
```

Provider baru:

```text id="kgaxme"
GET /customers/C404
-> 200 null
```

Consumer lama bisa:

```text id="r5l51y"
decode null
NPE
business logic salah
```

Rule:

```text id="86v6mp"
Status code adalah bagian dari kontrak.
Jangan ubah tanpa compatibility review.
```

---

## 16. Error Code Evolution

`errorCode` lebih stabil daripada `message`.

Breaking:

```json id="h0u17n"
{
  "errorCode": "ORDER_NOT_FOUND"
}
```

diubah menjadi:

```json id="3wryxa"
{
  "errorCode": "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND"
}
```

Consumer lama mungkin punya logic:

```java id="qmswvq"
if ("ORDER_NOT_FOUND".equals(error.errorCode())) {
    return Optional.empty();
}
```

Rule:

| Change | Policy |
|---|---|
| Tambah errorCode baru | Minor, consumer harus punya fallback |
| Rename errorCode | Breaking |
| Remove errorCode | Breaking |
| Ubah semantics errorCode | Breaking |
| Ubah message | Patch/minor, jika tidak diparse |

---

## 17. Semantic Breaking Change

Ini yang paling sulit dideteksi tool.

Contoh field sama, arti berubah:

```yaml id="qjheei"
status:
  type: string
  enum:
    - ACTIVE
    - SUSPENDED
```

Versi lama:

```text id="8gggx4"
ACTIVE = customer can place orders
```

Versi baru:

```text id="zwyq6n"
ACTIVE = customer account exists, but may still be blocked by risk policy
```

Schema tidak berubah, tapi consumer logic bisa salah.

Contoh lain:

| Change | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Default sort berubah | Pagination consumer kehilangan/mengulang item |
| `availableBalance` berubah dari gross ke net | Keputusan financial salah |
| `dueDate` timezone berubah | SLA calculation salah |
| `status=CLOSED` sekarang termasuk auto-closed | Reporting berubah |
| Search filter default berubah | Data yang tampil berbeda |

Mitigasi:

```text id="vav7g3"
Semantics penting harus ditulis di description,
example,
contract tests,
dan changelog.
```

---

## 18. Operational Breaking Change

Schema sama, tapi runtime behavior berubah.

| Operational change | Kenapa breaking |
|---|---|
| Latency naik dari 100ms ke 3s | Consumer timeout |
| Endpoint sekarang butuh auth scope baru | Consumer `403` |
| Rate limit diperketat | Consumer gagal saat batch |
| Pagination max turun | Consumer request lama ditolak |
| Response payload jauh lebih besar | Memory/latency issue |
| Endpoint pindah region/network | Connectivity issue |
| TLS cert chain berubah | Client truststore gagal |
| Header correlation wajib | Consumer lama `400` |
| mTLS diwajibkan | Client lama tidak bisa connect |

Contract-first harus mencatat constraint operasional penting, bukan hanya JSON schema.

---

## 19. Deprecation Strategy

Deprecation berarti API masih berjalan, tapi tidak disarankan untuk penggunaan baru dan akan dihapus pada waktu tertentu.

OpenAPI mendukung field `deprecated: true` pada operation, parameter, schema property, dan beberapa elemen lain tergantung versi/spec usage. Dalam praktik, tambahkan juga description dan migration guidance.

Contoh operation deprecated:

```yaml id="pnk75e"
paths:
  /v1/orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      operationId: getOrderByIdV1
      deprecated: true
      summary: Get order by id.
      description: >
        Deprecated. Use GET /v2/orders/{orderId}.
        This endpoint will be removed after 2027-01-01.
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Order found
```

Tambahkan response header:

```yaml id="cs7dyg"
headers:
  Deprecation:
    description: Indicates that this endpoint is deprecated.
    schema:
      type: string
  Sunset:
    description: Planned date when this endpoint will stop being available.
    schema:
      type: string
      format: date-time
```

---

## 20. Deprecation Lifecycle

```text id="ew0w0f"
1. Introduce replacement
   - v2 endpoint or new field

2. Mark old API deprecated
   - OpenAPI deprecated: true
   - docs/changelog
   - response headers/logs

3. Observe usage
   - metrics by consumer/service
   - access logs
   - generated client dependency scan

4. Notify owners
   - migration guide
   - deadline
   - support window

5. Block new adoption
   - lint/API review prevents new usage

6. Migrate consumers
   - update generated client
   - adapter changes
   - contract tests

7. Sunset
   - remove only after usage zero or approved exception
```

GitHub’s REST API documentation is a useful example of public API governance: it documents REST API breaking changes per API version and provides upgrade guidance by API version. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/about-the-rest-api/breaking-changes?apiVersion=2026-03-10&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 21. Deprecation Observability

Jangan deprecate tanpa mengukur usage.

Minimal metric/log:

```text id="7tw0b8"
deprecated_api_usage_total{
  api="order-api",
  operation="getOrderByIdV1",
  consumer="case-service"
}

deprecated_api_usage_last_seen{
  operation="getOrderByIdV1",
  consumer="case-service"
}
```

Log:

```text id="eczs2w"
level=WARN
event=DEPRECATED_API_USED
operation=getOrderByIdV1
consumer=case-service
correlationId=...
sunsetDate=2027-01-01
```

Response header contoh:

```text id="p6l2m8"
Deprecation: true
Sunset: Fri, 01 Jan 2027 00:00:00 GMT
Link: </v2/orders/{orderId}>; rel="successor-version"
```

---

## 22. Coexistence Strategy: v1 dan v2 Bersama

Saat breaking change perlu dilakukan:

```text id="wy2zvf"
/v1/orders/{orderId}
/v2/orders/{orderId}
```

Provider internal bisa:

```text id="o4t0m6"
Jersey Resource v1
  -> V1 mapper
  -> same application service
  -> domain model

Jersey Resource v2
  -> V2 mapper
  -> same application service
  -> domain model
```

Jangan duplicate semua business logic.

Struktur:

```text id="9tr8tz"
api
  ├── v1
  │   ├── OrderV1Resource.java
  │   ├── OrderV1Mapper.java
  │   └── model
  └── v2
      ├── OrderV2Resource.java
      ├── OrderV2Mapper.java
      └── model

application
  └── OrderApplicationService.java

domain
  └── Order.java
```

Prinsip:

| Layer | Version-aware? |
|---|---:|
| API resource | Ya |
| API DTO | Ya |
| Mapper | Ya |
| Application service | Sebisa mungkin tidak |
| Domain model | Tidak |
| MyBatis mapper | Tidak langsung |
| Database | Tidak per API version kecuali terpaksa |

---

## 23. Adapter Strategy untuk Consumer Migration

Consumer jangan expose generated v1/v2 ke business logic.

```text id="vzi7l5"
Business use case
  |
  v
OrderGateway interface
  |
  +--> OrderV1HttpGateway
  +--> OrderV2HttpGateway
```

Feature toggle / config:

```text id="ou7qmx"
order.gateway.version=v2
```

Migration step:

```text id="oy11vs"
1. Add v2 generated client dependency
2. Implement OrderV2HttpGateway
3. Keep same OrderGateway interface if possible
4. Run contract/integration tests
5. Switch config in staging
6. Observe metrics
7. Roll out production
8. Remove v1 dependency after deprecation window
```

---

## 24. API Diff in CI

Contract-first membutuhkan automated diff.

Pipeline:

```text id="n3azxi"
Pull request changes openapi.yaml
  |
  v
Validate OpenAPI syntax
  |
  v
Lint style
  |
  v
Diff against latest released spec
  |
  v
Classify changes:
  - compatible
  - potentially breaking
  - breaking
  |
  v
Require approval / version bump / migration plan
```

Minimal policy:

| Diff result | Action |
|---|---|
| No contract change | Build normal |
| Compatible additive | Require MINOR bump |
| Documentation-only | Require PATCH bump |
| Potentially breaking | Manual API review |
| Breaking | Block unless MAJOR/v2 migration plan |
| Version not bumped | Fail CI |

OpenAPI Initiative describes OpenAPI as supporting code generation, tests, and design standards; using it in CI for lint/diff/testing is exactly where contract-first becomes operational rather than decorative. ([OpenAPI Initiative](https://www.openapis.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 25. Breaking Change Review Checklist

| Area | Question |
|---|---|
| Endpoint | Apakah path/method berubah? |
| Operation | Apakah `operationId` berubah? |
| Request | Ada required field baru? Constraint diperketat? |
| Response | Ada field dihapus/rename/type berubah? |
| Enum | Ada value baru/hapus/rename? |
| Status | Ada status code berubah? |
| Error | Ada `errorCode` berubah? |
| Auth | Ada scope/header/token baru yang wajib? |
| Pagination | Default/max/sort berubah? |
| Semantics | Makna field/status berubah? |
| Runtime | Latency/rate limit/payload size berubah signifikan? |
| Generated Java | Method/class/getter berubah? |
| Consumer | Consumer mana yang terdampak? |
| Migration | Apakah v1/v2 coexist? |
| Deprecation | Ada sunset date dan observability? |

---

## 26. Changelog Format

Setiap release contract sebaiknya punya changelog.

```markdown id="kxgion"
# Order API 1.4.0

Release date: 2026-07-13

## Added
- Added optional field `priority` to `OrderResponse`.
- Added endpoint `GET /orders/{orderId}/history`.

## Changed
- Clarified description of `OrderStatus.SUBMITTED`.
- Increased max page size from 100 to 200.

## Deprecated
- Deprecated `GET /v1/orders/{orderId}/events`.
  Use `GET /v1/orders/{orderId}/history`.

## Compatibility
- Backward-compatible minor release.
- No required request fields added.
- No response fields removed.
- No status codes changed.

## Consumer action
- Regenerate client if using new fields/endpoints.
- No mandatory migration.
```

Untuk breaking release:

```markdown id="zqv82q"
# Order API 2.0.0

## Breaking
- Replaced `customerName` with structured `customer` object.
- Changed `OrderStatus` values.
- `GET /v1/orders/{orderId}` deprecated; use `/v2/orders/{orderId}`.

## Migration
- Use `customer.displayName` instead of `customerName`.
- Map old status `SUBMITTED` to new `AWAITING_REVIEW` or `CONFIRMED`.
- Migrate before 2027-01-01.
```

---

## 27. Versioning OpenAPI File dan Maven Artifact

Recommended repository layout:

```text id="828ck8"
src/main/openapi
  ├── order-api.yaml
  └── changelog.md

pom.xml
```

Artifact:

```text id="isga4x"
com.company.api:order-api-contract:1.4.0
com.company.api:order-api-java-client:1.4.0
```

Consumer dependency:

```xml id="40hpuq"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company.api</groupId>
    <artifactId>order-api-java-client</artifactId>
    <version>1.4.0</version>
</dependency>
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Client artifact version follows contract version | Consumer tahu kontrak mana dipakai |
| Generated client tidak pakai `LATEST` | Reproducibility |
| Version bump required on spec change | CI enforce |
| Breaking change requires major bump | SemVer discipline |
| Publish spec and client together | Avoid mismatch |

---

## 28. Contract Compatibility dengan Camunda / Workflow

Untuk workflow/case-management, breaking API change bisa merusak process instance lama.

Contoh:

```text id="dr5ih6"
Camunda process instance v12
  -> service task calls POST /cases/{caseId}/assign
```

Jika endpoint berubah:

```text id="rm44zn"
POST /case-assignments
```

Process lama bisa gagal saat retry atau compensation.

Rules:

| Area | Rule |
|---|---|
| Long-running process | Pertahankan endpoint lama sampai process lama selesai |
| External task worker | Version worker dan contract bersama |
| Process variables | Jangan rename field tanpa migration |
| Callback endpoint | Jangan ubah payload tanpa compatibility |
| Error code | BPMN error mapping bergantung pada code |
| Retry behavior | Jangan ubah sync/async semantics sembarangan |

Untuk workflow, deprecation window harus mempertimbangkan durasi process instance, bukan hanya durasi sprint.

---

## 29. Contract Compatibility dengan MyBatis/PostgreSQL

API contract tidak boleh mengikuti DB schema mentah.

Bad:

```text id="3hrcco"
OrderResponse field = table column names
```

Jika DB berubah:

```sql id="6vnlhp"
ALTER TABLE order_hdr RENAME COLUMN cust_nm TO customer_name;
```

API ikut berubah:

```json id="krmt9x"
{
  "custNm": "..."
}
```

menjadi:

```json id="lqpqxe"
{
  "customerName": "..."
}
```

Itu breaking.

Better:

```text id="owrj4z"
DB schema
  -> MyBatis result model
  -> domain/application model
  -> API mapper
  -> stable API response
```

Prinsip:

```text id="64db4w"
Database migration boleh terjadi tanpa API breaking change,
selama API mapper menjaga kontrak eksternal.
```

---

## 30. Contract Compatibility dengan Kubernetes / Cloud

Versioning bukan hanya kode.

| Concern | Compatibility issue |
|---|---|
| Kubernetes Service name | Consumer base URL/DNS berubah |
| Ingress path rewrite | Path version bisa salah route |
| Service mesh mTLS | Client lama tidak punya cert |
| ConfigMap base URL | Consumer route ke v2 tanpa siap |
| Canary release | Consumer lama bisa kena provider baru |
| Blue/green | v1/v2 harus tersedia selama switch |
| API gateway | Header/path version routing harus deterministik |

Deployment rule:

```text id="4l6lda"
Provider baru harus compatible dengan consumer lama
sebelum traffic diarahkan ke provider baru.
```

---

## 31. Safe Rollout Pattern

```text id="uplpgd"
1. Release provider backward-compatible
2. Publish new contract/client artifact
3. Upgrade one consumer in staging
4. Run contract/integration tests
5. Canary provider if needed
6. Observe error/latency/schema decode metrics
7. Roll out consumers gradually
8. Deprecate old contract only after migration
```

Untuk breaking v2:

```text id="j0o95k"
1. Add v2 without removing v1
2. Publish v2 client
3. Migrate consumers one by one
4. Monitor v1 usage
5. Announce sunset
6. Remove v1 after usage zero / approved window
```

---

## 32. Anti-pattern Versioning

| Anti-pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Ubah API tanpa bump version | Consumer tidak punya sinyal |
| `/v2` untuk perubahan minor | Version sprawl |
| Hapus v1 setelah v2 deploy | Consumer lama langsung rusak |
| Generated client pakai latest otomatis | Build tidak reproducible |
| ErrorCode rename diam-diam | Consumer mapping rusak |
| Enum value baru tanpa review | Generated Java consumer gagal |
| Contract file tidak dipublish | Consumer generate dari branch/source tidak stabil |
| Breaking change dianggap “internal” | Microservice lain tetap consumer |
| Deprecation tanpa metrics | Tidak tahu siapa masih pakai |
| API mengikuti DB rename | DB refactor jadi API breaking |
| Satu service support 5 versi tanpa plan sunset | Maintenance cost meledak |
| Semantics berubah tanpa changelog | Tool diff tidak menangkap |

---

## 33. Onboarding Checklist di Repo Baru

Saat masuk codebase, cari:

| Pertanyaan | Lokasi umum |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI spec ada di mana? | `src/main/openapi`, `api/`, repo contract |
| Version contract di mana? | `info.version`, `pom.xml`, artifact registry |
| Ada changelog? | `CHANGELOG.md`, release notes |
| Ada API diff CI? | GitHub Actions, Jenkins, Maven plugin |
| Generated client di-commit atau generated saat build? | `target/generated-sources`, `src/generated` |
| Versioning path/header? | OpenAPI `servers`, `paths`, parameters |
| Ada deprecated endpoint? | `deprecated: true` |
| Ada usage metric deprecated API? | dashboard/log query |
| Ada SemVer rule? | engineering handbook |
| Error code dianggap contract? | error registry/docs |
| Enum unknown handling? | generated config/serializer |
| Consumer dependency pinned? | `pom.xml` |
| v1/v2 coexist pattern? | resource packages |

---

## 34. Practical Governance Policy

Contoh policy ringan tapi efektif:

```text id="5owz9p"
Every OpenAPI change must:
  1. pass OpenAPI validation
  2. pass style lint
  3. generate Java client successfully
  4. compile generated client
  5. run API diff against latest released spec
  6. include version bump
  7. include changelog entry
  8. require API owner approval for potential breaking change
```

Breaking change allowed only if:

```text id="qwr9gg"
- new version/path/header strategy exists
- migration guide exists
- impacted consumers identified
- deprecation/sunset plan exists
- usage metrics exist
- rollback plan exists
```

---

## 35. Ringkasan

```text id="fu94ym"
API versioning =
  contract version
  + compatibility rules
  + generated client stability
  + changelog
  + API diff
  + deprecation lifecycle
  + coexistence strategy
  + consumer migration
```

Core rules:

```text id="lmrb1d"
Additive change -> MINOR
Documentation/clarification -> PATCH
Breaking change -> MAJOR or v2 endpoint
Deprecated != removed
ErrorCode/status/schema names are contract
Enum expansion is risky for Java generated clients
Operational changes can be breaking
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Tujuan versioning bukan membuat banyak versi, tetapi memungkinkan perubahan tanpa merusak consumer dan tanpa menghentikan release independen antar microservice.**
