# Seri 7/16 - OpenAPI Generator dengan Maven

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada:

> **Bagaimana men-setup OpenAPI Generator di Maven agar Java client/server artifact bisa digenerate secara repeatable, compile-safe, dan cocok untuk Jersey/JAX-RS microservice contract-first.**

Di contract-first workflow, Maven generation biasanya menjadi titik penghubung antara:

```text id="mixk69"
openapi.yaml
  -> generated Java API client
  -> generated DTO/model
  -> generated invoker/runtime config
  -> compile check
  -> client adapter
  -> consumer microservice
```

OpenAPI Generator mendukung pembuatan client libraries, server stubs, dokumentasi, dan konfigurasi dari OpenAPI spec. Java generator-nya berstatus **STABLE** dan mendukung berbagai HTTP library seperti Jersey, Retrofit, OpenFeign, dan lainnya. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Posisi OpenAPI Generator dalam Contract-first Flow

```text id="muhchd"
OpenAPI Contract
  |
  v
Maven openapi-generator-maven-plugin
  |
  +--> Java client API classes
  +--> Java model/DTO classes
  +--> ApiClient / invoker classes
  +--> optional docs/tests
  |
  v
Compile generated code
  |
  v
Wrap generated client in adapter/gateway
  |
  v
Business service uses stable internal interface
```

Prinsip utamanya:

```text id="68ze69"
Generated code boleh dipakai,
tetapi jangan biarkan generated code menjadi domain boundary.
```

Generated client adalah **transport contract artifact**, bukan domain model.

---

## 3. Maven Plugin Dasar

OpenAPI Generator menyediakan Maven plugin `openapi-generator-maven-plugin`; dokumentasi plugin menunjukkan goal `generate`, dan default phase-nya adalah `generate-sources`. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/plugins/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh minimal:

```xml id="gjuvep"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.openapitools</groupId>
    <artifactId>openapi-generator-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${openapi-generator.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>generate-customer-client</id>
            <goals>
                <goal>generate</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <inputSpec>${project.basedir}/src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml</inputSpec>
                <generatorName>java</generatorName>
                <library>jersey3</library>
                <apiPackage>com.company.customer.client.api</apiPackage>
                <modelPackage>com.company.customer.client.model</modelPackage>
                <invokerPackage>com.company.customer.client.invoker</invokerPackage>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

Dokumentasi plugin yang saya cek menampilkan contoh versi `7.23.0`; gunakan versi yang sudah distandardisasi di perusahaan agar toolchain CI, generator output, dan dependency runtime tidak berubah mendadak. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/plugins/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Struktur Project yang Direkomendasikan

Untuk microservice consumer:

```text id="g0xwv6"
order-service
  ├── pom.xml
  ├── src/main/openapi
  │   └── customer-api.yaml
  ├── src/main/java
  │   └── com/company/order
  │       ├── application
  │       ├── domain
  │       └── infrastructure
  │           └── customer
  │               ├── CustomerGateway.java
  │               ├── CustomerHttpGateway.java
  │               └── CustomerMapper.java
  └── target/generated-sources/openapi
      └── src/main/java
          └── com/company/customer/client
              ├── api
              ├── model
              └── invoker
```

Boundary yang sehat:

| Area | Isi |
|---|---|
| `src/main/openapi` | Contract file yang menjadi input generator |
| `target/generated-sources/openapi` | Output generated code |
| `infrastructure/customer` | Adapter yang membungkus generated client |
| `application/domain` | Tidak tahu generated classes |
| `pom.xml` | Version pinning, generator config, compile integration |

---

## 5. Konfigurasi Penting Maven Plugin

| Config | Fungsi | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|---|
| `inputSpec` | Lokasi OpenAPI YAML/JSON | Pakai path repo atau artifact unpacked |
| `generatorName` | Generator yang dipakai | `java` untuk client Java |
| `library` | HTTP client library target | `jersey3` atau `feign`, sesuai standard |
| `apiPackage` | Package class API operation | Stabilkan sejak awal |
| `modelPackage` | Package DTO/model | Jangan berubah sembarangan |
| `invokerPackage` | Package runtime client/helper | Pisahkan dari domain package |
| `output` | Folder output | Umumnya `target/generated-sources/openapi/...` |
| `generateApiTests` | Generate test skeleton | Sering dimatikan |
| `generateModelTests` | Generate model test skeleton | Sering dimatikan |
| `configOptions` | Generator-specific options | Pin opsi penting |
| `additionalProperties` | Template/additional properties | Pakai hati-hati |

OpenAPI Generator Maven plugin juga mendukung konfigurasi seperti `typeMappings`, `nameMappings`, `modelNameMappings`, `parameterNameMappings`, `inlineSchemaNameMappings`, dan `additionalProperties`, yang berguna untuk mengendalikan hasil generated code ketika spec punya nama/type yang kurang ideal. ([GitHub](https://github.com/OpenAPITools/openapi-generator/blob/master/modules/openapi-generator-maven-plugin/README.md?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. `jersey3` Client Generation

Untuk stack Jersey/JAX-RS modern, pilihan natural adalah Java generator dengan library Jersey 3.

Contoh:

```xml id="cjxea1"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.openapitools</groupId>
    <artifactId>openapi-generator-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${openapi-generator.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>generate-customer-jersey-client</id>
            <phase>generate-sources</phase>
            <goals>
                <goal>generate</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <inputSpec>${project.basedir}/src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml</inputSpec>
                <generatorName>java</generatorName>
                <library>jersey3</library>
                <output>${project.build.directory}/generated-sources/openapi/customer-client</output>

                <apiPackage>com.company.customer.client.api</apiPackage>
                <modelPackage>com.company.customer.client.model</modelPackage>
                <invokerPackage>com.company.customer.client.invoker</invokerPackage>

                <generateApiTests>false</generateApiTests>
                <generateModelTests>false</generateModelTests>
                <generateApiDocumentation>false</generateApiDocumentation>
                <generateModelDocumentation>false</generateModelDocumentation>

                <configOptions>
                    <dateLibrary>java8</dateLibrary>
                    <serializationLibrary>jackson</serializationLibrary>
                    <useJakartaEe>true</useJakartaEe>
                    <hideGenerationTimestamp>true</hideGenerationTimestamp>
                </configOptions>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

Catatan penting:

| Option | Catatan |
|---|---|
| `library=jersey3` | Cocok untuk stack Jakarta/Jersey modern |
| `useJakartaEe=true` | Penting agar generated code memakai namespace Jakarta jika generator/library mendukungnya |
| `dateLibrary=java8` | Umumnya menghasilkan type Java time API |
| `serializationLibrary=jackson` | Pilih sesuai runtime standard perusahaan |
| `hideGenerationTimestamp=true` | Mengurangi noise diff generated code |

Dokumentasi Java generator menyebut generator `java` menghasilkan Java client library dan mendukung HTTP library termasuk Jersey dan OpenFeign. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. `feign` / OpenFeign Client Generation

Jika tim memakai OpenFeign untuk declarative HTTP client:

```xml id="jix2id"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.openapitools</groupId>
    <artifactId>openapi-generator-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${openapi-generator.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>generate-customer-feign-client</id>
            <phase>generate-sources</phase>
            <goals>
                <goal>generate</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <inputSpec>${project.basedir}/src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml</inputSpec>
                <generatorName>java</generatorName>
                <library>feign</library>
                <output>${project.build.directory}/generated-sources/openapi/customer-feign-client</output>

                <apiPackage>com.company.customer.client.api</apiPackage>
                <modelPackage>com.company.customer.client.model</modelPackage>
                <invokerPackage>com.company.customer.client.invoker</invokerPackage>

                <generateApiTests>false</generateApiTests>
                <generateModelTests>false</generateModelTests>

                <configOptions>
                    <dateLibrary>java8</dateLibrary>
                    <serializationLibrary>jackson</serializationLibrary>
                    <hideGenerationTimestamp>true</hideGenerationTimestamp>
                </configOptions>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

Perlu dibedakan:

| Item | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| OpenFeign core | Declarative HTTP client library |
| Spring Cloud OpenFeign | Integrasi khusus Spring ecosystem |
| Jersey/JAX-RS app non-Spring | Biasanya butuh wiring manual via HK2/CDI/factory sendiri |

Untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS non-Spring, jangan berasumsi annotation `@FeignClient` akan tersedia. Gunakan OpenFeign core atau generated Feign client sesuai hasil generator dan dependency yang dipakai.

---

## 8. Dependency Runtime yang Perlu Dicek

Generator hanya membuat source code. Anda tetap perlu dependency runtime yang cocok.

Contoh kasar untuk Jersey 3 client:

```xml id="ny4wmh"
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.core</groupId>
        <artifactId>jersey-client</artifactId>
        <version>${jersey.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.inject</groupId>
        <artifactId>jersey-hk2</artifactId>
        <version>${jersey.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.media</groupId>
        <artifactId>jersey-media-json-jackson</artifactId>
        <version>${jersey.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Contoh kasar untuk Feign:

```xml id="elt9vs"
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>io.github.openfeign</groupId>
        <artifactId>feign-core</artifactId>
        <version>${feign.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>io.github.openfeign</groupId>
        <artifactId>feign-jackson</artifactId>
        <version>${feign.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>io.github.openfeign</groupId>
        <artifactId>feign-slf4j</artifactId>
        <version>${feign.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Jangan copy dependency mentah tanpa melihat generated output. Cek import yang dihasilkan:

```text id="qu2pmd"
jakarta.ws.rs.*
javax.ws.rs.*
org.glassfish.jersey.*
feign.*
com.fasterxml.jackson.*
```

Jika generated code memakai `javax.*` sementara aplikasi modern Jakarta memakai `jakarta.*`, itu harus diselesaikan lewat generator config, versi generator, atau pilihan library.

---

## 9. Menambahkan Generated Sources ke Maven Compile

Banyak setup plugin sudah menambahkan generated sources otomatis, tetapi jangan asumsikan. Jika perlu, gunakan `build-helper-maven-plugin`.

```xml id="wzz1hr"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.codehaus.mojo</groupId>
    <artifactId>build-helper-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${build-helper-maven-plugin.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>add-openapi-generated-sources</id>
            <phase>generate-sources</phase>
            <goals>
                <goal>add-source</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <sources>
                    <source>${project.build.directory}/generated-sources/openapi/customer-client/src/main/java</source>
                </sources>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

CI harus minimal menjalankan:

```bash id="cl3cqo"
mvn clean verify
```

Bukan hanya:

```bash id="axevsv"
mvn test
```

Karena Anda ingin memastikan:

```text id="tq488y"
OpenAPI valid
  -> generated code berhasil dibuat
  -> generated code masuk compile path
  -> generated code compile
  -> adapter compile terhadap generated API
```

---

## 10. Multi-contract Generation

Satu service bisa consume banyak downstream API:

```text id="zqnccl"
order-service
  consumes:
    customer-api
    payment-api
    inventory-api
```

Maven plugin bisa punya beberapa execution:

```xml id="oaux9d"
<executions>
    <execution>
        <id>generate-customer-client</id>
        ...
    </execution>

    <execution>
        <id>generate-payment-client</id>
        ...
    </execution>

    <execution>
        <id>generate-inventory-client</id>
        ...
    </execution>
</executions>
```

Pastikan package tidak tabrakan:

```text id="6edxth"
com.company.customer.client.api
com.company.customer.client.model
com.company.customer.client.invoker

com.company.payment.client.api
com.company.payment.client.model
com.company.payment.client.invoker

com.company.inventory.client.api
com.company.inventory.client.model
com.company.inventory.client.invoker
```

Anti-pattern:

```text id="ghol5b"
com.company.generated.api
com.company.generated.model
```

untuk semua downstream. Itu akan bentrok dan sulit dimengerti.

---

## 11. Generated Code: Commit atau Tidak?

Ada dua strategi.

### A. Generate saat build, tidak commit generated code

```text id="gvjwzs"
src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml
  -> mvn generate-sources
  -> target/generated-sources/openapi
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Repo bersih | Tidak banyak generated noise |
| Output reproducible | Jika generator version dipin |
| Review fokus ke spec dan adapter | Bukan file generated |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Build bergantung generator | Wajib stabil di CI |
| Diff generated tidak terlihat | Kadang sulit lihat impact Java |
| IDE setup perlu benar | Generated sources harus dikenali |

### B. Commit generated code

```text id="uc494x"
src/generated/java/...
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Reviewer bisa lihat Java impact | Terutama method/class berubah |
| IDE langsung mengenali | Tidak perlu generate dulu |
| Build lebih eksplisit | Source sudah ada |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Diff besar | Noise tinggi |
| Merge conflict | Sering terjadi |
| Bisa drift dari spec | Jika tidak regenerate konsisten |

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="x6ydda"
Untuk internal service biasa:
  generate saat build, jangan commit target generated code.

Untuk published client SDK artifact:
  generate dalam dedicated module,
  publish JAR,
  jangan biarkan tiap consumer generate sendiri dari source random.
```

---

## 12. Dedicated API Client Module

Untuk enterprise, lebih rapi membuat module khusus:

```text id="2gpapv"
customer-api-client
  ├── pom.xml
  ├── src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml
  └── target/generated-sources/openapi
```

Lalu publish:

```text id="a1584w"
com.company.api:customer-api-client:1.4.0
```

Consumer:

```xml id="bjzaa1"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company.api</groupId>
    <artifactId>customer-api-client</artifactId>
    <version>1.4.0</version>
</dependency>
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Consumer tidak generate sendiri | Dependency lebih stabil |
| Version jelas | Client version mengikuti contract |
| Build consumer lebih cepat | Tidak generate banyak spec |
| Governance lebih kuat | API owner publish client artifact |
| Compatibility lebih mudah | Version bump terkontrol |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Perlu release pipeline client | Ada proses tambahan |
| Consumer menunggu artifact | Perlu publish discipline |
| Adapter tetap dibutuhkan | Jangan pakai generated client langsung di domain |

---

## 13. Parent POM Version Pinning

Jangan hardcode versi plugin di banyak service. Gunakan parent/platform POM.

```xml id="48l8j9"
<properties>
    <openapi-generator.version>7.23.0</openapi-generator.version>
    <jersey.version>3.x.x</jersey.version>
    <jackson.version>2.x.x</jackson.version>
</properties>
```

Atau di `pluginManagement`:

```xml id="gyta9p"
<build>
    <pluginManagement>
        <plugins>
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.openapitools</groupId>
                <artifactId>openapi-generator-maven-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>${openapi-generator.version}</version>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </pluginManagement>
</build>
```

Rules:

| Rule | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Pin generator version | Output generated code bisa berubah antar versi |
| Pin runtime dependencies | Hindari mismatch compile/runtime |
| Upgrade generator terkontrol | Treat seperti platform migration |
| Jangan pakai floating/latest | Build tidak reproducible |
| Dokumentasikan generator options | Agar service lain konsisten |

---

## 14. `configOptions` yang Umum Dipakai

| Option | Tujuan | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `dateLibrary` | Mapping date/time | Pilih sesuai standard Java time |
| `serializationLibrary` | JSON library | Jackson/JSON-B/Gson tergantung generator/library |
| `useJakartaEe` | Namespace Jakarta | Penting untuk Jakarta EE modern bila didukung |
| `hideGenerationTimestamp` | Output lebih deterministic | Kurangi diff noise |
| `enumPropertyNaming` | Naming enum constant | Default Java-friendly belum tentu cocok semua |
| `sourceFolder` | Folder source di output | Berguna untuk layout custom |
| `useRuntimeException` | Exception checked/unchecked | Pertimbangkan adapter style |
| `openApiNullable` | Nullable handling | Perlu dites dengan serializer |
| `disallowAdditionalPropertiesIfNotPresent` | Additional properties behavior | Perlu align dengan schema governance |

Dokumentasi generator Java mencantumkan berbagai configuration options seperti package, enum naming, date library, dynamic operations, dan opsi lain yang memengaruhi generated code. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Type Mapping dan Name Mapping

Kadang spec external tidak ideal, tetapi Anda belum bisa mengubahnya.

Contoh masalah:

```yaml id="3zed7o"
schemas:
  _Response:
    type: object
```

Atau property:

```yaml id="qll61i"
properties:
  class:
    type: string
```

Gunakan mappings:

```xml id="pkodjz"
<configuration>
    ...
    <modelNameMappings>
        <modelNameMapping>_Response=CustomerResponse</modelNameMapping>
    </modelNameMappings>

    <nameMappings>
        <nameMapping>class=clazz</nameMapping>
    </nameMappings>

    <typeMappings>
        <typeMapping>string+date-time=java.time.OffsetDateTime</typeMapping>
    </typeMappings>
</configuration>
```

Prinsip:

| Gunakan mapping saat | Jangan gunakan mapping untuk |
|---|---|
| Spec milik vendor/external dan tidak bisa diubah | Menutupi spec internal yang buruk |
| Nama bentrok reserved word Java | Menghindari API review |
| Type mapping butuh standard runtime | Mengubah semantic diam-diam |
| Inline schema name jelek | Mengabaikan schema naming governance |

Untuk internal API, lebih baik perbaiki OpenAPI spec daripada menambal generator config.

---

## 16. Inline Schema Problem

Bad OpenAPI:

```yaml id="7m7awn"
responses:
  '200':
    description: OK
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          type: object
          properties:
            customerId:
              type: string
            displayName:
              type: string
```

Generator bisa membuat nama model otomatis yang buruk:

```text id="ku36gy"
GetCustomerById200Response
InlineObject1
```

Better:

```yaml id="bak0lb"
responses:
  '200':
    description: Customer found
    content:
      application/json:
        schema:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/CustomerResponse'
```

Schema:

```yaml id="mwke2u"
components:
  schemas:
    CustomerResponse:
      type: object
      required:
        - customerId
        - displayName
      properties:
        customerId:
          type: string
        displayName:
          type: string
```

Rule:

```text id="h7wpnm"
Untuk contract-first Java,
hindari anonymous inline object untuk request/response utama.
Beri nama schema eksplisit.
```

---

## 17. OperationId dan Generated Method

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="fvsxl5"
operationId: getCustomerById
```

Generated client kira-kira:

```java id="jmd7jm"
customerApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
```

Bad:

```yaml id="zc8cyp"
operationId: get
```

Generated:

```java id="my7jqp"
customerApi.get(...);
```

Atau jika operationId tidak ada, generator bisa membuat nama dari method/path yang tidak enak dibaca.

Rules:

| Rule | Alasan |
|---|---|
| `operationId` wajib | Generated method stabil |
| Unik dalam satu spec | Hindari collision |
| Verb + object + qualifier | `getCustomerById`, `searchOrders` |
| Jangan rename tanpa major/breaking review | Method generated berubah |
| Jangan pakai overloaded ambiguity | Java client readability turun |

---

## 18. Package Governance

Gunakan package yang menunjukkan **provider API**, bukan consumer service.

Misal `order-service` consume `customer-api`.

Good:

```text id="zbn85i"
com.company.customer.client.api
com.company.customer.client.model
com.company.customer.client.invoker
```

Avoid:

```text id="w2wlg7"
com.company.order.generated.api
com.company.order.generated.model
```

Kenapa?

| Alasan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Jelas sumber kontrak | Ini client untuk Customer API |
| Multi-client tidak tabrakan | Payment/Inventory punya package sendiri |
| Mudah migrate version | Bisa `customer.v1.client`, `customer.v2.client` |
| Review lebih jelas | Import menunjukkan dependency service |

Untuk v1/v2 coexist:

```text id="97cxv7"
com.company.customer.v1.client.api
com.company.customer.v1.client.model

com.company.customer.v2.client.api
com.company.customer.v2.client.model
```

---

## 19. Adapter Setelah Generation

Generated client jangan langsung masuk use case.

Bad:

```java id="069dss"
public class SubmitOrderUseCase {
    private final CustomersApi customersApi;

    public void submit(String customerId) {
        CustomerResponse customer = customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
        ...
    }
}
```

Better:

```java id="xs0aco"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

```java id="f8zc9l"
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomersApi customersApi;
    private final CustomerClientErrorMapper errorMapper;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(map(response));
        } catch (ApiException e) {
            return errorMapper.mapCustomerLookupError(e, customerId);
        }
    }

    private CustomerProfile map(CustomerResponse response) {
        return new CustomerProfile(
                new CustomerId(response.getCustomerId()),
                response.getDisplayName()
        );
    }
}
```

Boundary:

```text id="2lkhyv"
Generated CustomerResponse
  exists only in infrastructure adapter.

Application/domain sees:
  CustomerProfile
  CustomerGateway
  CustomerId
```

---

## 20. Runtime Config: Base URL

Generated client biasanya butuh base URL.

Bad:

```java id="4nn8vb"
apiClient.setBasePath("http://customer-service.prod.svc.cluster.local");
```

Better:

```text id="itgd5q"
customer.client.base-url=http://customer-service.default.svc.cluster.local
customer.client.connect-timeout-ms=100
customer.client.read-timeout-ms=500
```

Factory:

```java id="hxgosw"
public final class CustomerClientFactory {

    public CustomersApi create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        ApiClient apiClient = new ApiClient();
        apiClient.setBasePath(properties.baseUrl());

        // configure timeout, auth, filters, object mapper if supported

        return new CustomersApi(apiClient);
    }
}
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Base URL environment-driven | Kubernetes/on-prem/cloud beda |
| Jangan hardcode prod URL | Sulit test/staging |
| Config harus validated at startup | Fail fast jika kosong |
| Support service DNS | Kubernetes Service, ingress, gateway |
| Observability tag pakai logical service name | Jangan parse dari URL |

---

## 21. Runtime Config: Timeout

Generated client sering tidak cukup jika timeout tidak disetel.

Minimal config:

```text id="yw202b"
customer.client.connect-timeout-ms=100
customer.client.read-timeout-ms=500
customer.client.max-attempts=2
```

Jangan lakukan:

```text id="ivclki"
Tidak tahu default timeout library.
```

Adapter/factory harus memastikan:

| Timeout | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Connect timeout | Batas membuka koneksi |
| Read/request timeout | Batas menunggu response |
| Overall deadline | Batas seluruh use case |
| Retry budget | Total retry tidak melebihi caller SLA |

Rule:

```text id="we372y"
Generated client setup belum production-ready
sampai timeout, auth, error decoding, logging, dan tracing dikonfigurasi.
```

---

## 22. Runtime Config: Auth Header

Generated client bisa mendukung auth scheme dari OpenAPI, tetapi integrasi real sering perlu token provider.

Contoh wrapper:

```java id="6rufm3"
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomersApi customersApi;
    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;

    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId) {
        String token = tokenProvider.getToken("customer-api.read");

        // Depending on generated client:
        // apiClient.setBearerToken(token)
        // or request filter injects Authorization header

        ...
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

| Concern | Rule |
|---|---|
| Service token | Jangan hardcode |
| User token propagation | Harus eksplisit policy |
| Token refresh | Jangan refresh di setiap call jika mahal |
| mTLS | Biasanya di HTTP client/platform config |
| Tenant/user context | Header contract harus jelas |
| Auth failure | Map `401/403` berbeda dari system failure |

---

## 23. Generated Client dan ObjectMapper

Jika runtime memakai Jackson, pastikan ObjectMapper generated client selaras dengan service.

Concern:

| Area | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Date/time | `OffsetDateTime` vs `LocalDateTime` |
| Unknown fields | Additive response field bisa gagal jika fail-on-unknown |
| Enum unknown | Generated enum bisa gagal deserialize |
| Null handling | Field absent/null semantics |
| BigDecimal | Precision financial/regulatory |
| Java records | Generator/library support |
| JSON-B vs Jackson | Annotation/runtime mismatch |

Rule:

```text id="1db0kn"
Serializer config adalah bagian dari compatibility.
Jangan biarkan generated client memakai ObjectMapper default yang beda dari standard platform.
```

---

## 24. CI Compile Check

Contract-first pipeline minimal:

```text id="ubestl"
mvn clean verify
  |
  v
openapi-generator:generate
  |
  v
compile generated code
  |
  v
compile adapter
  |
  v
run tests
```

Tambahkan quality gate:

| Gate | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Validate OpenAPI | Spec syntactically valid |
| Lint OpenAPI | Naming/status/error convention |
| Generate client | Spec generator-compatible |
| Compile generated client | Type/package/dependency benar |
| Compile adapter | Consumer code sesuai generated API |
| Run unit tests | Mapping/error handling benar |
| Run contract tests | Provider/consumer alignment |
| API diff | Breaking change detection |

OpenAPI Generator docs menekankan generation dari OpenAPI spec ke client/server/docs/configuration; dalam CI, compile generated output adalah cara praktis memastikan spec bukan hanya valid YAML, tapi juga bisa menjadi Java artifact yang usable. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openapitools/openapi-generator?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 25. Maven Profile untuk Generation

Kadang generation ingin bisa dikontrol.

```xml id="pyr5ps"
<profiles>
    <profile>
        <id>generate-openapi-clients</id>
        <build>
            <plugins>
                <!-- openapi-generator plugin here -->
            </plugins>
        </build>
    </profile>
</profiles>
```

Run:

```bash id="k1x3gy"
mvn -Pgenerate-openapi-clients clean verify
```

Tapi hati-hati:

| Jika generation profile optional | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Developer lupa enable profile | Compile lokal beda dari CI |
| Generated code stale | Adapter compile terhadap output lama |
| CI dan lokal tidak sama | Bug baru muncul di pipeline |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="dkvmc7"
Untuk service utama:
  generate-sources aktif default.

Untuk task khusus:
  profile boleh, tapi CI wajib menjalankannya.
```

---

## 26. Validasi OpenAPI Sebelum Generate

Tambahkan validasi eksplisit jika toolchain mendukung.

Pseudo pipeline:

```bash id="z5p0ce"
mvn openapi-generator:validate
mvn openapi-generator:generate
mvn compile
```

Atau gunakan linter/diff tool terpisah di CI.

Validasi yang perlu:

| Validasi | Contoh error |
|---|---|
| YAML syntax | Indentation salah |
| OpenAPI schema | Missing required `responses` |
| `$ref` valid | Broken reference |
| OperationId unique | Duplicate method |
| Schema naming | Inline/generated ugly |
| Response status | Missing error matrix |
| Security scheme | Undefined scheme |
| Generator compatibility | Java reserved word |

---

## 27. Handling Generated Test/Docs Noise

Generator bisa menghasilkan API/model tests dan docs. Untuk service consumer, sering lebih baik dimatikan:

```xml id="dvlqfk"
<generateApiTests>false</generateApiTests>
<generateModelTests>false</generateModelTests>
<generateApiDocumentation>false</generateApiDocumentation>
<generateModelDocumentation>false</generateModelDocumentation>
```

Kenapa?

| Output | Masalah |
|---|---|
| Generated tests | Sering skeleton kosong/noisy |
| Generated docs | Bisa duplikat dengan API catalog |
| Generated model docs | Jarang dibaca di service repo |
| Generated README | Noise jika output tidak dipublish |

Tetapi untuk dedicated SDK artifact, docs bisa berguna.

Decision:

| Context | Docs/tests |
|---|---|
| Internal consumer service | Biasanya disable |
| Published API client SDK | Pertimbangkan enable docs |
| API contract module | Docs bisa dipublish |
| CI compile-only | Disable generated tests |

---

## 28. Template Customization

OpenAPI Generator menggunakan template. Dokumentasi templating menjelaskan bahwa Java generator mendukung `library` option; base templates bisa dioverride oleh library-specific templates, lalu fallback ke default template jika tidak dioverride. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/templating/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan custom template hanya jika perlu.

Use case yang masuk akal:

| Use case | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Standard logging hook | Tambahkan interceptor/filter |
| Standard auth injection | Integrasi token provider |
| Standard exception model | Custom error decoder |
| Corporate annotations | Add generated annotations |
| Deterministic code style | Formatting/naming tertentu |

Risiko:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Upgrade generator lebih sulit | Template internal bisa conflict |
| Knowledge tersentral tapi tersembunyi | Developer harus paham template |
| Debug generated code lebih susah | Output tidak sama dengan default docs |
| Over-customization | Anda membangun framework sendiri |

Rule:

```text id="u28oyi"
Mulai dari default generator.
Custom template hanya jika adapter/config tidak cukup.
```

---

## 29. Common Build Problems

| Problem | Gejala | Penyebab umum | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| Generated code tidak compile | Missing imports | Dependency runtime kurang | Tambah dependency sesuai generated import |
| `javax.*` vs `jakarta.*` | Compile error namespace | Config/library/generator mismatch | Aktifkan Jakarta option atau ganti library/version |
| Duplicate classes | Class already exists | Multi execution package sama | Pisahkan package/output |
| Method name aneh | `operationUsingGET` | `operationId` buruk/hilang | Perbaiki OpenAPI |
| Model name aneh | `InlineObject1` | Inline schema | Buat named schema |
| Enum compile issue | Invalid enum names | Enum values tidak Java-friendly | Gunakan enum naming/mapping |
| Date type salah | `Date` bukan `OffsetDateTime` | `dateLibrary` default | Set `dateLibrary` |
| Error body sulit decode | Generated client tidak decode error | Perlu custom decoder/adapter | Implement error mapper |
| Build berubah tiap run | Timestamp/noisy generation | Timestamp output | `hideGenerationTimestamp` |
| CI beda dari lokal | Plugin/profile beda | Tidak pin version/profile | Pin dan jalankan default |

---

## 30. Example: Complete Consumer Setup

```xml id="vt5x0z"
<properties>
    <openapi-generator.version>7.23.0</openapi-generator.version>
    <jersey.version>3.x.x</jersey.version>
    <jackson.version>2.x.x</jackson.version>
</properties>

<build>
    <plugins>
        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.openapitools</groupId>
            <artifactId>openapi-generator-maven-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>${openapi-generator.version}</version>
            <executions>
                <execution>
                    <id>generate-customer-client</id>
                    <phase>generate-sources</phase>
                    <goals>
                        <goal>generate</goal>
                    </goals>
                    <configuration>
                        <inputSpec>${project.basedir}/src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml</inputSpec>
                        <generatorName>java</generatorName>
                        <library>jersey3</library>
                        <output>${project.build.directory}/generated-sources/openapi/customer-client</output>

                        <apiPackage>com.company.customer.client.api</apiPackage>
                        <modelPackage>com.company.customer.client.model</modelPackage>
                        <invokerPackage>com.company.customer.client.invoker</invokerPackage>

                        <generateApiTests>false</generateApiTests>
                        <generateModelTests>false</generateModelTests>
                        <generateApiDocumentation>false</generateApiDocumentation>
                        <generateModelDocumentation>false</generateModelDocumentation>

                        <configOptions>
                            <dateLibrary>java8</dateLibrary>
                            <serializationLibrary>jackson</serializationLibrary>
                            <useJakartaEe>true</useJakartaEe>
                            <hideGenerationTimestamp>true</hideGenerationTimestamp>
                        </configOptions>
                    </configuration>
                </execution>
            </executions>
        </plugin>

        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.codehaus.mojo</groupId>
            <artifactId>build-helper-maven-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>${build-helper-maven-plugin.version}</version>
            <executions>
                <execution>
                    <id>add-openapi-generated-sources</id>
                    <phase>generate-sources</phase>
                    <goals>
                        <goal>add-source</goal>
                    </goals>
                    <configuration>
                        <sources>
                            <source>${project.build.directory}/generated-sources/openapi/customer-client/src/main/java</source>
                        </sources>
                    </configuration>
                </execution>
            </executions>
        </plugin>
    </plugins>
</build>
```

Catatan: versi dependency `jersey.version`, `jackson.version`, dan `build-helper-maven-plugin.version` harus mengikuti platform POM/internal BOM perusahaan, bukan angka random.

---

## 31. Example: Generated Client Factory

```java id="u3a741"
public final class CustomerApiClientFactory {

    public CustomersApi create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        ApiClient apiClient = new ApiClient();

        apiClient.setBasePath(properties.baseUrl());

        // Depending on generated client support:
        // apiClient.setConnectTimeout(properties.connectTimeoutMs());
        // apiClient.setReadTimeout(properties.readTimeoutMs());
        // apiClient.addDefaultHeader("X-Service-Name", properties.serviceName());

        return new CustomersApi(apiClient);
    }
}
```

Properties:

```java id="1d8cc6"
public record CustomerClientProperties(
        String baseUrl,
        int connectTimeoutMs,
        int readTimeoutMs,
        String serviceName
) {
    public CustomerClientProperties {
        if (baseUrl == null || baseUrl.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("customer client baseUrl is required");
        }
        if (connectTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("connectTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
        if (readTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("readTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 32. Example: Adapter Wrapping Generated Client

```java id="fmpyiq"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

```java id="w3z8jp"
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomersApi customersApi;
    private final CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper;

    public CustomerHttpGateway(
            CustomersApi customersApi,
            CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper
    ) {
        this.customersApi = customersApi;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(toCustomerProfile(response));
        } catch (ApiException exception) {
            return errorMapper.mapFindByIdError(exception, customerId);
        }
    }

    private CustomerProfile toCustomerProfile(CustomerResponse response) {
        return new CustomerProfile(
                new CustomerId(response.getCustomerId()),
                response.getDisplayName(),
                mapStatus(response.getStatus())
        );
    }

    private CustomerStatus mapStatus(CustomerResponse.StatusEnum status) {
        if (status == null) {
            return CustomerStatus.UNKNOWN;
        }

        return switch (status) {
            case ACTIVE -> CustomerStatus.ACTIVE;
            case SUSPENDED -> CustomerStatus.SUSPENDED;
            case CLOSED -> CustomerStatus.CLOSED;
            default -> CustomerStatus.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }
}
```

---

## 33. Review Checklist untuk OpenAPI Generator Maven Setup

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Version | Generator version dipin |
| Input spec | Path jelas dan masuk repo/artifact |
| Generator | `generatorName=java` untuk client Java |
| Library | `jersey3` atau `feign` dipilih sadar |
| Package | `apiPackage`, `modelPackage`, `invokerPackage` stabil |
| Output | Ke `target/generated-sources`, bukan random folder |
| Tests/docs | Disabled/enabled sesuai kebutuhan |
| Jakarta namespace | `useJakartaEe` dicek jika stack modern Jakarta |
| Date/time | `dateLibrary` sesuai standard |
| Serialization | Jackson/JSON-B sesuai runtime |
| Generated sources | Masuk compile path |
| Runtime deps | Jersey/Feign/Jackson deps sesuai generated imports |
| CI | `mvn clean verify` generate + compile |
| Adapter | Generated client tidak langsung masuk business logic |
| Error handling | `ApiException`/Feign error didecode di adapter |
| Config | Base URL/timeout/auth environment-driven |
| Reproducibility | Tidak pakai latest/floating version |

---

## 34. Red Flags

| Red flag | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Generator version tidak dipin | Output bisa berubah mendadak |
| Generated client langsung dipakai use case | Coupling tinggi |
| `operationId` buruk | Method generated buruk |
| Inline response schema | Class name generated buruk |
| Package semua client sama | Class collision |
| Base URL hardcoded | Deploy multi-env sulit |
| Timeout tidak diset | Risiko thread hang/cascading failure |
| `javax`/`jakarta` mismatch | Compile/runtime error |
| Generated code diubah manual | Hilang saat regenerate |
| Spec tidak compile jadi client di CI | Contract hanya dekorasi |
| Consumer generate dari branch provider | Tidak reproducible |
| Error decoder tidak ada | Non-2xx handling liar |
| `LATEST` dependency client | Build tidak deterministik |
| Custom template terlalu awal | Upgrade generator susah |

---

## 35. Ringkasan

OpenAPI Generator dengan Maven harus diperlakukan sebagai bagian dari build contract-first:

```text id="h79yx7"
OpenAPI spec
  + pinned generator version
  + stable package config
  + selected HTTP library
  + generated source compile check
  + runtime dependency alignment
  + adapter boundary
  + CI validation
```

Untuk stack Anda:

```text id="fyovqn"
Jersey/JAX-RS microservice:
  prefer library=jersey3 jika ingin konsisten dengan Jakarta/Jersey stack

Declarative client style:
  use library=feign jika tim punya standard OpenFeign core

Keduanya:
  tetap bungkus generated client dengan adapter/gateway
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Generator bukan pengganti desain API. Generator memperkuat kontrak yang sudah didesain baik. Kalau OpenAPI buruk, generated Java client juga akan buruk.**
