## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:33:59 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 8/16**. Fokusnya sekarang bukan lagi setup Maven, tetapi membaca hasil generated code: mana class API, model, invoker, exception, dan batas aman pemakaiannya di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS.

# Seri 8/16 - Generated Java Client: Package, Model, API, Invoker

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada kemampuan praktis:

> **Bagaimana membaca, memahami, dan memakai hasil generated Java client dari OpenAPI Generator tanpa membiarkannya bocor ke domain/application layer.**

Setelah Seri 7, kita sudah tahu cara generate client via Maven. Sekarang pertanyaannya:

```text id="hyhg9y"
Generated code itu isinya apa?
Class mana yang harus dipakai?
Class mana yang jangan dipakai langsung?
Bagaimana configure ApiClient?
Bagaimana decode error?
Bagaimana mapping generated DTO ke domain model?
Bagaimana menjaga agar generated code tidak mencemari use case?
```

OpenAPI Generator Java generator menghasilkan **Java client library** dan dokumentasi resminya menyebut generator ini sebagai generator bertipe **CLIENT** dengan dukungan HTTP library seperti Jersey, Retrofit, dan OpenFeign. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jersey Client API sendiri adalah fluent Java API untuk komunikasi dengan RESTful web service, sedangkan Jakarta REST menyediakan Client API untuk mengakses REST resource dari aplikasi Java lain. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest3x/client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Generated Client

Generated client biasanya menghasilkan empat kelompok besar:

```text id="uxqddi"
target/generated-sources/openapi
  └── src/main/java
      └── com/company/customer/client
          ├── api
          │   └── CustomersApi.java
          │
          ├── model
          │   ├── CustomerResponse.java
          │   ├── CreateCustomerRequest.java
          │   ├── ErrorResponse.java
          │   └── CustomerStatus.java
          │
          └── invoker
              ├── ApiClient.java
              ├── ApiException.java
              ├── Configuration.java
              ├── Pair.java
              ├── ApiResponse.java
              └── auth
                  └── HttpBearerAuth.java
```

Mental model:

```text id="ivc2fy"
api package
  = method-level HTTP operations

model package
  = request/response/error DTOs from OpenAPI schema

invoker package
  = runtime plumbing:
      base URL,
      HTTP client,
      serializer,
      auth,
      exception,
      response wrapper
```

Kunci:

```text id="7h4cxk"
Generated client adalah transport-layer artifact.
Ia bukan domain model,
bukan application service,
bukan persistence model,
dan bukan workflow state model.
```

---

## 3. Package Anatomy

| Package | Isi umum | Peran | Boleh bocor ke domain? |
|---|---|---|---:|
| `*.client.api` | `CustomersApi`, `OrdersApi` | Method per `operationId` | Tidak |
| `*.client.model` | Request/response DTO, enum, error DTO | JSON schema model | Tidak |
| `*.client.invoker` | `ApiClient`, `ApiException`, auth, serializer helper | Runtime client plumbing | Tidak |
| `*.client.invoker.auth` | Bearer/API key/basic auth support | Security helper | Tidak |
| `*.client.infrastructure` custom | Factory/config wrapper buatan Anda | Integrasi runtime | Tidak, tetap infrastructure |
| `*.infrastructure.customer` | Adapter/gateway implementation | Boundary ke application | Boleh expose interface saja |
| `*.application` | Use case/service | Business orchestration | Tidak tahu generated |
| `*.domain` | Domain model/value object | Business invariant | Tidak tahu generated |

---

## 4. Contoh Struktur yang Sehat

```text id="ppvhzq"
com.company.order
  ├── application
  │   └── SubmitOrderUseCase.java
  │
  ├── domain
  │   ├── CustomerId.java
  │   ├── CustomerProfile.java
  │   └── Order.java
  │
  └── infrastructure
      └── customer
          ├── CustomerGateway.java
          ├── CustomerHttpGateway.java
          ├── CustomerClientFactory.java
          ├── CustomerClientProperties.java
          ├── CustomerErrorMapper.java
          ├── CustomerDtoMapper.java
          └── generated
              ├── api
              ├── model
              └── invoker
```

Lebih baik lagi, generated package menunjukkan provider API:

```text id="hrfmxp"
com.company.customer.client.api
com.company.customer.client.model
com.company.customer.client.invoker
```

Adapter berada di consumer service:

```text id="v6s4ke"
com.company.order.infrastructure.customer
```

Dengan begitu import menjelaskan dependency arah:

```java id="dijw5l"
import com.company.customer.client.api.CustomersApi;
import com.company.customer.client.model.CustomerResponse;
```

---

## 5. `*Api` Class

`*Api` class adalah class yang merepresentasikan group operation dari OpenAPI `tags` atau generator grouping.

Contoh OpenAPI:

```yaml id="k08trc"
paths:
  /customers/{customerId}:
    get:
      tags:
        - Customers
      operationId: getCustomerById
```

Generated kira-kira:

```java id="j7zmsd"
CustomersApi customersApi = new CustomersApi(apiClient);

CustomerResponse response =
        customersApi.getCustomerById("CUST-001");
```

Peran `CustomersApi`:

| Peran | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Memanggil endpoint | Method sesuai `operationId` |
| Encode parameter | Path/query/header/body |
| Serialize request | DTO → JSON |
| Decode response | JSON → generated model |
| Lempar exception | Non-2xx / client error, tergantung generator |
| Kadang return wrapper | `ApiResponse<T>` jika method varian tersedia |

Yang perlu dicek:

```text id="h8sv0t"
Apakah method return body langsung?
Apakah ada variant withHttpInfo?
Apakah non-2xx throw ApiException?
Apakah error body bisa diakses?
Apakah headers response bisa dibaca?
```

---

## 6. Generated Method Naming

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="llw05j"
operationId: getCustomerById
```

Generated method:

```java id="t8rptk"
customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
```

OpenAPI buruk:

```yaml id="u8fqa7"
operationId: get
```

Generated buruk:

```java id="kmhd2k"
customersApi.get(id);
```

Implikasi:

| OpenAPI design | Generated Java impact |
|---|---|
| `operationId` bagus | Method readable |
| `operationId` berubah | Consumer compile break |
| `operationId` duplicate | Generation bisa gagal/rename otomatis |
| `operationId` terlalu generic | Adapter sulit dibaca |
| Tidak ada `operationId` | Generator menebak nama method |

Rule:

```text id="buzg4b"
Kalau generated method jelek,
biasanya problem-nya di OpenAPI contract,
bukan di Java client code.
```

---

## 7. Model Classes

Model class berasal dari `components.schemas`.

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="ic6hci"
CustomerResponse:
  type: object
  required:
    - customerId
    - displayName
    - status
  properties:
    customerId:
      type: string
    displayName:
      type: string
    status:
      $ref: '#/components/schemas/CustomerStatus'
```

Generated kira-kira:

```java id="lvkux4"
CustomerResponse response = customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId);

String id = response.getCustomerId();
String name = response.getDisplayName();
CustomerResponse.StatusEnum status = response.getStatus();
```

Atau jika enum schema terpisah:

```java id="ys4rja"
CustomerStatus status = response.getStatus();
```

Model class adalah **external contract DTO**.

Jangan jadikan ini domain object:

```java id="sgdqb0"
// Bad
public class SubmitOrderUseCase {
    public void submit(CustomerResponse customer) {
        ...
    }
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="m9egky"
public record CustomerProfile(
        CustomerId customerId,
        String displayName,
        CustomerLifecycleStatus status
) {}
```

Mapping:

```java id="x3a2v8"
private CustomerProfile toProfile(CustomerResponse response) {
    return new CustomerProfile(
            new CustomerId(response.getCustomerId()),
            response.getDisplayName(),
            mapStatus(response.getStatus())
    );
}
```

---

## 8. Generated Request Models

Untuk command:

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="ptn5ah"
CreateOrderRequest:
  type: object
  required:
    - customerId
    - items
```

Generated:

```java id="he5o6p"
CreateOrderRequest request = new CreateOrderRequest()
        .customerId("CUST-001")
        .items(items);
```

Risiko:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Generated request dipakai di application layer | Business logic bergantung pada transport DTO |
| Generated model validation tidak cukup | Required di OpenAPI belum tentu runtime-validated di client |
| Nullability ambiguity | Field absent/null perlu dicek |
| Generated builder/fluent method berubah | Jika schema berubah |
| Field name mengikuti contract | Tidak selalu cocok domain internal |

Lebih baik application membuat internal command:

```java id="gkf7qb"
public record SubmitOrderCommand(
        CustomerId customerId,
        List<OrderItem> items
) {}
```

Adapter mapping ke generated request:

```java id="bc2bvx"
private CreateOrderRequest toCreateOrderRequest(SubmitOrderCommand command) {
    return new CreateOrderRequest()
            .customerId(command.customerId().value())
            .items(command.items().stream()
                    .map(this::toGeneratedItem)
                    .toList());
}
```

---

## 9. Generated Enum

OpenAPI:

```yaml id="t94n1x"
CustomerStatus:
  type: string
  enum:
    - ACTIVE
    - SUSPENDED
    - CLOSED
```

Generated Java biasanya membuat enum.

Masalah utama:

```text id="epafry"
Provider menambah enum value baru:
  PENDING_REVIEW

Consumer lama:
  generated enum tidak tahu value baru
  deserialization bisa gagal
  switch bisa tidak handle
```

Safe mapping:

```java id="ar1ttn"
private CustomerLifecycleStatus mapStatus(CustomerStatus status) {
    if (status == null) {
        return CustomerLifecycleStatus.UNKNOWN;
    }

    return switch (status) {
        case ACTIVE -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.ACTIVE;
        case SUSPENDED -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.SUSPENDED;
        case CLOSED -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.CLOSED;
        default -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.UNKNOWN;
    };
}
```

Kalau generated enum tidak punya `default`, strategi fallback bergantung pada serializer/generator config. OpenAPI Generator Java generator menyediakan banyak opsi konfigurasi, termasuk opsi terkait enum naming dan library; hasil final enum sangat bergantung pada versi generator dan config yang dipakai. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rule:

```text id="p4rpzp"
External enum jangan langsung menjadi domain enum.
Selalu map lewat adapter.
```

---

## 10. `ApiClient`

`ApiClient` biasanya class paling penting di package `invoker`.

Peran umum:

| Peran | Detail |
|---|---|
| Base URL | `setBasePath(...)` atau equivalent |
| HTTP client config | Jersey/Feign/OkHttp/Apache client tergantung library |
| Serialization | ObjectMapper/JSON provider |
| Auth | Bearer/API key/basic auth |
| Default headers | Correlation/source headers |
| Timeout | Jika exposed oleh generated client |
| Interceptors/filters | Logging/tracing/auth injection |
| Response handling | Build request, invoke API |

Contoh:

```java id="gkxsf7"
ApiClient apiClient = new ApiClient();
apiClient.setBasePath(customerClientProperties.baseUrl());

CustomersApi customersApi = new CustomersApi(apiClient);
```

Yang harus Anda cari saat membaca `ApiClient`:

```text id="6z1mnm"
- bagaimana set base URL?
- bagaimana set timeout?
- bagaimana inject auth?
- apakah thread-safe?
- apakah reusable singleton?
- apakah ObjectMapper bisa dikustomisasi?
- bagaimana default headers ditambahkan?
- apakah ada HTTP client underlying yang bisa dikonfigurasi?
```

Jangan asumsikan semua generated `ApiClient` sama. Output berbeda tergantung:

```text id="dkoxac"
OpenAPI Generator version
generatorName
library
configOptions
serializationLibrary
template customization
```

---

## 11. `Configuration`

Sering ada class seperti:

```java id="d7tb49"
Configuration.getDefaultApiClient()
```

atau:

```java id="fb3u5b"
Configuration.setDefaultApiClient(apiClient);
```

Hati-hati dengan global default client.

Bad:

```java id="d4e93p"
Configuration.setDefaultApiClient(customerApiClient);
```

lalu service lain ikut memakai default yang sama.

Risiko:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Global mutable state | Test dan runtime bisa saling memengaruhi |
| Multi-client conflict | Customer/payment/inventory client tertukar config |
| Thread-safety tidak jelas | Perubahan default runtime berbahaya |
| Auth leakage | Token/header client A dipakai client B |
| Sulit test paralel | Static state bocor antar test |

Better:

```java id="w090hr"
ApiClient customerApiClient = customerClientFactory.create(...);
CustomersApi customersApi = new CustomersApi(customerApiClient);

ApiClient paymentApiClient = paymentClientFactory.create(...);
PaymentsApi paymentsApi = new PaymentsApi(paymentApiClient);
```

Rule:

```text id="fp8q04"
Prefer explicit ApiClient per downstream service.
Avoid global default client unless platform standard guarantees safe behavior.
```

---

## 12. `ApiException`

Generated client sering melempar `ApiException` untuk non-2xx atau invocation failure.

Umumnya berisi:

```text id="fdlaqs"
status code
response body
response headers
message
cause
```

Contoh handling:

```java id="wt9z42"
try {
    CustomerResponse response = customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
    return Optional.of(toProfile(response));
} catch (ApiException exception) {
    int status = exception.getCode();
    String responseBody = exception.getResponseBody();

    ErrorResponse error = errorDecoder.decode(responseBody);

    if (status == 404 && "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND".equals(error.getErrorCode())) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    throw mapDownstreamFailure(status, error, exception);
}
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Jangan catch `Exception` generik | Hilang klasifikasi failure |
| Jangan return `null` | Failure jadi NPE nanti |
| Decode error body defensively | Body bisa empty/non-JSON |
| Status code tetap utama | Body bisa rusak |
| ErrorCode digunakan untuk business mapping | Jangan parsing message |
| Jangan lempar `ApiException` ke use case | Map ke internal exception/result |

---

## 13. `ApiResponse<T>`

Beberapa generated client menyediakan method variant:

```java id="hc3j3n"
ApiResponse<CustomerResponse> response =
        customersApi.getCustomerByIdWithHttpInfo(customerId);
```

Biasanya berguna untuk:

| Kebutuhan | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Membaca status code | Distinguish `200` vs `204` |
| Membaca response headers | `Location`, `ETag`, `Retry-After` |
| Debugging/observability | Log status/header tertentu |
| Pagination via header | Jika API pakai header |
| Rate limit | `X-RateLimit-Remaining` |

Contoh:

```java id="ywv657"
ApiResponse<CreateOrderResponse> response =
        ordersApi.createOrderWithHttpInfo(request);

String location = response.getHeaders()
        .getOrDefault("Location", List.of())
        .stream()
        .findFirst()
        .orElse(null);
```

Rule:

```text id="58exk7"
Jika endpoint memakai headers sebagai bagian kontrak,
gunakan method variant yang expose HTTP info,
bukan body-only method.
```

---

## 14. Auth Classes

Jika OpenAPI punya security scheme:

```yaml id="kjl5vg"
components:
  securitySchemes:
    bearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT
```

Generated client bisa menghasilkan auth helper seperti:

```text id="lyd3tc"
HttpBearerAuth
ApiKeyAuth
HttpBasicAuth
```

Contoh umum:

```java id="dlyfku"
ApiClient apiClient = new ApiClient();
HttpBearerAuth bearerAuth =
        (HttpBearerAuth) apiClient.getAuthentication("bearerAuth");
bearerAuth.setBearerToken(tokenProvider.getToken());
```

Namun di production, token sering perlu dynamic per request.

Problem:

```text id="6x4j0a"
Token diset saat startup,
lalu expired.
```

Better:

```text id="l1fj4p"
Use request filter/interceptor
  -> fetch token per request or refresh-aware cache
  -> inject Authorization header
```

Untuk Jersey Client, Jersey menyediakan Client API dan extension mechanism di ekosistem Jersey/JAX-RS; cara detail filter/interceptor tergantung generated `ApiClient` dan Jersey version yang dipakai. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest3x/client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Jersey Client Generated Runtime

Jika `library=jersey3`, generated client umumnya memakai Jersey/Jakarta REST style.

Yang perlu dicari:

```text id="h0e9ql"
ApiClient
  -> underlying Client
  -> ClientConfig
  -> ObjectMapper provider
  -> request filters
  -> connector provider
  -> timeout properties
```

Jersey Client API adalah fluent API untuk komunikasi dengan RESTful web service dan bagian dari Jakarta EE 9+ client-side model. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest3x/client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jakarta REST juga menyediakan client API untuk mengakses REST resource dari aplikasi Java. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest-client/rest-client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Checklist untuk generated Jersey client:

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Namespace | `jakarta.*` atau `javax.*`? |
| JSON provider | Jackson/JSON-B/MOXy? |
| Timeout | Bagaimana set connect/read timeout? |
| Auth | Header/filter/auth helper? |
| Correlation ID | Bisa inject per request? |
| Connection pool | Underlying connector mendukung pooling? |
| TLS/mTLS | Truststore/keystore config di mana? |
| Proxy | Apakah perlu untuk on-prem/cloud? |
| Thread safety | ApiClient/Client reusable? |
| Closing | Perlu close HTTP client saat shutdown? |

---

## 16. OpenFeign Generated Runtime

Jika `library=feign`, generated client biasanya lebih declarative/interface-driven.

OpenFeign mendeskripsikan dirinya sebagai Java-to-HTTP client binder yang mengurangi kompleksitas binding Java ke HTTP API. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openfeign/feign?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Feign juga memiliki konsep decoder/error decoder; `ErrorDecoder` digunakan untuk memetakan response error menjadi exception. ([GitHub](https://github.com/OpenFeign/feign/blob/master/core/src/main/java/feign/codec/ErrorDecoder.java?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Generated Feign client biasanya membutuhkan konfigurasi:

```text id="z2555l"
Feign builder
  + encoder
  + decoder
  + errorDecoder
  + logger
  + requestInterceptor
  + retryer
  + client
  + target base URL
```

Contoh manual-ish:

```java id="azxwwj"
CustomersApi customersApi = Feign.builder()
        .encoder(new JacksonEncoder(objectMapper))
        .decoder(new JacksonDecoder(objectMapper))
        .errorDecoder(new CustomerErrorDecoder(errorBodyDecoder))
        .requestInterceptor(template -> {
            template.header("Authorization", "Bearer " + tokenProvider.getToken());
            template.header("X-Correlation-Id", correlationIdProvider.currentOrNew());
        })
        .target(CustomersApi.class, properties.baseUrl());
```

Cek generated code Anda karena exact API bisa berbeda.

Checklist untuk generated Feign client:

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Encoder/decoder | Jackson/JSON-B sesuai platform? |
| ErrorDecoder | Error body didecode konsisten? |
| Retryer | Default retry dimatikan/diset? |
| Timeout | Di Feign options/client config? |
| Logger | Jangan log token/body sensitif |
| RequestInterceptor | Auth/correlation/tenant |
| HTTP transport | OkHttp/Apache/default? |
| Thread safety | Client proxy biasanya reusable, deps harus thread-safe |
| Non-Spring | Jangan bergantung `@FeignClient` jika bukan Spring |

---

## 17. Generated Code dan Thread Safety

Pertanyaan penting:

```text id="4ulzqm"
Apakah generated ApiClient aman dijadikan singleton?
Apakah auth token disimpan sebagai mutable field?
Apakah default headers mutable?
Apakah ObjectMapper shared thread-safe?
Apakah underlying HTTP Client pooled?
```

Rule umum:

| Item | Praktik |
|---|---|
| `*Api` instance | Biasanya bisa singleton jika underlying client thread-safe |
| `ApiClient` | Cek mutable config; configure once at startup |
| Token | Jangan mutable global per user request |
| Headers per request | Gunakan filter/interceptor/context |
| ObjectMapper | Shared setelah configured biasanya aman |
| Jersey `Client` | Umumnya reusable; close saat shutdown |
| Feign proxy | Umumnya reusable jika dependencies aman |

Anti-pattern:

```java id="g6yr5t"
apiClient.setBearerToken(currentUserToken);
customersApi.getCustomerById(id);
```

Dalam concurrent service, request user A dan B bisa tabrakan jika token disimpan global.

Better:

```text id="9eqyyr"
Request interceptor reads token from request context
or service token provider per call.
```

---

## 18. Generated DTO vs Domain Model

Generated DTO:

```java id="k09n8t"
CustomerResponse
```

Domain model:

```java id="w6dg21"
CustomerProfile
```

Kenapa harus dipisah?

| Alasan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| External schema evolves independently | Domain tidak ikut berubah setiap field API berubah |
| Generated code bisa regenerate | Domain tetap stabil |
| Nullability external tidak aman | Domain bisa enforce invariant |
| Enum external bisa bertambah | Domain punya `UNKNOWN`/fallback |
| API contract bukan business truth mutlak | Domain bisa punya interpretation sendiri |
| Testing lebih mudah | Use case mock interface domain-level |

Bad dependency:

```text id="qudt4r"
application service -> generated CustomerResponse
```

Good dependency:

```text id="886rpx"
application service -> CustomerGateway -> CustomerProfile
```

---

## 19. Mapper Pattern

Mapper explicit:

```java id="rtqlep"
public final class CustomerDtoMapper {

    public CustomerProfile toProfile(CustomerResponse response) {
        return new CustomerProfile(
                new CustomerId(required(response.getCustomerId(), "customerId")),
                required(response.getDisplayName(), "displayName"),
                toLifecycleStatus(response.getStatus())
        );
    }

    private CustomerLifecycleStatus toLifecycleStatus(CustomerStatus status) {
        if (status == null) {
            return CustomerLifecycleStatus.UNKNOWN;
        }

        return switch (status) {
            case ACTIVE -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.ACTIVE;
            case SUSPENDED -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.SUSPENDED;
            case CLOSED -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.CLOSED;
            default -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }

    private String required(String value, String field) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new DownstreamContractViolationException(
                    "CUSTOMER_RESPONSE_MISSING_" + field.toUpperCase()
            );
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

Mapping policy:

| External issue | Internal policy |
|---|---|
| Required field null | Contract violation |
| Unknown enum | Map to `UNKNOWN` or fail fast, tergantung domain |
| Optional field absent | Use `Optional`/nullable internal with explicit semantics |
| Invalid date | Contract violation |
| Extra field | Ignore |
| Error response malformed | Downstream protocol error |

---

## 20. Downstream Contract Violation

Kadang provider return `200`, tetapi body tidak sesuai kontrak.

Contoh:

```json id="o2upew"
{
  "customerId": null,
  "displayName": "Acme"
}
```

Jika OpenAPI mengatakan `customerId` required non-null, ini **provider contract violation**.

Jangan diam-diam map ke invalid domain:

```java id="e2xhk9"
new CustomerId(null);
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="s5ftpl"
throw new DownstreamContractViolationException(
        "CUSTOMER_RESPONSE_MISSING_CUSTOMER_ID"
);
```

Log:

```text id="xnsfd8"
level=ERROR
event=DOWNSTREAM_CONTRACT_VIOLATION
downstream=customer-service
operation=getCustomerById
field=customerId
correlationId=...
```

Rule:

```text id="p2r43w"
Generated client decode success belum berarti response valid secara domain.
Adapter tetap harus menjaga invariant.
```

---

## 21. Error Body Decoder

Generated `ApiException` biasanya memberi raw response body. Anda butuh decoder defensif.

```java id="dealgv"
public final class DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    public DecodedError decode(String body, int status) {
        if (body == null || body.isBlank()) {
            return DecodedError.synthetic(
                    status,
                    "EMPTY_ERROR_BODY",
                    "Downstream returned empty error body."
            );
        }

        try {
            ErrorResponse error =
                    objectMapper.readValue(body, ErrorResponse.class);

            return DecodedError.from(error, status);
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return DecodedError.synthetic(
                    status,
                    "MALFORMED_ERROR_BODY",
                    "Downstream returned non-decodable error body."
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Reason:

| Scenario | Handling |
|---|---|
| `404` with valid error body | Business/domain mapping |
| `500` with empty body | Synthetic system error |
| `502` HTML from gateway | Synthetic gateway error |
| JSON error but unknown field | Should still decode if ObjectMapper allows |
| Error body schema changed | Contract violation / synthetic error |

---

## 22. Error Mapper

```java id="rmzvbo"
public final class CustomerErrorMapper {

    private final DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder decoder;

    public Optional<CustomerProfile> mapFindByIdError(
            ApiException exception,
            CustomerId customerId
    ) {
        int status = exception.getCode();
        DecodedError error =
                decoder.decode(exception.getResponseBody(), status);

        if (status == 404 &&
                "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND".equals(error.errorCode())) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        if (status == 401 || status == 403) {
            throw new DownstreamAuthorizationException(
                    "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_AUTH_FAILED",
                    error
            );
        }

        if (status == 409 || status == 422) {
            throw new DownstreamBusinessException(error);
        }

        if (status >= 500 || status == 429) {
            throw new DownstreamUnavailableException(
                    "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE",
                    error,
                    exception
            );
        }

        throw new DownstreamProtocolException(
                "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_UNEXPECTED_ERROR",
                error,
                exception
        );
    }
}
```

Important:

```text id="hpqtrf"
Adapter boleh return Optional.empty untuk 404 lookup.
Adapter sebaiknya tidak return Optional.empty untuk 500/timeout.
```

---

## 23. Request Context Propagation

Generated client tidak otomatis tahu inbound request context.

Yang perlu dipropagasikan:

| Context | Header umum |
|---|---|
| Correlation ID | `X-Correlation-Id` |
| Trace context | `traceparent` |
| Authorization | `Authorization` |
| Tenant | `X-Tenant-Id` |
| Actor/user | `X-User-Id`, jika policy mengizinkan |
| Idempotency | `Idempotency-Key` |
| Source service | `X-Request-Source` |

Jersey/Feign injection point:

| Client type | Injection place |
|---|---|
| Jersey Client | Client request filter / generated ApiClient default headers |
| Feign | RequestInterceptor |
| Manual wrapper | Adapter method parameters |
| Service mesh | Some transport/security context handled outside app |

Rule:

```text id="gow8bk"
Context propagation harus terjadi di infrastructure boundary,
bukan disuruh manual oleh every use case.
```

---

## 24. Timeout dan Retry Tidak Selalu Ada di Generated Code

Generated client sering hanya membuat HTTP call. Production policy tetap Anda tambahkan.

```text id="pb3jro"
Generated API method:
  customersApi.getCustomerById(id)

Production wrapper:
  timeout
  retry
  circuit breaker
  metrics
  tracing
  error mapping
  DTO mapping
```

Adapter skeleton:

```java id="agwhji"
public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
    return downstreamPolicy.execute(
            "customer-service",
            "getCustomerById",
            () -> callCustomerService(customerId)
    );
}

private Optional<CustomerProfile> callCustomerService(CustomerId customerId) {
    try {
        CustomerResponse response =
                customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

        return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(response));
    } catch (ApiException exception) {
        return errorMapper.mapFindByIdError(exception, customerId);
    }
}
```

Where `downstreamPolicy` can include:

```text id="9flqbr"
timeout
retry
bulkhead
circuit breaker
metric timer
trace span
```

---

## 25. Observability Around Generated Client

Jangan bergantung pada generated client log default.

Minimal metric:

```text id="um2u4j"
downstream_http_client_requests_total{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById",
  status_class="2xx"
}

downstream_http_client_duration_ms{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById"
}
```

Minimal structured log on failure:

```text id="30t1a3"
level=WARN
event=DOWNSTREAM_HTTP_ERROR
downstream=customer-service
operation=getCustomerById
status=404
errorCode=CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND
latencyMs=37
correlationId=...
```

For system failure:

```text id="vywukm"
level=ERROR
event=DOWNSTREAM_HTTP_ERROR
downstream=customer-service
operation=getCustomerById
status=503
errorCode=DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE
latencyMs=501
attempt=2
correlationId=...
```

Rule:

```text id="v68l1l"
Generated client should be inside an observable boundary.
```

---

## 26. Testing Generated Client Adapter

Yang dites bukan generated code-nya, tetapi boundary Anda.

### Unit test mapper

```text id="ykv6c5"
CustomerResponse -> CustomerProfile
unknown enum -> UNKNOWN
missing required field -> DownstreamContractViolationException
```

### Unit test error mapper

```text id="er1fvb"
404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND -> Optional.empty
403 ACCESS_DENIED -> DownstreamAuthorizationException
500 INTERNAL_ERROR -> DownstreamUnavailableException
malformed error body -> DownstreamProtocolException / synthetic error
```

### Integration-ish test with mock HTTP

```text id="htqdca"
Mock server returns:
  200 valid CustomerResponse
  404 ErrorResponse
  500 ErrorResponse
  502 HTML gateway error
  invalid JSON success body
```

### Contract test

```text id="tktciv"
Provider response matches OpenAPI schema.
Consumer mock expectations derive from OpenAPI.
```

---

## 27. Example: Clean Adapter End-to-End

Interface:

```java id="xf02ak"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

Implementation:

```java id="6ev5h4"
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomersApi customersApi;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper;
    private final CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    public CustomerHttpGateway(
            CustomersApi customersApi,
            CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper,
            CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper,
            DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy
    ) {
        this.customersApi = customersApi;
        this.dtoMapper = dtoMapper;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
        this.callPolicy = callPolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findCustomerById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invokeFindCustomerById(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invokeFindCustomerById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(dtoMapper.toProfile(response));
        } catch (ApiException exception) {
            return errorMapper.mapFindByIdError(exception, customerId);
        }
    }
}
```

Application layer:

```java id="7p77bj"
public final class SubmitOrderUseCase {

    private final CustomerGateway customerGateway;

    public void submit(SubmitOrderCommand command) {
        CustomerProfile customer = customerGateway
                .findCustomerById(command.customerId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CustomerNotFoundException(command.customerId()));

        // continue business logic
    }
}
```

No generated import in use case.

---

## 28. Example: Client Factory

```java id="9bdc36"
public final class CustomerClientFactory {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final TokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider;

    public CustomerClientFactory(
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            TokenProvider tokenProvider,
            CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider
    ) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.correlationIdProvider = correlationIdProvider;
    }

    public CustomersApi create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        ApiClient apiClient = new ApiClient();
        apiClient.setBasePath(properties.baseUrl());

        // Exact APIs depend on generated code.
        // Configure:
        // - objectMapper / JSON provider
        // - connect/read timeout
        // - auth header provider
        // - correlation ID propagation
        // - TLS/proxy if needed

        return new CustomersApi(apiClient);
    }
}
```

Property record:

```java id="yej27d"
public record CustomerClientProperties(
        String baseUrl,
        int connectTimeoutMs,
        int readTimeoutMs,
        int maxAttempts
) {
    public CustomerClientProperties {
        if (baseUrl == null || baseUrl.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("customer.baseUrl is required");
        }
        if (connectTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("connectTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
        if (readTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("readTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
        if (maxAttempts <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("maxAttempts must be positive");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 29. Generated Client Upgrade Workflow

Saat contract/client version naik:

```text id="k6ou6p"
1. Update OpenAPI spec or client dependency version
2. Regenerate client
3. Compile
4. Review generated API changes:
   - method names
   - model fields
   - enum values
   - exception behavior
5. Update adapter mapper/error mapper
6. Run adapter tests
7. Run integration/contract tests
8. Deploy behind safe config
```

Checklist upgrade:

| Area | Cek |
|---|---|
| `operationId` changes | Method compile break |
| Schema rename | Import/class change |
| Field removed/renamed | Mapper compile break |
| Enum added | Runtime compatibility |
| Required field added | Request mapping update |
| Error code changed | Error mapper update |
| Status code changed | Branch logic update |
| Auth scheme changed | Factory/interceptor update |
| Base URL/server changed | Config update |
| Library config changed | Runtime dependency update |

---

## 30. Generated Code Ownership

Rule:

```text id="781km1"
Do not manually edit generated code.
```

Jika generated output salah, perbaiki:

| Problem | Fix location |
|---|---|
| Method name buruk | OpenAPI `operationId` |
| Class name buruk | Schema name |
| Field name buruk | Property name |
| Inline class buruk | Move to `components.schemas` |
| Type mapping salah | Generator config/type mapping |
| Error handling kurang | Adapter/ErrorDecoder |
| Auth injection kurang | Factory/interceptor |
| Repeated custom logic needed | Template customization only if justified |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="cwu6pb"
Edit CustomersApi.java manually
  -> regenerate
  -> change hilang
```

---

## 31. Generated Code dan MyBatis Boundary

Jangan pernah:

```java id="b2bio6"
@Mapper
public interface CustomerMapper {
    void insert(CustomerResponse response);
}
```

Lebih aman:

```text id="f2rxt4"
CustomerResponse
  -> CustomerProfile / CustomerSnapshot
  -> Persistence DTO / MyBatis parameter
```

Contoh:

```java id="nzlsx1"
public record CustomerSnapshotRecord(
        String customerId,
        String displayName,
        String status
) {}
```

Mapping:

```java id="tumt0a"
CustomerSnapshotRecord toRecord(CustomerProfile profile) {
    return new CustomerSnapshotRecord(
            profile.customerId().value(),
            profile.displayName(),
            profile.status().name()
    );
}
```

Kenapa?

| Jika generated DTO masuk MyBatis | Risiko |
|---|---|
| API field rename | SQL mapping rusak |
| External enum berubah | DB mapping rusak |
| Generated class regenerated | Persistence compile break |
| Nullable external field | DB constraint violation |
| API version migration | Persistence layer ikut terdampak |

---

## 32. Generated Code dan Camunda Boundary

Jangan masukkan generated DTO langsung ke process variable jangka panjang.

Bad:

```java id="djqy08"
runtimeService.setVariable(processInstanceId, "customer", customerResponse);
```

Risiko:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Class version berubah | Deserialization process lama gagal |
| Payload terlalu besar | Process variable bloat |
| External schema berubah | Process instance lama terdampak |
| Generated package berubah | Historic variable tidak bisa dibaca |
| Coupling workflow ke API client | Migrasi susah |

Better:

```java id="tmqf96"
runtimeService.setVariable(processInstanceId, "customerId", customerId.value());
runtimeService.setVariable(processInstanceId, "customerStatus", profile.status().name());
```

Atau gunakan stable internal process variable DTO:

```java id="mp8mrx"
public record CustomerProcessSnapshot(
        String customerId,
        String displayName,
        String status
) {}
```

---

## 33. Review Checklist Generated Java Client

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Package | API/model/invoker package jelas dan tidak bentrok |
| Namespace | `jakarta.*` vs `javax.*` sesuai stack |
| `*Api` class | Method sesuai `operationId` |
| Models | Request/response/error DTO sesuai schema |
| Enums | Unknown enum strategy dipikirkan |
| `ApiClient` | Base URL, timeout, auth, serializer bisa dikonfigurasi |
| `Configuration` | Hindari global mutable default |
| `ApiException` | Status/body/header bisa diakses |
| Error decoder | Body decode defensively |
| DTO mapper | Generated DTO tidak bocor ke domain |
| Adapter | Business use case hanya lihat gateway interface |
| Observability | Metrics/log/tracing di wrapper |
| Retry/timeout | Tidak bergantung default generated client |
| Tests | Mapper, error mapper, adapter tested |
| Upgrade | Generated diff direview |
| Ownership | Generated code tidak diedit manual |

---

## 34. Red Flags

| Red flag | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Use case import `*.client.model.*` | Generated DTO bocor |
| Domain enum sama dengan generated enum | External enum change merusak domain |
| `ApiException` dilempar sampai controller/resource | HTTP downstream detail bocor |
| Static `Configuration.setDefaultApiClient` sembarangan | Multi-client config conflict |
| Token disimpan mutable di singleton ApiClient | Cross-request auth leak |
| Base URL hardcoded | Deploy multi-env sulit |
| Timeout tidak dikonfigurasi | Cascading failure |
| Error body tidak didecode | Semua non-2xx jadi generic failure |
| Generated code diedit manual | Hilang saat regenerate |
| Inline schema menghasilkan `InlineObject1` | Contract design buruk |
| Generated DTO masuk MyBatis mapper | DB coupling ke external API |
| Generated DTO jadi Camunda process variable | Long-running process rentan |
| Unknown enum tidak dipikirkan | Runtime decode failure |
| ObjectMapper default berbeda | Compatibility bug |
| Adapter tidak punya tests | Upgrade client berisiko |

---

## 35. Ringkasan

Generated Java client terdiri dari:

```text id="3c8r1q"
api
  -> operation methods

model
  -> generated request/response/error DTOs

invoker
  -> ApiClient, ApiException, auth, serializer, runtime plumbing
```

Cara pakai yang sehat:

```text id="j2jptv"
Generated *Api
  -> infrastructure adapter
  -> DTO mapper
  -> error mapper
  -> domain/application interface
```

Bukan:

```text id="5ril76"
Generated *Api / model
  -> langsung dipakai business use case
  -> langsung masuk MyBatis
  -> langsung disimpan sebagai Camunda variable
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Generated client mempercepat integrasi, tetapi adapter-lah yang menjaga architecture boundary, failure semantics, observability, dan compatibility.**

## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:38:47 AM

# Seri 9/16 - Jersey Client untuk Service-to-Service Call

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada:

> **Bagaimana memakai Jersey Client / Jakarta REST Client API untuk komunikasi HTTP antar microservice secara aman, eksplisit, observable, dan cocok dengan contract-first OpenAPI.**

Di seri sebelumnya, kita sudah membahas generated Java client. Tetapi dalam codebase nyata, Anda akan menemukan dua pola:

```text id="17m6k8"
A. Generated Jersey Client dari OpenAPI Generator
B. Manual Jersey Client menggunakan Client, WebTarget, Invocation.Builder
```

Keduanya tetap perlu pemahaman runtime Jersey Client karena generated client pada akhirnya tetap melakukan hal-hal ini:

```text id="pu0l2q"
build URL
set path/query/header
serialize body
send HTTP request
wait response
deserialize body
handle non-2xx
close/reuse connection
propagate auth/correlation
apply timeout
```

Jakarta REST menyediakan **Client API** untuk mengakses REST resource dari aplikasi Java lain, dan Jersey Client adalah implementasi/ekstensi populer dari API tersebut. ([jakarta.ee](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest-client/rest-client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jersey Client juga mendukung arsitektur pluggable connector, termasuk connector berbasis JDK `HttpURLConnection`, Apache HTTP Client, Jetty HTTP client, dan Grizzly Async Client. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/3.0.0/client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Jersey Client

```text id="y5vnex"
Client
  |
  v
WebTarget
  |
  v
Invocation.Builder
  |
  v
HTTP request
  |
  v
Response
  |
  v
readEntity(...)
```

Secara praktis:

| Object | Peran |
|---|---|
| `Client` | Runtime HTTP client, config, connector, filters, providers |
| `WebTarget` | Target URL/resource yang bisa ditambah path/query |
| `Invocation.Builder` | Request builder: accept, headers, method |
| `Entity<T>` | Request body + media type |
| `Response` | Raw HTTP response: status, headers, body |
| `readEntity` | Decode body ke Java type |
| `ClientRequestFilter` | Intercept request sebelum dikirim |
| `ClientResponseFilter` | Intercept response setelah diterima |

Jakarta REST `ClientBuilder` menyediakan cara membuat `Client` baru, misalnya melalui `ClientBuilder.newClient()` atau builder/configuration lain. ([jakarta.ee](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.0/apidocs/jakarta/ws/rs/client/clientbuilder?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Basic Manual Jersey Client Call

Contoh paling sederhana:

```java id="ep64lo"
Client client = ClientBuilder.newClient();

CustomerResponse response = client
        .target("http://customer-service")
        .path("/customers/{customerId}")
        .resolveTemplate("customerId", "CUST-001")
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .get(CustomerResponse.class);
```

Tapi untuk production microservice, contoh di atas belum cukup karena belum ada:

```text id="psb3pl"
timeout
auth
correlation ID
error handling
metrics
tracing
connection management
base URL config
response validation
```

Versi yang lebih realistis:

```java id="q7vk0c"
public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
    Response response = customerTarget
            .path("/customers/{customerId}")
            .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId.value())
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .header("X-Correlation-Id", correlationIdProvider.currentOrNew())
            .get();

    try {
        return switch (response.getStatus()) {
            case 200 -> Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(
                    response.readEntity(CustomerResponse.class)
            ));
            case 404 -> Optional.empty();
            default -> throw errorMapper.map(response);
        };
    } finally {
        response.close();
    }
}
```

---

## 4. Layering yang Sehat

```text id="g84rm1"
Application Service
  |
  v
CustomerGateway interface
  |
  v
CustomerJerseyGateway
  |
  v
Jersey Client / WebTarget
  |
  v
Customer Service HTTP API
```

Jangan:

```java id="z2qu1x"
public class SubmitOrderUseCase {
    private final Client jerseyClient;

    public void submit(...) {
        jerseyClient.target(...).request().get();
    }
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="zhj0c0"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

```java id="gcj4zu"
public final class CustomerJerseyGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final WebTarget customerTarget;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper mapper;
    private final JerseyErrorMapper errorMapper;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        Response response = customerTarget
                .path("/customers/{customerId}")
                .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId.value())
                .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .get();

        try {
            if (response.getStatus() == 200) {
                return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(
                        response.readEntity(CustomerResponse.class)
                ));
            }

            if (response.getStatus() == 404) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }

            throw errorMapper.map(response);
        } finally {
            response.close();
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 5. `Client` Lifecycle

`Client` sebaiknya tidak dibuat per request.

Bad:

```java id="djbeng"
public CustomerResponse getCustomer(String id) {
    Client client = ClientBuilder.newClient();
    return client.target(baseUrl)
            .path("/customers/" + id)
            .request()
            .get(CustomerResponse.class);
}
```

Masalah:

| Masalah | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Membuat runtime client berulang | Overhead |
| Connection reuse buruk | Latency naik |
| Config tersebar | Timeout/auth/logging tidak konsisten |
| Resource leak | Client tidak ditutup |
| Sulit observability | Tidak ada filter standard |

Better:

```java id="arqeko"
public final class CustomerJerseyClientFactory {

    public CustomerGateway create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        Client client = buildClient(properties);

        WebTarget target = client.target(properties.baseUrl());

        return new CustomerJerseyGateway(target, mapper, errorMapper);
    }
}
```

Lifecycle rule:

```text id="knt4cj"
Create Client once per downstream configuration.
Reuse it.
Close it on application shutdown.
```

---

## 6. `WebTarget` Reuse

`WebTarget` merepresentasikan base target.

```java id="uyjdxu"
WebTarget customerTarget = client.target("http://customer-service");
```

Lalu di call:

```java id="q1p9r1"
customerTarget
        .path("/customers/{customerId}")
        .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId.value())
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .get();
```

Praktik:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Simpan `WebTarget` per downstream service | Base URL config tunggal |
| Jangan simpan target sampai path spesifik jika banyak variasi | Bisa membingungkan |
| Gunakan `resolveTemplate` untuk path param | Hindari string concatenation |
| Gunakan `queryParam` untuk filter/page | Jangan manual build URL |
| Jangan hardcode environment | Base URL dari config |

Bad:

```java id="k19j5z"
client.target(baseUrl + "/customers/" + customerId);
```

Good:

```java id="xzz9xb"
client.target(baseUrl)
        .path("/customers/{customerId}")
        .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId);
```

---

## 7. Timeout Configuration

Timeout wajib eksplisit.

Jersey menyediakan `ClientProperties.CONNECT_TIMEOUT` dan `ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT`. Dokumentasi Jersey API menyebut keduanya dalam milidetik, nilai `0` berarti infinity, dan default-nya infinity untuk properti tersebut. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/2.45/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/client/ClientProperties.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java id="cysczn"
ClientConfig config = new ClientConfig()
        .property(ClientProperties.CONNECT_TIMEOUT, 100)
        .property(ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT, 500);

Client client = ClientBuilder.newClient(config);
```

Makna praktis:

| Timeout | Arti |
|---|---|
| Connect timeout | Waktu maksimal membuka koneksi ke host/port |
| Read timeout | Waktu maksimal menunggu response setelah koneksi terbuka |
| Overall deadline | Batas total use case; biasanya perlu ditangani di layer policy |

Rule:

```text id="4wwr2v"
Jangan mengandalkan default timeout.
Default infinity berarti thread bisa tertahan sangat lama saat dependency bermasalah.
```

Contoh config:

```text id="nqoszk"
customer.client.connect-timeout-ms=100
customer.client.read-timeout-ms=500

payment.client.connect-timeout-ms=100
payment.client.read-timeout-ms=1000

report.client.connect-timeout-ms=200
report.client.read-timeout-ms=5000
```

---

## 8. Timeout per Operation

Tidak semua endpoint punya timeout sama.

| Operation | Contoh timeout |
|---|---:|
| Lookup customer | 300-500 ms |
| Validate permission | 200-300 ms |
| Create order | 500-1500 ms |
| Payment command | 1-3 s, tergantung domain |
| Report export start | 1-2 s |
| File download | Berbeda, biasanya streaming-specific |
| Health check | Sangat pendek |

Jersey config bisa di level client/target/request tergantung properti dan runtime. Tetapi praktik terbaik: jangan terlalu banyak variasi granular tanpa alasan.

```java id="o9yaks"
Invocation.Builder builder = customerTarget
        .path("/customers/{customerId}")
        .resolveTemplate("customerId", id)
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .property(ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT, 300);
```

Catatan: property per target/request bisa membuat perilaku sulit dipahami jika tersebar. Lebih baik pusatkan di policy/factory kecuali ada kebutuhan operation-specific yang jelas.

---

## 9. Client Factory Pattern

```java id="wl8heo"
public final class JerseyClientFactory {

    public Client create(JerseyHttpClientProperties properties) {
        ClientConfig config = new ClientConfig();

        config.property(ClientProperties.CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
                properties.connectTimeoutMs());

        config.property(ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT,
                properties.readTimeoutMs());

        config.register(new CorrelationIdRequestFilter(
                properties.serviceName()
        ));

        config.register(new AuthRequestFilter(
                properties.tokenProvider()
        ));

        config.register(new JsonProviderConfig());

        return ClientBuilder.newClient(config);
    }
}
```

Properties:

```java id="z7vqha"
public record JerseyHttpClientProperties(
        String serviceName,
        String baseUrl,
        int connectTimeoutMs,
        int readTimeoutMs,
        TokenProvider tokenProvider
) {
    public JerseyHttpClientProperties {
        if (serviceName == null || serviceName.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("serviceName is required");
        }
        if (baseUrl == null || baseUrl.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("baseUrl is required");
        }
        if (connectTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("connectTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
        if (readTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("readTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 10. Request Filter untuk Correlation ID

Jakarta REST/JAX-RS punya konsep filter untuk request/response client. `ClientRequestFilter` memfilter client request sebelum dikirim dan menerima `ClientRequestContext`. ([jakarta.ee](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/client/clientrequestfilter?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java id="z83rql"
@Provider
public final class CorrelationIdRequestFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider;
    private final String sourceService;

    public CorrelationIdRequestFilter(
            CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider,
            String sourceService
    ) {
        this.correlationIdProvider = correlationIdProvider;
        this.sourceService = sourceService;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                "X-Correlation-Id",
                correlationIdProvider.currentOrNew()
        );

        requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                "X-Request-Source",
                sourceService
        );
    }
}
```

Register:

```java id="a291f2"
Client client = ClientBuilder.newBuilder()
        .register(new CorrelationIdRequestFilter(
                correlationIdProvider,
                "order-service"
        ))
        .build();
```

Prinsip:

```text id="2jvwd8"
Use case tidak perlu manual set correlation ID.
Infrastructure client boundary yang bertanggung jawab.
```

---

## 11. Request Filter untuk Auth

Contoh service token:

```java id="m4ebwt"
@Provider
public final class BearerTokenRequestFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final String scope;

    public BearerTokenRequestFilter(
            ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            String scope
    ) {
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.scope = scope;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        String token = tokenProvider.getToken(scope);

        requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION,
                "Bearer " + token
        );
    }
}
```

Untuk user token propagation:

```java id="fhh96o"
@Provider
public final class UserTokenPropagationFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final RequestSecurityContext securityContext;

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        securityContext.currentBearerToken()
                .ifPresent(token -> requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                        HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION,
                        "Bearer " + token
                ));
    }
}
```

Design decision:

| Model | Kapan dipakai |
|---|---|
| Service token | Downstream hanya perlu caller service identity |
| User token propagation | Downstream perlu authorization berdasarkan user asli |
| Token exchange | Downstream perlu scoped delegated token |
| mTLS | Identity service-to-service di transport layer |
| Tenant/user context header | Untuk audit/routing, jika policy mengizinkan |

---

## 12. Response Filter untuk Logging Metadata

`ClientResponseFilter` bisa dipakai untuk metadata response, tetapi hati-hati jangan log body sensitif.

```java id="ghdxsv"
@Provider
public final class DownstreamLoggingResponseFilter
        implements ClientResponseFilter {

    private static final Logger log =
            LoggerFactory.getLogger(DownstreamLoggingResponseFilter.class);

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ClientRequestContext requestContext,
            ClientResponseContext responseContext
    ) {
        log.info(
                "downstream_http_response method={} uri={} status={}",
                requestContext.getMethod(),
                requestContext.getUri(),
                responseContext.getStatus()
        );
    }
}
```

Lebih baik gabungkan dengan timer/metrics di wrapper policy, karena response filter saja tidak selalu tahu logical operation name seperti `getCustomerById`.

---

## 13. Manual Error Handling dengan `Response`

Untuk service-to-service, lebih baik gunakan `Response` saat perlu kontrol status/error body.

```java id="5m6z79"
Response response = target
        .path("/customers/{customerId}")
        .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId.value())
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .get();

try {
    int status = response.getStatus();

    if (status == 200) {
        CustomerResponse body = response.readEntity(CustomerResponse.class);
        return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(body));
    }

    if (status == 404) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    ErrorResponse error = readErrorResponse(response);
    throw errorMapper.map(status, error);

} finally {
    response.close();
}
```

Kenapa `finally response.close()`?

| Alasan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Release resource | Response stream/connection perlu dilepas |
| Connection reuse | Pooling lebih sehat |
| Hindari leak | Terutama saat tidak membaca entity |
| Explicit behavior | Aman saat error branch |

Kalau memakai `get(CustomerResponse.class)`, error non-2xx bisa menjadi exception/behavior runtime yang kurang eksplisit tergantung API dan provider.

---

## 14. Defensive Error Decoder

```java id="vcfsza"
private ErrorResponse readErrorResponse(Response response) {
    if (!response.hasEntity()) {
        return ErrorResponse.synthetic(
                "EMPTY_ERROR_BODY",
                "Downstream returned empty error body.",
                correlationIdProvider.currentOrNew()
        );
    }

    try {
        return response.readEntity(ErrorResponse.class);
    } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
        return ErrorResponse.synthetic(
                "MALFORMED_ERROR_BODY",
                "Downstream returned non-decodable error body.",
                correlationIdProvider.currentOrNew()
        );
    }
}
```

Kenapa perlu defensif?

| Skenario | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Gateway error HTML | `502 Bad Gateway` dengan HTML body |
| Empty body | `503` tanpa entity |
| Error schema berubah | Field/type tidak sesuai |
| Timeout exception | Tidak ada response body |
| Provider bug | Return `text/plain` |

Rule:

```text id="f89xqf"
Error decoder tidak boleh crash tanpa klasifikasi.
Kalau body tidak bisa didecode, buat synthetic downstream error.
```

---

## 15. Mapping Status Code ke Domain Result

```java id="nghcrn"
public RuntimeException map(int status, ErrorResponse error) {
    if (status == 401 || status == 403) {
        return new DownstreamAuthorizationException(error);
    }

    if (status == 409) {
        return new DownstreamConflictException(error);
    }

    if (status == 422) {
        return new DownstreamBusinessValidationException(error);
    }

    if (status == 429 || status >= 500) {
        return new DownstreamUnavailableException(error);
    }

    return new DownstreamProtocolException(error);
}
```

Mapping prinsip:

| HTTP | Consumer meaning |
|---|---|
| `400` | Caller/request bug atau contract mismatch |
| `401/403` | Security failure |
| `404` | Bisa normal outcome untuk lookup |
| `409` | State conflict |
| `422` | Business validation |
| `429` | Rate limited, retry later |
| `500/503/504` | System/downstream failure |
| Non-JSON error | Protocol/gateway failure |

---

## 16. JSON Provider: Jackson / JSON-B

Jersey Client perlu JSON provider yang sesuai.

Untuk Jackson:

```java id="53bkyh"
ClientConfig config = new ClientConfig();
config.register(JacksonFeature.class);
```

Maven dependency biasanya:

```xml id="fl9ajy"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.media</groupId>
    <artifactId>jersey-media-json-jackson</artifactId>
    <version>${jersey.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Hal yang perlu distandardisasi:

| Area | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Date/time | Offset/timezone salah |
| Unknown fields | Additive response field bisa gagal |
| Enum unknown | Consumer lama gagal decode |
| BigDecimal | Precision loss |
| Null handling | Required vs nullable |
| Naming strategy | snake_case vs camelCase |
| Java records | Provider support perlu dicek |
| Validation | JSON decode bukan Bean Validation |

Rule:

```text id="df5oh4"
Jersey Client ObjectMapper/JSON provider harus align dengan OpenAPI contract dan platform standard.
```

---

## 17. Content Negotiation

Set header dengan jelas:

```java id="2dpdyt"
.request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
```

Untuk POST:

```java id="3q5i65"
CreateOrderRequest request = ...

Response response = orderTarget
        .path("/orders")
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .post(Entity.entity(request, MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE));
```

Makna:

| Header | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `Accept` | Format response yang diinginkan |
| `Content-Type` | Format request body |
| `Authorization` | Credential |
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Trace/log correlation |
| `Idempotency-Key` | Dedup command |
| `traceparent` | Distributed tracing |

Jangan biarkan content type implisit untuk API contract penting.

---

## 18. Query Parameters dan Pagination

```java id="hrrj8f"
Response response = orderTarget
        .path("/orders")
        .queryParam("status", "OPEN")
        .queryParam("page", 0)
        .queryParam("size", 50)
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .get();
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Gunakan `queryParam`, bukan string concat | Encoding aman |
| Batasi `size` | Hindari payload besar |
| Jangan queryParam untuk JSON besar | Gunakan `POST /search` jika kompleks |
| Pagination response jangan array mentah | Butuh metadata |
| Sort harus eksplisit | Hindari default berubah diam-diam |

---

## 19. POST Command dengan Idempotency Key

```java id="vrr5og"
Response response = orderTarget
        .path("/orders")
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .header("Idempotency-Key", idempotencyKey.value())
        .post(Entity.entity(request, MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE));
```

Handling:

```java id="0qic3h"
try {
    return switch (response.getStatus()) {
        case 201 -> mapCreated(response.readEntity(CreateOrderResponse.class));
        case 409 -> throw conflict(response);
        case 422 -> throw businessValidation(response);
        default -> throw unexpected(response);
    };
} finally {
    response.close();
}
```

Rule:

```text id="m4k50a"
Retry POST command hanya aman jika idempotency semantics ada di contract dan provider mendukung deduplication.
```

---

## 20. PUT/PATCH Partial Update

PATCH ambiguity:

```json id="dw0lsa"
{
  "email": null
}
```

Apakah artinya:

```text id="qoqjfc"
clear email?
ignore email?
invalid?
```

Contract harus jelas.

Jersey call:

```java id="dwf19i"
Response response = customerTarget
        .path("/customers/{customerId}")
        .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId.value())
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .method("PATCH", Entity.entity(
                patchRequest,
                MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE
        ));
```

Checklist:

| Area | Cek |
|---|---|
| Null vs absent | Semantics jelas |
| Concurrency | ETag/version/If-Match perlu? |
| Validation | Partial validation berbeda dari create |
| Response | `200` state baru atau `204` no body |
| Conflict | `409` untuk version/state conflict |

---

## 21. Conditional Request: ETag / If-Match

Untuk optimistic concurrency:

```java id="bwivzi"
Response response = customerTarget
        .path("/customers/{customerId}")
        .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId.value())
        .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
        .header(HttpHeaders.IF_MATCH, etag)
        .put(Entity.json(updateRequest));
```

Interpretasi:

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `200/204` | Update sukses |
| `404` | Resource tidak ada |
| `409` | Domain conflict |
| `412 Precondition Failed` | ETag/precondition tidak cocok |

Jika API memilih version field dibanding ETag, tetap harus ada conflict response yang jelas.

---

## 22. Connection Pool dan Connector

Jersey Client mendukung pluggable connector architecture; dokumentasi Jersey menyebut connector default berbasis JDK `HttpURLConnection`, serta connector berbasis Apache HTTP Client, Jetty HTTP Client, dan Grizzly Async Client. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/3.0.0/client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Kenapa connector penting?

| Concern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Connection pooling | Latency dan throughput |
| TLS/mTLS | Keystore/truststore support |
| Proxy | On-prem/corporate network |
| HTTP method support | PATCH behavior, redirect, etc |
| Performance | High concurrency downstream call |
| Observability | Instrumentasi transport |
| Timeout behavior | Connector-specific detail |

Rule praktis:

```text id="k24sm9"
Untuk service-to-service traffic serius,
cek connector yang dipakai, bukan hanya Jersey API-level code.
```

Jika perusahaan punya platform standard, ikuti itu. Jangan mengganti connector sendiri tanpa performance/regression test.

---

## 23. TLS, mTLS, Truststore

Di on-prem/AWS/Azure enterprise, outbound HTTP sering memerlukan TLS atau mTLS.

Mental model:

```text id="eolq8b"
Client
  -> truststore validates server certificate
  -> optional keystore presents client certificate
  -> hostname verification
  -> proxy / service mesh may terminate TLS
```

Hal yang perlu dicari:

| Config | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Truststore | CA internal ada? |
| Keystore | Client certificate ada? |
| Rotation | Cert rotate bagaimana? |
| Hostname verification | Jangan disable sembarangan |
| mTLS location | App-level atau service mesh? |
| Cloud/on-prem | Endpoint dan CA berbeda? |
| Secret storage | Kubernetes Secret / vault? |

Red flag:

```java id="axz0ag"
// Trust all certificates
```

Ini tidak boleh menjadi production default.

---

## 24. Proxy dan Network Policy

Dalam enterprise, outbound call bisa lewat:

```text id="k7g22z"
Kubernetes Service DNS
Ingress/internal gateway
API gateway
Service mesh
Corporate proxy
PrivateLink / VNet / VPC endpoint
On-prem load balancer
```

Config yang perlu environment-driven:

```text id="1a3avt"
baseUrl
proxyHost
proxyPort
tls truststore
mTLS cert
connect timeout
read timeout
DNS/service name
```

Jangan hardcode:

```text id="8cu8mz"
http://customer-service.prod.svc.cluster.local
```

di source code. Gunakan config per environment.

---

## 25. Observability Wrapper

Buat wrapper policy agar semua call punya metrics/log/tracing.

```java id="9nrkk7"
public final class DownstreamCallPolicy {

    public <T> T execute(
            String downstream,
            String operation,
            Supplier<T> call
    ) {
        long startNanos = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            T result = call.get();
            recordSuccess(downstream, operation, startNanos);
            return result;
        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
            recordFailure(downstream, operation, startNanos, ex);
            throw ex;
        }
    }
}
```

Pemakaian:

```java id="05baye"
return callPolicy.execute(
        "customer-service",
        "getCustomerById",
        () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
);
```

Metrics:

```text id="qs1d80"
downstream_http_client_duration
downstream_http_client_requests_total
downstream_http_client_errors_total
downstream_http_client_timeouts_total
```

Log fields:

| Field | Contoh |
|---|---|
| `downstream` | `customer-service` |
| `operation` | `getCustomerById` |
| `method` | `GET` |
| `pathTemplate` | `/customers/{customerId}` |
| `status` | `404` |
| `latencyMs` | `37` |
| `errorCode` | `CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND` |
| `correlationId` | `01J...` |
| `attempt` | `1` |

---

## 26. Retry di Sekitar Jersey Client

Jersey Client sendiri bukan tempat utama untuk business-aware retry. Retry sebaiknya di policy layer.

```java id="cev1qp"
public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
    return retryPolicy.execute(
            "customer-service",
            "getCustomerById",
            RetryPolicy.queryPolicy(),
            () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
    );
}
```

Retry rules:

| Operation | Retry |
|---|---|
| `GET` lookup | Bounded retry ok untuk timeout/503 |
| `POST /search` read-only | Bounded retry jika benar-benar no side effect |
| `POST /orders` | Hanya dengan idempotency key |
| Payment command | Sangat hati-hati |
| Workflow transition | Sinkron dengan Camunda retry |
| Callback receive | Receiver harus idempotent |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="thbiyn"
Retry semua JerseyProcessingException 3x untuk semua endpoint.
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="srsl6o"
Retry only:
  connect timeout
  read timeout for safe/idempotent operation
  503/504
  maybe 429 after Retry-After

Do not retry:
  400, 401, 403, 404, 409, 422
```

---

## 27. Exception Types yang Umum Terlihat

Dalam Jersey/JAX-RS client code, Anda akan sering melihat:

| Exception | Makna umum |
|---|---|
| `ProcessingException` | Client-side processing/IO/serialization issue |
| `ResponseProcessingException` | Response diterima tapi processing entity gagal |
| `WebApplicationException` | Exception terkait HTTP response, tergantung cara call |
| JSON mapping exception | Body tidak sesuai model |
| Timeout exception wrapped | Tergantung connector |
| SSL exception wrapped | TLS/truststore issue |

Mapping jangan terlalu generic:

```java id="wufpu0"
catch (ProcessingException ex) {
    throw new DownstreamTransportException("CUSTOMER_SERVICE_TRANSPORT_ERROR", ex);
}
```

Untuk response non-2xx, lebih baik baca `Response` eksplisit daripada membiarkan exception default sulit diklasifikasi.

---

## 28. Resource Cleanup

### Client shutdown

Jika Anda membuat `Client` singleton, tutup saat aplikasi shutdown.

```java id="343mn1"
public final class ManagedJerseyClient implements AutoCloseable {

    private final Client client;

    public ManagedJerseyClient(Client client) {
        this.client = client;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        client.close();
    }
}
```

Integrasikan dengan lifecycle DI container/HK2/CDI/app bootstrap.

### Response cleanup

```java id="mqlhdi"
Response response = target.request().get();

try {
    ...
} finally {
    response.close();
}
```

Jika Anda memakai `get(EntityClass.class)`, response handling dilakukan oleh runtime, tapi Anda kehilangan kontrol status/error body.

Rule:

```text id="hvcp1k"
Kalau Anda menerima Response raw,
Anda bertanggung jawab menutupnya.
```

---

## 29. Manual Jersey Client vs Generated Jersey Client

| Aspek | Manual Jersey Client | Generated Jersey Client |
|---|---|---|
| Boilerplate | Lebih banyak | Lebih sedikit |
| Type safety | Manual DTO usage | Generated method/model |
| Contract alignment | Harus disiplin sendiri | Lebih dekat ke OpenAPI |
| Error handling | Sangat eksplisit | Tergantung generated client |
| Flexibility | Tinggi | Dibatasi generator |
| Risk | URL/status/header typo | Generated code coupling |
| Best use | Edge/custom calls, special behavior | Standard contract-first API |
| Tetap butuh adapter | Ya | Ya |

Decision rule:

```text id="d9kkag"
Jika OpenAPI contract tersedia dan stabil:
  prefer generated client.

Jika butuh kontrol khusus atau endpoint belum masuk generator:
  manual Jersey Client boleh, tapi tetap ikuti contract dan adapter pattern.
```

---

## 30. Complete Example: Customer Gateway Manual Jersey

```java id="jkbe3b"
public final class CustomerJerseyGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final WebTarget customerTarget;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper;
    private final JerseyErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    public CustomerJerseyGateway(
            WebTarget customerTarget,
            CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper,
            JerseyErrorMapper errorMapper,
            DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy
    ) {
        this.customerTarget = customerTarget;
        this.dtoMapper = dtoMapper;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
        this.callPolicy = callPolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invokeFindById(CustomerId customerId) {
        Response response = customerTarget
                .path("/customers/{customerId}")
                .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId.value())
                .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .get();

        try {
            int status = response.getStatus();

            if (status == 200) {
                CustomerResponse body =
                        response.readEntity(CustomerResponse.class);

                return Optional.of(dtoMapper.toProfile(body));
            }

            if (status == 404) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }

            throw errorMapper.map(response);

        } catch (ProcessingException ex) {
            throw new DownstreamTransportException(
                    "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_TRANSPORT_ERROR",
                    ex
            );
        } finally {
            response.close();
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 31. Complete Example: Factory

```java id="n2v1ck"
public final class CustomerJerseyGatewayFactory {

    private final CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper;
    private final JerseyErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;
    private final CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider;
    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;

    public CustomerJerseyGatewayFactory(
            CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper,
            JerseyErrorMapper errorMapper,
            DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy,
            CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider,
            ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider
    ) {
        this.dtoMapper = dtoMapper;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
        this.callPolicy = callPolicy;
        this.correlationIdProvider = correlationIdProvider;
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
    }

    public CustomerGateway create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        ClientConfig config = new ClientConfig()
                .property(ClientProperties.CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
                        properties.connectTimeoutMs())
                .property(ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT,
                        properties.readTimeoutMs());

        config.register(JacksonFeature.class);
        config.register(new CorrelationIdRequestFilter(
                correlationIdProvider,
                "order-service"
        ));
        config.register(new BearerTokenRequestFilter(
                tokenProvider,
                "customer-api.read"
        ));

        Client client = ClientBuilder.newClient(config);
        WebTarget target = client.target(properties.baseUrl());

        return new CustomerJerseyGateway(
                target,
                dtoMapper,
                errorMapper,
                callPolicy
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 32. Testing Strategy

### Unit test mapper

```text id="0pgqs8"
CustomerResponse valid -> CustomerProfile
CustomerResponse missing required field -> contract violation
Unknown enum -> UNKNOWN/fail per policy
```

### Unit test error mapper

```text id="8yonyf"
400 ErrorResponse -> DownstreamRequestException
404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND -> Optional.empty at gateway
409 -> DownstreamConflictException
422 -> DownstreamBusinessValidationException
500 -> DownstreamUnavailableException
HTML error body -> DownstreamProtocolException
```

### Mock HTTP test

Gunakan mock server untuk memvalidasi actual Jersey call:

```text id="oeap8e"
Given mock GET /customers/CUST-001 returns 200 JSON
When gateway.findById(CUST-001)
Then CustomerProfile returned
And X-Correlation-Id header sent
And Authorization header sent
```

### Timeout test

```text id="57yjsh"
Given mock server delays response beyond read timeout
When gateway.findById(...)
Then DownstreamTransportException or timeout-specific exception
And metric timeout incremented
```

---

## 33. Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Client lifecycle | `Client` reused, not per request |
| Shutdown | `Client.close()` handled |
| Base URL | Config-driven |
| Timeout | Connect/read timeout explicit |
| WebTarget | Path/query built safely |
| Headers | Auth/correlation/tenant propagated |
| JSON provider | Jackson/JSON-B aligned with platform |
| Error handling | Non-2xx mapped deterministically |
| Response cleanup | Raw `Response` closed |
| Retry | Outside Jersey call, operation-aware |
| Observability | Metric/log/trace around call |
| TLS/mTLS | Truststore/keystore configured via platform |
| Connector | Connector choice understood |
| DTO mapping | External DTO mapped to internal model |
| No domain leakage | Jersey types not in application/domain |
| Tests | Success/error/timeout/header covered |

---

## 34. Red Flags

| Red flag | Dampak |
|---|---|
| `ClientBuilder.newClient()` di setiap method | Resource/connection overhead |
| Tidak ada timeout | Thread bisa menggantung |
| Base URL hardcoded | Deployment sulit |
| String concat URL | Encoding bug / path injection |
| Auth header diset manual di setiap use case | Inconsistent dan rawan lupa |
| Correlation ID tidak dipropagasikan | RCA sulit |
| Raw `Response` tidak ditutup | Resource leak |
| Semua non-2xx dianggap `RuntimeException` sama | Error semantics hilang |
| Error body parsing message | Brittle |
| Retry global semua method | Duplicate command |
| DTO response langsung masuk domain/MyBatis/Camunda | Coupling tinggi |
| Trust-all SSL di production | Security risk |
| Logging full body/token | Data leak |
| Tidak tahu connector yang dipakai | Performance/TLS/proxy issue tersembunyi |
| Tidak ada mock HTTP test | Actual HTTP contract tidak teruji |

---

## 35. Ringkasan

Jersey Client yang sehat untuk microservice:

```text id="1y34db"
Client singleton/reused
  + WebTarget per downstream
  + explicit timeout
  + JSON provider aligned
  + request filters for auth/correlation
  + response/error mapping
  + DTO-to-domain mapper
  + observability wrapper
  + operation-aware retry policy
  + lifecycle shutdown
```

Manual Jersey Client cocok untuk kontrol penuh, tetapi harus tetap mengikuti contract-first discipline:

```text id="0780b6"
OpenAPI contract
  -> endpoint/status/schema/error/header
  -> Jersey Client call
  -> adapter mapping
  -> domain/application interface
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Jersey Client bukan sekadar `.target(...).request().get()`. Dalam production microservice, ia adalah boundary yang harus mengendalikan timeout, auth, correlation, error semantics, observability, dan resource lifecycle.**
