**Seri 9/16 selesai.**

Rekomendasi lanjut: **Seri 10 - OpenFeign untuk Declarative HTTP Client**, fokus pada Feign core, generated Feign client dari OpenAPI Generator, encoder/decoder, `ErrorDecoder`, `RequestInterceptor`, timeout, retry, logging, dan cara menggunakannya di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS non-Spring.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:42:41 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 10/16**. Kali ini kita bahas OpenFeign sebagai declarative HTTP client, terutama bagaimana memakainya di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS non-Spring tanpa mencampur Feign detail ke business layer.

# Seri 10/16 - OpenFeign untuk Declarative HTTP Client

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada:

> **Bagaimana memakai OpenFeign sebagai declarative HTTP client untuk service-to-service communication dalam aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS, terutama dengan contract-first OpenAPI.**

Feign adalah **Java-to-HTTP client binder** yang bertujuan mengurangi kompleksitas binding Java ke HTTP API. Feign memproses interface/annotation menjadi HTTP request, lalu memakai encoder, decoder, client transport, retryer, request interceptor, dan error decoder untuk menjalankan call. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openfeign/feign?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Dalam stack Anda, Feign bisa muncul dalam dua bentuk:

```text id="mij8sm"
A. Generated Feign client dari OpenAPI Generator
B. Handwritten Feign interface
```

Keduanya tetap harus dibungkus adapter/gateway agar detail Feign tidak bocor ke domain/application layer.

---

## 2. Mental Model OpenFeign

```text id="ud33wo"
Java Interface Method
  |
  v
Feign Contract / Annotation Processing
  |
  v
Request Template
  |
  +--> RequestInterceptor
  |      - Authorization
  |      - Correlation ID
  |      - Tenant/User context
  |
  v
Encoder
  - Java object -> JSON/body
  |
  v
HTTP Client
  - transport
  - timeout
  - TLS/proxy
  |
  v
HTTP Response
  |
  +--> Decoder for 2xx
  +--> ErrorDecoder for non-2xx
  |
  v
Generated/declared Java result or exception
```

Perbandingan mental dengan Jersey Client:

| Aspek | Jersey Client | OpenFeign |
|---|---|---|
| Style | Programmatic/fluent | Declarative interface |
| Request building | Manual via `target().path().request()` | Method call + annotation |
| Error handling | Manual `Response` atau exception | `ErrorDecoder` |
| Header injection | `ClientRequestFilter` | `RequestInterceptor` |
| JSON | Provider/ObjectMapper | Encoder/Decoder |
| Best boundary | Adapter/gateway | Adapter/gateway |
| Cocok untuk | Kontrol detail request | Interface HTTP client yang ringkas |

---

## 3. OpenFeign vs Spring Cloud OpenFeign

Ini penting untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS non-Spring.

| Item | Makna |
|---|---|
| **OpenFeign core** | Library Feign asli untuk declarative HTTP client |
| **Spring Cloud OpenFeign** | Integrasi OpenFeign untuk aplikasi Spring Boot, termasuk autoconfiguration dan binding ke Spring Environment |
| **Jersey/JAX-RS non-Spring** | Biasanya memakai OpenFeign core secara manual atau via factory/HK2/CDI |

Spring Cloud OpenFeign memang menyediakan integrasi khusus untuk Spring Boot melalui autoconfiguration dan idiom Spring. ([Home](https://docs.spring.io/spring-cloud-openfeign/docs/current/reference/html/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jadi, dalam aplikasi Jersey non-Spring, jangan berasumsi fitur seperti `@FeignClient`, Spring bean autowiring, atau konfigurasi `spring.cloud.openfeign.*` tersedia.

Rule:

```text id="0cbfsn"
Jersey/JAX-RS application:
  use OpenFeign core,
  wire client via factory / HK2 / CDI,
  keep Feign behind adapter.
```

---

## 4. Generated Feign dari OpenAPI Generator

OpenAPI Generator Java generator mendukung pembuatan Java client library dan mencantumkan OpenFeign sebagai salah satu HTTP library yang didukung. Generator `java` berstatus `STABLE` dan bertipe `CLIENT`. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh Maven config:

```xml id="d5j7mk"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.openapitools</groupId>
    <artifactId>openapi-generator-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${openapi-generator.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>generate-customer-feign-client</id>
            <phase>generate-sources</phase>
            <goals>
                <goal>generate</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <inputSpec>${project.basedir}/src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml</inputSpec>
                <generatorName>java</generatorName>
                <library>feign</library>

                <apiPackage>com.company.customer.client.api</apiPackage>
                <modelPackage>com.company.customer.client.model</modelPackage>
                <invokerPackage>com.company.customer.client.invoker</invokerPackage>

                <generateApiTests>false</generateApiTests>
                <generateModelTests>false</generateModelTests>

                <configOptions>
                    <dateLibrary>java8</dateLibrary>
                    <serializationLibrary>jackson</serializationLibrary>
                    <hideGenerationTimestamp>true</hideGenerationTimestamp>
                </configOptions>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

Generated output biasanya tetap punya struktur:

```text id="x0euvu"
com.company.customer.client
  ├── api
  │   └── CustomersApi.java
  ├── model
  │   └── CustomerResponse.java
  └── invoker
      └── ApiClient.java / helper classes
```

Tetapi detail output bergantung pada versi OpenAPI Generator, pilihan `library`, config options, dan template.

---

## 5. Handwritten Feign Interface

Jika tidak memakai generator, Feign bisa dibuat manual.

Contoh konseptual:

```java id="sz8ecz"
public interface CustomerFeignApi {

    @RequestLine("GET /customers/{customerId}")
    @Headers("Accept: application/json")
    CustomerResponse getCustomerById(
            @Param("customerId") String customerId
    );

    @RequestLine("POST /customers/search")
    @Headers({
            "Accept: application/json",
            "Content-Type: application/json"
    })
    CustomerSearchResponse searchCustomers(CustomerSearchRequest request);
}
```

Lalu build client:

```java id="tztiwr"
CustomerFeignApi api = Feign.builder()
        .encoder(new JacksonEncoder(objectMapper))
        .decoder(new JacksonDecoder(objectMapper))
        .errorDecoder(new CustomerErrorDecoder(errorBodyDecoder))
        .requestInterceptor(new CorrelationIdFeignInterceptor(correlationIdProvider))
        .requestInterceptor(new BearerTokenFeignInterceptor(tokenProvider))
        .options(new Request.Options(
                100, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                true
        ))
        .retryer(Retryer.NEVER_RETRY)
        .target(CustomerFeignApi.class, properties.baseUrl());
```

Catatan: exact constructor/API Feign bisa berbeda antar versi, jadi sesuaikan dengan versi platform perusahaan. Prinsipnya tetap sama: builder mengikat interface ke base URL + encoder/decoder + policy runtime.

---

## 6. Struktur Layering yang Sehat

```text id="8z4czb"
Application Service / Use Case
  |
  v
CustomerGateway interface
  |
  v
CustomerFeignGateway
  |
  v
Generated or handwritten Feign API
  |
  v
OpenFeign runtime
  |
  v
Customer Service HTTP API
```

Bad:

```java id="3fz0on"
public class SubmitOrderUseCase {
    private final CustomerFeignApi customerFeignApi;

    public void submit(String customerId) {
        CustomerResponse response =
                customerFeignApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
    }
}
```

Better:

```java id="rdmtrw"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

```java id="2xrgyo"
public final class CustomerFeignGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomerFeignApi customerApi;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper mapper;
    private final CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invokeFindById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    customerApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(response));
        } catch (CustomerNotFoundException ex) {
            return Optional.empty();
        } catch (FeignException ex) {
            throw errorMapper.mapUnexpectedFeignException(ex);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 7. Encoder dan Decoder

Feign memerlukan encoder untuk request body dan decoder untuk response body.

```text id="2qy6a8"
Encoder:
  CreateOrderRequest -> JSON request body

Decoder:
  JSON response body -> CustomerResponse
```

Contoh dengan Jackson:

```java id="7j7zx0"
Feign.builder()
        .encoder(new JacksonEncoder(objectMapper))
        .decoder(new JacksonDecoder(objectMapper));
```

Hal yang perlu disamakan dengan kontrak OpenAPI:

| Area | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Date/time | `OffsetDateTime`, timezone, format |
| Unknown fields | Additive field compatibility |
| Enum unknown | Deserialization failure |
| BigDecimal | Precision |
| Null handling | Nullable vs required |
| Naming strategy | camelCase/snake_case |
| Error body | Decoder biasa bukan ErrorDecoder |

Rule:

```text id="lzluvw"
ObjectMapper Feign harus align dengan ObjectMapper platform dan OpenAPI contract.
```

Jangan biarkan Feign memakai konfigurasi JSON berbeda dari Jersey server/client lain.

---

## 8. `RequestInterceptor`

`RequestInterceptor` dipakai untuk menambahkan/mengubah request sebelum dikirim.

Use case:

```text id="ef6t9b"
Authorization
X-Correlation-Id
traceparent
X-Tenant-Id
X-Request-Source
Idempotency-Key, kalau bisa dari context
```

Contoh correlation ID:

```java id="9zx3js"
public final class CorrelationIdFeignInterceptor
        implements RequestInterceptor {

    private final CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider;
    private final String sourceService;

    public CorrelationIdFeignInterceptor(
            CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider,
            String sourceService
    ) {
        this.correlationIdProvider = correlationIdProvider;
        this.sourceService = sourceService;
    }

    @Override
    public void apply(RequestTemplate template) {
        template.header(
                "X-Correlation-Id",
                correlationIdProvider.currentOrNew()
        );

        template.header(
                "X-Request-Source",
                sourceService
        );
    }
}
```

Contoh bearer token:

```java id="hwu2vy"
public final class BearerTokenFeignInterceptor
        implements RequestInterceptor {

    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final String scope;

    public BearerTokenFeignInterceptor(
            ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            String scope
    ) {
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.scope = scope;
    }

    @Override
    public void apply(RequestTemplate template) {
        String token = tokenProvider.getToken(scope);

        template.header(
                "Authorization",
                "Bearer " + token
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="g3minx"
Jangan set auth/correlation header manual di setiap use case.
Gunakan interceptor/factory agar konsisten.
```

---

## 9. Idempotency Key dengan Feign

Untuk command endpoint:

```text id="xo699t"
POST /orders
Header: Idempotency-Key
```

Ada dua pola.

### A. Explicit method parameter

```java id="ncu7ys"
public interface OrderFeignApi {

    @RequestLine("POST /orders")
    @Headers({
            "Content-Type: application/json",
            "Accept: application/json",
            "Idempotency-Key: {idempotencyKey}"
    })
    CreateOrderResponse createOrder(
            @Param("idempotencyKey") String idempotencyKey,
            CreateOrderRequest request
    );
}
```

### B. Context/interceptor

```java id="ev4yd5"
public final class IdempotencyKeyInterceptor implements RequestInterceptor {

    private final IdempotencyContext idempotencyContext;

    @Override
    public void apply(RequestTemplate template) {
        idempotencyContext.currentKey()
                .ifPresent(key -> template.header("Idempotency-Key", key));
    }
}
```

Recommendation:

| Pattern | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| Explicit parameter | Command-specific key yang harus terlihat di adapter |
| Interceptor context | Cross-cutting key untuk command pipeline |
| Jangan auto-generate random key tersembunyi | Consumer perlu kontrol retry semantics |

Rule:

```text id="6te8dy"
Idempotency key adalah bagian dari command semantics.
Jangan disembunyikan total sampai caller tidak tahu retry behavior.
```

---

## 10. `ErrorDecoder`

`ErrorDecoder` adalah komponen utama untuk non-2xx response. Feign menyediakan konsep error decoder untuk memetakan response error menjadi exception. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openfeign/feign?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java id="k89gwu"
public final class CustomerErrorDecoder implements ErrorDecoder {

    private final ErrorDecoder defaultDecoder = new ErrorDecoder.Default();
    private final DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder errorBodyDecoder;

    public CustomerErrorDecoder(
            DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder errorBodyDecoder
    ) {
        this.errorBodyDecoder = errorBodyDecoder;
    }

    @Override
    public Exception decode(String methodKey, Response response) {
        DecodedError error = errorBodyDecoder.decode(response);

        return switch (response.status()) {
            case 400 -> new DownstreamRequestException(error);
            case 401, 403 -> new DownstreamAuthorizationException(error);
            case 404 -> new CustomerNotFoundException(error);
            case 409 -> new DownstreamConflictException(error);
            case 422 -> new DownstreamBusinessValidationException(error);
            case 429 -> new DownstreamRateLimitedException(error);
            case 500, 502, 503, 504 -> new DownstreamUnavailableException(error);
            default -> defaultDecoder.decode(methodKey, response);
        };
    }
}
```

Important:

```text id="dgsbki"
ErrorDecoder harus defensive:
  error body bisa empty,
  HTML,
  malformed JSON,
  schema lama/baru,
  gateway-generated.
```

---

## 11. Defensive Error Body Decoder

```java id="qsn0t1"
public final class DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    public DecodedError decode(Response response) {
        String body = readBodySafely(response);

        if (body == null || body.isBlank()) {
            return DecodedError.synthetic(
                    response.status(),
                    "EMPTY_ERROR_BODY",
                    "Downstream returned empty error body."
            );
        }

        try {
            ErrorResponse error =
                    objectMapper.readValue(body, ErrorResponse.class);

            return DecodedError.from(response.status(), error);
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return DecodedError.synthetic(
                    response.status(),
                    "MALFORMED_ERROR_BODY",
                    "Downstream returned non-decodable error body."
            );
        }
    }

    private String readBodySafely(Response response) {
        try {
            if (response.body() == null) {
                return null;
            }

            try (Reader reader = response.body().asReader(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                return CharStreams.toString(reader);
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return null;
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan: kode `CharStreams` di atas contoh konseptual; gunakan utility yang tersedia di platform Anda, atau implementasi pembacaan body sendiri.

Mapping defensive:

| Response | Error result |
|---|---|
| `404` + valid JSON | Domain not found |
| `500` + valid `ErrorResponse` | Downstream system failure |
| `502` + HTML gateway body | Synthetic `MALFORMED_ERROR_BODY` |
| `503` empty body | Synthetic `EMPTY_ERROR_BODY` |
| Timeout/no response | Biasanya exception transport, bukan ErrorDecoder |

---

## 12. FeignException vs Domain Exception

Feign bisa melempar `FeignException` untuk HTTP/runtime errors yang tidak dimap khusus.

Jangan biarkan `FeignException` bocor sampai application layer.

Bad:

```java id="g47lr0"
public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId id) {
    try {
        return Optional.of(map(api.getCustomerById(id.value())));
    } catch (FeignException.NotFound e) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }
}
```

Masih terlalu Feign-specific jika tersebar di banyak adapter/use case.

Better:

```java id="yzxgqe"
public final class CustomerFeignGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    customerApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(response));
        } catch (CustomerNotFoundException ex) {
            return Optional.empty();
        } catch (DownstreamClientException ex) {
            throw ex;
        } catch (FeignException ex) {
            throw errorMapper.mapUnexpectedFeignException(ex);
        }
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="2g31d5"
FeignException is infrastructure detail.
Application layer should see domain/application exceptions or result types.
```

---

## 13. Timeout dengan Feign

Feign memiliki konsep request options untuk connect/read timeout, meski exact API tergantung versi.

Contoh umum:

```java id="yc7zvk"
.options(new Request.Options(
        100, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
        500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
        true
))
```

Policy:

| Timeout | Contoh |
|---|---:|
| Connect timeout | 100 ms |
| Read timeout | 300-1000 ms |
| Long-running endpoint | Pertimbangkan async `202` daripada read timeout besar |
| File download | Policy khusus, jangan samakan lookup API |
| Workflow call | Sinkron dengan retry Camunda/process engine |

Rule:

```text id="d7wjc5"
Timeout tidak boleh default/tidak diketahui.
Set timeout eksplisit per downstream atau per operation class.
```

Jangan meng-copy timeout Spring Cloud OpenFeign property ke aplikasi Jersey non-Spring; config-nya berbeda karena Anda memakai OpenFeign core secara manual.

---

## 14. Retryer

Feign memiliki konsep `Retryer`. Feign documentation dan API menunjukkan retry/error handling sebagai bagian dari builder/runtime, tetapi retry harus tetap didesain berdasarkan semantics operation, bukan global semua call.

Default yang aman untuk mulai:

```java id="wl82ee"
.retryer(Retryer.NEVER_RETRY)
```

Lalu retry di policy layer yang operation-aware:

```java id="zuwzni"
return retryPolicy.execute(
        "customer-service",
        "getCustomerById",
        RetryPolicy.queryPolicy(),
        () -> customerApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value())
);
```

Rules:

| Operation | Retry |
|---|---|
| `GET` lookup | Bounded retry ok untuk timeout/503 |
| `POST /search` read-only | Bisa retry jika no side effect |
| `POST /orders` | Hanya dengan idempotency key |
| Payment command | Sangat hati-hati |
| Workflow transition | Sinkron dengan workflow retry |
| `400/401/403/404/409/422` | Jangan retry |
| `429` | Retry hanya jika policy menghormati `Retry-After` |
| `503/504` | Bisa retry bounded untuk safe operation |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="d1z8do"
Global Feign retry untuk semua method.
```

---

## 15. `Retry-After` dan `429`

Feign memiliki utility terkait retry-after dalam error decoder API; beberapa dokumentasi API menyebut `RetryAfterDecoder` yang membaca header `Retry-After`. ([Javadoc](https://javadoc.io/static/io.github.openfeign/feign-core/10.7.0/feign/codec/ErrorDecoder.RetryAfterDecoder.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Design handling:

```text id="6ed1ev"
429 Too Many Requests
  -> read Retry-After if present
  -> if operation safe and within budget, retry later
  -> otherwise surface rate limited error
```

Jangan otomatis sleep lama di request thread jika melebihi SLA caller.

Better:

| Context | Handling |
|---|---|
| Synchronous API request | Fail fast jika retry-after > remaining deadline |
| Background job | Bisa schedule retry |
| Camunda worker | Throw retryable error / let workflow retry |
| Batch processor | Backoff queue |

---

## 16. Logging

Feign menyediakan logging integration, termasuk SLF4J module di ekosistem Feign. Tetapi logging harus hati-hati.

Do not log:

```text id="mf3bl8"
Authorization header
tokens
PII
full request/response body
password/secret
large payload
```

Log metadata:

```text id="hoxx3w"
downstream=customer-service
operation=getCustomerById
method=GET
pathTemplate=/customers/{customerId}
status=404
latencyMs=37
errorCode=CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND
correlationId=...
attempt=1
```

Feign logger bisa membantu saat debugging, tetapi production observability sebaiknya ada di wrapper policy agar konsisten dengan Jersey Client dan generated clients lain.

---

## 17. HTTP Transport Client

Feign bisa memakai transport berbeda, misalnya default, OkHttp, Apache HttpClient, dan lain-lain tergantung dependency.

Transport concern:

| Concern | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| Connection pooling | Throughput dan latency |
| TLS/mTLS | Keystore/truststore |
| Proxy | On-prem/corporate gateway |
| HTTP/2 | Jika diperlukan |
| Timeout behavior | Berbeda per transport |
| Metrics instrumentation | Transport-specific |
| Resource lifecycle | Client harus reusable/closed jika perlu |

Rule:

```text id="niwyhj"
Feign interface bukan seluruh runtime.
Cek underlying HTTP client yang dipakai.
```

---

## 18. Factory Pattern untuk Feign Client

```java id="gp6cay"
public final class CustomerFeignClientFactory {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider;
    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder errorBodyDecoder;

    public CustomerFeignClientFactory(
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider,
            ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder errorBodyDecoder
    ) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.correlationIdProvider = correlationIdProvider;
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.errorBodyDecoder = errorBodyDecoder;
    }

    public CustomerFeignApi create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        return Feign.builder()
                .encoder(new JacksonEncoder(objectMapper))
                .decoder(new JacksonDecoder(objectMapper))
                .errorDecoder(new CustomerErrorDecoder(errorBodyDecoder))
                .requestInterceptor(new CorrelationIdFeignInterceptor(
                        correlationIdProvider,
                        "order-service"
                ))
                .requestInterceptor(new BearerTokenFeignInterceptor(
                        tokenProvider,
                        "customer-api.read"
                ))
                .options(new Request.Options(
                        properties.connectTimeoutMs(),
                        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                        properties.readTimeoutMs(),
                        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                        true
                ))
                .retryer(Retryer.NEVER_RETRY)
                .target(CustomerFeignApi.class, properties.baseUrl());
    }
}
```

Property:

```java id="g88w5b"
public record CustomerClientProperties(
        String baseUrl,
        int connectTimeoutMs,
        int readTimeoutMs
) {
    public CustomerClientProperties {
        if (baseUrl == null || baseUrl.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("baseUrl is required");
        }
        if (connectTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("connectTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
        if (readTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("readTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 19. Adapter Pattern dengan Feign

```java id="9qr0ix"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

```java id="6q6dya"
public final class CustomerFeignGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomerFeignApi api;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper mapper;
    private final CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    public CustomerFeignGateway(
            CustomerFeignApi api,
            CustomerDtoMapper mapper,
            CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper,
            DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy
    ) {
        this.api = api;
        this.mapper = mapper;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
        this.callPolicy = callPolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invokeFindById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    api.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(response));
        } catch (CustomerNotFoundException ex) {
            return Optional.empty();
        } catch (DownstreamClientException ex) {
            throw ex;
        } catch (FeignException ex) {
            throw errorMapper.mapUnexpectedFeignException(ex);
        }
    }
}
```

Business layer:

```java id="p2vyhw"
public final class SubmitOrderUseCase {

    private final CustomerGateway customerGateway;

    public void submit(SubmitOrderCommand command) {
        CustomerProfile customer = customerGateway
                .findById(command.customerId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CustomerNotFoundException(command.customerId()));

        // continue use case
    }
}
```

No Feign import in application layer.

---

## 20. Generated Feign Client + Adapter

Jika `CustomerApi` berasal dari OpenAPI Generator:

```java id="vteyru"
public final class CustomerFeignGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomersApi generatedApi;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper mapper;
    private final CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> {
                    try {
                        CustomerResponse response =
                                generatedApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

                        return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(response));
                    } catch (CustomerNotFoundException ex) {
                        return Optional.empty();
                    } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
                        throw errorMapper.map(ex);
                    }
                }
        );
    }
}
```

Checklist khusus generated Feign:

| Area | Cek |
|---|---|
| Package | Tidak bentrok dengan client lain |
| Interface annotations | Sesuai Feign contract/library |
| Model DTO | Tidak bocor ke domain |
| Error behavior | Non-2xx jadi apa? |
| Auth config | Bagaimana inject token? |
| Timeout config | Di generated ApiClient atau builder? |
| ObjectMapper | Bisa override? |
| Retry default | Apakah ada retry implisit? |
| Upgrade | Generated method/model berubah? |

---

## 21. Feign dalam HK2/CDI/Jersey App

Karena ini bukan Spring, wiring harus eksplisit.

Contoh pseudo HK2 binder:

```java id="ll3nrb"
public final class ClientBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        bindFactory(CustomerGatewayFactory.class)
                .to(CustomerGateway.class)
                .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

Factory:

```java id="hk5846"
public final class CustomerGatewayFactory
        implements Factory<CustomerGateway> {

    @Override
    public CustomerGateway provide() {
        CustomerFeignApi api = feignClientFactory.create(properties);

        return new CustomerFeignGateway(
                api,
                mapper,
                errorMapper,
                callPolicy
        );
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose(CustomerGateway instance) {
        // close underlying transport if needed
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="eumwhd"
Feign client proxy biasanya dibuat saat startup dan reused.
Jangan instantiate Feign.builder() per request.
```

---

## 22. Feign dan Contract-first Design

Feign interface harus mengikuti OpenAPI contract.

Bad:

```java id="hyba86"
@RequestLine("GET /getCustomer?id={id}")
CustomerResponse get(String id);
```

Better:

```java id="l7alwo"
@RequestLine("GET /customers/{customerId}")
CustomerResponse getCustomerById(@Param("customerId") String customerId);
```

OpenAPI alignment checklist:

| OpenAPI | Feign |
|---|---|
| `operationId` | Method name |
| Path parameter | `@Param` |
| Query parameter | Query template / generated client |
| Header parameter | Explicit param or interceptor |
| Request body | Method body argument |
| Content-Type | `@Headers` |
| Response schema | Return type |
| Error response | `ErrorDecoder` |
| Security scheme | RequestInterceptor/auth config |

---

## 23. Query Parameter Handling

Example:

```java id="1v5ofo"
@RequestLine("GET /orders?status={status}&page={page}&size={size}")
OrderPageResponse listOrders(
        @Param("status") String status,
        @Param("page") int page,
        @Param("size") int size
);
```

Risks:

| Risk | Notes |
|---|---|
| Null query param | Apakah dikirim sebagai `null`, kosong, atau dihilangkan? |
| List params | Format `ids=a&ids=b` vs `ids=a,b` |
| Encoding | Pastikan Feign handles encoding sesuai expectation |
| Optional filters | Jangan buat banyak method overload liar |
| Pagination max | Validate sebelum call |

Untuk query kompleks, pakai request body:

```java id="l82d9g"
@RequestLine("POST /orders/search")
@Headers({
        "Content-Type: application/json",
        "Accept: application/json"
})
OrderSearchResponse searchOrders(OrderSearchRequest request);
```

---

## 24. Error Mapping Table untuk Feign

| Status / failure | Feign handling | Internal mapping |
|---|---|---|
| `200` | Decoder | Domain DTO mapping |
| `201` | Decoder | Created result |
| `204` | Void/empty handling | Success no content |
| `400` | ErrorDecoder | Downstream request/contract issue |
| `401/403` | ErrorDecoder | Security/authorization failure |
| `404` | ErrorDecoder | Optional.empty for lookup or not found exception |
| `409` | ErrorDecoder | Conflict exception/result |
| `422` | ErrorDecoder | Business validation result |
| `429` | ErrorDecoder | Rate limited |
| `500` | ErrorDecoder | Downstream system failure |
| `502/503/504` | ErrorDecoder | Gateway/unavailable/timeout |
| IO/connect timeout | FeignException/transport exception | Downstream transport failure |
| Decode error on 2xx | Decoder exception | Downstream contract violation |

---

## 25. Decode Error on Success Response

Feign decoder can fail even when status is 2xx.

Example provider returns:

```json id="m0d6hu"
{
  "customerId": null,
  "displayName": "Acme"
}
```

Or invalid enum:

```json id="0yt9tg"
{
  "status": "NEW_UNKNOWN_STATUS"
}
```

Handling:

```text id="3ucqbu"
2xx response
  -> decoder/mapping fails
  -> classify as downstream contract violation
  -> log as provider contract issue
```

Do not classify as:

```text id="gmlgb8"
customer not found
business validation
caller error
```

It is provider/contract/runtime compatibility problem.

---

## 26. Feign Logging and Sensitive Data

Recommended log policy:

| Log | Avoid |
|---|---|
| method | Authorization token |
| URL path template | Full query with sensitive data |
| status | Full body by default |
| latency | PII |
| errorCode | Secrets |
| correlationId | Payment/card data |
| downstream service | Passwords/API keys |

If Feign full logging is enabled temporarily, restrict to safe environments or scrub data.

Production default should rely on structured wrapper logs, not full request/response dumps.

---

## 27. Feign with Circuit Breaker / Bulkhead

Feign itself should not be your only resilience boundary.

Recommended:

```text id="351vl3"
CustomerGateway
  |
  v
Resilience policy
  - timeout/deadline
  - retry if safe
  - circuit breaker
  - bulkhead
  - metrics
  |
  v
Feign client call
```

Pseudo:

```java id="vw9lfo"
return resiliencePolicy.execute(
        "customer-service",
        "getCustomerById",
        Policy.query(),
        () -> api.getCustomerById(customerId.value())
);
```

Policy should know:

```text id="rslaoy"
operation type:
  query / command / workflow transition

retry safety:
  idempotent / non-idempotent

timeout budget:
  caller deadline / downstream deadline
```

---

## 28. Feign and Camunda / Workflow

Dalam Camunda worker/delegate, jangan biarkan Feign retry dan workflow retry bertabrakan.

Bad:

```text id="1hefhk"
Feign retry 3x
  + Camunda retry 5x
  = 15 downstream attempts
```

Better:

```text id="ew454i"
For workflow command:
  Feign retryer = NEVER_RETRY
  Let workflow retry handle retryable system failures
  Use idempotency key/business key
  Map business errors to BPMN error / non-retryable result
```

Mapping:

| Error | Workflow action |
|---|---|
| `400` contract bug | Incident / non-retryable technical error |
| `404` expected business missing | BPMN business error or modeled branch |
| `409` invalid state | BPMN business error / compensation |
| `422` validation failure | Business error |
| `503/504` | Retryable technical error |
| Timeout | Retryable if idempotency safe |

---

## 29. Feign and MyBatis Boundary

Jangan:

```java id="087j5j"
myBatisMapper.insertCustomer(customerFeignApi.getCustomerById(id));
```

Better:

```text id="hg22r0"
Feign CustomerResponse
  -> CustomerProfile
  -> CustomerSnapshotRecord
  -> MyBatis mapper
```

Reason:

| Problem | Impact |
|---|---|
| Generated DTO changes | Persistence compile break |
| External nullable fields | DB constraint issue |
| External enum expands | DB invalid value |
| API version migration | Database layer affected |
| Generated class annotations | Persistence behavior accidental |

---

## 30. Testing Strategy

### Unit test mapper

```text id="gnam8j"
CustomerResponse -> CustomerProfile
Unknown enum -> UNKNOWN
Missing required field -> contract violation
```

### Unit test ErrorDecoder

```text id="9unnk1"
404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND -> CustomerNotFoundException
409 ORDER_CONFLICT -> DownstreamConflictException
422 VALIDATION -> DownstreamBusinessValidationException
503 empty body -> DownstreamUnavailableException with synthetic error
502 HTML body -> DownstreamUnavailableException / protocol error
```

### Mock HTTP test

```text id="jwvyb3"
Mock server:
  GET /customers/CUST-001 returns 200 JSON

Assert:
  gateway returns CustomerProfile
  Authorization header sent
  X-Correlation-Id sent
  Accept header sent
```

### Timeout test

```text id="gcu9t3"
Mock server delays beyond read timeout
Expect:
  downstream timeout/transport exception
  metric incremented
  no retry for non-idempotent command
```

---

## 31. Complete Example: Feign API + Gateway

Feign API:

```java id="v1p5lh"
public interface CustomerFeignApi {

    @RequestLine("GET /customers/{customerId}")
    @Headers("Accept: application/json")
    CustomerResponse getCustomerById(
            @Param("customerId") String customerId
    );
}
```

Gateway:

```java id="1epk9y"
public final class CustomerFeignGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomerFeignApi api;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper mapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    public CustomerFeignGateway(
            CustomerFeignApi api,
            CustomerDtoMapper mapper,
            DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy
    ) {
        this.api = api;
        this.mapper = mapper;
        this.callPolicy = callPolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invoke(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invoke(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    api.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(response));
        } catch (CustomerNotFoundException ex) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 32. Complete Example: ErrorDecoder

```java id="x6t34m"
public final class CustomerErrorDecoder implements ErrorDecoder {

    private final ErrorDecoder defaultDecoder = new ErrorDecoder.Default();
    private final DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder decoder;

    public CustomerErrorDecoder(DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder decoder) {
        this.decoder = decoder;
    }

    @Override
    public Exception decode(String methodKey, Response response) {
        DecodedError error = decoder.decode(response);

        if (response.status() == 404
                && "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND".equals(error.errorCode())) {
            return new CustomerNotFoundException(error);
        }

        if (response.status() == 401 || response.status() == 403) {
            return new DownstreamAuthorizationException(error);
        }

        if (response.status() == 409) {
            return new DownstreamConflictException(error);
        }

        if (response.status() == 422) {
            return new DownstreamBusinessValidationException(error);
        }

        if (response.status() == 429) {
            return new DownstreamRateLimitedException(error);
        }

        if (response.status() >= 500) {
            return new DownstreamUnavailableException(error);
        }

        return defaultDecoder.decode(methodKey, response);
    }
}
```

---

## 33. Complete Example: Factory

```java id="y78r8f"
public final class CustomerFeignFactory {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider;
    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder errorBodyDecoder;

    public CustomerFeignFactory(
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider,
            ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder errorBodyDecoder
    ) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.correlationIdProvider = correlationIdProvider;
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.errorBodyDecoder = errorBodyDecoder;
    }

    public CustomerFeignApi create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        return Feign.builder()
                .encoder(new JacksonEncoder(objectMapper))
                .decoder(new JacksonDecoder(objectMapper))
                .errorDecoder(new CustomerErrorDecoder(errorBodyDecoder))
                .requestInterceptor(new CorrelationIdFeignInterceptor(
                        correlationIdProvider,
                        "order-service"
                ))
                .requestInterceptor(new BearerTokenFeignInterceptor(
                        tokenProvider,
                        "customer-api.read"
                ))
                .options(new Request.Options(
                        properties.connectTimeoutMs(),
                        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                        properties.readTimeoutMs(),
                        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                        true
                ))
                .retryer(Retryer.NEVER_RETRY)
                .target(CustomerFeignApi.class, properties.baseUrl());
    }
}
```

---

## 34. Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| OpenFeign vs Spring Cloud | Jangan pakai konsep Spring jika app Jersey non-Spring |
| Client lifecycle | Feign proxy dibuat saat startup, reused |
| Base URL | Config-driven |
| Timeout | Connect/read timeout eksplisit |
| Retry | Default safe; retry operation-aware |
| Encoder/decoder | ObjectMapper align dengan platform |
| ErrorDecoder | Non-2xx mapped deterministically |
| RequestInterceptor | Auth/correlation/tenant propagated |
| Logging | Metadata only, no token/PII |
| Transport | Underlying client/connection pool/TLS/proxy dipahami |
| Adapter | Feign tidak bocor ke application/domain |
| DTO mapper | Generated/Feign DTO mapped to domain |
| Error mapper | FeignException tidak bocor |
| Idempotency | Command retry pakai key |
| Tests | Decoder, mapper, gateway, timeout, headers |
| Generated client | Package/method/model stable |

---

## 35. Red Flags

| Red flag | Dampak |
|---|---|
| `@FeignClient` dipakai di Jersey non-Spring tanpa Spring Cloud | Tidak sesuai runtime |
| Feign client dibuat per request | Overhead dan config tidak konsisten |
| Retry global semua method | Duplicate command |
| Timeout tidak eksplisit | Request thread bisa tertahan |
| ErrorDecoder tidak ada | Non-2xx handling liar |
| ErrorDecoder parsing message text | Brittle |
| RequestInterceptor log token/body | Security risk |
| Generated DTO masuk domain/MyBatis/Camunda | Coupling tinggi |
| FeignException sampai use case | Infrastructure leak |
| ObjectMapper berbeda dari platform | Serialization bug |
| Unknown enum tidak dipikirkan | Runtime decode failure |
| Idempotency key auto-random tersembunyi | Retry semantics tidak jelas |
| Full Feign logging di production | Data leak/noise |
| Tidak tahu underlying HTTP client | Pooling/TLS/proxy issue tersembunyi |
| Spring Cloud docs dicopy ke OpenFeign core | Config tidak bekerja |

---

## 36. Ringkasan

OpenFeign cocok jika Anda ingin HTTP client terlihat seperti Java interface:

```text id="520cfa"
CustomerFeignApi.getCustomerById(customerId)
```

Tetapi di production, Feign tetap harus dikurung dalam boundary:

```text id="hgwuts"
Feign API / Generated Feign client
  -> ErrorDecoder
  -> RequestInterceptor
  -> Encoder/Decoder
  -> Timeout/Retry policy
  -> Adapter/Gateway
  -> Domain/Application interface
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **OpenFeign menyederhanakan syntax HTTP client, bukan menyederhanakan failure model. Timeout, retry, idempotency, auth propagation, error contract, observability, dan adapter boundary tetap wajib didesain eksplisit.**

---

**Seri 10/16 selesai.**
