## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:50:42 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 11/16**. Fokusnya adalah pola paling penting secara arsitektural: adapter/gateway sebagai pagar antara business logic dan detail HTTP client/generated code.

# Seri 11/16 - Client Adapter / Gateway Pattern

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada pola arsitektur yang paling penting dalam HTTP communication antar microservice:

> **Bagaimana membungkus generated Jersey Client, manual Jersey Client, atau OpenFeign client di balik internal adapter/gateway agar business logic tidak bergantung langsung pada detail HTTP, DTO eksternal, generated code, dan exception transport.**

Dalam contract-first architecture, OpenAPI membantu mendefinisikan API, generate client code, membuat test, dan menerapkan design standard. OpenAPI Initiative menjelaskan OpenAPI sebagai standar formal untuk mendeskripsikan HTTP APIs, termasuk untuk memahami API, generate client code, membuat tests, dan menerapkan design standards. ([OpenAPI Initiative](https://www.openapis.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Tetapi ada batas penting:

```text
OpenAPI / generated client
  membantu integrasi HTTP

Adapter / gateway
  menjaga architecture boundary
```

Generated client tidak otomatis menyelesaikan:

```text
domain mapping
error semantics
retry policy
idempotency
observability
fallback
security context
Camunda workflow semantics
MyBatis persistence boundary
```

---

## 2. Problem yang Diselesaikan Adapter / Gateway

Tanpa adapter:

```text
Application Service
  -> Generated CustomersApi
  -> CustomerResponse DTO
  -> ApiException / FeignException
  -> HTTP status code
  -> raw error body
```

Dengan adapter:

```text
Application Service
  -> CustomerGateway
  -> CustomerProfile / CustomerLookupResult
  -> domain/application exception
```

Perbandingan:

| Tanpa adapter | Dengan adapter |
|---|---|
| Use case tahu HTTP client | Use case tahu business capability |
| Generated DTO bocor | DTO eksternal dikurung |
| `ApiException` / `FeignException` bocor | Error dipetakan ke domain/application result |
| Retry tersebar | Retry dipusatkan per operation |
| Timeout tersebar | Timeout dipusatkan di policy/factory |
| Observability tidak konsisten | Metric/log/trace standar |
| Sulit ganti Jersey ↔ Feign | Ganti implementation adapter saja |
| MyBatis/Camunda bisa ikut tercemar DTO eksternal | Boundary tetap bersih |

---

## 3. Mental Model Adapter / Gateway

```text
Business Use Case
  |
  v
Internal Gateway Interface
  |
  v
HTTP Gateway Implementation
  |
  +--> generated Jersey client
  +--> manual Jersey client
  +--> OpenFeign client
  |
  v
External Microservice API
```

Contoh:

```text
SubmitOrderUseCase
  |
  v
CustomerGateway
  |
  v
CustomerHttpGateway
  |
  v
CustomersApi / Jersey Client / Feign API
  |
  v
Customer Service
```

Rule utama:

```text
Application layer speaks domain/application language.
Infrastructure layer speaks HTTP/client/generated language.
```

---

## 4. Nama Pattern: Adapter vs Gateway vs Client

Dalam praktik enterprise Java, istilahnya bisa berbeda.

| Istilah | Makna praktis |
|---|---|
| `Gateway` | Interface internal untuk capability external service |
| `HttpGateway` | Implementation gateway berbasis HTTP |
| `Adapter` | Boundary translation dari external ke internal |
| `Client` | Low-level HTTP/generated client |
| `Api` | Generated API class/interface dari OpenAPI |
| `Connector` | Kadang dipakai untuk integration boundary |
| `Port` | Dalam hexagonal architecture, interface dependency |
| `Outbound adapter` | Implementation dari outbound port |

Contoh naming yang jelas:

```text
CustomerGateway                // internal interface
CustomerHttpGateway            // HTTP implementation
CustomersApi                   // generated OpenAPI client
CustomerDtoMapper              // external DTO -> internal model
CustomerErrorMapper            // HTTP/provider error -> internal error/result
CustomerClientFactory          // configure generated/Jersey/Feign client
```

---

## 5. Struktur Package yang Direkomendasikan

```text
com.company.order
  ├── application
  │   └── SubmitOrderUseCase.java
  │
  ├── domain
  │   ├── Order.java
  │   ├── CustomerId.java
  │   └── CustomerProfile.java
  │
  └── infrastructure
      └── customer
          ├── CustomerGateway.java
          ├── CustomerHttpGateway.java
          ├── CustomerClientFactory.java
          ├── CustomerClientProperties.java
          ├── CustomerDtoMapper.java
          ├── CustomerErrorMapper.java
          └── generated
              ├── api
              ├── model
              └── invoker
```

Atau jika generated client dipublish sebagai dependency:

```text
com.company.customer.client.api
com.company.customer.client.model
com.company.customer.client.invoker

com.company.order.infrastructure.customer
  ├── CustomerGateway.java
  ├── CustomerHttpGateway.java
  └── CustomerDtoMapper.java
```

Poin penting:

```text
Generated package boleh berbeda module/dependency.
Adapter tetap milik consumer service.
```

---

## 6. Dependency Direction

Dependency yang sehat:

```text
application
  depends on:
    domain
    gateway interface

infrastructure
  depends on:
    application/domain abstractions
    generated client
    Jersey/Feign
    ObjectMapper
    resilience/observability library
```

Jangan:

```text
domain
  depends on generated client model

application
  depends on ApiException / FeignException

MyBatis mapper
  depends on OpenAPI DTO

Camunda process variable
  stores generated DTO
```

Dependency rule:

```text
Generated client is an implementation detail.
It must not become an architectural dependency of the core.
```

---

## 7. Gateway Interface Design

Gateway interface harus berbicara dalam bahasa use case.

Buruk:

```java
public interface CustomerGateway {
    CustomerResponse getCustomerById(String customerId) throws ApiException;
}
```

Masalah:

| Masalah | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| `CustomerResponse` generated DTO | External schema bocor |
| `String customerId` | Tidak pakai value object domain |
| `ApiException` | HTTP client detail bocor |
| Method name mengikuti endpoint | Belum tentu business intent |
| Tidak jelas not found behavior | Return null? exception? optional? |

Lebih baik:

```java
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

Atau jika butuh hasil lebih kaya:

```java
public interface CustomerGateway {
    CustomerLookupResult lookup(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

Dengan sealed result:

```java
public sealed interface CustomerLookupResult {
    record Found(CustomerProfile profile) implements CustomerLookupResult {}
    record NotFound(CustomerId customerId) implements CustomerLookupResult {}
    record Failed(String reason) implements CustomerLookupResult {}
}
```

---

## 8. Kapan Pakai `Optional`, Exception, atau Result Type?

| Situasi | Bentuk return yang cocok |
|---|---|
| Lookup by id, not found normal | `Optional<T>` |
| Command sukses/gagal business | Result type |
| Validation/business rejection expected | Result type atau domain exception |
| System/downstream failure | Exception |
| Authorization failure | Exception |
| Conflict yang harus diproses caller | Result type atau exception, konsisten |
| Workflow transition | Result type lebih eksplisit |
| Query list | Empty list/page, bukan exception |
| Contract violation provider | Exception |

Contoh:

```java
Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId);
```

Untuk command:

```java
CancelOrderResult cancelOrder(CancelOrderCommand command);
```

Result type:

```java
public sealed interface CancelOrderResult {
    record Cancelled(OrderId orderId) implements CancelOrderResult {}
    record NotFound(OrderId orderId) implements CancelOrderResult {}
    record AlreadyShipped(OrderId orderId) implements CancelOrderResult {}
    record Rejected(String reasonCode) implements CancelOrderResult {}
}
```

Rule:

```text
Expected business outcome boleh menjadi result.
Unexpected technical failure sebaiknya exception.
```

---

## 9. Adapter untuk Generated Jersey Client

Generated client dari OpenAPI Generator Java dapat dibuat dengan berbagai library, termasuk Jersey dan OpenFeign, serta memiliki opsi konfigurasi package, enum naming, dan behavior lain yang memengaruhi output Java. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh generated API:

```java
CustomersApi customersApi = new CustomersApi(apiClient);
CustomerResponse response = customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId);
```

Adapter:

```java
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomersApi customersApi;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper;
    private final CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    public CustomerHttpGateway(
            CustomersApi customersApi,
            CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper,
            CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper,
            DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy
    ) {
        this.customersApi = customersApi;
        this.dtoMapper = dtoMapper;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
        this.callPolicy = callPolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invokeFindById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(dtoMapper.toProfile(response));
        } catch (ApiException exception) {
            return errorMapper.mapFindByIdError(exception, customerId);
        }
    }
}
```

Application layer tidak tahu:

```text
CustomersApi
CustomerResponse
ApiClient
ApiException
HTTP status code
```

---

## 10. Adapter untuk Manual Jersey Client

Jakarta REST menyediakan Client API untuk mengakses REST resources dari aplikasi Java lain. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest-client/rest-client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Manual Jersey Client tetap harus dibungkus adapter.

```java
public final class CustomerJerseyGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final WebTarget customerTarget;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper;
    private final JerseyErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invokeFindById(CustomerId customerId) {
        Response response = customerTarget
                .path("/customers/{customerId}")
                .resolveTemplate("customerId", customerId.value())
                .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .get();

        try {
            if (response.getStatus() == 200) {
                CustomerResponse body =
                        response.readEntity(CustomerResponse.class);

                return Optional.of(dtoMapper.toProfile(body));
            }

            if (response.getStatus() == 404) {
                return Optional.empty();
            }

            throw errorMapper.map(response);
        } finally {
            response.close();
        }
    }
}
```

Manual Jersey Client memberi kontrol penuh, tapi risikonya boilerplate dan typo path/status/header. Karena itu, adapter + test wajib.

---

## 11. Adapter untuk OpenFeign

Feign memosisikan dirinya sebagai Java-to-HTTP client binder untuk mengurangi kompleksitas binding Java ke HTTP API. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openfeign/feign?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Tetapi Feign tetap infrastructure detail.

Feign API:

```java
public interface CustomerFeignApi {

    @RequestLine("GET /customers/{customerId}")
    @Headers("Accept: application/json")
    CustomerResponse getCustomerById(
            @Param("customerId") String customerId
    );
}
```

Gateway:

```java
public final class CustomerFeignGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomerFeignApi api;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper dtoMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallPolicy callPolicy;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invokeFindById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    api.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(dtoMapper.toProfile(response));
        } catch (CustomerNotFoundException ex) {
            return Optional.empty();
        } catch (DownstreamClientException ex) {
            throw ex;
        } catch (FeignException ex) {
            throw new DownstreamProtocolException(
                    "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_UNMAPPED_FEIGN_ERROR",
                    ex
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Feign API interface is not the gateway interface.
Feign API is transport binding.
Gateway is application boundary.
```

---

## 12. DTO Mapping Boundary

External DTO:

```java
CustomerResponse
```

Internal model:

```java
public record CustomerProfile(
        CustomerId customerId,
        String displayName,
        CustomerLifecycleStatus status
) {}
```

Mapper:

```java
public final class CustomerDtoMapper {

    public CustomerProfile toProfile(CustomerResponse response) {
        return new CustomerProfile(
                new CustomerId(required(response.getCustomerId(), "customerId")),
                required(response.getDisplayName(), "displayName"),
                mapStatus(response.getStatus())
        );
    }

    private CustomerLifecycleStatus mapStatus(CustomerStatus status) {
        if (status == null) {
            return CustomerLifecycleStatus.UNKNOWN;
        }

        return switch (status) {
            case ACTIVE -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.ACTIVE;
            case SUSPENDED -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.SUSPENDED;
            case CLOSED -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.CLOSED;
            default -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }

    private String required(String value, String field) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new DownstreamContractViolationException(
                    "CUSTOMER_RESPONSE_MISSING_" + field.toUpperCase()
            );
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

Mapping policy:

| External condition | Internal decision |
|---|---|
| Required field missing | Contract violation |
| Unknown enum | Map to `UNKNOWN` or fail fast |
| Optional field absent | Explicit `Optional`/nullable policy |
| Extra field | Ignore |
| Invalid date format | Contract violation |
| Malformed success body | Contract/protocol violation |

---

## 13. Error Mapping Boundary

External error:

```text
HTTP status
raw error body
ApiException / FeignException
ErrorResponse / ProblemDetail
```

Internal error:

```text
CustomerNotFound
DownstreamConflict
DownstreamBusinessValidation
DownstreamUnavailable
DownstreamAuthorizationFailure
DownstreamContractViolation
```

Error mapper example:

```java
public final class CustomerErrorMapper {

    private final DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder decoder;

    public Optional<CustomerProfile> mapFindByIdError(
            ApiException exception,
            CustomerId customerId
    ) {
        int status = exception.getCode();
        DecodedError error =
                decoder.decode(status, exception.getResponseBody());

        if (status == 404 &&
                "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND".equals(error.errorCode())) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        if (status == 401 || status == 403) {
            throw new DownstreamAuthorizationException(
                    "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_AUTH_FAILED",
                    error,
                    exception
            );
        }

        if (status == 409) {
            throw new DownstreamConflictException(error, exception);
        }

        if (status == 422) {
            throw new DownstreamBusinessValidationException(error, exception);
        }

        if (status == 429 || status >= 500) {
            throw new DownstreamUnavailableException(error, exception);
        }

        throw new DownstreamProtocolException(
                "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_UNEXPECTED_RESPONSE",
                error,
                exception
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
HTTP status + errorCode decide mapping.
Never parse human message for control flow.
```

---

## 14. Error Mapping Table

| HTTP / failure | Adapter decision |
|---|---|
| `200` valid body | Map DTO to internal model |
| `200` invalid body | Contract violation |
| `201` | Created result |
| `202` | Accepted/async result |
| `204` | Success no content |
| `400` | Caller bug or contract mismatch |
| `401` | Downstream authentication failure |
| `403` | Downstream authorization failure |
| `404` lookup | `Optional.empty()` or `NotFound` result |
| `404` command target | Domain not found result/exception |
| `409` | Conflict result/exception |
| `422` | Business validation result/exception |
| `429` | Rate limited / unavailable |
| `500` | Downstream system failure |
| `502/503/504` | Gateway/unavailable/timeout |
| Connect timeout | Transport failure |
| Read timeout | Timeout; retry only if operation-safe |
| Malformed error body | Protocol/contract violation |
| Unknown enum on success | Contract compatibility issue |

---

## 15. Result Type untuk Command

Command lebih baik tidak dipaksakan menjadi `Optional`.

Misal cancel order:

```java
public interface OrderCommandGateway {
    CancelOrderResult cancel(CancelOrderCommand command);
}
```

Result:

```java
public sealed interface CancelOrderResult {
    record Cancelled(OrderId orderId) implements CancelOrderResult {}
    record NotFound(OrderId orderId) implements CancelOrderResult {}
    record AlreadyCancelled(OrderId orderId) implements CancelOrderResult {}
    record AlreadyShipped(OrderId orderId) implements CancelOrderResult {}
    record Rejected(String reasonCode, String message) implements CancelOrderResult {}
}
```

Adapter mapping:

```java
private CancelOrderResult mapCancelError(
        int status,
        DecodedError error,
        OrderId orderId
) {
    if (status == 404 && "ORDER_NOT_FOUND".equals(error.errorCode())) {
        return new CancelOrderResult.NotFound(orderId);
    }

    if (status == 409 &&
            "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED".equals(error.errorCode())) {
        return new CancelOrderResult.AlreadyShipped(orderId);
    }

    if (status == 409 &&
            "ORDER_ALREADY_CANCELLED".equals(error.errorCode())) {
        return new CancelOrderResult.AlreadyCancelled(orderId);
    }

    if (status == 422) {
        return new CancelOrderResult.Rejected(
                error.errorCode(),
                error.message()
        );
    }

    throw new DownstreamUnavailableException(error);
}
```

Benefit:

| Benefit | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Business branch eksplisit | Use case bisa switch result |
| Tidak semua error jadi exception | Expected outcome lebih jelas |
| Workflow integration lebih mudah | Map ke BPMN business path |
| Test lebih mudah | Assert result type |
| Retry decision lebih aman | System failure tetap exception |

---

## 16. Adapter dan Retry Policy

Adapter adalah tempat paling cocok untuk memberi label operation:

```text
operation = getCustomerById
type = query
retrySafe = true

operation = createPayment
type = command
retrySafe = only if idempotency key exists
```

Contoh:

```java
return callPolicy.execute(
        DownstreamOperation.query(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById"
        ),
        () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
);
```

Untuk command:

```java
return callPolicy.execute(
        DownstreamOperation.idempotentCommand(
                "order-service",
                "cancelOrder",
                command.idempotencyKey()
        ),
        () -> invokeCancel(command)
);
```

Retry policy:

| Operation | Retry |
|---|---|
| Query | Bounded retry untuk timeout/503 |
| Read-only search | Bounded retry jika no side effect |
| Command tanpa idempotency | Jangan retry otomatis |
| Command dengan idempotency key | Bounded retry boleh |
| Workflow transition | Koordinasikan dengan Camunda retry |
| Callback receiver | Dedup by event id |
| `400/401/403/404/409/422` | Jangan retry |
| `429` | Retry hanya jika budget dan `Retry-After` masuk akal |
| `503/504` | Retry jika safe |

---

## 17. Adapter dan Timeout Policy

Timeout jangan tersebar di generated client call tanpa dokumentasi.

```java
DownstreamOperation operation = DownstreamOperation.query(
        "customer-service",
        "getCustomerById",
        Duration.ofMillis(500)
);
```

Atau config:

```text
customer.get-by-id.connect-timeout-ms=100
customer.get-by-id.read-timeout-ms=400
customer.search.read-timeout-ms=1000
payment.create.read-timeout-ms=2000
```

Rule:

```text
Timeout adalah bagian dari integration contract.
Adapter harus tahu operation budget.
```

Danger:

```text
HTTP client read timeout = 10s
Inbound API SLA = 2s

Result:
  caller sudah timeout,
  service masih menunggu downstream,
  thread pool terpakai,
  cascading latency.
```

---

## 18. Adapter dan Observability

Adapter harus emit metrics/log/traces dengan logical operation name, bukan sekadar raw URL.

Metric:

```text
downstream_http_client_requests_total{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById",
  result="success"
}

downstream_http_client_duration_ms{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById"
}
```

Structured log:

```text
level=WARN
event=DOWNSTREAM_HTTP_ERROR
downstream=customer-service
operation=getCustomerById
status=404
errorCode=CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND
latencyMs=42
correlationId=01J...
```

Why adapter?

| Di low-level client | Di adapter |
|---|---|
| Tahu URL/method | Tahu business operation |
| Tidak tahu domain result | Tahu `NotFound`, `Conflict`, `Rejected` |
| Sulit klasifikasi retry | Bisa tahu query/command |
| Logging generic | Logging meaningful |
| Sulit map SLA | Bisa per operation |

---

## 19. Adapter dan Security Context

Adapter/factory harus memastikan propagation standar.

| Context | Dari mana | Ke mana |
|---|---|---|
| Correlation ID | Inbound request / generated | `X-Correlation-Id` |
| Trace context | OpenTelemetry/request context | `traceparent` |
| Service token | Token provider | `Authorization` |
| User token | Security context | `Authorization`, jika propagation model |
| Tenant ID | Request/user context | `X-Tenant-Id` |
| Actor ID | Auth context | Audit header, jika allowed |
| Idempotency key | Command context | `Idempotency-Key` |

Jangan:

```java
submitOrderUseCase.setAuthorizationHeader(...)
```

Better:

```text
Application use case passes command.
Adapter/factory handles auth/correlation headers.
```

Kecuali idempotency key: karena ia punya semantics bisnis, sebaiknya terlihat pada command/result boundary.

---

## 20. Adapter dan Idempotency

Untuk command:

```java
public record CreatePaymentCommand(
        PaymentRequestId requestId,
        OrderId orderId,
        Money amount,
        IdempotencyKey idempotencyKey
) {}
```

Gateway:

```java
public interface PaymentGateway {
    CreatePaymentResult createPayment(CreatePaymentCommand command);
}
```

Adapter:

```java
private CreatePaymentResult invokeCreatePayment(
        CreatePaymentCommand command
) {
    CreatePaymentRequest request = mapper.toRequest(command);

    try {
        CreatePaymentResponse response = paymentApi.createPayment(
                command.idempotencyKey().value(),
                request
        );

        return mapper.toResult(response);
    } catch (ApiException ex) {
        return errorMapper.mapCreatePaymentError(ex, command);
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Idempotency key should be explicit for state-changing operations.
Do not hide command deduplication completely in HTTP utility code.
```

---

## 21. Adapter dan Fallback

Fallback harus domain-aware, bukan generic.

| Operation | Possible fallback | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Customer profile lookup | Cached snapshot | Jika stale data acceptable |
| Permission lookup | Fail closed | Security-sensitive |
| Pricing lookup | Usually fail | Salah harga berbahaya |
| Notification send | Queue/outbox | Async retry |
| Report metadata | Cached | Bisa degraded |
| Payment command | No blind fallback | Harus explicit failure |
| Workflow transition | BPMN retry/incident | Jangan pretend success |

Contoh:

```java
public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
    try {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    } catch (DownstreamUnavailableException ex) {
        return customerSnapshotCache.findById(customerId);
    }
}
```

Tapi dokumentasikan:

```text
Fallback returns possibly stale profile.
Not allowed for authorization decisions.
```

---

## 22. Adapter dan Circuit Breaker / Bulkhead

Adapter adalah tempat logical grouping dependency:

```text
customer-service:getCustomerById
customer-service:searchCustomers
payment-service:createPayment
inventory-service:reserveStock
```

Circuit breaker jangan cuma berdasarkan host.

| Circuit key | Cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `customer-service` | Kadang | Semua operation ikut open |
| `customer-service:getCustomerById` | Lebih granular | Bisa isolate endpoint |
| `payment-service:createPayment` | Cocok | Command kritikal |
| Raw URL | Kurang ideal | URL template/version bisa berubah |

Bulkhead:

```text
Limit concurrent calls to slow downstream
so it does not exhaust service thread pool.
```

Rule:

```text
Adapter knows dependency identity.
Use that identity for resilience and observability.
```

---

## 23. Adapter dan MyBatis Boundary

Jangan biarkan DTO eksternal masuk persistence.

Bad:

```java
customerSnapshotMapper.insert(customerResponse);
```

Better:

```text
CustomerResponse
  -> CustomerProfile
  -> CustomerSnapshotRecord
  -> MyBatis mapper
```

Example:

```java
public record CustomerSnapshotRecord(
        String customerId,
        String displayName,
        String lifecycleStatus
) {}
```

Mapper:

```java
public CustomerSnapshotRecord toSnapshotRecord(CustomerProfile profile) {
    return new CustomerSnapshotRecord(
            profile.customerId().value(),
            profile.displayName(),
            profile.status().name()
    );
}
```

Kenapa?

| Jika generated DTO masuk MyBatis | Risiko |
|---|---|
| API rename field | SQL mapping rusak |
| Generated class berubah | Persistence compile break |
| Nullability external | DB constraint violation |
| Enum external bertambah | DB invalid value |
| API version migration | Persistence ikut terdampak |

---

## 24. Adapter dan Camunda Boundary

Jangan simpan generated DTO sebagai process variable jangka panjang.

Bad:

```java
runtimeService.setVariable(
        processInstanceId,
        "customer",
        customerResponse
);
```

Better:

```java
runtimeService.setVariable(
        processInstanceId,
        "customerId",
        profile.customerId().value()
);

runtimeService.setVariable(
        processInstanceId,
        "customerStatus",
        profile.status().name()
);
```

Atau stable internal snapshot:

```java
public record CustomerProcessSnapshot(
        String customerId,
        String displayName,
        String status
) {}
```

Kenapa?

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Generated class version berubah | Process instance lama gagal deserialize |
| Package/class name berubah | Historic data sulit dibaca |
| Payload terlalu besar | Variable bloat |
| External schema berubah | Workflow state terdampak |
| Long-running process | API client version berubah saat process belum selesai |

Rule:

```text
Camunda variables should be stable process data,
not generated transport DTOs.
```

---

## 25. Adapter dan Domain Invariant

Adapter bukan hanya mapper mekanis. Adapter juga menjaga invariant minimal sebelum data masuk domain.

Contoh:

```java
private String required(String value, String field) {
    if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
        throw new DownstreamContractViolationException(
                "CUSTOMER_RESPONSE_MISSING_" + field.toUpperCase()
        );
    }
    return value;
}
```

External response valid secara JSON belum tentu valid secara domain.

| External response | Adapter action |
|---|---|
| Required field null | Contract violation |
| Negative amount | Contract violation |
| Unknown status | Map to unknown/fail based on use case |
| Missing id | Contract violation |
| Unsupported currency | Business/contract violation |
| Date in invalid format | Decode/contract violation |

Rule:

```text
Do not create invalid domain object from external data.
Fail or degrade explicitly.
```

---

## 26. Adapter dan API Version Migration

Misal provider punya v1 dan v2 client.

```text
CustomerGateway
  +--> CustomerV1HttpGateway
  +--> CustomerV2HttpGateway
```

Same interface:

```java
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

V1 implementation:

```java
public final class CustomerV1HttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {
    private final CustomerV1Api api;
}
```

V2 implementation:

```java
public final class CustomerV2HttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {
    private final CustomerV2Api api;
}
```

Config switch:

```text
customer.gateway.version=v2
```

Migration flow:

```text
1. Add v2 generated dependency
2. Implement v2 adapter
3. Keep internal gateway interface stable
4. Run contract tests
5. Shadow/canary if needed
6. Switch config
7. Remove v1 after deprecation window
```

Without adapter, v1/v2 migration spreads across use cases.

---

## 27. Adapter dan Testing Strategy

Test target adapter, not generated code internals.

### Unit test DTO mapper

```text
CustomerResponse valid -> CustomerProfile
missing customerId -> DownstreamContractViolationException
unknown status -> UNKNOWN / fail as policy
```

### Unit test error mapper

```text
404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND -> Optional.empty
403 ACCESS_DENIED -> DownstreamAuthorizationException
409 INVALID_STATE -> DownstreamConflictException
422 BUSINESS_VALIDATION_FAILED -> DownstreamBusinessValidationException
503 empty body -> DownstreamUnavailableException
HTML error body -> DownstreamProtocolException
```

### Mock HTTP test

```text
Mock server:
  GET /customers/CUST-001 returns 200 valid JSON

Assert:
  gateway returns CustomerProfile
  Authorization header present
  X-Correlation-Id present
  metric recorded
```

### Contract test

```text
Provider response matches OpenAPI.
Consumer expectations match OpenAPI examples/schema.
```

### Resilience test

```text
timeout -> classified timeout exception
503 -> retry if query
409 -> no retry
POST command without idempotency -> no retry
```

---

## 28. Adapter Test Example

```java
@Test
void shouldReturnEmptyWhenCustomerNotFound() {
    ApiException exception = new ApiException(
            404,
            Map.of(),
            """
            {
              "errorCode": "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND",
              "message": "Customer was not found.",
              "correlationId": "test-correlation"
            }
            """
    );

    Optional<CustomerProfile> result =
            errorMapper.mapFindByIdError(
                    exception,
                    new CustomerId("CUST-404")
            );

    assertThat(result).isEmpty();
}
```

Mapper test:

```java
@Test
void shouldRejectMissingCustomerIdFromProvider() {
    CustomerResponse response = new CustomerResponse()
            .displayName("Acme")
            .status(CustomerStatus.ACTIVE);

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> mapper.toProfile(response))
            .isInstanceOf(DownstreamContractViolationException.class);
}
```

---

## 29. Adapter as Anti-Corruption Layer

Dalam domain-driven architecture, adapter ini berfungsi sebagai anti-corruption layer.

```text
External service model:
  CustomerResponse.status = ACTIVE/SUSPENDED/CLOSED

Internal domain:
  CustomerLifecycleStatus = CAN_TRANSACT / BLOCKED / CLOSED / UNKNOWN
```

Mapping bukan selalu 1:1.

```java
private CustomerLifecycleStatus mapStatus(CustomerStatus status) {
    return switch (status) {
        case ACTIVE -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.CAN_TRANSACT;
        case SUSPENDED -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.BLOCKED;
        case CLOSED -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.CLOSED;
        default -> CustomerLifecycleStatus.UNKNOWN;
    };
}
```

Benefit:

| Benefit | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Domain language tetap bersih | Tidak ikut istilah provider |
| Perubahan provider bisa diserap | Mapping layer yang berubah |
| Business invariant lebih kuat | Domain tidak menerima data mentah |
| Testing lebih stabil | Mock gateway, bukan HTTP client |
| Migration lebih mudah | v1/v2 map ke model internal sama |

---

## 30. Adapter dan Configuration

Adapter/factory harus memakai config typed dan validated.

```java
public record CustomerClientProperties(
        String baseUrl,
        int connectTimeoutMs,
        int readTimeoutMs,
        int maxAttempts,
        boolean fallbackEnabled
) {
    public CustomerClientProperties {
        if (baseUrl == null || baseUrl.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("customer.baseUrl is required");
        }
        if (connectTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("connectTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
        if (readTimeoutMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("readTimeoutMs must be positive");
        }
        if (maxAttempts <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("maxAttempts must be positive");
        }
    }
}
```

Config anti-pattern:

```text
String baseUrl read directly in use case
magic timeout scattered in methods
retry count hardcoded near HTTP call
auth scope string repeated everywhere
```

Better:

```text
CustomerClientProperties
CustomerClientFactory
CustomerGateway
```

---

## 31. Adapter dan Dependency Injection

Dengan HK2/CDI/manual bootstrap, wiring harus eksplisit.

Pseudo:

```java
public final class CustomerModule {

    public CustomerGateway customerGateway() {
        CustomersApi api = customerClientFactory.create(properties);

        return new CustomerHttpGateway(
                api,
                customerDtoMapper(),
                customerErrorMapper(),
                downstreamCallPolicy()
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Construct HTTP clients at startup.
Validate config at startup.
Do not build client per request.
```

For testing:

```java
CustomerGateway fakeGateway = customerId ->
        Optional.of(new CustomerProfile(
                customerId,
                "Test Customer",
                CustomerLifecycleStatus.CAN_TRANSACT
        ));
```

Application service tests should mock/fake `CustomerGateway`, not Jersey/Feign.

---

## 32. Adapter and Contract-first Generated Client

Adapter should be the only place that changes when generated client changes.

| Contract change | Adapter impact |
|---|---|
| Add optional response field | Mapper may ignore |
| Rename field | Mapper compile failure, good signal |
| Add enum value | Mapper fallback test needed |
| Change errorCode | Error mapper test fails |
| Change status code | Error mapper test fails |
| Add required request field | Request mapper compile/test fails |
| Change generated library Jersey → Feign | Gateway implementation/factory changes |
| Change base path | Factory/config changes |

Good architecture makes changes localized:

```text
Generated client changed
  -> adapter compile/test fails
  -> fix mapper/error mapper/factory
  -> application/domain unaffected
```

Bad architecture spreads changes:

```text
Generated client changed
  -> use case compile failures
  -> MyBatis compile failures
  -> Camunda variable issues
  -> tests across app fail randomly
```

---

## 33. Adapter Design Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Interface | Uses domain/application language |
| Return type | `Optional`, result type, or exception chosen intentionally |
| DTO mapping | External DTO not exposed |
| Error mapping | HTTP status + errorCode mapped deterministically |
| Exception boundary | `ApiException`/`FeignException` not exposed |
| Timeout | Explicit and operation-aware |
| Retry | Safe only by operation semantics |
| Idempotency | Explicit for command |
| Auth | Token/header propagation centralized |
| Correlation | Correlation/trace propagated |
| Observability | Metrics/log/tracing around call |
| Fallback | Domain-aware and documented |
| Circuit breaker | Keyed by downstream/operation |
| Config | Typed and validated |
| MyBatis | No generated DTO in persistence |
| Camunda | No generated DTO as long-lived process variable |
| Testing | Mapper, error mapper, gateway, resilience covered |
| Migration | v1/v2 client hidden behind same interface where possible |

---

## 34. Red Flags

| Red flag | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Use case imports `CustomersApi` | HTTP client leaks into application |
| Use case imports `CustomerResponse` | External DTO leaks into business logic |
| Domain enum equals generated enum | Provider enum change breaks domain |
| MyBatis mapper accepts generated DTO | Persistence coupled to API contract |
| Camunda variable stores generated DTO | Long-running process fragile |
| `ApiException`/`FeignException` thrown upward | Infrastructure failure leaks |
| `catch Exception return null` | Failure hidden |
| All non-2xx same exception | Error semantics lost |
| Retry inside low-level client globally | Duplicate command risk |
| Timeout magic numbers in adapter method | Hard to govern |
| No contract violation handling | Invalid domain object may be created |
| Error mapper parses message text | Brittle |
| Adapter has business workflow logic too deep | Boundary bloated |
| Fallback silently returns stale data | Business correctness risk |
| No adapter tests | Generated client upgrade risky |

---

## 35. Ringkasan

Client Adapter / Gateway Pattern adalah pagar utama:

```text
Application / Domain
  sees:
    CustomerGateway
    CustomerProfile
    CustomerLookupResult
    domain/application exceptions

Infrastructure
  hides:
    Jersey Client
    OpenFeign
    generated OpenAPI client
    HTTP status
    raw error body
    token/header mechanics
    retry/timeout details
```

Bentuk sehat:

```text
Generated/Manual HTTP Client
  -> HTTP Gateway
  -> DTO Mapper
  -> Error Mapper
  -> Resilience/Observability Policy
  -> Gateway Interface
  -> Use Case
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Generated client mempercepat integrasi, tetapi adapter/gateway-lah yang membuat integrasi aman secara arsitektural, evolvable, testable, dan tahan terhadap failure produksi.**

## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:53:23 AM

# Seri 12/16 - Timeout, Retry, Idempotency, Circuit Breaker, Bulkhead

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **failure model produksi**:

> **Bagaimana mencegah satu downstream HTTP service yang lambat, error, atau tidak stabil menjatuhkan service lain melalui timeout buruk, retry liar, duplicate command, thread exhaustion, dan cascading failure.**

Dalam HTTP microservice, failure bukan kasus pinggiran. Failure adalah kondisi normal yang harus dimodelkan.

Resilience layer harus menjawab:

```text id="5plhjs"
Berapa lama kita boleh menunggu downstream?
Kapan boleh retry?
Kapan tidak boleh retry?
Apakah operasi idempotent?
Apakah retry bisa membuat duplicate command?
Kapan circuit harus open?
Berapa concurrency maksimum ke downstream?
Apa fallback yang aman?
Bagaimana interaksi retry HTTP dengan Camunda retry?
```

RFC 9110 menjelaskan bahwa HTTP adalah stateless application-level protocol dan mendefinisikan semantics method/status. RFC tersebut juga membedakan idempotent methods karena request dapat diulang otomatis bila communication failure terjadi sebelum client membaca response. ([rfc-editor.org](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9110/)) ([datatracker.ietf.org](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9110))

---

## 2. Mental Model: Remote Call Bukan Local Call

Local method call:

```text id="hprjzv"
result = service.calculate(input)
```

Remote HTTP call:

```text id="uk5eqz"
result = downstream.call(input)

can fail because:
  - DNS gagal
  - TCP connect timeout
  - TLS handshake gagal
  - service unavailable
  - pod not ready
  - gateway timeout
  - response lambat
  - response malformed
  - schema berubah
  - auth token expired
  - retry menggandakan command
  - circuit open
  - bulkhead penuh
```

Rule senior-level:

```text id="ne5btn"
Setiap outbound HTTP call harus punya:
  timeout
  retry decision
  idempotency decision
  error classification
  concurrency boundary
  observability
```

---

## 3. Big Picture Resilience Stack

```text id="kba8x2"
Application Use Case
  |
  v
Gateway / Adapter
  |
  v
Resilience Policy
  ├── Timeout / deadline
  ├── Retry with backoff + jitter
  ├── Circuit breaker
  ├── Bulkhead
  ├── Rate limiter, jika perlu
  ├── Fallback, jika aman
  └── Metrics / tracing / logging
  |
  v
Generated Jersey Client / OpenFeign / Manual Jersey Client
  |
  v
Network / Kubernetes / Gateway / Service Mesh
  |
  v
Downstream Service
```

Libraries seperti **Resilience4j** menyediakan building blocks seperti `CircuitBreaker`, `Retry`, `RateLimiter`, `Bulkhead`, `ThreadPoolBulkhead`, dan `TimeLimiter`. Dokumentasi Resilience4j menunjukkan komponen ini bisa dikonfigurasi dan juga bisa mendekorasi lambda/function call. ([resilience4j.readme.io](https://resilience4j.readme.io/docs/getting-started-3)) ([github.com](https://github.com/resilience4j/resilience4j))

---

## 4. Failure Taxonomy

| Failure | Contoh | Retry? | Catatan |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Connect timeout | Host tidak bisa dijangkau | Mungkin | Untuk query/idempotent call |
| Read timeout | Downstream lambat | Hati-hati | Command result bisa unknown |
| DNS failure | Service name salah/unavailable | Mungkin terbatas | Bisa platform issue |
| TLS failure | Truststore/cert problem | Tidak | Config/security issue |
| `400` | Invalid request | Tidak | Caller/contract bug |
| `401` | Token invalid/expired | Mungkin setelah refresh | Bukan retry buta |
| `403` | Forbidden | Tidak | Authorization failure |
| `404` | Resource not found | Tidak | Bisa normal outcome |
| `409` | Conflict/state mismatch | Tidak | Business/state issue |
| `422` | Business validation failed | Tidak | Request dipahami tapi ditolak |
| `429` | Rate limited | Mungkin | Honor `Retry-After`, budget-aware |
| `500` | Provider bug | Mungkin terbatas | Hanya safe operation |
| `502` | Gateway bad upstream | Mungkin | Infrastruktur/downstream |
| `503` | Service unavailable | Mungkin | Query/idempotent call |
| `504` | Gateway timeout | Hati-hati | Command mungkin sudah diproses |
| Malformed success body | Schema mismatch | Tidak | Contract violation |
| Circuit open | Dependency unhealthy | Tidak langsung | Fail fast |
| Bulkhead full | Too many concurrent calls | Tidak langsung | Backpressure |

---

## 5. Timeout: Batas Waktu Wajib

Timeout menjawab:

```text id="8fyfk6"
Berapa lama caller boleh menunggu downstream sebelum menyerah?
```

Tanpa timeout eksplisit, service bisa menggantung menunggu dependency. Pada Jersey, properti `CONNECT_TIMEOUT` dan `READ_TIMEOUT` bernilai milidetik, dan nilai `0` berarti infinity; default-nya infinity untuk properti tersebut. Ini alasan timeout eksplisit wajib di production. ([eclipse-ee4j.github.io](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/2.45/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/client/ClientProperties.html))

### Jenis timeout

| Timeout | Makna | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Connect timeout | Waktu membuka koneksi | 100 ms |
| Read timeout | Waktu menunggu response | 300-1000 ms |
| Request timeout | Waktu per attempt | 500 ms |
| Overall deadline | Batas total use case | 2 s |
| Workflow retry timeout | Waktu sebelum engine retry | Camunda-specific |
| Client-side time limiter | Batas eksekusi future/callable | Resilience policy |

---

## 6. Timeout Budgeting

Jangan set timeout downstream lebih besar dari SLA inbound.

```text id="ycyr1k"
Inbound request SLA internal: 2s

Use case:
  validate auth        100ms
  load local DB        300ms
  call customer svc    400ms
  call pricing svc     500ms
  save result          300ms
  buffer               400ms
```

Bad:

```text id="7zhyjn"
Inbound SLA: 2s
customer read timeout: 10s
payment read timeout: 10s
```

Good:

```text id="on1cx1"
Inbound SLA: 2s
customer read timeout: 400ms
payment read timeout: 800ms
overall use case deadline: 1800ms
```

Rule:

```text id="6xs1s4"
Downstream timeout must fit inside caller deadline.
Retry budget must also fit inside caller deadline.
```

---

## 7. Timeout Configuration Example

### Jersey Client

```java id="upxkl9"
ClientConfig config = new ClientConfig()
        .property(ClientProperties.CONNECT_TIMEOUT, 100)
        .property(ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT, 500);

Client client = ClientBuilder.newClient(config);
```

### Feign Core

```java id="73ybpl"
CustomerFeignApi api = Feign.builder()
        .options(new Request.Options(
                100, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                true
        ))
        .retryer(Retryer.NEVER_RETRY)
        .target(CustomerFeignApi.class, baseUrl);
```

Exact Feign API can differ by version, but the policy is stable:

```text id="o5bnrn"
Set connect timeout.
Set read timeout.
Do not rely on unknown defaults.
```

---

## 8. Timeout per Operation

| Operation | Example timeout | Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| Permission check | 200-300 ms | Inbound request often blocked by auth |
| Customer lookup | 300-500 ms | Query should be fast |
| Order create | 800-1500 ms | Command may touch DB/workflow |
| Payment authorization | 1-3 s | Domain-specific, but bounded |
| Report export start | 1-2 s | Should return job id, not finish export |
| File download | Separate streaming policy | Not normal JSON call |
| Camunda external service call | Align with worker lock/retry | Avoid duplicate work |

Jangan memakai satu timeout global untuk semua dependency tanpa evaluasi.

---

## 9. Retry: Bukan Obat Universal

Retry menjawab:

```text id="gyj0l7"
Jika attempt pertama gagal,
apakah aman mencoba lagi?
```

RFC 9110 menyebut idempotent methods dibedakan karena request dapat diulang otomatis bila komunikasi gagal sebelum client membaca response. ([datatracker.ietf.org](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9110)) Tetapi dalam microservice, **HTTP method saja tidak cukup**. Yang penting adalah business side effect.

### Retry-safe secara umum

| Operation | Retry policy |
|---|---|
| `GET /customers/{id}` | Bounded retry ok |
| `GET /orders?...` | Bounded retry ok |
| `POST /orders/search` read-only | Bounded retry ok jika benar no side effect |
| `PUT /resources/{id}` full replace | Bisa, jika idempotent benar |
| `DELETE /resources/{id}` | Bisa, jika delete idempotent |
| `POST /payments` | Tidak, kecuali idempotency key |
| `POST /orders/{id}/cancel` | Hanya jika idempotency/state semantics jelas |
| Workflow transition | Harus sinkron dengan workflow retry |

---

## 10. Retry Decision Matrix

| Failure | Query `GET` | Idempotent command | Non-idempotent command |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Connect timeout | Retry bounded | Retry bounded | Usually no |
| Read timeout | Retry bounded | Hati-hati | No |
| `429` | Maybe, with budget | Maybe | Usually no |
| `500` | Maybe | Maybe | No |
| `502` | Maybe | Maybe | No |
| `503` | Maybe | Maybe | No |
| `504` | Maybe | Hati-hati | No |
| `400` | No | No | No |
| `401` | Refresh then retry once maybe | Refresh then retry once maybe | Refresh then retry once maybe |
| `403` | No | No | No |
| `404` | No | No | No |
| `409` | No | No | No |
| `422` | No | No | No |

---

## 11. Retry with Backoff + Jitter

Bad retry:

```text id="81j9yq"
retry immediately 3 times
```

Saat dependency down, semua caller retry bersamaan → retry storm.

Better:

```text id="uo8h18"
attempt 1
wait 100ms + jitter
attempt 2
wait 200ms + jitter
attempt 3
fail
```

Rule:

```text id="06qt6i"
Retry must be:
  bounded
  delayed
  jittered
  operation-aware
  deadline-aware
```

Contoh policy:

```text id="uz4t0i"
customer lookup:
  maxAttempts=2
  wait=50-100ms jitter
  retryOn=connect timeout, 503, 504

payment create:
  maxAttempts=1 by default
  maxAttempts=2 only if idempotency key present
```

---

## 12. Retry Budget

Retry tidak boleh membuat request melebihi SLA.

Bad:

```text id="hxm1la"
read timeout = 1s
maxAttempts = 3
backoff = 500ms
total worst case > 4s
inbound SLA = 2s
```

Good:

```text id="pkso9a"
overall deadline = 1500ms
attempt timeout = 400ms
maxAttempts = 2
backoff = 100ms
remaining budget checked before retry
```

Pseudo:

```java id="xgwv31"
if (remainingDeadlineMs < nextAttemptTimeoutMs + backoffMs) {
    throw new DownstreamTimeoutBudgetExceededException(...);
}
```

---

## 13. Idempotency: Kunci Retry Command

Idempotency berarti request dapat diulang tanpa menggandakan efek bisnis.

### Problem

```text id="o89joz"
POST /payments
  -> provider creates payment
  -> network timeout before response reaches client
  -> client retries
  -> provider creates second payment
```

### Dengan idempotency key

```text id="vfpacx"
POST /payments
Idempotency-Key: PAYREQ-001

Attempt 1:
  creates payment PAY-123

Attempt 2 with same key:
  returns same result PAY-123
  or conflict if payload differs
```

Idempotency key harus masuk kontrak OpenAPI sebagai header parameter untuk command yang retryable.

```yaml id="vgpnzx"
parameters:
  - name: Idempotency-Key
    in: header
    required: true
    schema:
      type: string
      minLength: 16
      maxLength: 128
```

---

## 14. Provider-side Idempotency Store

Idempotency bukan hanya header. Provider harus menyimpan dan menegakkan semantics.

```text id="2hc7xu"
idempotency table:
  key
  request_hash
  status
  response_body
  created_at
  expires_at
```

Flow:

```text id="krh9qx"
Receive command with Idempotency-Key
  |
  v
Check key exists?
  ├── No:
  │     store key + request hash as IN_PROGRESS
  │     execute command
  │     store final result
  │     return result
  │
  └── Yes:
        same request hash?
          ├── Yes -> return stored result or current status
          └── No  -> 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT
```

Status:

| State | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `IN_PROGRESS` | First request still processing |
| `SUCCEEDED` | Return stored success |
| `FAILED_RETRYABLE` | Caller may retry |
| `FAILED_FINAL` | Return stored failure |
| `EXPIRED` | Key no longer available |

---

## 15. Idempotency Conflict

Jika key sama dipakai untuk payload berbeda:

```text id="mx4dtb"
Idempotency-Key: ABC
Payload hash: H1

Retry:
Idempotency-Key: ABC
Payload hash: H2
```

Response:

```text id="jk2n4t"
409 Conflict
```

Body:

```json id="yyz374"
{
  "errorCode": "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
  "message": "Idempotency key was reused with a different request payload.",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Rule:

```text id="cnr2kw"
Same idempotency key must mean same logical command.
Different payload with same key is a client bug or safety risk.
```

---

## 16. Circuit Breaker

Circuit breaker mencegah service terus memanggil dependency yang sedang rusak.

Mental model:

```text id="uzp1yz"
CLOSED
  normal calls allowed

OPEN
  calls fail fast

HALF_OPEN
  limited trial calls allowed
  success -> CLOSED
  failure -> OPEN
```

Resilience4j menyediakan `CircuitBreaker` sebagai salah satu modul fault tolerance dan dokumentasinya memperlihatkan penggunaan dekorator untuk membungkus lambda/function call. ([resilience4j.readme.io](https://resilience4j.readme.io/docs/getting-started)) 

Tujuan:

| Tujuan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Fail fast | Jangan habiskan thread menunggu dependency down |
| Reduce load | Jangan bombard service yang sakit |
| Give recovery window | Biarkan downstream pulih |
| Protect caller | Hindari cascading failure |
| Emit signal | Circuit open menjadi alert/metric |

---

## 17. Circuit Breaker Tidak Sama dengan Retry

| Pattern | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Timeout | Membatasi waktu tunggu |
| Retry | Mencoba ulang failure transient |
| Circuit breaker | Stop sementara saat failure rate tinggi |
| Bulkhead | Membatasi concurrency/resource |
| Rate limiter | Membatasi rate request |
| Fallback | Memberi hasil alternatif/degraded |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="l5pq8a"
Dependency down
  -> retry agresif
  -> semua thread penuh
  -> circuit tidak ada
  -> caller service ikut down
```

Better:

```text id="xu33e0"
Dependency down
  -> timeout cepat
  -> retry terbatas
  -> failure rate naik
  -> circuit open
  -> fail fast / fallback
  -> service utama tetap hidup
```

---

## 18. Circuit Breaker Configuration Concepts

| Config | Makna |
|---|---|
| Failure rate threshold | Persentase failure untuk open circuit |
| Slow call threshold | Durasi yang dianggap lambat |
| Slow call rate threshold | Persentase slow call |
| Sliding window | Jumlah call/periode pengamatan |
| Minimum number of calls | Sample minimal sebelum evaluasi |
| Wait duration in open state | Lama circuit open sebelum half-open |
| Permitted half-open calls | Jumlah trial calls |
| Record exception | Exception mana dihitung failure |
| Ignore exception | Exception mana tidak dihitung failure |

Yang harus dihitung failure:

```text id="8vrwo9"
timeout
connect error
503/504
malformed provider response
```

Yang sering tidak dihitung failure:

```text id="nhxvxh"
404 expected not found
409 business conflict
422 validation error
403 authorization denial
```

Kenapa? Karena `404/409/422` sering menunjukkan outcome bisnis, bukan dependency health failure.

---

## 19. Circuit Key Design

Circuit breaker harus diberi key yang tepat.

| Key | Dampak |
|---|---|
| `customer-service` | Semua operation ke customer kena circuit yang sama |
| `customer-service:getCustomerById` | Lebih granular |
| `customer-service:searchCustomers` | Search heavy tidak menjatuhkan lookup |
| raw URL | Kurang stabil dan terlalu teknis |
| operation group | Balance granularity dan simplicity |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="04oxr1"
Start:
  circuit per downstream service + critical operation group

For high-risk operations:
  separate circuit per operation
```

Contoh:

```text id="mzn7fq"
customer-service:lookup
customer-service:search
payment-service:command
document-service:download
```

---

## 20. Bulkhead

Bulkhead membatasi jumlah concurrent call ke dependency supaya satu dependency lambat tidak menghabiskan semua thread.

Analogi:

```text id="38wwci"
Kapal punya sekat.
Jika satu kompartemen bocor,
seluruh kapal tidak langsung tenggelam.
```

Resilience4j menyediakan `Bulkhead` dan `ThreadPoolBulkhead` sebagai komponen fault tolerance. ([resilience4j.readme.io](https://resilience4j.readme.io/docs/getting-started-3))

### Problem tanpa bulkhead

```text id="ghbnkb"
request threads = 200
customer-service slow
200 threads menunggu customer-service
order-service tidak bisa melayani endpoint lain
```

### Dengan bulkhead

```text id="qjusw5"
max concurrent calls to customer-service = 30

If 30 already busy:
  fail fast / fallback / reject
```

---

## 21. Bulkhead Types

| Type | Cara kerja | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Semaphore bulkhead | Membatasi concurrent call di thread caller | Synchronous HTTP call cepat |
| Thread pool bulkhead | Menjalankan call di thread pool terpisah | Blocking call berat/isolasi thread |
| Connection pool limit | Batas koneksi HTTP | Transport-level protection |
| Queue limit | Antrian terbatas | Background/batch, bukan request latency-sensitive |

Rule:

```text id="pizk5y"
Bulkhead size harus lebih kecil dari total worker/request thread.
Sisakan kapasitas untuk endpoint lain.
```

---

## 22. Bulkhead Sizing

Contoh:

```text id="s7svj1"
Service request thread pool: 200

Downstream limits:
  customer-service lookup: 40 concurrent
  payment-service command: 20 concurrent
  document-service download: 10 concurrent
```

Sizing factors:

| Factor | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Downstream capacity | Berapa QPS/concurrency aman? |
| Caller thread pool | Jangan habiskan semua thread |
| Latency | Semakin lambat, concurrency makin mahal |
| Criticality | Payment vs optional enrichment |
| Fallback availability | Bisa degrade? |
| SLO | Operation budget berapa? |

Little’s law intuition:

```text id="n7kcry"
concurrency ≈ throughput × latency
```

Jika 100 rps dan latency 500ms:

```text id="zqty54"
concurrency ≈ 100 × 0.5 = 50
```

---

## 23. Rate Limiter

Rate limiter membatasi jumlah request per periode.

Bulkhead membatasi concurrent in-flight calls.

| Pattern | Membatasi |
|---|---|
| Rate limiter | Request per second/minute |
| Bulkhead | Request concurrent |
| Circuit breaker | Calls saat dependency unhealthy |
| Timeout | Waktu tunggu |
| Retry | Attempts |

Rate limiter cocok jika:

```text id="36ac9d"
downstream punya quota
external API rate limit
batch job bisa overwhelm service
cost-sensitive endpoint
```

Untuk internal microservice, bulkhead + circuit breaker sering lebih prioritas daripada rate limiter, kecuali ada limit eksplisit.

---

## 24. Fallback

Fallback menjawab:

```text id="kp04uq"
Jika downstream gagal,
apakah ada hasil alternatif yang aman?
```

Fallback tidak selalu boleh.

| Operation | Fallback aman? | Contoh |
|---|---:|---|
| Customer display info | Mungkin | Cached snapshot |
| Permission check | Biasanya tidak | Fail closed |
| Pricing | Biasanya tidak | Salah harga berbahaya |
| Payment | Tidak | Jangan fake success |
| Notification | Bisa | Queue/outbox |
| Recommendation | Bisa | Default recommendations |
| Regulatory decision | Hati-hati | Audit/defensibility penting |
| Report metadata | Bisa | Last known snapshot |

Rule:

```text id="n0mcqg"
Fallback must be domain-approved.
Never fallback to success for command unless explicitly designed.
```

---

## 25. Fallback Example

```java id="9md7vr"
public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
    try {
        return callPolicy.execute(
                DownstreamOperation.query(
                        "customer-service",
                        "getCustomerById"
                ),
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    } catch (DownstreamUnavailableException ex) {
        return customerSnapshotCache.findById(customerId);
    }
}
```

Document:

```text id="xn39cc"
Fallback:
  source = customer snapshot cache
  freshness = up to 24h
  allowed for display only
  not allowed for authorization, payment, regulatory decision
```

---

## 26. Policy Composition Order

Urutan composition penting.

Common order:

```text id="qtdkt3"
Bulkhead
  -> CircuitBreaker
    -> TimeLimiter/Timeout
      -> Retry
        -> HTTP call
```

Atau retry di dalam circuit?

```text id="lgnz1k"
CircuitBreaker around final outcome:
  one logical call including retries counts as one result

CircuitBreaker around each attempt:
  each failed retry increments failure count
```

Keduanya bisa valid, tapi harus sadar efeknya.

Rekomendasi praktis awal:

```text id="gt9m57"
For most service-to-service calls:
  bulkhead outside
  circuit breaker around logical operation
  retry inside logical operation
  timeout per attempt
  overall deadline outside all
```

Mental model:

```text id="0krnqr"
overall deadline
  bulkhead acquire
    circuit allow?
      retry loop
        per-attempt timeout
          HTTP call
```

---

## 27. Example Policy Skeleton

```java id="qtye0u"
public final class DownstreamCallPolicy {

    public <T> T execute(
            DownstreamOperation operation,
            Supplier<T> supplier
    ) {
        long start = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            T result = executeWithResilience(operation, supplier);
            recordSuccess(operation, start);
            return result;
        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
            recordFailure(operation, start, ex);
            throw ex;
        }
    }

    private <T> T executeWithResilience(
            DownstreamOperation operation,
            Supplier<T> supplier
    ) {
        // Conceptual order:
        // 1. check bulkhead
        // 2. check circuit breaker
        // 3. execute retry loop if operation retry-safe
        // 4. apply timeout/deadline
        // 5. classify exceptions
        return supplier.get();
    }
}
```

Operation metadata:

```java id="1tfcf9"
public record DownstreamOperation(
        String downstream,
        String operation,
        OperationType type,
        boolean retrySafe,
        Duration timeout,
        int maxAttempts
) {
    public static DownstreamOperation query(
            String downstream,
            String operation
    ) {
        return new DownstreamOperation(
                downstream,
                operation,
                OperationType.QUERY,
                true,
                Duration.ofMillis(500),
                2
        );
    }

    public static DownstreamOperation command(
            String downstream,
            String operation
    ) {
        return new DownstreamOperation(
                downstream,
                operation,
                OperationType.COMMAND,
                false,
                Duration.ofMillis(1000),
                1
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 28. Resilience4j Conceptual Example

Resilience4j docs show fault-tolerance primitives that decorate lambdas/functions, including retry and circuit breaker examples. ([resilience4j.readme.io](https://resilience4j.readme.io/docs/getting-started))

Conceptual usage:

```java id="8dhvbd"
Supplier<CustomerResponse> supplier =
        () -> customersApi.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

Supplier<CustomerResponse> decorated =
        Decorators.ofSupplier(supplier)
                .withBulkhead(customerBulkhead)
                .withCircuitBreaker(customerCircuitBreaker)
                .withRetry(customerRetry)
                .decorate();

CustomerResponse response = decorated.get();
```

Important:

```text id="43ni1f"
Jangan langsung copy global retry/circuit config.
Set policy berdasarkan:
  operation type,
  idempotency,
  timeout budget,
  downstream SLO,
  fallback safety.
```

---

## 29. Retry + Circuit Breaker Interaction

Bad interaction:

```text id="c03dtw"
maxAttempts=3
circuit breaker counts every failed attempt
1 logical user request = 3 failures
circuit opens too quickly
```

Alternative:

```text id="x95vu0"
Retry inside logical operation
Circuit breaker sees final result
1 logical user request = 1 success/failure
```

But sometimes counting each attempt is useful to detect real downstream pain faster.

Rule:

```text id="qukpli"
Decide intentionally:
  circuit counts attempts?
  or circuit counts logical operations?
```

For most business APIs, logical operation counting is easier to reason about.

---

## 30. Retry Storm

Retry storm terjadi ketika banyak service retry pada saat dependency sedang down.

```text id="i2b1a3"
1000 original requests
maxAttempts=3
= up to 3000 downstream attempts
while dependency is already unhealthy
```

Mitigation:

| Control | How |
|---|---|
| Circuit breaker | Stop calls temporarily |
| Backoff + jitter | Avoid synchronized retry |
| Retry budget | Limit retries |
| Bulkhead | Limit concurrent pressure |
| Rate limiter | Cap request rate |
| Overall deadline | Stop useless work |
| Queue/backpressure | For background tasks |
| Fallback | Reduce dependency need |

---

## 31. Timeout + Retry + Idempotency Example

### Query

```text id="dugbds"
GET /customers/{id}
```

Policy:

```text id="bq53uf"
connectTimeout=100ms
readTimeout=300ms
maxAttempts=2
backoff=50ms+jitter
retryOn=connectTimeout, readTimeout, 503, 504
```

### Command without idempotency

```text id="sq92di"
POST /payments
```

Policy:

```text id="6gs039"
connectTimeout=100ms
readTimeout=1000ms
maxAttempts=1
retryOn=none
```

### Command with idempotency

```text id="pg6ebp"
POST /payments
Idempotency-Key: PAYREQ-001
```

Policy:

```text id="5xus1s"
connectTimeout=100ms
readTimeout=1000ms
maxAttempts=2
retryOn=connectTimeout, 503
carefulWith=readTimeout, 504
```

Read timeout/504 are tricky because provider may have processed the command. Idempotency key makes retry safer.

---

## 32. Camunda / Workflow Retry Interaction

Dalam Camunda 7 external task, failure reporting includes retries and retry timeout; Camunda forum discussion quoting documentation notes that the process engine does not automatically decrement retries and worker code typically reports failure with `task.getRetries() - 1`. ([forum.camunda.io](https://forum.camunda.io/t/external-task-retry-java/13799))

Untuk workflow:

```text id="mk3r3d"
HTTP client retry
  × workflow engine retry
  = multiplicative attempts
```

Example bad:

```text id="xld4ya"
Feign retry: 3 attempts
Camunda external task retries: 5
Total possible downstream attempts: 15
```

Better:

```text id="tjjsr6"
For workflow command:
  HTTP maxAttempts=1 or very limited
  Camunda controls retry schedule
  command has idempotency key / business key
  business errors become BPMN errors
  technical errors become retryable failures/incidents
```

Mapping:

| Downstream result | Workflow handling |
|---|---|
| `200/204` | Complete task |
| `202` | Store job id, wait/poll/callback |
| `400` | Incident / modeling bug |
| `404` expected | BPMN business error/branch |
| `409` invalid state | BPMN business error |
| `422` business validation | BPMN business error |
| `503/504` | Retryable technical failure |
| Timeout | Retryable only if idempotency safe |
| Unknown result after command | Query status before retry if possible |

---

## 33. Unknown Outcome Problem

The hardest case:

```text id="v6ufjs"
Client sends command.
Provider processes command.
Response lost / timeout.
Client does not know outcome.
```

This is not simply failure. It is **unknown outcome**.

Example:

```text id="pnrhmm"
POST /orders/{id}/submit
  -> timeout
```

Possible truth:

```text id="8to6pt"
A. provider never received request
B. provider received but failed before commit
C. provider committed submission but response lost
D. provider still processing
```

Strategies:

| Strategy | Description |
|---|---|
| Idempotency key | Retry same command safely |
| Command id | Track command outcome |
| Status query | `GET /commands/{commandId}` or resource status |
| Outbox/event | Observe eventual result |
| Workflow compensation | Model uncertainty |
| Avoid long sync command | Return `202 Accepted` and job id |

Rule:

```text id="g87135"
For important commands, model unknown outcome explicitly.
Do not blindly retry or blindly fail.
```

---

## 34. Asynchronous Command Alternative

Instead of long synchronous command:

```text id="u38il0"
POST /reports/export
```

Return:

```text id="1j36tw"
202 Accepted
Location: /reports/export-jobs/JOB-001
```

Then:

```text id="n28vlw"
GET /reports/export-jobs/JOB-001
```

This reduces read timeout pressure and makes retry safer.

Use async when:

| Condition | Why |
|---|---|
| Operation may exceed request budget | Avoid long-held HTTP thread |
| Requires multiple downstream systems | Better orchestration |
| Needs progress/status | Job resource |
| Failure needs retry/compensation | Workflow/job semantics |
| Result large | Download later |

---

## 35. Observability for Resilience

Metrics per downstream operation:

```text id="drwfre"
downstream_http_duration_ms
downstream_http_requests_total
downstream_http_errors_total
downstream_http_timeouts_total
downstream_http_retries_total
downstream_circuit_state
downstream_bulkhead_available_concurrent_calls
downstream_bulkhead_rejected_total
downstream_fallback_used_total
```

Log fields:

| Field | Example |
|---|---|
| `downstream` | `customer-service` |
| `operation` | `getCustomerById` |
| `attempt` | `2` |
| `maxAttempts` | `2` |
| `status` | `503` |
| `errorCode` | `DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE` |
| `latencyMs` | `382` |
| `timeoutMs` | `500` |
| `circuitState` | `OPEN` |
| `bulkheadRejected` | `true` |
| `idempotencyKey` | Hash only, not raw if sensitive |
| `correlationId` | `01J...` |

Alert examples:

```text id="9jrnpz"
circuit open for payment-service:createPayment
timeout rate > 5% for customer-service lookup
bulkhead rejection > threshold
retry attempts spike
fallback usage spike
```

---

## 36. Classification for Metrics

Do not count all failures the same.

| Classification | Examples |
|---|---|
| `success` | `2xx` |
| `business_not_found` | `404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND` |
| `business_conflict` | `409 INVALID_STATE` |
| `validation_error` | `422` |
| `auth_error` | `401/403` |
| `client_contract_error` | `400` from bad caller |
| `downstream_unavailable` | `503` |
| `downstream_timeout` | read/connect timeout, `504` |
| `downstream_bug` | `500` |
| `protocol_error` | malformed body, HTML error |
| `circuit_open` | fail fast |
| `bulkhead_rejected` | concurrency limit |

Circuit breaker failure calculation should usually exclude expected business outcomes like `404`, `409`, `422`.

---

## 37. Kubernetes / Cloud Considerations

Resilience settings must fit deployment reality.

| Platform issue | Resilience implication |
|---|---|
| Pod rolling restart | Short transient `503` possible |
| Readiness probe delay | Retry/circuit should handle small windows |
| DNS change | Connection reuse/pool behavior matters |
| Service mesh mTLS | TLS failures are not retryable business errors |
| Ingress timeout | Must align with app read timeout |
| HPA scaling | Downstream capacity changes |
| NetworkPolicy | Connection failure may be config issue |
| Cloud LB idle timeout | Long calls may reset |
| On-prem proxy | Proxy timeout may differ from app timeout |

Rule:

```text id="8ur03m"
App timeout < gateway/ingress timeout < caller SLA
```

Avoid:

```text id="ie1tze"
App waits 60s
Ingress times out at 30s
Caller receives 504
App still doing work
```

---

## 38. Configuration Example

```yaml id="w4amav"
downstream:
  customer-service:
    baseUrl: http://customer-service.default.svc.cluster.local
    operations:
      getCustomerById:
        type: query
        connectTimeoutMs: 100
        readTimeoutMs: 400
        maxAttempts: 2
        retryBackoffMs: 50
        circuitBreaker:
          failureRateThreshold: 50
          slowCallDurationMs: 300
          minimumNumberOfCalls: 20
        bulkhead:
          maxConcurrentCalls: 40

      searchCustomers:
        type: query
        connectTimeoutMs: 100
        readTimeoutMs: 1000
        maxAttempts: 1
        bulkhead:
          maxConcurrentCalls: 15

  payment-service:
    operations:
      createPayment:
        type: command
        connectTimeoutMs: 100
        readTimeoutMs: 1500
        maxAttempts: 1
        requiresIdempotencyKey: true
        circuitBreaker:
          failureRateThreshold: 30
        bulkhead:
          maxConcurrentCalls: 20
```

---

## 39. Testing Resilience

### Timeout test

```text id="lhi25d"
Given downstream delays > read timeout
When gateway calls downstream
Then timeout exception classified
And metric downstream_timeout increments
```

### Retry test

```text id="z3s53m"
Given first GET returns 503
And second GET returns 200
When query gateway called
Then result success
And attempts=2
```

### No retry for command

```text id="44hdvb"
Given POST command returns 503
When command has no idempotency key
Then only one attempt
And DownstreamUnavailable thrown
```

### Idempotent retry test

```text id="5nq6np"
Given POST command with Idempotency-Key
And first attempt connect timeout
And second returns 201
Then result created
And same idempotency key used both attempts
```

### Circuit breaker test

```text id="eerzsx"
Given repeated 503 responses
When failure threshold exceeded
Then circuit opens
And subsequent call fails fast
```

### Bulkhead test

```text id="gmgqdx"
Given maxConcurrentCalls=2
When 3 calls are in-flight
Then third call is rejected
And bulkhead metric increments
```

---

## 40. Common Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Impact |
|---|---|
| No timeout | Thread exhaustion |
| Timeout larger than caller SLA | Wasted work/cascading latency |
| Retry all errors | Duplicate command, retry storm |
| Retry POST without idempotency | Duplicate side effects |
| Circuit breaker counts `404` as failure | Circuit opens for normal business misses |
| No bulkhead | One dependency consumes all threads |
| Fallback to success | Data corruption/business false positive |
| Full retry in HTTP client + Camunda retry | Multiplicative attempts |
| Idempotency key generated invisibly | Caller cannot reason about command |
| No observability on retries | Hidden latency amplification |
| Global policy for all downstreams | Misaligned with operation semantics |
| Ignoring unknown outcome | Payment/order/workflow inconsistency |
| Trusting default library retry | Behavior unknown |
| Retry-after ignored | Rate limit worsens |
| Bulkhead too large | No real protection |
| Circuit breaker too sensitive | Flapping |
| Circuit breaker too slow | Cascading failure before open |

---

## 41. Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Timeout | Connect/read/overall deadline explicit |
| Retry | Per operation, bounded, backoff+jitter |
| Idempotency | Command retry requires key/dedup |
| Unknown outcome | Modeled for important commands |
| Circuit breaker | Configured per dependency/operation group |
| Bulkhead | Concurrent call limit defined |
| Rate limiter | Used if downstream quota/rate constraint exists |
| Fallback | Domain-approved, not fake success |
| Error classification | `4xx` business vs `5xx` system separated |
| Metrics | Timeout/retry/circuit/bulkhead visible |
| Logging | Attempt/status/errorCode/correlation present |
| Camunda | HTTP retry aligned with workflow retry |
| Kubernetes/gateway | Timeout alignment checked |
| Testing | Timeout/retry/no-retry/circuit/bulkhead tested |
| Config | Typed, validated, environment-driven |
| Adapter | Policy applied at gateway boundary |

---

## 42. Ringkasan

Production-safe HTTP communication membutuhkan:

```text id="xib8ko"
Timeout
  -> batas waktu

Retry
  -> hanya untuk failure transient dan operation-safe

Idempotency
  -> syarat retry untuk command

Circuit breaker
  -> stop sementara saat dependency unhealthy

Bulkhead
  -> batasi concurrent calls agar service tetap hidup

Fallback
  -> hanya jika domain-approved

Observability
  -> semua policy terlihat di metrics/log/trace
```

Rule paling penting:

```text id="hqlp89"
Query boleh retry terbatas.
Command hanya boleh retry jika idempotency dan unknown outcome sudah dimodelkan.
Workflow retry harus diselaraskan dengan HTTP retry.
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Resilience bukan menambahkan retry. Resilience adalah membatasi kerusakan: waktu tunggu dibatasi, percobaan ulang dikontrol, efek samping dicegah, dependency rusak diisolasi, dan semua keputusan terlihat di observability.**

---
