## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:57:12 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 13/16**. Fokusnya security context untuk outbound HTTP: service token, user token propagation, authorization boundary, mTLS, header governance, dan cara implementasinya di Jersey Client/OpenFeign/generated client.

# Seri 13/16 - Authentication, Authorization, Token Propagation, mTLS

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada:

> **Bagaimana membawa security context antar microservice melalui Jersey Client, OpenFeign, atau generated OpenAPI client tanpa membocorkan token, tanpa mencampur authorization logic ke business layer, dan tetap cocok untuk audit/regulatory systems.**

Dalam service-to-service HTTP, security bukan hanya “tambahkan header `Authorization`”. Kita perlu menjawab:

```text id="g2ru0s"
Siapa caller-nya?
Apakah caller adalah service, user, atau service atas nama user?
Token apa yang dikirim?
Scope/permission apa yang dicek?
Apakah tenant context dibawa?
Apakah mTLS dipakai?
Apakah token boleh dipropagasikan?
Apakah downstream boleh percaya header user?
Bagaimana audit trail dibentuk?
Bagaimana error 401/403 dimapping?
```

OAuth 2.0 sendiri adalah framework authorization yang memungkinkan aplikasi memperoleh limited access ke HTTP service, baik atas nama resource owner maupun atas nama aplikasi itu sendiri. Bearer token usage didefinisikan di RFC 6750, dan bearer token berarti pihak yang memegang token dapat menggunakannya untuk akses sehingga token harus dilindungi dari disclosure. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6749?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Security Context

```text id="2u1ctx"
Inbound Request
  |
  +--> User identity
  +--> Service identity
  +--> Tenant context
  +--> Permission/scope
  +--> Correlation/trace context
  |
  v
Application Use Case
  |
  v
Gateway / Adapter
  |
  v
Outbound Security Policy
  ├── service token
  ├── user token propagation
  ├── token exchange / delegated token
  ├── mTLS
  ├── tenant/user headers
  └── audit metadata
  |
  v
Downstream Service
```

Rule utama:

```text id="yxcakw"
Authentication answers:
  who is calling?

Authorization answers:
  is this caller allowed to do this action on this resource?

Token propagation answers:
  which identity/context should be carried to downstream?
```

---

## 3. Security Bukan Hanya Token

| Layer | Concern | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Transport security | Channel aman | TLS, mTLS |
| Service authentication | Service caller valid | mTLS cert, client credentials token |
| User authentication | User asli valid | JWT/access token |
| Delegation | Service bertindak atas nama user | token exchange/delegated token |
| Authorization | Permission/scope/resource access | `order:read`, ACL, ABAC |
| Tenant isolation | Data tenant tidak bocor | `tenantId`, claims |
| Audit | Siapa melakukan apa | actor, service, correlation ID |
| Observability | Debugging aman | no token in logs |
| Secret management | Token/cert/keys aman | Vault/K8s Secret/KMS |

---

## 4. OpenAPI Security Contract

OpenAPI dapat mendeskripsikan security scheme seperti API keys, HTTP authentication, mutual TLS, OAuth 2.0, dan OpenID Connect; security requirement dapat didefinisikan global atau per operation. ([OpenAPI Documentation](https://learn.openapis.org/specification/security.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh bearer JWT:

```yaml id="55kq83"
components:
  securitySchemes:
    bearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT

security:
  - bearerAuth: []
```

Per operation:

```yaml id="8tt6h7"
paths:
  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      operationId: getOrderById
      security:
        - bearerAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Order found
```

Public/unauthenticated endpoint:

```yaml id="prlbq6"
paths:
  /health:
    get:
      operationId: healthCheck
      security: []
      responses:
        '200':
          description: OK
```

Rule:

```text id="xzuzqo"
Security requirement harus machine-readable di OpenAPI.
Jangan hanya ditulis di description bebas.
```

---

## 5. Bearer Token Model

Bearer token biasanya dikirim sebagai:

```text id="83yyiq"
Authorization: Bearer <access-token>
```

RFC 6750 mendefinisikan bearer token untuk HTTP requests ke protected resources; karena siapa pun yang memiliki bearer token dapat menggunakannya, token perlu dilindungi dari disclosure dalam storage maupun transport. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Implikasi praktis:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Jangan log token | Token bisa dipakai ulang |
| Jangan simpan token di process variable/log/error | Leak risk |
| Pakai TLS | Bearer token harus dilindungi in transit |
| Token short-lived | Kurangi blast radius |
| Validasi audience/issuer/scope | Jangan terima token salah target |
| Jangan forward token sembarangan | Downstream audience/scope bisa beda |
| Jangan percaya header user tanpa auth | Header bisa dipalsukan jika tidak dilindungi |

---

## 6. Service Token vs User Token

### Service token

Service A memanggil Service B sebagai service.

```text id="km16y1"
order-service
  -> customer-service

identity:
  service = order-service
```

Biasanya cocok untuk:

| Use case | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Internal lookup | order-service membaca customer status |
| Backend batch | reconciliation job |
| System automation | scheduled process |
| Service-owned command | inventory reservation by order-service |

### User token propagation

Service A memanggil Service B atas nama user.

```text id="rlmhzo"
user Alice
  -> order-service
    -> customer-service

identity:
  user = Alice
  caller service = order-service
```

Cocok untuk:

| Use case | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Downstream harus enforce user permission | Customer API memeriksa Alice boleh lihat customer X |
| Audit end-user wajib | Regulatory action |
| Fine-grained authorization di downstream | ACL/resource ownership |
| Multi-tenant access | Tenant claim user perlu dibawa |

### Token exchange / delegated token

Service A tidak forward token asli, tetapi menukar menjadi token downstream-specific.

```text id="w0h9q6"
inbound user token
  -> token exchange
  -> token for customer-service audience
```

Cocok jika:

| Problem | Token exchange membantu |
|---|---|
| Audience token berbeda | Token khusus downstream |
| Scope perlu dipersempit | Least privilege |
| Tidak ingin forward raw user token | Better containment |
| Downstream perlu delegated identity | Audit lebih jelas |

---

## 7. Decision Matrix: Token Mana yang Dikirim?

| Scenario | Recommended identity model |
|---|---|
| Service internal reads config/cache | Service token |
| User reads own resource and downstream enforces ownership | User token or delegated user token |
| Service performs backend workflow step | Service token + process/audit context |
| Service performs action initiated by user | Delegated token or service token + actor context |
| Batch process without user | Service token |
| Regulatory action needs user audit | Delegated user context |
| Downstream only trusts service identity | Service token |
| Downstream needs both service and user | Service token + signed/user claims or delegated token |
| Cross-tenant access | Token with tenant claim and strict validation |

Rule:

```text id="6jv3mu"
Do not blindly forward inbound Authorization header.
Choose propagation model intentionally per operation.
```

---

## 8. Authentication vs Authorization Boundary

Authentication:

```text id="06c268"
Token valid?
Issuer trusted?
Signature valid?
Expired?
Audience correct?
Client/service identity known?
```

Authorization:

```text id="bdd2fd"
Can this caller perform this action?
On this resource?
In this tenant?
With this role/scope/permission?
At this workflow state?
```

OpenAPI security scheme bisa mendeskripsikan authentication mechanism, tetapi object-level authorization tetap perlu di-implement di service karena akses ke resource tertentu sering bergantung pada ownership, ACL, tenant, state, atau policy bisnis.

Contoh:

```text id="f8nuky"
GET /customers/{customerId}
```

Token valid saja belum cukup. Provider harus mengecek:

```text id="7i99ij"
caller has customer:read?
caller belongs to same tenant?
caller can access this specific customerId?
resource not masked by policy?
```

---

## 9. Scopes, Roles, Permissions, ACL

| Model | Contoh | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| Scope | `customer:read`, `order:write` | API-level capability |
| Role | `ADMIN`, `CASE_MANAGER` | Coarse-grained business role |
| Permission | `ORDER_CANCEL`, `CASE_ASSIGN` | Fine-grained action |
| ACL | user/service X can access resource Y | Object-level access |
| ABAC | tenant, region, risk, status, owner | Policy berbasis atribut |
| Entitlement | product/module access | Enterprise product capability |

Recommended layering:

```text id="w7oyhl"
Token claims:
  identity + coarse scope

Provider authorization:
  resource-level check + policy

Domain/application:
  business state authorization
```

Jangan masukkan semua authorization logic ke API gateway saja. Gateway tidak selalu tahu resource ownership, workflow state, atau domain-specific constraints.

---

## 10. Header Governance

Headers umum untuk service-to-service:

| Header | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| `Authorization` | Bearer/service/delegated token |
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Debugging cross-service |
| `traceparent` | Distributed tracing |
| `X-Tenant-Id` | Tenant context, jika tidak cukup dari token |
| `X-User-Id` | Audit context, jika policy mengizinkan |
| `X-Request-Source` | Caller service |
| `Idempotency-Key` | Dedup command |
| `X-Client-Id` | Client/application identity |
| `X-Forwarded-For` | Network origin, biasanya gateway-managed |

Rules:

```text id="eces5h"
Identity/security-critical data should come from trusted token/cert,
not arbitrary unsigned headers.

Headers like X-User-Id or X-Tenant-Id must be validated against token claims
or set only by trusted gateway/service mesh.
```

Anti-pattern:

```text id="9e6m0q"
Downstream trusts:
  X-User-Id: admin
without validating Authorization token.
```

---

## 11. Jersey Client: Inject Authorization Header

Jakarta REST `ClientRequestContext` adalah mutable request-specific context yang menyediakan request URI, headers, entity, dan request-scoped properties; setter-nya memungkinkan filter memodifikasi request sebelum dikirim. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/client/clientrequestcontext?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh service token filter:

```java id="zd6do6"
@Provider
public final class ServiceTokenRequestFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final String scope;

    public ServiceTokenRequestFilter(
            ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            String scope
    ) {
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.scope = scope;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        String token = tokenProvider.getToken(scope);

        requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION,
                "Bearer " + token
        );
    }
}
```

Register:

```java id="vqip6j"
Client client = ClientBuilder.newBuilder()
        .register(new ServiceTokenRequestFilter(
                tokenProvider,
                "customer-api.read"
        ))
        .build();
```

Rule:

```text id="cxvd6m"
Use case tidak boleh tahu cara mengambil token.
Token injection berada di infrastructure client boundary.
```

---

## 12. Jersey Client: User Token Propagation

```java id="yc2ers"
@Provider
public final class UserTokenPropagationFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final RequestSecurityContext securityContext;

    public UserTokenPropagationFilter(RequestSecurityContext securityContext) {
        this.securityContext = securityContext;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        securityContext.currentBearerToken()
                .ifPresent(token -> requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                        HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION,
                        "Bearer " + token
                ));
    }
}
```

Caution:

```text id="4zrw7f"
Forwarding inbound user token is valid only if:
  token audience/scope accepted by downstream,
  policy allows propagation,
  token lifetime is appropriate,
  downstream can validate issuer/signature,
  audit model expects end-user identity.
```

Jika tidak, gunakan token exchange/delegated token atau service token + actor context.

---

## 13. OpenFeign: RequestInterceptor

Untuk OpenFeign core, gunakan `RequestInterceptor`.

```java id="4tr9st"
public final class BearerTokenFeignInterceptor
        implements RequestInterceptor {

    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final String scope;

    public BearerTokenFeignInterceptor(
            ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            String scope
    ) {
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.scope = scope;
    }

    @Override
    public void apply(RequestTemplate template) {
        String token = tokenProvider.getToken(scope);

        template.header(
                "Authorization",
                "Bearer " + token
        );
    }
}
```

Correlation/user/tenant context:

```java id="bu5iyb"
public final class ContextPropagationFeignInterceptor
        implements RequestInterceptor {

    private final RequestContext requestContext;

    @Override
    public void apply(RequestTemplate template) {
        template.header(
                "X-Correlation-Id",
                requestContext.correlationId()
        );

        requestContext.tenantId()
                .ifPresent(tenant -> template.header("X-Tenant-Id", tenant));

        requestContext.userId()
                .ifPresent(user -> template.header("X-User-Id", user));
    }
}
```

---

## 14. Generated Client Auth

OpenAPI generated clients sering menghasilkan helper auth berdasarkan `securitySchemes`.

Contoh OpenAPI:

```yaml id="zw013z"
components:
  securitySchemes:
    bearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT
```

Generated usage bisa kira-kira seperti:

```java id="uwlon8"
ApiClient apiClient = new ApiClient();

HttpBearerAuth bearerAuth =
        (HttpBearerAuth) apiClient.getAuthentication("bearerAuth");

bearerAuth.setBearerToken(token);
```

Tetapi hati-hati:

```text id="8gph00"
Jika token disimpan di singleton ApiClient,
request concurrent dengan token berbeda bisa saling bocor.
```

Untuk service token yang sama bagi seluruh service, ini bisa acceptable jika token provider refresh-safe. Untuk user token per request, lebih aman memakai request filter/interceptor yang mengambil token dari request context.

---

## 15. Token Provider Design

Bad:

```java id="7d70e5"
String token = System.getenv("STATIC_TOKEN");
```

Better:

```java id="t6j5fq"
public interface ServiceTokenProvider {
    String getToken(String scope);
}
```

Implementation concern:

| Concern | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Token caching | Jangan request token baru setiap call |
| Expiry awareness | Refresh sebelum expired |
| Clock skew | Refresh lebih awal |
| Scope/audience | Token sesuai downstream |
| Failure handling | Token endpoint down harus diklasifikasi |
| Metrics | Token refresh failure terlihat |
| Secret protection | Client secret/cert aman |
| Thread safety | Banyak request concurrent |

Pseudo:

```java id="p4utqp"
public final class CachingServiceTokenProvider
        implements ServiceTokenProvider {

    private final TokenClient tokenClient;
    private final ConcurrentMap<String, CachedToken> cache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @Override
    public String getToken(String scope) {
        CachedToken token = cache.get(scope);

        if (token != null && !token.expiresSoon()) {
            return token.value();
        }

        CachedToken refreshed = tokenClient.requestClientCredentialsToken(scope);
        cache.put(scope, refreshed);

        return refreshed.value();
    }
}
```

---

## 16. Client Credentials Flow

Untuk service-to-service, OAuth 2.0 client credentials grant umum dipakai ketika client meminta access token menggunakan client authentication, tanpa user resource owner langsung. RFC 6749 mendefinisikan OAuth 2.0 framework dan grant types termasuk client credentials. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6749?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Mental model:

```text id="ekg7f3"
order-service
  -> token endpoint
      client_id + client_secret/cert
      scope=customer-api.read
  <- access_token
  -> customer-service
      Authorization: Bearer access_token
```

Use case:

| Cocok | Tidak cocok |
|---|---|
| backend-to-backend | user-specific permission |
| scheduled job | resource owner delegation |
| service identity | end-user audit as primary actor |
| system workflow | user consent flow |

Rule:

```text id="8744ag"
Client credentials token represents the service,
not the end user.
```

Jika action dipicu user, audit field harus membedakan:

```text id="nzbvji"
actorUser = Alice
callingService = order-service
authModel = delegated/service-token-with-actor-context
```

---

## 17. Token Exchange / Delegation Model

Untuk operation yang butuh user context tapi tidak ingin forward raw user token:

```text id="dvzjb1"
Inbound token:
  user=Alice
  audience=order-service
  scope=order:create

Token exchange:
  subject=Alice
  actor=order-service
  audience=customer-service
  scope=customer:read

Outbound token:
  user=Alice
  actor=order-service
  audience=customer-service
```

Keuntungan:

| Keuntungan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Audience tepat | Token hanya untuk downstream target |
| Scope dipersempit | Least privilege |
| Audit lebih jelas | Actor + subject |
| Token asli tidak tersebar | Containment |
| Downstream policy lebih kuat | Validasi audience/scope lebih mudah |

Konsep ini tergantung identity provider perusahaan. Jangan implement custom token exchange sendiri tanpa security team/platform standard.

---

## 18. mTLS

mTLS memberi autentikasi mutual di level TLS: server membuktikan identitasnya ke client, dan client juga membuktikan identitasnya ke server menggunakan certificate.

RFC 8705 mendeskripsikan OAuth client authentication dan certificate-bound access/refresh tokens menggunakan mutual TLS dengan X.509 certificates; OAuth clients dapat diautentikasi ke authorization server menggunakan mutual TLS berbasis PKI atau self-signed certificate. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8705?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Mental model:

```text id="2qd1p1"
Client Service
  presents client certificate
  validates server certificate
  |
  TLS handshake
  |
  Server Service / Gateway
  validates client certificate
```

mTLS bisa dipakai untuk:

| Use case | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Service identity | Membuktikan caller service |
| Transport security | Strong channel authentication |
| OAuth client auth | Client auth ke token endpoint |
| Certificate-bound token | Token hanya valid dengan cert tertentu |
| Zero-trust/service mesh | Workload identity |

---

## 19. mTLS vs Bearer Token

| Aspect | Bearer token | mTLS |
|---|---|---|
| Identity proof | Possession of token | Possession of private key/cert |
| If stolen | Token bisa dipakai oleh pencuri | Cert-bound token lebih sulit dipakai tanpa key |
| Layer | Application header | Transport layer |
| Rotation | Token short-lived | Cert lifecycle/rotation |
| Authorization claims | Scope/claims dalam token | Cert identity perlu mapping |
| Operational complexity | Medium | Higher PKI/platform complexity |
| Common use | OAuth protected API | Service mesh, high-trust internal calls |

Sering dipakai bersama:

```text id="aa9rm2"
mTLS:
  authenticates workload/service

Bearer token:
  carries authorization scopes/claims

Certificate-bound access token:
  token bound to client certificate
```

---

## 20. OpenAPI mTLS Scheme

OpenAPI security schemes mencakup mutual TLS sebagai tipe security. ([OpenAPI Documentation](https://learn.openapis.org/specification/security.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```yaml id="py46df"
components:
  securitySchemes:
    mtlsAuth:
      type: mutualTLS
      description: Client must present a valid service certificate.

security:
  - mtlsAuth: []
```

mTLS + bearer:

```yaml id="2g9btl"
components:
  securitySchemes:
    mtlsAuth:
      type: mutualTLS
    bearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT

security:
  - mtlsAuth: []
    bearerAuth: []
```

Makna:

```text id="r4dfgu"
Call requires:
  valid client certificate
  and valid bearer token
```

Catatan: dukungan tooling untuk `mutualTLS` bisa berbeda tergantung versi OpenAPI/generator/tool. Tetap cek toolchain internal.

---

## 21. TLS/mTLS Config di Java Client

Mental model Java:

```text id="wlz6lb"
Truststore:
  trusted CA certificates
  used to validate server certificate

Keystore:
  client certificate + private key
  used to present client identity

Hostname verifier:
  validates server hostname matches cert
```

Jersey/Feign exact config tergantung connector/transport yang dipakai. Yang perlu distandardisasi:

| Config | Source |
|---|---|
| Truststore path/secret | Kubernetes Secret/Vault |
| Keystore path/secret | Kubernetes Secret/Vault |
| Key password | Secret manager |
| Cert rotation | Platform process |
| Hostname verification | Enabled |
| Protocol/cipher | Platform security baseline |
| Service mesh mode | App-level TLS or sidecar TLS |

Anti-pattern:

```text id="wkqu78"
Disable hostname verification
Trust all certificates
Hardcode keystore password
Commit cert/private key to repo
```

---

## 22. Service Mesh mTLS vs App-level mTLS

| Model | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| App-level mTLS | Java client config mengelola cert/truststore |
| Service mesh mTLS | Sidecar/proxy mengelola workload identity dan mTLS |
| Gateway-level mTLS | API gateway terminates/validates client cert |
| Hybrid | Mesh untuk internal, app token untuk auth claims |

Decision questions:

```text id="0n3mn5"
Apakah mTLS diterminasi di sidecar/gateway atau app?
Apakah app melihat client cert identity?
Apakah service identity diterjemahkan ke header trusted?
Apakah header itu hanya bisa diset oleh gateway/mesh?
Bagaimana cert rotation dilakukan?
```

Rule:

```text id="4z6adq"
If mTLS is handled by mesh/gateway,
do not reimplement mTLS in every Java client unless required.
```

---

## 23. Authorization di Provider Side

Consumer mengirim token/context. Provider tetap harus authorize.

Provider flow:

```text id="tdo0vu"
Receive request
  |
  v
Authenticate
  - validate token/cert
  - issuer/audience/expiry/signature
  |
  v
Extract principal
  - service identity
  - user identity
  - tenant
  - scopes
  |
  v
Authorize operation
  - scope/permission
  - resource ownership
  - tenant boundary
  - workflow state
  |
  v
Execute use case
```

Example:

```java id="exfjzq"
public OrderResponse getOrderById(String orderId, SecurityPrincipal principal) {
    Order order = orderRepository.findById(orderId)
            .orElseThrow(OrderNotFoundException::new);

    authorizationService.assertCanReadOrder(principal, order);

    return mapper.toResponse(order);
}
```

Jangan:

```text id="qf8z42"
Gateway validated JWT,
therefore service skips object-level authorization.
```

---

## 24. 401 vs 403

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `401 Unauthorized` | Authentication missing/invalid/expired |
| `403 Forbidden` | Authenticated but not allowed |

Mapping:

| Scenario | Status |
|---|---:|
| No token | `401` |
| Invalid signature | `401` |
| Expired token | `401` |
| Wrong audience | `401` or `403`, convention-dependent |
| Valid token but missing scope | `403` |
| Valid user but cannot access resource | `403` |
| Valid service but not allowed operation | `403` |
| Tenant mismatch | Usually `403`, sometimes masked as `404` |

Provider error examples:

```json id="erw0ld"
{
  "errorCode": "AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED",
  "message": "Authentication is required.",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

```json id="u2kb91"
{
  "errorCode": "ACCESS_DENIED",
  "message": "Caller is not allowed to access this resource.",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

---

## 25. Consumer-side Handling of 401/403

Adapter should map:

```text id="p7uf29"
401:
  token missing/expired/invalid
  maybe refresh token once if service token provider says expired
  otherwise DownstreamAuthenticationException

403:
  authorization denied
  do not retry blindly
  map to DownstreamAuthorizationException
```

Example:

```java id="a91yc4"
if (status == 401) {
    throw new DownstreamAuthenticationException(
            "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_AUTHENTICATION_FAILED",
            error
    );
}

if (status == 403) {
    throw new DownstreamAuthorizationException(
            "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_ACCESS_DENIED",
            error
    );
}
```

Do not:

```text id="6sjy9b"
Retry 403 three times.
```

A valid token without permission will not become permitted through retry.

---

## 26. Token Refresh Retry

One special case: service token expired.

```text id="ngmlt4"
Call downstream
  -> 401 TOKEN_EXPIRED
  -> invalidate cached token
  -> fetch new token
  -> retry once
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Retry at most once | Hindari auth loop |
| Only for token-expiry class | Jangan retry all 401 |
| Invalidate cache | Jangan reuse token expired |
| Metrics | Token refresh failures visible |
| Thread-safe refresh | Avoid thundering herd |
| Still respect deadline | Auth retry masuk total budget |

Pseudo:

```java id="cuvx0h"
try {
    return callDownstream();
} catch (DownstreamAuthenticationException ex) {
    if (ex.isTokenExpired() && !alreadyRetried) {
        tokenProvider.invalidate(scope);
        return callDownstream();
    }
    throw ex;
}
```

---

## 27. Multi-tenant Context

Token biasanya harus membawa tenant claim, misalnya:

```json id="fj35x2"
{
  "sub": "user-123",
  "tenant_id": "tenant-a",
  "scope": "order:read"
}
```

Header `X-Tenant-Id` boleh dipakai untuk routing/observability, tetapi provider harus validasi terhadap token:

```text id="giekg8"
X-Tenant-Id == token.tenant_id
```

atau untuk multi-tenant admin:

```text id="eut5cx"
token has cross-tenant permission
requested tenant is allowed
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Tenant dari token lebih trusted daripada header | Header mudah dipalsukan jika tidak protected |
| Tenant mismatch harus ditolak | Hindari data leakage |
| Tenant harus masuk audit | Regulatory/debugging |
| SQL query harus tenant-scoped | MyBatis/PostgreSQL layer tetap harus aman |
| Cross-tenant admin harus eksplisit | Jangan implicit superuser |

---

## 28. Audit Context

Untuk action penting, audit minimal:

```text id="yr044j"
actorUserId
actorServiceId
tenantId
operation
resourceType
resourceId
decision
correlationId
timestamp
authMethod
scopes/permissions used
```

Contoh:

```json id="mxd7ze"
{
  "actorUserId": "user-123",
  "actorServiceId": "order-service",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "operation": "cancelOrder",
  "resourceType": "ORDER",
  "resourceId": "ORD-001",
  "decision": "DENIED",
  "reason": "ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Rule:

```text id="p3y5md"
Audit should record who initiated,
which service performed,
and what authorization decision was made.
```

Jangan simpan full token di audit log.

---

## 29. Logging Security Rules

Never log:

```text id="pjzqhb"
Authorization header
access token
refresh token
client secret
private key
certificate private material
password
full JWT payload if contains PII
full request body with sensitive data
```

Safe-ish to log:

```text id="8oxkbi"
token issuer
token audience
scope list, if not sensitive
subject hash
tenant id
service client id
correlation id
operation
status code
errorCode
```

Example safe log:

```text id="vaw3ic"
level=WARN
event=DOWNSTREAM_ACCESS_DENIED
downstream=customer-service
operation=getCustomerById
status=403
errorCode=ACCESS_DENIED
callerService=order-service
tenantId=tenant-a
subjectHash=sha256:...
correlationId=01J...
```

---

## 30. Secrets Management

Secrets include:

```text id="x85x7q"
client secret
private key
keystore password
API key
refresh token
database password
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Jangan commit ke Git | Obvious but common |
| Jangan taruh di image | Image bisa tersebar |
| Gunakan secret manager | Vault, KMS, Kubernetes Secret, cloud secret manager |
| Rotate regularly | Cert/key/token lifecycle |
| Least privilege | Secret per service/scope |
| Mount/read securely | File permission/env var policy |
| Avoid printing config | Startup logs jangan dump secret |

---

## 31. Kubernetes / Cloud Considerations

| Concern | Notes |
|---|---|
| Kubernetes Secret | Bisa mount cert/token config, tapi perlu RBAC dan rotation |
| Service account | Workload identity bisa dipakai di cloud-native auth |
| Service mesh | mTLS dan identity bisa dikelola sidecar |
| Ingress/API gateway | Bisa enforce auth/mTLS sebelum service |
| NetworkPolicy | Batasi service-to-service connectivity |
| AWS/Azure identity | Managed identity/workload identity bisa mengganti static secret |
| On-prem PKI | Cert issuance/rotation lebih manual |
| ConfigMap vs Secret | Jangan taruh secret di ConfigMap |

Rule:

```text id="mw2hdh"
Authentication should not rely only on network location.
Network policy helps, but identity/token/cert validation still matters.
```

---

## 32. Security in Adapter / Gateway Pattern

Adapter boundary:

```text id="fu4hfp"
CustomerGateway
  |
  v
CustomerHttpGateway
  |
  +--> adds auth token
  +--> propagates correlation/tenant context
  +--> maps 401/403
  +--> logs safely
  +--> emits metrics
```

Application use case:

```java id="sfml5z"
public final class SubmitOrderUseCase {

    private final CustomerGateway customerGateway;

    public void submit(SubmitOrderCommand command) {
        CustomerProfile customer =
                customerGateway.findById(command.customerId())
                        .orElseThrow(...);

        // business logic
    }
}
```

Application layer should not:

```text id="926huz"
build Authorization header
read raw JWT
call token endpoint
know mTLS config
catch FeignException.Unauthorized
```

---

## 33. Example: Secure Jersey Client Factory

```java id="0x1ziv"
public final class CustomerJerseyClientFactory {

    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider;
    private final RequestContext requestContext;

    public Client create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        ClientConfig config = new ClientConfig()
                .property(ClientProperties.CONNECT_TIMEOUT,
                        properties.connectTimeoutMs())
                .property(ClientProperties.READ_TIMEOUT,
                        properties.readTimeoutMs());

        config.register(new ServiceTokenRequestFilter(
                tokenProvider,
                "customer-api.read"
        ));

        config.register(new CorrelationIdRequestFilter(
                correlationIdProvider,
                properties.sourceService()
        ));

        config.register(new TenantContextRequestFilter(
                requestContext
        ));

        return ClientBuilder.newClient(config);
    }
}
```

Tenant filter:

```java id="zpc3q9"
public final class TenantContextRequestFilter
        implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final RequestContext requestContext;

    public TenantContextRequestFilter(RequestContext requestContext) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        this.requestContext.tenantId()
                .ifPresent(tenantId -> requestContext.getHeaders()
                        .putSingle("X-Tenant-Id", tenantId));
    }
}
```

---

## 34. Example: Secure Feign Factory

```java id="w5w2q0"
public final class CustomerFeignFactory {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final ServiceTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final CorrelationIdProvider correlationIdProvider;
    private final RequestContext requestContext;
    private final DownstreamErrorBodyDecoder errorDecoder;

    public CustomerFeignApi create(CustomerClientProperties properties) {
        return Feign.builder()
                .encoder(new JacksonEncoder(objectMapper))
                .decoder(new JacksonDecoder(objectMapper))
                .errorDecoder(new CustomerErrorDecoder(errorDecoder))
                .requestInterceptor(new BearerTokenFeignInterceptor(
                        tokenProvider,
                        "customer-api.read"
                ))
                .requestInterceptor(new ContextPropagationFeignInterceptor(
                        correlationIdProvider,
                        requestContext,
                        properties.sourceService()
                ))
                .options(new Request.Options(
                        properties.connectTimeoutMs(),
                        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                        properties.readTimeoutMs(),
                        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                        true
                ))
                .retryer(Retryer.NEVER_RETRY)
                .target(CustomerFeignApi.class, properties.baseUrl());
    }
}
```

---

## 35. Example: Authorization Error Mapper

```java id="qnkhu9"
public final class CustomerSecurityErrorMapper {

    public RuntimeException mapSecurityError(
            int status,
            DecodedError error,
            Throwable cause
    ) {
        if (status == 401) {
            return new DownstreamAuthenticationException(
                    "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_AUTHENTICATION_FAILED",
                    error,
                    cause
            );
        }

        if (status == 403) {
            return new DownstreamAuthorizationException(
                    "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_ACCESS_DENIED",
                    error,
                    cause
            );
        }

        throw new DownstreamProtocolException(
                "CUSTOMER_SERVICE_UNEXPECTED_SECURITY_STATUS",
                error,
                cause
        );
    }
}
```

Handling in gateway:

```java id="auq1re"
if (status == 401 || status == 403) {
    throw securityErrorMapper.mapSecurityError(status, error, exception);
}
```

---

## 36. Testing Security Propagation

### Header propagation test

```text id="zktbnh"
Given request context has correlationId and tenantId
When gateway calls downstream
Then X-Correlation-Id sent
And X-Tenant-Id sent
And Authorization header sent
```

### No token logging test

```text id="x26hkf"
Given downstream returns 403
When adapter logs error
Then log does not contain raw Authorization token
```

### Token refresh test

```text id="pdxvv6"
Given first call returns 401 TOKEN_EXPIRED
When token provider refreshes
Then adapter retries once
And second call uses new token
```

### Forbidden test

```text id="ivfvq6"
Given downstream returns 403 ACCESS_DENIED
When gateway called
Then DownstreamAuthorizationException thrown
And no retry
```

### Tenant mismatch provider test

```text id="zl7jft"
Given token tenant=tenant-a
And header tenant=tenant-b
When provider receives request
Then 403 ACCESS_DENIED
```

### mTLS config smoke test

```text id="jlvv49"
Given client cert not configured
When calling mTLS endpoint
Then TLS handshake fails
And failure classified as transport/security config failure
```

---

## 37. Common Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Forward inbound `Authorization` blindly | Wrong audience/scope, token leakage |
| Trust `X-User-Id` without token validation | Impersonation risk |
| Log full token/JWT | Credential leak |
| Store token in Camunda variable | Long-lived secret leak |
| Put client secret in Git/image | Credential compromise |
| Use static never-expiring token | Huge blast radius |
| Retry `403` | Useless load/noise |
| Treat `401` same as `503` | Wrong failure classification |
| Disable TLS hostname verification | MITM risk |
| Trust-all certificates | Broken TLS security |
| Generated singleton ApiClient stores per-user token | Cross-request token leak |
| API gateway auth only, no object authorization | BOLA/resource access risk |
| Tenant from header only | Tenant spoofing |
| No audit actor/service distinction | Poor regulatory traceability |
| Full Feign/Jersey body logging in prod | PII/secret leakage |
| mTLS cert rotation manual/forgotten | Outage on expiry |

---

## 38. Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI | Security schemes defined globally/per operation |
| Token model | Service token/user token/delegated token chosen intentionally |
| Scope/audience | Token audience and scopes match downstream |
| Propagation | Authorization/correlation/tenant handled in filter/interceptor |
| Adapter | Application layer does not build auth headers |
| 401/403 | Mapped separately, no blind retry |
| Token refresh | At most once, cache invalidation safe |
| mTLS | App-level vs mesh-level ownership clear |
| Cert management | Truststore/keystore/rotation defined |
| Tenant | Tenant claim/header validation clear |
| Authorization | Provider does object/resource-level check |
| Audit | Actor user + caller service captured |
| Logging | No token/secret/PII leakage |
| Secrets | Secret manager/K8s Secret/cloud identity used |
| Tests | Headers, auth failure, refresh, tenant mismatch tested |
| MyBatis | Queries tenant-scoped where required |
| Camunda | No token/generated auth DTO as process variable |

---

## 39. Ringkasan

Security context untuk service-to-service HTTP harus didesain sebagai kontrak dan runtime policy:

```text id="t2amj6"
OpenAPI security scheme
  + service/user/delegated token model
  + mTLS strategy
  + header governance
  + provider-side authorization
  + adapter-side propagation
  + safe error mapping
  + audit trail
  + secret management
  + no-token logging discipline
```

Core rules:

```text id="cxjw6o"
Do not blindly forward tokens.
Do not trust identity headers without validation.
Do not leak tokens into logs, DB, process variables, or errors.
401 != 403.
mTLS authenticates transport/client identity, not business permission by itself.
Authorization must still check resource, tenant, and business state.
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Security propagation bukan sekadar menyalin header. Ia adalah keputusan arsitektural tentang identity, delegation, least privilege, authorization boundary, auditability, dan operational safety.**

# Seri 14/16 - Contract Testing, Mock Server, WireMock, Schema Validation

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada:

> **Bagaimana memastikan provider dan consumer benar-benar mengikuti OpenAPI contract, bukan hanya punya file `openapi.yaml` yang terlihat rapi.**

Dalam contract-first approach, kontrak harus diuji dari beberapa sisi:

```text id="xajsc3"
OpenAPI file valid?
Generated client bisa dibuat dan compile?
Consumer adapter benar saat success/error/timeout?
Provider Jersey resource benar-benar return schema/status/error sesuai contract?
Mock downstream cukup realistis?
Breaking change terdeteksi di CI?
```

OpenAPI Specification mendefinisikan format standar untuk mendeskripsikan HTTP API; OpenAPI Initiative juga menekankan bahwa OpenAPI bisa digunakan untuk memahami API, generate client code, membuat test, dan menerapkan design standards. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Testing Contract-first

```text id="bocugj"
OpenAPI Contract
  |
  +--> Static validation
  |      - syntax
  |      - $ref
  |      - required fields
  |      - style/lint rules
  |
  +--> Generation validation
  |      - generate Java client
  |      - compile generated code
  |      - compile adapter
  |
  +--> Consumer-side tests
  |      - adapter mapping
  |      - error mapping
  |      - timeout/retry behavior
  |      - mock downstream with WireMock
  |
  +--> Provider-side tests
  |      - Jersey resource status code
  |      - request validation
  |      - response schema
  |      - error schema
  |
  +--> Compatibility tests
         - API diff
         - backward compatibility
         - deprecation checks
```

Contract testing bukan hanya satu jenis test. Ia adalah kombinasi dari static validation, generated client compile check, consumer mock test, provider response validation, dan compatibility check.

---

## 3. Testing Layer Map

| Layer | Pertanyaan | Tool/teknik |
|---|---|---|
| OpenAPI validation | Apakah spec valid secara format? | OpenAPI validator/generator validate |
| OpenAPI lint | Apakah naming/status/error mengikuti standard tim? | Spectral/custom linter, CI rule |
| Generated compile | Apakah spec bisa menjadi Java client? | OpenAPI Generator + Maven compile |
| Consumer adapter test | Apakah consumer memproses downstream benar? | Unit test + WireMock |
| Provider resource test | Apakah Jersey endpoint return contract yang benar? | Jersey test/container test |
| Schema validation | Apakah request/response match OpenAPI schema? | OpenAPI schema validator |
| Error contract test | Apakah error body/status/errorCode benar? | Provider/consumer tests |
| Compatibility diff | Apakah change breaking? | API diff tool + review |
| Smoke integration | Apakah service benar-benar connect di env? | Staging smoke test |
| Workflow integration | Apakah Camunda retry/error mapping benar? | Worker/delegate tests |

---

## 4. Yang Tidak Cukup

Hal-hal ini **tidak cukup** sebagai contract testing:

```text id="5oqnvx"
- hanya membuka Swagger UI dan melihat endpoint
- hanya test 200 OK
- hanya compile service provider
- hanya generate client tanpa compile adapter
- hanya mock happy path
- hanya test controller/resource tanpa error response
- hanya end-to-end test besar yang lambat
```

Kenapa?

| Anti-pattern | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Hanya test `200` | Error contract rusak tidak ketahuan |
| Tidak test generated client | `operationId`/schema rename bisa lolos |
| Tidak test consumer adapter | 404/409/422 bisa salah mapping |
| Tidak test provider schema | Provider return field null/missing |
| Tidak test timeout | Production hanging tidak ketahuan |
| Tidak test auth headers | Integration gagal di env |
| Tidak test idempotency | Retry command duplicate |

---

## 5. Contract-first Testing Flow

```text id="1jm57h"
PR changes openapi.yaml
  |
  v
1. Validate OpenAPI
  |
  v
2. Lint API style
  |
  v
3. Generate Java client
  |
  v
4. Compile generated client
  |
  v
5. Compile consumer adapter
  |
  v
6. Run adapter tests with WireMock
  |
  v
7. Run provider resource + schema tests
  |
  v
8. Run API diff against released spec
  |
  v
9. Publish contract/client artifact
```

Tujuannya bukan “menambah banyak test”, tetapi membuat perubahan API gagal cepat saat masih di pull request.

---

## 6. OpenAPI Static Validation

Static validation mengecek apakah file OpenAPI valid secara struktur.

Contoh validasi:

```text id="95dn59"
openapi.yaml:
  - YAML/JSON valid
  - required top-level fields ada
  - paths valid
  - operations punya responses
  - $ref tidak broken
  - schema valid
  - security scheme direferensikan dengan benar
```

OpenAPI Generator Maven plugin memiliki goal `validate` untuk memvalidasi satu atau lebih OpenAPI specifications; dokumentasi plugin juga menjelaskan plugin Maven/Gradle dapat dipakai dalam build lifecycle. ([GitHub](https://github.com/OpenAPITools/openapi-generator/blob/master/modules/openapi-generator-maven-plugin/README.md?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh Maven:

```xml id="726f1w"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.openapitools</groupId>
    <artifactId>openapi-generator-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${openapi-generator.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>validate-openapi</id>
            <phase>validate</phase>
            <goals>
                <goal>validate</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <inputSpec>${project.basedir}/src/main/openapi/order-api.yaml</inputSpec>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

---

## 7. OpenAPI Lint / Style Validation

Static validation menjawab:

```text id="x7mt7t"
Apakah spec valid?
```

Lint menjawab:

```text id="3bs0ri"
Apakah spec sesuai standard engineering perusahaan?
```

Contoh rule lint internal:

| Rule | Contoh |
|---|---|
| `operationId` wajib | `getOrderById` |
| Error response wajib punya schema | `ErrorResponse` / `ProblemDetail` |
| Tidak boleh semua error `default` saja | Define `400`, `401`, `403`, `404`, `409`, `422`, `500` sesuai operation |
| Path harus plural noun | `/orders/{orderId}` |
| Tidak boleh `/getX`, `/process`, `/execute` | Hindari RPC-ish generic path |
| Pagination wajib untuk list besar | `page`, `size`, `items` |
| Command penting wajib `Idempotency-Key` | `POST /payments` |
| Security scheme wajib untuk non-public endpoint | `bearerAuth`, `mtlsAuth` |
| `operationId` tidak boleh berubah tanpa review | Generated method stability |
| Schema request/response tidak boleh inline untuk API utama | Avoid `InlineObject1` |

---

## 8. Generation Validation

OpenAPI valid belum tentu bagus untuk Java.

Generation validation mengecek:

```text id="g6wl12"
openapi.yaml
  -> generated Java client
  -> generated source compile
```

Contoh Maven check:

```bash id="7g3q5m"
mvn clean verify
```

Yang ingin ditangkap:

| Masalah | Contoh |
|---|---|
| `operationId` duplicate | Method conflict |
| Schema name buruk | Class name aneh |
| Reserved word | Field `class`, `default` |
| Enum value buruk | Java enum invalid |
| Inline schema | `InlineObject1` |
| `javax`/`jakarta` mismatch | Compile failure |
| Date/time mapping salah | Wrong Java type |
| Missing dependency | Generated import tidak resolve |
| Bad package config | Class collision |

OpenAPI Generator Java generator adalah generator client Java dan menyediakan banyak konfigurasi yang memengaruhi output, termasuk package, enum naming, dan opsi generation lain; artinya compile check terhadap output generated code adalah gate penting. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/generators/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. Consumer Adapter Test

Consumer adapter test memastikan consumer tidak salah memahami downstream.

Target test:

```text id="zpbrka"
CustomerHttpGateway
  - maps 200 to CustomerProfile
  - maps 404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND to Optional.empty()
  - maps 409 to conflict
  - maps 422 to business validation
  - maps 500/503/504 to downstream failure
  - sends Authorization
  - sends X-Correlation-Id
  - respects timeout/retry policy
```

Adapter interface:

```java id="8e4qoj"
public interface CustomerGateway {
    Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

Adapter test tidak perlu memanggil real customer-service. Gunakan mock HTTP server.

---

## 10. WireMock sebagai Mock Server

WireMock adalah tool open-source populer untuk API mock testing di Java/JVM; dokumentasinya memposisikan WireMock untuk membuat mock API yang stabil untuk test dan development. ([WireMock](https://wiremock.org/docs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

WireMock cocok untuk:

| Use case | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Mock downstream service | customer-service, payment-service |
| Test request matching | method/path/query/header/body |
| Test response variants | `200`, `404`, `409`, `500`, timeout |
| Test retry behavior | first `503`, then `200` |
| Test timeout | delayed response |
| Test header propagation | `Authorization`, `X-Correlation-Id` |
| Test idempotency | same `Idempotency-Key` on retry |

---

## 11. WireMock Request Matching

WireMock mendukung request matching, termasuk path templates sejak WireMock 3.0.0; path template matching memungkinkan path variable direferensikan by name, termasuk dalam response templates. ([WireMock](https://wiremock.org/docs/request-matching/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh stub konseptual:

```java id="r1ekqh"
wireMock.stubFor(get(urlPathTemplate("/customers/{customerId}"))
        .withPathParam("customerId", equalTo("CUST-001"))
        .withHeader("Authorization", matching("Bearer .+"))
        .withHeader("X-Correlation-Id", matching(".+"))
        .willReturn(okJson("""
            {
              "customerId": "CUST-001",
              "displayName": "Acme Corp",
              "status": "ACTIVE"
            }
            """)));
```

Request matching yang penting:

| Match | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| HTTP method | `GET` vs `POST` |
| Path template | `/customers/{customerId}` |
| Query params | pagination/filter |
| Headers | auth, correlation, idempotency |
| Body JSON | command/search request |
| Content-Type | request contract |
| Accept | response negotiation |

---

## 12. Consumer Happy Path Test

```java id="fbmz8c"
@Test
void shouldReturnCustomerProfileWhenCustomerExists() {
    wireMock.stubFor(get(urlPathEqualTo("/customers/CUST-001"))
            .withHeader("Authorization", matching("Bearer .+"))
            .withHeader("X-Correlation-Id", matching(".+"))
            .willReturn(okJson("""
                {
                  "customerId": "CUST-001",
                  "displayName": "Acme Corp",
                  "status": "ACTIVE"
                }
                """)));

    Optional<CustomerProfile> result =
            customerGateway.findById(new CustomerId("CUST-001"));

    assertThat(result).isPresent();
    assertThat(result.get().displayName()).isEqualTo("Acme Corp");
}
```

Yang divalidasi:

```text id="qcwo76"
- adapter membuat HTTP call ke path benar
- auth header dikirim
- correlation ID dikirim
- response JSON didecode
- DTO external dimap ke domain model
```

---

## 13. Consumer Error Path Test

```java id="sa7lfy"
@Test
void shouldReturnEmptyWhenCustomerNotFound() {
    wireMock.stubFor(get(urlPathEqualTo("/customers/CUST-404"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(404)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                        {
                          "errorCode": "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND",
                          "message": "Customer was not found.",
                          "correlationId": "test-correlation"
                        }
                        """)));

    Optional<CustomerProfile> result =
            customerGateway.findById(new CustomerId("CUST-404"));

    assertThat(result).isEmpty();
}
```

Test lain yang wajib:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| `400 REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED` | Downstream request/contract exception |
| `401 AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED` | Authentication exception |
| `403 ACCESS_DENIED` | Authorization exception |
| `409 INVALID_STATE` | Conflict exception/result |
| `422 BUSINESS_VALIDATION_FAILED` | Business validation exception/result |
| `500 INTERNAL_ERROR` | Downstream unavailable/system exception |
| `502` HTML body | Protocol/gateway error |
| Empty error body | Synthetic error |
| Malformed error body | Synthetic protocol error |

---

## 14. Consumer Timeout Test

```java id="0ht6sg"
@Test
void shouldFailWithTimeoutWhenDownstreamTooSlow() {
    wireMock.stubFor(get(urlPathEqualTo("/customers/CUST-001"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withFixedDelay(2_000)
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                        {
                          "customerId": "CUST-001",
                          "displayName": "Acme Corp",
                          "status": "ACTIVE"
                        }
                        """)));

    assertThatThrownBy(() ->
            customerGateway.findById(new CustomerId("CUST-001"))
    ).isInstanceOf(DownstreamTimeoutException.class);
}
```

Yang ingin dicek:

```text id="20ie1d"
- timeout benar-benar aktif
- exception diklasifikasi sebagai downstream timeout
- tidak menggantung
- metric timeout tercatat
- retry behavior sesuai operation
```

---

## 15. Retry Test dengan WireMock

Untuk query safe:

```java id="uu3mvv"
@Test
void shouldRetryQueryOnceWhenFirstAttemptReturns503() {
    wireMock.stubFor(get(urlPathEqualTo("/customers/CUST-001"))
            .inScenario("retry")
            .whenScenarioStateIs(STARTED)
            .willReturn(aResponse().withStatus(503))
            .willSetStateTo("second-attempt"));

    wireMock.stubFor(get(urlPathEqualTo("/customers/CUST-001"))
            .inScenario("retry")
            .whenScenarioStateIs("second-attempt")
            .willReturn(okJson("""
                {
                  "customerId": "CUST-001",
                  "displayName": "Acme Corp",
                  "status": "ACTIVE"
                }
                """)));

    Optional<CustomerProfile> result =
            customerGateway.findById(new CustomerId("CUST-001"));

    assertThat(result).isPresent();

    wireMock.verify(2, getRequestedFor(
            urlPathEqualTo("/customers/CUST-001")
    ));
}
```

Untuk command non-idempotent:

```text id="8o742d"
Given POST /payments returns 503
Then verify only 1 request was sent
```

---

## 16. Idempotency Test

Untuk command dengan retry:

```java id="0bsewm"
@Test
void shouldUseSameIdempotencyKeyOnRetry() {
    wireMock.stubFor(post(urlPathEqualTo("/payments"))
            .withHeader("Idempotency-Key", equalTo("PAYREQ-001"))
            .inScenario("payment-retry")
            .whenScenarioStateIs(STARTED)
            .willReturn(aResponse().withStatus(503))
            .willSetStateTo("retry-success"));

    wireMock.stubFor(post(urlPathEqualTo("/payments"))
            .withHeader("Idempotency-Key", equalTo("PAYREQ-001"))
            .inScenario("payment-retry")
            .whenScenarioStateIs("retry-success")
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(201)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                        {
                          "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                          "status": "AUTHORIZED"
                        }
                        """)));

    CreatePaymentResult result =
            paymentGateway.createPayment(commandWithKey("PAYREQ-001"));

    assertThat(result).isInstanceOf(CreatePaymentResult.Authorized.class);

    wireMock.verify(2, postRequestedFor(urlPathEqualTo("/payments"))
            .withHeader("Idempotency-Key", equalTo("PAYREQ-001")));
}
```

Yang dites:

```text id="fy4yf3"
- retry command hanya terjadi jika idempotency key ada
- key yang sama dipakai pada semua attempt
- adapter tidak generate key random tersembunyi
```

---

## 17. Response Templating WireMock

WireMock mendukung response templating dengan Handlebars dan helper untuk membuat response dinamis; dokumentasinya menyebut helper seperti `toJson` dan kemampuan mengakses request data dalam template. ([WireMock](https://wiremock.org/docs/response-templating/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh penggunaan konseptual:

```json id="5tpw7i"
{
  "request": {
    "method": "GET",
    "urlPathTemplate": "/customers/{customerId}"
  },
  "response": {
    "status": 200,
    "headers": {
      "Content-Type": "application/json"
    },
    "jsonBody": {
      "customerId": "{{request.path.customerId}}",
      "displayName": "Mock Customer",
      "status": "ACTIVE"
    },
    "transformers": ["response-template"]
  }
}
```

Kapan berguna?

| Use case | Manfaat |
|---|---|
| Path variable echo | Test dynamic ID |
| Error response correlation | Return same correlation id |
| Pagination mock | Generate page metadata |
| Callback/event mock | Dynamic event payload |
| Multi-scenario test | Lebih sedikit stub duplikasi |

---

## 18. Provider-side Contract Test

Provider-side test memastikan Jersey resource benar-benar mengikuti OpenAPI.

Target:

```text id="h19uvs"
Jersey Resource
  -> status code sesuai contract
  -> response body sesuai schema
  -> error body sesuai schema
  -> content type benar
  -> validation error benar
  -> auth error benar
```

Contoh provider behavior:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Valid request | `200/201` + response schema |
| Missing required field | `400 REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| Invalid enum | `400 REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| Not found | `404 {DOMAIN}_NOT_FOUND` |
| Invalid state | `409 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` |
| Business validation | `422 BUSINESS_VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| Unauthorized | `401 AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED` |
| Forbidden | `403 ACCESS_DENIED` |
| Unexpected exception | `500 INTERNAL_ERROR`, no stacktrace |

---

## 19. Provider Jersey Resource Test Example

```java id="pqclo2"
@Test
void shouldReturnOrderWhenFound() {
    Response response = target("/orders/ORD-001")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .header("Authorization", "Bearer test-token")
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);

    OrderResponse body = response.readEntity(OrderResponse.class);

    assertThat(body.getOrderId()).isEqualTo("ORD-001");
    assertThat(body.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.SUBMITTED);
}
```

Error test:

```java id="4vhfof"
@Test
void shouldReturn409WhenOrderCannotBeCancelled() {
    Response response = target("/orders/ORD-001/cancel")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .header("Authorization", "Bearer test-token")
            .post(Entity.json("""
                {
                  "reasonCode": "CUSTOMER_REQUEST"
                }
                """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(409);

    ErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ErrorResponse.class);

    assertThat(error.getErrorCode()).isEqualTo("ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED");
    assertThat(error.getCorrelationId()).isNotBlank();
}
```

---

## 20. Schema Validation: Apa yang Harus Divalidasi?

Schema validation memastikan payload match OpenAPI schema.

Validasi minimal:

```text id="3zfuku"
Request:
  path params
  query params
  headers
  request body

Response:
  status code
  content type
  response body schema
  response headers

Error:
  status code
  ErrorResponse / ProblemDetail schema
  required fields
```

OpenAPI document sendiri bisa berupa JSON atau YAML dan field names dalam specification bersifat case-sensitive; ini penting karena typo field di OpenAPI atau payload dapat membuat contract berbeda dari yang diharapkan. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 21. Response Schema Validation Example

Pseudo flow:

```text id="y8fzk0"
Given OpenAPI spec
And operationId = getOrderById
When provider returns 200 body
Then response body validates against:
  paths./orders/{orderId}.get.responses.200.content.application/json.schema
```

Example test intent:

```java id="0xlyxz"
@Test
void shouldMatchOpenApiSchemaForGetOrderById() {
    Response response = target("/orders/ORD-001")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .get();

    String body = response.readEntity(String.class);

    openApiValidator.validateResponse(
            "GET",
            "/orders/{orderId}",
            200,
            "application/json",
            body
    );
}
```

Catatan: nama API validator Java bisa berbeda tergantung library/platform. Prinsipnya adalah test response provider terhadap schema OpenAPI, bukan hanya deserialize ke class Java.

---

## 22. Kenapa Deserialize Saja Tidak Cukup?

Misal response provider:

```json id="if85ux"
{
  "orderId": "ORD-001",
  "status": "SUBMITTED",
  "extraInternalField": "secret"
}
```

Java DTO mungkin bisa deserialize dan mengabaikan unknown field. Tapi schema policy bisa melarang field tambahan.

Atau:

```json id="zfmk6s"
{
  "orderId": "ORD-001"
}
```

DTO mungkin tidak langsung gagal jika field nullable, tetapi OpenAPI contract mengatakan `status` required.

Jadi perlu dua hal:

| Check | Menangkap |
|---|---|
| Deserialize test | Runtime client bisa membaca |
| Schema validation | Provider benar-benar mengikuti contract |

---

## 23. Validasi Error Response

Error response harus dites sama seriusnya dengan success response.

```java id="82b8xz"
@Test
void validationErrorShouldMatchErrorSchema() {
    Response response = target("/orders")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                {
                  "customerId": "",
                  "items": []
                }
                """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);

    String body = response.readEntity(String.class);

    openApiValidator.validateResponse(
            "POST",
            "/orders",
            400,
            "application/json",
            body
    );
}
```

Cek tambahan:

```text id="x5m6oj"
- errorCode exists
- message exists
- correlationId exists
- details contains invalid fields
- no stacktrace
- content-type application/json
```

---

## 24. Contract Test untuk ExceptionMapper

ExceptionMapper adalah bagian kontrak provider.

Test:

| Exception | Expected status | Expected errorCode |
|---|---:|---|
| `ConstraintViolationException` | `400` | `REQUEST_VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| `AuthenticationException` | `401` | `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED` |
| `AccessDeniedException` | `403` | `ACCESS_DENIED` |
| `OrderNotFoundException` | `404` | `ORDER_NOT_FOUND` |
| `InvalidStateTransitionException` | `409` | `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` |
| `BusinessValidationException` | `422` | domain-specific |
| `DownstreamTimeoutException` | `503/504` | `DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT` |
| unexpected `RuntimeException` | `500` | `INTERNAL_ERROR` |

Test unhandled exception:

```java id="jgt6wx"
@Test
void shouldNotLeakStacktraceOnInternalError() {
    Response response = target("/test/internal-error")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(500);

    String body = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertThat(body).contains("INTERNAL_ERROR");
    assertThat(body).doesNotContain("java.lang.NullPointerException");
    assertThat(body).doesNotContain("at com.company");
}
```

---

## 25. Pact / Consumer-driven Contract Testing

Pact adalah tool **code-first consumer-driven contract testing**; dokumentasi Pact menjelaskan bahwa contract dihasilkan saat automated consumer tests dijalankan, dan pola ini menguji bagian komunikasi yang benar-benar digunakan consumer. ([Pact Docs](https://docs.pact.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Pact cocok ketika:

| Cocok | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Banyak consumer punya kebutuhan berbeda | Contract berdasarkan consumer interaction |
| Provider ingin tahu consumer mana terdampak | Pact broker/workflow |
| API tidak sepenuhnya provider-driven | Consumer menentukan ekspektasi |
| Ingin test interaction yang benar-benar dipakai | Avoid testing unused provider behavior |

Pact consumer test biasanya dimulai dari menulis unit test API client; dokumentasi Pact menyebut golden rule: mulai dari tujuan menulis unit test yang baik untuk API client, dan side effect-nya adalah menghasilkan contract untuk contract testing. ([Pact Docs](https://docs.pact.io/consumer?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 26. OpenAPI-driven vs Pact-driven

| Aspek | OpenAPI-driven | Pact consumer-driven |
|---|---|---|
| Source of truth | Provider/API contract spec | Consumer interaction contract |
| Style | Spec-first | Test/code-first |
| Cocok untuk | Standard enterprise API, generated client | Consumer-specific expectations |
| Test coverage | Seluruh API surface yang didefinisikan | Bagian yang dipakai consumer |
| Artifact | `openapi.yaml`, generated client | Pact file |
| Strength | Governance, docs, generation | Consumer impact visibility |
| Risk | Spec bisa tidak dites cukup | Butuh discipline per consumer |
| Best use | Provider-owned contract-first API | Consumer-driven integration safety |

Rekomendasi untuk stack Anda:

```text id="vo1s7g"
Jika perusahaan sudah contract-first OpenAPI:
  pakai OpenAPI sebagai provider-owned source of truth.

Tambahkan Pact atau consumer-driven contract bila:
  consumer sangat banyak,
  consumer behavior kritikal,
  provider sering mengubah API,
  impact analysis consumer penting.
```

---

## 27. Hybrid Strategy

Strategi paling praktis untuk enterprise microservices:

```text id="q967p9"
Provider-owned OpenAPI:
  defines official API contract

Generated client:
  ensures Java compatibility

Consumer adapter tests:
  ensure local behavior with mock downstream

Provider schema tests:
  ensure implementation matches OpenAPI

API diff:
  prevents breaking changes

Optional Pact:
  captures critical consumer interactions
```

Diagram:

```text id="18iim7"
OpenAPI Spec
  |
  +--> Generate Java client
  +--> Provider schema tests
  +--> Mock/stub definitions
  +--> API diff CI

Consumer Tests
  |
  +--> WireMock stubs aligned with OpenAPI
  +--> Optional Pact interactions

Provider Tests
  |
  +--> Jersey resource test
  +--> ExceptionMapper test
  +--> Response schema validation
```

---

## 28. Mock Server vs Contract Test

| Item | Mock server | Contract test |
|---|---|---|
| Tujuan | Simulasi downstream | Membuktikan agreement |
| Contoh | WireMock stub customer-service | Provider response validates OpenAPI |
| Risiko | Stub bisa drift dari spec | Lebih dekat ke contract |
| Cocok untuk | Consumer adapter/integration tests | CI gate provider/consumer |
| Harus dijaga | Stub align dengan OpenAPI | Schema/status/error coverage |

Rule:

```text id="j8tj2t"
WireMock stub harus dianggap test fixture yang mengikuti OpenAPI,
bukan kontrak baru yang hidup sendiri.
```

---

## 29. Mencegah Mock Drift

Mock drift terjadi ketika WireMock stub tidak lagi sesuai OpenAPI/provider.

Contoh:

```text id="3y2ao0"
OpenAPI:
  status field enum = SUBMITTED

WireMock:
  status = SUBMITED typo

Consumer test:
  either fails for wrong reason
  or worse, consumer adapts to wrong mock
```

Mitigasi:

| Cara | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Generate stubs dari examples | Jika platform mendukung |
| Validate mock responses against OpenAPI | Saat test startup |
| Use shared examples from spec | Jangan duplikasi manual |
| Keep fixtures versioned with contract | `customer-api-1.4.0` |
| Run provider schema tests | Provider tetap source of truth |
| Review mock changes with contract changes | PR discipline |

---

## 30. Test Data dan Examples

OpenAPI examples sebaiknya bukan dekorasi. Jadikan test fixture.

Example dalam OpenAPI:

```yaml id="tqoxjc"
components:
  examples:
    CustomerActive:
      value:
        customerId: CUST-001
        displayName: Acme Corp
        status: ACTIVE
```

WireMock stub bisa menggunakan payload yang sama:

```json id="h7wxi7"
{
  "customerId": "CUST-001",
  "displayName": "Acme Corp",
  "status": "ACTIVE"
}
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Examples harus valid schema | Jangan asal dokumentasi |
| Examples harus mencakup error | Not found, conflict, validation |
| Examples harus realistis | Jangan `foo`, `bar` untuk domain penting |
| Examples harus versioned | Ikut contract version |
| Examples boleh dipakai mock/test | Mengurangi drift |

---

## 31. Contract Test untuk Headers

Headers sering dilupakan.

Test request headers:

| Header | Test |
|---|---|
| `Authorization` | Sent and non-empty |
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Sent |
| `traceparent` | Sent jika tracing enabled |
| `X-Tenant-Id` | Sent dan sesuai context |
| `Idempotency-Key` | Required for command |
| `Accept` | `application/json` |
| `Content-Type` | `application/json` for body |

Test response headers:

| Header | Use case |
|---|---|
| `Location` | `201 Created`, `202 Accepted` |
| `Retry-After` | `429`, `503` |
| `ETag` | optimistic concurrency |
| `Deprecation` | deprecated endpoint |
| `Sunset` | API removal plan |
| correlation header | echo/debug if standard |

---

## 32. Contract Test untuk Pagination

List endpoint:

```text id="bmqr94"
GET /orders?page=0&size=50
```

Test:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Missing page/size | Default applied |
| size > max | `400` or max capped, per contract |
| negative page | `400` |
| valid request | response has `items`, `page`, `size`, `totalElements` |
| empty result | `items: []`, not `null` |
| sort invalid | `400` |
| cursor invalid | `400` |

Response schema:

```json id="375gib"
{
  "items": [],
  "page": 0,
  "size": 50,
  "totalElements": 0
}
```

---

## 33. Contract Test untuk Versioning / Deprecation

Jika endpoint deprecated:

```yaml id="l8tx23"
deprecated: true
```

Test governance:

| Check | Expected |
|---|---|
| Deprecated endpoint marked | `deprecated: true` |
| Replacement documented | Description/changelog |
| Response header | `Deprecation`, possibly `Sunset` |
| Usage metric | Deprecated usage observable |
| CI rule | New consumer usage blocked/reviewed |
| Removal | Only after migration window |

Compatibility test:

```text id="1y454p"
Compare current openapi.yaml to latest released openapi.yaml
Classify:
  additive
  documentation
  potentially breaking
  breaking
```

---

## 34. Contract Test untuk Security

Provider:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| No token | `401 AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED` |
| Invalid token | `401` |
| Valid token missing scope | `403 ACCESS_DENIED` |
| Tenant mismatch | `403` or masked `404`, per policy |
| Valid token allowed | success |
| Public health endpoint | no auth required |

Consumer:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Service token required | Authorization header sent |
| User token propagation | inbound user context forwarded/delegated |
| 401 token expired | refresh once if policy |
| 403 | no retry |
| Token not logged | logs scrubbed |

---

## 35. Contract Test untuk Camunda / Workflow HTTP Calls

Untuk workflow integration:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Downstream returns `409 INVALID_STATE` | BPMN business error / modeled branch |
| Downstream returns `422` | Business validation path |
| Downstream returns `503` | Retryable technical failure |
| Timeout on idempotent command | Retry/workflow policy |
| Timeout on non-idempotent command | Unknown outcome handled |
| `202 Accepted` | Store job/command id |
| Callback received twice | Dedup by event id |

Test khusus:

```text id="v5yeev"
Given external task calls payment-service
When payment-service returns 503
Then worker reports retryable failure
And does not complete task
And does not convert it to business success
```

---

## 36. CI Pipeline untuk Contract Testing

Contoh GitHub Actions/Jenkins stage konseptual:

```text id="f2c4tt"
validate-openapi
  -> lint-openapi
  -> openapi-diff
  -> generate-client
  -> compile-generated
  -> unit-test-mappers
  -> wiremock-consumer-tests
  -> provider-resource-tests
  -> schema-validation-tests
  -> publish-contract-artifact
  -> publish-client-artifact
```

Maven flow:

```bash id="p8fsko"
mvn clean verify
```

Gate rules:

| Failure | Action |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI invalid | Block PR |
| Generated client compile fail | Block PR |
| Adapter test fail | Block PR |
| Provider schema validation fail | Block PR |
| Breaking diff without major/v2 plan | Block PR |
| Deprecated endpoint new usage | Warn/block depending policy |
| Error response missing schema | Block PR |
| Security scheme missing | Block PR for protected endpoints |

---

## 37. Folder Structure

```text id="wu4gyi"
src/main/openapi
  └── order-api.yaml

src/test/resources/wiremock
  ├── mappings
  │   ├── customer-get-200.json
  │   ├── customer-get-404.json
  │   └── payment-create-503.json
  └── __files
      ├── customer-active.json
      └── error-customer-not-found.json

src/test/java
  ├── infrastructure/customer
  │   ├── CustomerGatewayWireMockTest.java
  │   ├── CustomerDtoMapperTest.java
  │   └── CustomerErrorMapperTest.java
  ├── api
  │   ├── OrderResourceContractTest.java
  │   └── ExceptionMapperContractTest.java
  └── contract
      └── OpenApiSchemaValidationTest.java
```

Jika contract dipublish sebagai artifact:

```text id="mg0n85"
com.company.api:customer-api-contract:1.4.0
com.company.api:customer-api-client:1.4.0
```

Consumer test harus jelas memakai versi contract mana.

---

## 38. Test Naming Convention

Gunakan nama test yang menjelaskan contract behavior:

```text id="dgdl5m"
shouldSendAuthorizationHeaderWhenCallingCustomerService
shouldReturnEmptyWhenCustomerServiceReturnsCustomerNotFound
shouldThrowDownstreamConflictWhenCancelOrderReturns409
shouldNotRetryCreatePaymentWithoutIdempotencyKey
shouldRetryGetCustomerOnceWhen503
shouldReturnRequestValidationErrorWhenCustomerIdMissing
shouldReturnProblemDetailSchemaForBusinessValidationError
shouldNotLeakStacktraceOnInternalServerError
```

Hindari:

```text id="nqhyqn"
test1
testGet
testError
```

---

## 39. Provider vs Consumer Responsibility

| Responsibility | Provider | Consumer |
|---|---:|---:|
| OpenAPI source of truth | Ya | Mengacu |
| Success response schema | Ya | Decode/map |
| Error response schema | Ya | Decode/map |
| Status code semantics | Ya | Branch/map |
| Generated client compatibility | Publish/maintain | Pin/update |
| Adapter mapping | Tidak | Ya |
| Retry/idempotency behavior | Define support | Apply safely |
| Auth requirement | Define/validate | Send context |
| Mock fixture | Bisa provide examples | Use/validate |
| Contract diff | Ya | Review impact |
| Provider state/business rules | Ya | Handle result |

---

## 40. Common Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Contract test hanya happy path | Error integration rusak |
| WireMock stub tidak sesuai OpenAPI | Consumer percaya mock palsu |
| Provider test deserialize DTO saja | Schema required/additional field bisa lolos |
| Tidak test error schema | Consumer error decoder rusak |
| Tidak test auth headers | Staging/prod gagal |
| Tidak test timeout/retry | Production resilience tidak valid |
| Tidak test idempotency key | Command duplicate |
| Pact/OpenAPI dipakai tanpa ownership jelas | Conflicting source of truth |
| Generated client tidak compile di CI | Spec tidak Java-compatible |
| API diff tidak ada | Breaking change lolos |
| Examples tidak valid schema | Docs/mock menyesatkan |
| `404` selalu dianggap failure test | Lookup normal jadi ambigu |
| Mock semua downstream selalu 200 | Consumer tidak siap error |
| E2E test dijadikan satu-satunya contract test | Lambat, flaky, diagnosis sulit |
| Test body penuh PII/token | Security risk |

---

## 41. Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI validation | Spec valid di CI |
| Lint | Naming/status/error/security rules enforced |
| Generation | Java client generated and compiled |
| Adapter tests | Success/error/timeout/retry covered |
| WireMock | Request matching includes method/path/header/body |
| Mock drift | Mock response validated/aligned with OpenAPI |
| Provider tests | Jersey resource returns contract-compliant response |
| Schema validation | Success and error response validated |
| ExceptionMapper | All important exceptions mapped |
| Security | 401/403/header propagation tested |
| Idempotency | Command key and retry behavior tested |
| Camunda | Business vs technical error mapping tested |
| Compatibility | API diff gate exists |
| Examples | Valid and reusable in tests |
| CI | Contract gates block bad PRs |
| Artifacts | Contract/client version pinned and published |

---

## 42. Ringkasan

Contract testing untuk HTTP microservice yang sehat mencakup:

```text id="0l1ain"
OpenAPI static validation
  + lint/style governance
  + generated Java client compile
  + consumer adapter tests
  + WireMock downstream mocks
  + provider Jersey resource tests
  + schema validation for success/error
  + security/idempotency/timeout tests
  + API diff compatibility gate
```

Kunci senior-level:

```text id="43z230"
OpenAPI is the contract.
WireMock is a test double.
Generated client is a compatibility artifact.
Provider tests prove implementation follows contract.
Consumer tests prove adapter handles contract outcomes.
CI prevents drift and breaking changes.
```

Kesimpulan:

> **Contract-first tanpa contract testing hanya memindahkan dokumentasi ke YAML. Contract-first yang benar membuat YAML itu menjadi executable governance: divalidasi, digenerate, dites, dan dicegah breaking di CI.**
