# Seri 15/16 - Observability untuk Downstream HTTP Calls

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada:

> **Bagaimana membuat outbound HTTP call ke downstream service terlihat jelas di log, metrics, trace, dashboard, alert, dan incident analysis.**

Tanpa observability, failure antar microservice terlihat seperti ini:

```text id="2g96hc"
order-service lambat
  kenapa?
    mungkin DB?
    mungkin customer-service?
    mungkin payment-service?
    mungkin retry?
    mungkin timeout?
    mungkin circuit breaker?
    mungkin auth?
```

Dengan observability yang benar:

```text id="evc1dp"
order-service latency naik
  -> downstream=customer-service
  -> operation=getCustomerById
  -> p95 latency naik dari 80ms ke 650ms
  -> retry attempts naik
  -> 503 rate naik
  -> circuit half-open/open
  -> incident jelas diarahkan ke dependency tertentu
```

OpenTelemetry adalah framework observability vendor-neutral untuk menghasilkan, mengumpulkan, dan mengekspor telemetry data seperti traces, metrics, dan logs. OpenTelemetry juga mendefinisikan semantic conventions untuk HTTP spans, metrics, dan logs. ([opentelemetry.io](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/)) ([opentelemetry.io](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/http/))

---

## 2. Observability Signals

```text id="04kmut"
Observability
  ├── Logs
  │   └── event detail, errorCode, correlationId
  │
  ├── Metrics
  │   └── rate, latency, error ratio, retry count
  │
  ├── Traces
  │   └── request journey across services
  │
  └── Events / Audit
      └── important business/security decisions
```

Untuk downstream HTTP call, minimal harus terlihat:

| Signal | Yang harus terlihat |
|---|---|
| Logs | downstream, operation, status, errorCode, latency, correlationId |
| Metrics | request count, error count, latency histogram, retry count, timeout count |
| Traces | client span per outbound call, propagated context |
| Dashboard | top slow downstream, error rate, retry/circuit/bulkhead |
| Alerts | timeout/error/circuit open/bulkhead rejection |
| Audit | untuk security/business critical calls |

---

## 3. Mental Model Observability Boundary

Observability paling baik dipasang di **gateway/adapter boundary**, bukan tersebar di use case atau generated client.

```text id="k6rjts"
Application Use Case
  |
  v
CustomerGateway
  |
  v
CustomerHttpGateway
  |
  +--> start timer
  +--> create trace span
  +--> inject correlation/trace headers
  +--> call generated Jersey/Feign client
  +--> classify result
  +--> record metric
  +--> write structured log on failure
  |
  v
Downstream HTTP Client
```

Kenapa di adapter?

| Di generated client | Di adapter/gateway |
|---|---|
| Tahu raw URL | Tahu logical operation |
| Tahu status code | Tahu domain classification |
| Tidak tahu retry semantics | Tahu query/command/idempotency |
| Sulit log business result | Bisa log `NotFound`, `Conflict`, `Rejected` |
| Tergantung generator | Stabil meski Jersey ↔ Feign berubah |

---

## 4. Golden Signals untuk Downstream HTTP

Untuk outbound HTTP, golden signals praktis:

| Signal | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Traffic | Berapa call per second ke downstream? |
| Latency | Berapa p50/p95/p99 latency per operation? |
| Errors | Error rate per status/errorCode/classification? |
| Saturation | Bulkhead penuh? Connection pool penuh? Thread habis? |
| Retries | Berapa attempt tambahan? |
| Timeouts | Timeout rate per downstream operation? |
| Circuit | Circuit closed/open/half-open? |
| Fallback | Seberapa sering fallback dipakai? |
| Auth failures | `401/403` spike? |
| Contract violations | Response malformed/unknown enum/null required? |

---

## 5. Naming Metrics

Gunakan nama metric yang konsisten.

Contoh:

```text id="xba8w8"
downstream_http_client_requests_total
downstream_http_client_request_duration_seconds
downstream_http_client_errors_total
downstream_http_client_retries_total
downstream_http_client_timeouts_total
downstream_http_client_circuit_open_total
downstream_http_client_bulkhead_rejected_total
downstream_http_client_fallback_used_total
downstream_http_client_contract_violations_total
```

Label/tag minimal:

| Tag | Contoh | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `downstream` | `customer-service` | Logical service name |
| `operation` | `getCustomerById` | Bukan raw URL penuh |
| `method` | `GET` | HTTP method |
| `status_code` | `200`, `404`, `503` | Boleh |
| `status_class` | `2xx`, `4xx`, `5xx` | Berguna untuk aggregate |
| `outcome` | `success`, `timeout`, `conflict` | Classification |
| `client` | `jersey`, `feign`, `generated` | Opsional |
| `attempt` | `1`, `2` | Hati-hati cardinality |
| `exception` | class sederhana | Jangan high-cardinality |

---

## 6. Hati-hati Cardinality

Jangan jadikan dynamic value sebagai label metric.

Bad:

```text id="098lav"
downstream_http_client_duration{
  customerId="CUST-123",
  orderId="ORD-999",
  url="/customers/CUST-123"
}
```

Ini membuat time series meledak.

Good:

```text id="ifacrw"
downstream_http_client_duration{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById",
  method="GET",
  path_template="/customers/{customerId}",
  status_class="2xx"
}
```

Rules:

| Jangan jadi tag metric | Boleh jadi log field |
|---|---|
| customerId | Ya, jika aman/masked |
| orderId | Ya, jika aman/masked |
| full URL with ID | Path template saja |
| raw error message | errorCode saja |
| correlationId | Log/trace, bukan metric |
| idempotencyKey raw | Hash/log limited, bukan metric |
| userId raw | Hash/log/audit, bukan metric |

---

## 7. Metric Types

Prometheus mendefinisikan counter sebagai cumulative metric yang hanya naik atau reset saat restart; counter cocok untuk jumlah request/error/retry. ([prometheus.io](https://prometheus.io/docs/concepts/metric_types/)) Micrometer juga menyatakan `Counter` melaporkan satu metric berupa count dan increment harus positif. ([docs.micrometer.io](https://docs.micrometer.io/micrometer/reference/concepts/counters.html))

Mapping praktis:

| Metric type | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| Counter | request total, error total, retry total, timeout total |
| Timer / Histogram | latency distribution |
| Gauge | current circuit state, bulkhead available capacity, in-flight calls |
| Distribution summary | payload size, response size |
| Long task timer | long-running operation, jarang untuk normal HTTP JSON call |

Prometheus gauge cocok untuk nilai yang bisa naik/turun, seperti in-flight request atau thread count; Micrometer juga menjelaskan gauge sebagai handle untuk mendapatkan current value. ([docs.micrometer.io](https://docs.micrometer.io/micrometer/reference/concepts/gauges.html))

---

## 8. Latency Histogram

Latency jangan hanya average.

Average bisa menipu:

```text id="nmycjm"
99 calls = 50ms
1 call = 5000ms
average ≈ 99.5ms
p99 ≈ 5000ms
```

Gunakan histogram/timer agar bisa melihat:

```text id="bp0f3n"
p50
p90
p95
p99
max
SLO burn
```

Metric:

```text id="evbnbx"
downstream_http_client_request_duration_seconds_bucket
downstream_http_client_request_duration_seconds_count
downstream_http_client_request_duration_seconds_sum
```

Bucket contoh untuk HTTP internal:

```text id="9wbe0u"
0.01s
0.025s
0.05s
0.1s
0.25s
0.5s
1s
2.5s
5s
10s
```

Sesuaikan dengan SLO service Anda.

---

## 9. Classification Lebih Penting daripada Status Mentah

`4xx` tidak selalu error sistem.

| Status | Classification | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `404 CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND` pada lookup | `business_not_found` | Bisa normal |
| `409 ORDER_ALREADY_SHIPPED` | `business_conflict` | Bukan downstream unhealthy |
| `422 BUSINESS_VALIDATION_FAILED` | `business_validation` | Expected rejection |
| `401/403` | `security_failure` | Auth/authz |
| `429` | `rate_limited` | Capacity/quota |
| `500` | `downstream_bug` | Provider error |
| `502/503/504` | `downstream_unavailable` | Infra/dependency |
| timeout | `timeout` | Latency/failure |
| malformed 2xx body | `contract_violation` | Provider/schema issue |
| circuit open | `circuit_open` | Fail fast |
| bulkhead rejected | `bulkhead_rejected` | Saturation |

Metric tag:

```text id="j0j3fk"
outcome="business_not_found"
outcome="downstream_timeout"
outcome="contract_violation"
```

---

## 10. Metrics Wrapper Example

```java id="txo9ly"
public final class DownstreamCallObserver {

    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;

    public <T> T observe(
            DownstreamOperation operation,
            Supplier<T> supplier
    ) {
        long startNanos = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            T result = supplier.get();

            record(
                    operation,
                    "success",
                    null,
                    startNanos
            );

            return result;
        } catch (DownstreamException ex) {
            record(
                    operation,
                    ex.outcome(),
                    ex.statusCode().orElse(null),
                    startNanos
            );

            throw ex;
        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
            record(
                    operation,
                    "unexpected_client_error",
                    null,
                    startNanos
            );

            throw ex;
        }
    }

    private void record(
            DownstreamOperation operation,
            String outcome,
            Integer statusCode,
            long startNanos
    ) {
        Tags tags = Tags.of(
                "downstream", operation.downstream(),
                "operation", operation.operation(),
                "method", operation.method(),
                "outcome", outcome,
                "status_class", statusCode == null
                        ? "none"
                        : (statusCode / 100) + "xx"
        );

        meterRegistry.counter(
                "downstream_http_client_requests_total",
                tags
        ).increment();

        meterRegistry.timer(
                "downstream_http_client_request_duration",
                tags
        ).record(
                System.nanoTime() - startNanos,
                TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS
        );
    }
}
```

Catatan: Micrometer timer sudah membawa count dan timing; dokumentasi Micrometer bahkan mengingatkan agar tidak menghitung ulang sesuatu yang bisa diukur dengan `Timer` atau `DistributionSummary`, karena keduanya sudah mempublikasikan count event. ([docs.micrometer.io](https://docs.micrometer.io/micrometer/reference/concepts/counters.html))

---

## 11. Logs: Structured, Sparse, Useful

Jangan log setiap success call dengan body penuh. Log success secara sampling jika perlu.

Log failure dengan structured fields:

```json id="z0kqlv"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_HTTP_ERROR",
  "downstream": "customer-service",
  "operation": "getCustomerById",
  "method": "GET",
  "pathTemplate": "/customers/{customerId}",
  "status": 404,
  "errorCode": "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND",
  "outcome": "business_not_found",
  "latencyMs": 42,
  "attempt": 1,
  "correlationId": "01JABC...",
  "traceId": "7b3f..."
}
```

Untuk system failure:

```json id="r3odk6"
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_HTTP_ERROR",
  "downstream": "payment-service",
  "operation": "createPayment",
  "method": "POST",
  "pathTemplate": "/payments",
  "status": 503,
  "errorCode": "DOWNSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE",
  "outcome": "downstream_unavailable",
  "latencyMs": 1000,
  "attempt": 1,
  "maxAttempts": 1,
  "idempotent": false,
  "correlationId": "01JABC...",
  "traceId": "7b3f..."
}
```

---

## 12. Log Level Guideline

| Event | Level umum |
|---|---|
| Expected `404` lookup miss | `DEBUG`/`INFO`, tergantung volume |
| `409` business conflict | `INFO`/`WARN` |
| `422` business validation | `INFO` |
| `400` caused by our adapter bug | `ERROR` |
| `401/403` unexpected downstream auth failure | `WARN`/`ERROR` |
| Timeout | `WARN`/`ERROR` |
| `503/504` | `WARN`/`ERROR` |
| Circuit open | `WARN` |
| Bulkhead rejected | `WARN` |
| Contract violation | `ERROR` |
| Unhandled exception | `ERROR` |

Rule:

```text id="wxn4g5"
Log severity should reflect actionability,
not just HTTP status code.
```

`404` pada lookup bisa normal. `400` dari downstream bisa jadi bug consumer/adapter dan lebih actionable.

---

## 13. Jangan Log Data Sensitif

Do not log:

```text id="9yd8qf"
Authorization header
Bearer token
refresh token
client secret
private key
password
full JWT
full request/response body with PII
payment/card data
raw idempotency key if sensitive
```

Log aman:

```text id="ahbu33"
downstream
operation
status
errorCode
latency
attempt
correlationId
traceId
tenantId if allowed
subject hash if needed
idempotencyKey hash if needed
```

Example:

```text id="orrjk2"
idempotencyKeyHash=sha256:9f86d081...
```

Bukan:

```text id="yebfo7"
Idempotency-Key=PAYREQ-USER-123-ORDER-999
```

---

## 14. Correlation ID

Correlation ID adalah identifier aplikasi untuk menghubungkan log antar service.

Flow:

```text id="ubtebp"
Inbound request
  X-Correlation-Id: 01JABC
        |
        v
order-service logs correlationId=01JABC
        |
        v
outbound customer-service call
  X-Correlation-Id: 01JABC
        |
        v
customer-service logs correlationId=01JABC
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Jika inbound ada, propagate | Jangan buat baru sembarangan |
| Jika tidak ada, generate | Entry service/gateway |
| Jangan jadi metric label | High cardinality |
| Masukkan ke logs | RCA |
| Bisa echo di error response | Support/debugging |
| Jangan menggantikan trace ID | Correlation ID dan trace ID saling melengkapi |

---

## 15. Trace Context

Distributed tracing menunjukkan perjalanan request antar service.

OpenTelemetry mendefinisikan context propagation dan instrumentation. OpenTelemetry documentation menyebut OTel sebagai vendor-neutral framework untuk telemetry data, dan HTTP semantic conventions mendefinisikan conventions untuk HTTP spans, metrics, dan logs. ([opentelemetry.io](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/)) ([opentelemetry.io](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/http/))

Header umum:

```text id="3k774x"
traceparent
tracestate
```

Trace tree:

```text id="yhe9g9"
order-service inbound span
  |
  +-- DB query span
  |
  +-- HTTP CLIENT span: customer-service getCustomerById
  |
  +-- HTTP CLIENT span: payment-service createPayment
```

Manfaat:

| Manfaat | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Menemukan dependency lambat | Span duration |
| Melihat retry waterfall | Multiple spans/attempt events |
| Correlate errors | Status/error attributes |
| Debug cross-service | Trace ID |
| Find critical path | Which downstream dominates latency |

---

## 16. Span Naming

Span name sebaiknya low-cardinality.

Bad:

```text id="mi0n7e"
GET /customers/CUST-001
```

Good:

```text id="4swruz"
HTTP GET customer-service /customers/{customerId}
```

Atau:

```text id="3s37zv"
customer-service getCustomerById
```

Attributes:

```text id="9ggzrb"
server.address = customer-service
http.request.method = GET
url.template = /customers/{customerId}
http.response.status_code = 200
downstream.operation = getCustomerById
error.type = DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT
```

OpenTelemetry HTTP semantic conventions mendefinisikan atribut untuk HTTP telemetry; ikuti naming standard platform jika sudah ada. ([opentelemetry.io](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/http/))

---

## 17. Manual Span di Adapter

Jika auto-instrumentation belum cukup, tambahkan span manual di adapter.

```java id="y7447h"
public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
    Span span = tracer.spanBuilder("customer-service getCustomerById")
            .setSpanKind(SpanKind.CLIENT)
            .setAttribute("downstream.service", "customer-service")
            .setAttribute("downstream.operation", "getCustomerById")
            .setAttribute("http.request.method", "GET")
            .setAttribute("url.template", "/customers/{customerId}")
            .startSpan();

    try (Scope scope = span.makeCurrent()) {
        Optional<CustomerProfile> result = invokeFindById(customerId);
        span.setAttribute("outcome", result.isPresent()
                ? "found"
                : "not_found");
        return result;
    } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
        span.recordException(ex);
        span.setStatus(StatusCode.ERROR);
        throw ex;
    } finally {
        span.end();
    }
}
```

Catatan: jika Java agent / library instrumentation sudah membuat HTTP client span otomatis, hindari duplicate span yang membingungkan. Bisa gunakan manual span sebagai parent “logical downstream operation” dan auto span sebagai child, atau cukup tambahkan attributes/events ke existing span jika platform mendukung.

---

## 18. Auto-instrumentation vs Manual Instrumentation

| Approach | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| Java agent auto-instrumentation | Cepat, minim code | Operation name bisa kurang domain-aware |
| Library instrumentation | Lebih kontrol | Perlu integrasi |
| Manual instrumentation di adapter | Domain-aware | Perlu discipline |
| Hybrid | Best of both | Risiko duplicate spans |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="hbk10k"
Use auto-instrumentation for basic HTTP spans.
Use adapter-level instrumentation for logical operation classification,
errorCode, retry, idempotency, fallback, circuit/bulkhead events.
```

OpenTelemetry Java instrumentation menyediakan instrumentasi untuk banyak library; untuk Java HTTP Client, dokumentasi library instrumentation menyebut wrapper HttpClient berbasis OpenTelemetry spans dan context propagation. ([github.com](https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-java-instrumentation/blob/main/instrumentation/java-http-client/library/README.md))

---

## 19. Trace Events untuk Retry

Tambahkan event pada span logical operation:

```text id="bb94mg"
event=downstream.retry
attributes:
  attempt=2
  reason=503
  backoffMs=100
```

Event lain:

```text id="7592ds"
downstream.timeout
downstream.circuit_open
downstream.bulkhead_rejected
downstream.fallback_used
downstream.contract_violation
downstream.idempotency_conflict
```

Ini membuat trace berguna saat RCA:

```text id="ya4qhn"
call took 900ms
  attempt 1: 503 after 100ms
  backoff 100ms
  attempt 2: 200 after 650ms
```

Tanpa event, trace hanya terlihat “lama”.

---

## 20. Observability untuk Retry

Metrics:

```text id="nd3ig4"
downstream_http_client_retries_total{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById",
  reason="503"
}
```

Logs:

```json id="wkfqyg"
{
  "level": "INFO",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_HTTP_RETRY",
  "downstream": "customer-service",
  "operation": "getCustomerById",
  "attempt": 2,
  "maxAttempts": 2,
  "reason": "503",
  "backoffMs": 100,
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Count retry attempts | Retry bisa memperbesar traffic |
| Log only meaningful retries | Hindari noise volume tinggi |
| Track reason | Timeout vs 503 vs connect error |
| Track final outcome | Retry success vs final failure |
| Alert on retry spike | Early signal downstream degradation |

---

## 21. Observability untuk Timeout

Metric:

```text id="be1a8r"
downstream_http_client_timeouts_total{
  downstream="payment-service",
  operation="createPayment",
  timeout_type="read"
}
```

Log:

```json id="yktsu2"
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT",
  "downstream": "payment-service",
  "operation": "createPayment",
  "timeoutType": "read",
  "timeoutMs": 1000,
  "attempt": 1,
  "idempotent": false,
  "outcome": "unknown_command_outcome",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Command timeout harus membedakan:

| Query timeout | Command timeout |
|---|---|
| Bisa retry lebih aman | Outcome bisa unknown |
| Biasanya fail as unavailable | Perlu idempotency/status query |
| Less audit sensitivity | Audit/business impact lebih besar |

---

## 22. Observability untuk Circuit Breaker

Metrics:

```text id="xaazcn"
downstream_circuit_state{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="lookup"
}

downstream_circuit_open_total{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="lookup"
}
```

Log state transition:

```json id="rmlk2e"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_CIRCUIT_STATE_CHANGED",
  "downstream": "customer-service",
  "operation": "lookup",
  "from": "CLOSED",
  "to": "OPEN",
  "failureRate": 75.0,
  "slowCallRate": 60.0,
  "correlationId": "system"
}
```

Dashboard:

```text id="bjm5gl"
Circuit state by downstream
Circuit open events over time
Failure rate before open
Slow call rate before open
Half-open success/failure
```

---

## 23. Observability untuk Bulkhead

Metrics:

```text id="13mgx3"
downstream_bulkhead_in_flight{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="lookup"
}

downstream_bulkhead_rejected_total{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="lookup"
}

downstream_bulkhead_available_concurrent_calls{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="lookup"
}
```

Log:

```json id="265bya"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_BULKHEAD_REJECTED",
  "downstream": "customer-service",
  "operation": "lookup",
  "maxConcurrentCalls": 40,
  "outcome": "bulkhead_rejected",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Interpretasi:

| Symptom | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Bulkhead rejected naik | Caller pressure tinggi atau downstream lambat |
| In-flight stuck tinggi | Latency/downstream hang |
| Rejections + low traffic | Bulkhead too small or leak |
| Rejections + timeouts | Dependency degradation |

---

## 24. Observability untuk Fallback

Fallback harus terlihat.

Metric:

```text id="m7s73q"
downstream_fallback_used_total{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById",
  fallback="customer_snapshot_cache"
}
```

Log:

```json id="kwjqsf"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_FALLBACK_USED",
  "downstream": "customer-service",
  "operation": "getCustomerById",
  "fallback": "customer_snapshot_cache",
  "freshnessSeconds": 3600,
  "reason": "DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Rule:

```text id="foi43f"
Fallback is degraded behavior.
If it is invisible, business may think system is healthy when it is not.
```

---

## 25. Observability untuk Contract Violation

Contract violation contoh:

```text id="bf7tnq"
HTTP 200
body missing required field customerId
```

Metric:

```text id="2ulk7g"
downstream_contract_violations_total{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById",
  violation="missing_required_field"
}
```

Log:

```json id="n5cylv"
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_CONTRACT_VIOLATION",
  "downstream": "customer-service",
  "operation": "getCustomerById",
  "violation": "missing_required_field",
  "field": "customerId",
  "status": 200,
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Do not silently map invalid response into domain object.

---

## 26. Observability untuk Security Failures

Metrics:

```text id="vcceph"
downstream_security_failures_total{
  downstream="customer-service",
  operation="getCustomerById",
  status="403",
  errorCode="ACCESS_DENIED"
}
```

Safe log:

```json id="bu3mck"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_SECURITY_FAILURE",
  "downstream": "customer-service",
  "operation": "getCustomerById",
  "status": 403,
  "errorCode": "ACCESS_DENIED",
  "callerService": "order-service",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "subjectHash": "sha256:...",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Never log token.

---

## 27. Dashboard Produksi

Dashboard downstream HTTP minimal:

```text id="g9vlfc"
1. Request rate by downstream/operation
2. Latency p50/p95/p99 by downstream/operation
3. Error rate by outcome/status_class
4. Timeout rate
5. Retry rate and retry success/failure
6. Circuit breaker state
7. Bulkhead rejection/in-flight
8. Fallback usage
9. Contract violation count
10. Security failure rate
```

Tabel dashboard:

| Panel | Query idea |
|---|---|
| Top slow downstream | p95 latency by downstream |
| Top error downstream | error ratio by downstream |
| Retry amplification | retries / original requests |
| Circuit status | current state gauges |
| Bulkhead saturation | in-flight / max |
| Timeout heatmap | timeout rate per operation |
| Contract violations | count by provider/operation |
| Auth failures | 401/403 by downstream |
| Fallback usage | count by fallback type |
| Dependency map | service graph from traces |

---

## 28. Alerting

Alert jangan hanya “5xx tinggi”. Buat alert actionable.

Contoh alert:

| Alert | Trigger |
|---|---|
| Downstream high error rate | `5xx/timeout > threshold` per operation |
| Downstream latency SLO burn | p95/p99 above budget |
| Circuit open | circuit state open for critical dependency |
| Bulkhead rejection | rejection rate above threshold |
| Retry spike | retry ratio abnormal |
| Fallback spike | fallback usage abnormal |
| Contract violation | any violation for critical API |
| Auth failure spike | 401/403 sudden increase |
| Timeout spike | timeout rate above baseline |
| Unknown command outcome | timeout on non-idempotent command |

Alert message should include:

```text id="udj8ao"
downstream
operation
latency/error/timeout value
time window
example trace link
runbook link
recent deployment info if available
```

---

## 29. SLO untuk Downstream Calls

Contoh SLO internal:

```text id="rjr6kb"
customer-service getCustomerById:
  availability from caller perspective: 99.9%
  p95 latency: < 300ms
  timeout rate: < 0.1%
```

Payment command:

```text id="2z076w"
payment-service createPayment:
  successful or deterministic failure: 99.95%
  unknown outcome rate: < 0.01%
  p95 latency: < 1500ms
```

Perhatikan:

| SLO | Tidak sama dengan |
|---|---|
| Caller-observed downstream success | Provider internal uptime |
| Deterministic outcome | Always success |
| Error budget | Semua error sama |
| Business rejection | System failure |

`409/422` mungkin bukan SLO failure jika itu expected business rejection.

---

## 30. Trace Sampling

Tidak semua request harus disimpan penuh, tetapi error/slow traces penting.

Sampling strategy:

| Request type | Sampling |
|---|---|
| Normal success high volume | Low sample |
| Error response | High/always sample |
| Timeout | Always sample |
| Contract violation | Always sample |
| Circuit open/fallback | Always sample |
| Critical payment/workflow | Higher sample |
| Debug mode per correlation ID | On demand |

Jangan mengandalkan logs penuh untuk mengganti traces; keduanya punya peran berbeda.

---

## 31. Jersey Client Instrumentation Points

Untuk Jersey Client/manual Jersey:

| Concern | Instrumentation point |
|---|---|
| Header propagation | `ClientRequestFilter` |
| Response metadata | `ClientResponseFilter` |
| Latency/logical outcome | Adapter wrapper |
| Error body classification | Error mapper |
| Timeout | Exception mapper/policy |
| Trace context | OTel instrumentation/filter |
| Metrics | Adapter/policy |
| Circuit/bulkhead | Resilience policy |

Example request filter:

```java id="8y5d03"
public final class TraceContextRequestFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final TraceHeaderProvider traceHeaderProvider;

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        traceHeaderProvider.currentTraceparent()
                .ifPresent(traceparent -> requestContext.getHeaders()
                        .putSingle("traceparent", traceparent));
    }
}
```

---

## 32. Feign Instrumentation Points

Untuk Feign:

| Concern | Instrumentation point |
|---|---|
| Header propagation | `RequestInterceptor` |
| Error mapping | `ErrorDecoder` |
| Encode/decode errors | Decoder/ErrorDecoder wrapper |
| Timeout/retry | Feign options + external policy |
| Latency/logical outcome | Gateway wrapper |
| Logs | Feign logger + structured gateway logs |
| Trace context | RequestInterceptor / OTel instrumentation |
| Metrics | Gateway/policy |

Example:

```java id="j0iavw"
public final class TraceFeignInterceptor implements RequestInterceptor {

    private final TraceHeaderProvider traceHeaderProvider;

    @Override
    public void apply(RequestTemplate template) {
        traceHeaderProvider.currentTraceparent()
                .ifPresent(value -> template.header("traceparent", value));
    }
}
```

---

## 33. Generated Client Instrumentation

Generated client bisa berubah saat regenerate. Jangan patch generated code untuk observability.

Bad:

```text id="ln81ro"
Edit generated CustomersApi.java
add logging/timer manually
```

Better:

```text id="t6harp"
Wrap generated client in adapter:
  CustomerHttpGateway
    -> observe()
    -> generated CustomersApi.getCustomerById()
```

If needed, customize:

| Need | Better place |
|---|---|
| Add auth header | ApiClient config/filter/interceptor/factory |
| Add correlation ID | filter/interceptor |
| Add metrics | adapter/policy |
| Add error mapping | adapter/error decoder |
| Add tracing | OTel instrumentation or adapter span |
| Add retry | resilience policy |
| Add logging | wrapper structured logs |

---

## 34. Example End-to-End Adapter Observability

```java id="p7iqgy"
public final class CustomerHttpGateway implements CustomerGateway {

    private final CustomersApi api;
    private final CustomerDtoMapper mapper;
    private final CustomerErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final DownstreamCallObserver observer;

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerProfile> findById(CustomerId customerId) {
        DownstreamOperation operation = DownstreamOperation.query(
                "customer-service",
                "getCustomerById",
                "GET",
                "/customers/{customerId}"
        );

        return observer.observe(
                operation,
                () -> invokeFindById(customerId)
        );
    }

    private Optional<CustomerProfile> invokeFindById(CustomerId customerId) {
        try {
            CustomerResponse response =
                    api.getCustomerById(customerId.value());

            return Optional.of(mapper.toProfile(response));
        } catch (ApiException exception) {
            return errorMapper.mapFindByIdError(exception, customerId);
        }
    }
}
```

Observer handles:

```text id="2hgn2p"
timer
counter
structured failure logs
span attributes/events
classification
```

---

## 35. Example DownstreamOperation

```java id="00q1r2"
public record DownstreamOperation(
        String downstream,
        String operation,
        String method,
        String pathTemplate,
        OperationType type,
        boolean idempotent
) {
    public static DownstreamOperation query(
            String downstream,
            String operation,
            String method,
            String pathTemplate
    ) {
        return new DownstreamOperation(
                downstream,
                operation,
                method,
                pathTemplate,
                OperationType.QUERY,
                true
        );
    }

    public static DownstreamOperation command(
            String downstream,
            String operation,
            String method,
            String pathTemplate,
            boolean idempotent
    ) {
        return new DownstreamOperation(
                downstream,
                operation,
                method,
                pathTemplate,
                OperationType.COMMAND,
                idempotent
        );
    }
}
```

This gives every log/metric/span consistent identity.

---

## 36. Example Classification Exception

```java id="iglkql"
public abstract class DownstreamException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String downstream;
    private final String operation;
    private final String outcome;
    private final Integer statusCode;
    private final String errorCode;

    protected DownstreamException(
            String downstream,
            String operation,
            String outcome,
            Integer statusCode,
            String errorCode,
            Throwable cause
    ) {
        super(outcome, cause);
        this.downstream = downstream;
        this.operation = operation;
        this.outcome = outcome;
        this.statusCode = statusCode;
        this.errorCode = errorCode;
    }

    public String outcome() {
        return outcome;
    }

    public Optional<Integer> statusCode() {
        return Optional.ofNullable(statusCode);
    }

    public Optional<String> errorCode() {
        return Optional.ofNullable(errorCode);
    }
}
```

Classification drives:

```text id="juzp76"
metrics tag
log level
retry decision
circuit failure counting
alert routing
```

---

## 37. PromQL Examples

Request rate:

```promql id="emrr4q"
sum by (downstream, operation) (
  rate(downstream_http_client_requests_total[5m])
)
```

Error ratio:

```promql id="cjxae5"
sum by (downstream, operation) (
  rate(downstream_http_client_requests_total{outcome=~"downstream_.*|timeout|contract_violation"}[5m])
)
/
sum by (downstream, operation) (
  rate(downstream_http_client_requests_total[5m])
)
```

p95 latency:

```promql id="bqnmg0"
histogram_quantile(
  0.95,
  sum by (le, downstream, operation) (
    rate(downstream_http_client_request_duration_seconds_bucket[5m])
  )
)
```

Retry ratio:

```promql id="pwbxbn"
sum by (downstream, operation) (
  rate(downstream_http_client_retries_total[5m])
)
/
sum by (downstream, operation) (
  rate(downstream_http_client_requests_total[5m])
)
```

Timeout rate:

```promql id="4ux6no"
sum by (downstream, operation) (
  rate(downstream_http_client_timeouts_total[5m])
)
```

---

## 38. Runbook-Oriented Logs

A good log should answer:

```text id="bav2fb"
what failed?
where?
which downstream?
which operation?
what status/errorCode?
how long?
was it retried?
is command outcome unknown?
what correlation/trace id?
what should on-call check next?
```

Bad log:

```text id="yr7hfo"
java.lang.RuntimeException: failed
```

Good log:

```json id="5hb683"
{
  "level": "ERROR",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_UNKNOWN_COMMAND_OUTCOME",
  "downstream": "payment-service",
  "operation": "createPayment",
  "method": "POST",
  "pathTemplate": "/payments",
  "timeoutType": "read",
  "timeoutMs": 1500,
  "attempt": 1,
  "maxAttempts": 1,
  "idempotent": false,
  "action": "query payment status before retry",
  "correlationId": "01J...",
  "traceId": "..."
}
```

---

## 39. Observability untuk Camunda / Workflow HTTP Calls

Tambahkan workflow context:

| Field | Contoh |
|---|---|
| `processDefinitionKey` | `order-fulfillment` |
| `activityId` | `chargePaymentTask` |
| `businessKey` | `ORD-001` |
| `externalTaskId` | jika aman |
| `retryCount` | remaining retries |
| `bpmnErrorCode` | `PAYMENT_REJECTED` |
| `technicalFailure` | true/false |

Log:

```json id="4e7wcw"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "event": "WORKFLOW_DOWNSTREAM_HTTP_ERROR",
  "processDefinitionKey": "order-fulfillment",
  "activityId": "chargePaymentTask",
  "businessKey": "ORD-001",
  "downstream": "payment-service",
  "operation": "createPayment",
  "status": 503,
  "outcome": "downstream_unavailable",
  "retryCount": 2,
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Rule:

```text id="ktzked"
Workflow logs must distinguish:
  business error -> modeled BPMN path
  technical error -> retry/incident
```

---

## 40. Observability untuk Idempotency

For command:

```text id="vhddq0"
idempotencyKeyHash
commandId
requestHashMatch
duplicateReplay
idempotencyConflict
```

Metric:

```text id="qec3og"
downstream_idempotency_conflicts_total{
  downstream="payment-service",
  operation="createPayment"
}
```

Log:

```json id="t3k3c4"
{
  "level": "WARN",
  "event": "DOWNSTREAM_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
  "downstream": "payment-service",
  "operation": "createPayment",
  "status": 409,
  "errorCode": "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
  "idempotencyKeyHash": "sha256:...",
  "correlationId": "01J..."
}
```

Never log raw idempotency key if it embeds user/order/payment data.

---

## 41. Production Triage Cheatsheet

| Symptom | First checks |
|---|---|
| API latency high | p95/p99 downstream latency by operation |
| Error rate high | outcome/status/errorCode split |
| CPU low but latency high | Thread waiting, downstream timeout, bulkhead saturation |
| Retry spike | dependency 503/timeout, retry storm risk |
| Circuit open | dependency unhealthy or config too sensitive |
| Bulkhead rejected | downstream slow or limit too low |
| Many 401 | token refresh/issuer/audience problem |
| Many 403 | scope/permission/tenant policy problem |
| Contract violations | provider deploy/schema mismatch |
| Unknown command outcomes | command timeout without idempotency/status query |
| Camunda incidents | technical failure mapping/retry/idempotency |
| Fallback spike | downstream outage hidden from business flow |

---

## 42. Testing Observability

Test observability behavior too.

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Success call | request metric increments |
| 404 lookup | outcome=`business_not_found`, not system error |
| 503 | outcome=`downstream_unavailable` |
| Timeout | timeout metric increments |
| Retry | retry metric increments and attempts logged |
| Circuit open | circuit state metric/log emitted |
| Bulkhead reject | rejection metric/log emitted |
| Contract violation | contract violation metric/log emitted |
| Security failure | no token in logs |
| Correlation ID | propagated and logged |
| Trace context | outbound header present |

Example log safety test:

```text id="xgv0p5"
Given Authorization=Bearer secret-token
When downstream returns 403
Then captured logs do not contain "secret-token"
```

---

## 43. Common Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Metrics only at inbound API | Tidak tahu downstream mana lambat |
| Label metric pakai customerId/orderId | Cardinality explosion |
| Log full request/response body | PII/token leak |
| All 4xx counted as system errors | False alert |
| 404 lookup logged ERROR | Noise |
| No retry metrics | Retry storm tidak terlihat |
| No timeout metrics | Latency RCA sulit |
| No circuit/bulkhead metrics | Resilience invisible |
| No contract violation metric | Schema drift tersembunyi |
| Generated client patched for logging | Hilang saat regenerate |
| No trace propagation | Cross-service RCA sulit |
| Full URL as span name | High cardinality traces |
| Fallback invisible | Degraded mode tidak diketahui |
| Average latency only | Tail latency tersembunyi |
| Alert on raw 5xx only | Timeout/retry/circuit missed |
| Logs without correlationId | Sulit join antar service |

---

## 44. Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Metrics | Request, duration, error, retry, timeout tersedia |
| Tags | Low-cardinality: downstream, operation, method, status_class, outcome |
| Latency | Histogram/timer, p95/p99 visible |
| Logs | Structured, correlationId, traceId, errorCode |
| Security | No token/secret/PII logging |
| Traces | Outbound client spans/context propagation |
| Retry | Attempts/reasons/backoff visible |
| Timeout | Timeout type and budget visible |
| Circuit breaker | State transition and state gauge visible |
| Bulkhead | In-flight/rejected visible |
| Fallback | Usage visible and labeled |
| Contract violation | Count/log/alert for critical APIs |
| Auth failures | 401 vs 403 separated |
| Camunda | workflow context included for worker/delegate calls |
| Dashboard | Top slow/error downstream panels |
| Alerts | Actionable, operation-specific |
| Tests | Observability behavior verified |

---

## 45. Ringkasan

Observability untuk downstream HTTP call harus menjawab:

```text id="0ycb3l"
Who did we call?
Which operation?
How long did it take?
What happened?
Was it retried?
Was it timeout?
Was circuit open?
Was bulkhead full?
Was fallback used?
Was it business rejection or system failure?
Which correlation/trace id can prove it?
```

Pattern sehat:

```text id="1huq5o"
Gateway / Adapter
  + DownstreamOperation metadata
  + metrics timer/counter
  + structured logs
  + trace span/context
  + error classification
  + retry/circuit/bulkhead events
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Observability bukan menambah log. Observability adalah membuat failure model terlihat: latency, retry, timeout, status, errorCode, circuit, bulkhead, fallback, dan contract violation harus bisa dijawab cepat saat incident.**

# Seri 16/16 - Production Governance: CI Checks, API Diff, Release Strategy, Kubernetes/AWS/Azure/On-prem Considerations

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri penutup ini menyatukan semua materi sebelumnya menjadi **production governance model**:

> **Bagaimana memastikan HTTP communication antar microservice yang contract-first tetap aman saat API berubah, client digenerate ulang, service dideploy, traffic dialihkan, dan dependency gagal di production.**

OpenAPI Specification mendefinisikan standar language-agnostic untuk mendeskripsikan HTTP APIs sehingga manusia dan komputer bisa memahami capability service tanpa akses ke source code, dokumentasi tambahan, atau inspeksi network traffic. Karena itu, di production governance, OpenAPI harus diperlakukan sebagai **contract artifact**, bukan sekadar dokumentasi. ([OpenAPI Initiative Publications](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Big Picture Governance

```text
Production HTTP Governance
  ├── API Contract Governance
  │   ├── OpenAPI validation
  │   ├── lint/style rules
  │   ├── compatibility diff
  │   ├── SemVer/versioning
  │   └── deprecation/sunset policy
  │
  ├── Build & CI Governance
  │   ├── generate Java client
  │   ├── compile generated source
  │   ├── adapter tests
  │   ├── provider schema tests
  │   └── publish contract/client artifacts
  │
  ├── Runtime Governance
  │   ├── timeout/retry/idempotency
  │   ├── auth/token/mTLS
  │   ├── observability
  │   ├── circuit breaker/bulkhead
  │   └── fallback rules
  │
  ├── Release Governance
  │   ├── backward-compatible deploy
  │   ├── rolling/canary/blue-green
  │   ├── consumer migration
  │   ├── rollback
  │   └── usage tracking
  │
  └── Platform Governance
      ├── Kubernetes
      ├── on-prem
      ├── AWS/Azure
      ├── network/security
      └── artifact/config/secret management
```

Core idea:

```text
Contract-first production readiness =
  contract validity
  + generated client compatibility
  + adapter boundary
  + resilience policy
  + security propagation
  + observability
  + safe release process
```

---

## 3. Source of Truth

Harus jelas mana yang menjadi source of truth.

| Artifact | Source of truth untuk |
|---|---|
| `openapi.yaml` | HTTP API contract |
| `pom.xml` / parent BOM | Java dependency/plugin version |
| generated client artifact | Consumer Java integration artifact |
| changelog | Contract evolution history |
| API diff report | Compatibility decision |
| adapter tests | Consumer behavior |
| provider schema tests | Provider implementation correctness |
| deployment manifest | Runtime rollout |
| config/secrets | Environment-specific behavior |
| dashboard/alerts | Production health |

Anti-pattern:

```text
Swagger UI terlihat benar,
tapi generated client tidak compile,
consumer adapter tidak dites,
provider error body beda dari OpenAPI,
dan API diff tidak ada.
```

---

## 4. Contract Artifact Governance

Minimal contract artifact:

```text
customer-api
  ├── src/main/openapi/customer-api.yaml
  ├── CHANGELOG.md
  ├── compatibility-policy.md
  ├── examples/
  │   ├── customer-active.json
  │   └── customer-not-found-error.json
  └── pom.xml
```

Artifact yang dipublish:

```text
com.company.api:customer-api-contract:1.4.0
com.company.api:customer-api-java-client:1.4.0
```

Rules:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Contract version explicit | `info.version` dan artifact version jelas |
| Client artifact follows contract | Consumer bisa pin versi |
| Changelog wajib | Perubahan API bisa diaudit |
| Examples valid schema | Bisa dipakai docs/mock/test |
| Error schema reusable | Error contract konsisten |
| Security scheme explicit | Auth tidak hanya di description |
| Deprecation explicit | `deprecated: true`, migration note |
| API owner jelas | Review tidak anonim |

---

## 5. Semantic Versioning untuk Contract

Semantic Versioning memakai format `MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH`: `MAJOR` untuk incompatible API changes, `MINOR` untuk backward-compatible functionality, dan `PATCH` untuk backward-compatible bug fixes. ([Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk HTTP API contract:

| Version bump | Contoh |
|---|---|
| `PATCH` | Description/example diperbaiki, tidak ubah contract behavior |
| `MINOR` | Tambah optional response field, tambah endpoint baru, tambah optional query param |
| `MAJOR` | Rename/remove field, ubah type, tambah required request field, ubah status/error semantics |

Rule:

```text
Contract version harus mencerminkan impact ke consumer,
bukan sekadar mengikuti Docker image version.
```

Jangan samakan:

```text
service image version != API contract version != generated client version
```

---

## 6. API Diff Governance

Setiap PR yang mengubah OpenAPI harus dibandingkan dengan latest released contract.

```text
current openapi.yaml
  vs
latest released openapi.yaml
  |
  v
diff classification:
  - documentation only
  - additive compatible
  - potentially breaking
  - breaking
```

Policy:

| Diff result | Required action |
|---|---|
| Documentation only | PATCH bump |
| Add optional response field | MINOR bump |
| Add endpoint | MINOR bump |
| Add required request field | MAJOR/v2 required |
| Remove/rename field | MAJOR/v2 required |
| Change field type | MAJOR/v2 required |
| Change status code | Manual review; often breaking |
| Change errorCode | Breaking |
| Add enum value | Potentially breaking for generated Java clients |
| Change auth scope | Breaking or security review |
| Mark deprecated | MINOR + migration note |
| Remove deprecated endpoint | Only after sunset/migration approval |

---

## 7. Breaking Change Gate

Breaking change tidak otomatis dilarang, tapi harus masuk gate.

Breaking change allowed only if:

```text
- major version or v2 path/header strategy exists
- v1/v2 coexistence plan exists
- impacted consumers identified
- migration guide exists
- deprecation/sunset date defined
- usage metrics available
- rollback plan exists
- API owner approval recorded
```

Template review:

| Question | Required answer |
|---|---|
| Apa yang breaking? | Field/path/status/error/auth/semantic |
| Consumer mana terdampak? | List service/team |
| Apakah v1 tetap hidup? | Ya/tidak + sampai kapan |
| Bagaimana migrasi? | Client version, adapter change |
| Bagaimana monitor usage? | Metric/log/query |
| Bagaimana rollback? | Deployment/config/artifact plan |
| Siapa owner approval? | API owner + consumer representative |

---

## 8. CI Pipeline Governance

OpenAPI Generator Maven plugin mendukung build integration untuk OpenAPI Generator; dokumentasinya menunjukkan plugin Maven dengan goal generation, default phase `generate-sources`, serta banyak opsi konfigurasi untuk mengendalikan output generated code. ([OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech/docs/plugins/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Pipeline minimal:

```text
validate-openapi
  -> lint-openapi
  -> api-diff
  -> generate-java-client
  -> compile-generated-client
  -> compile-adapter
  -> unit-tests
  -> wiremock-consumer-tests
  -> provider-schema-tests
  -> security-tests
  -> resilience-tests
  -> package
  -> publish-contract/client-artifact
```

Gate table:

| Gate | Block PR if |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI validation | YAML/schema/ref invalid |
| Lint | operationId missing, error schema missing, bad path |
| API diff | Breaking without approval/version plan |
| Generate client | Generator fails |
| Compile generated | Java output does not compile |
| Compile adapter | Adapter incompatible with new client |
| Unit tests | Mapper/error mapping broken |
| WireMock tests | Consumer behavior broken |
| Provider schema tests | Jersey response violates contract |
| Security tests | Auth propagation/401/403 broken |
| Resilience tests | Retry/idempotency/timeout policy broken |

---

## 9. CI Example

```text
Pull Request
  |
  v
1. Contract validation
   - openapi validate
   - lint
   - examples validate
  |
  v
2. Compatibility
   - diff against latest released spec
   - require version bump
  |
  v
3. Generation
   - generate Jersey/Feign Java client
   - compile generated source
  |
  v
4. Consumer tests
   - adapter tests
   - error mapper tests
   - WireMock tests
  |
  v
5. Provider tests
   - Jersey resource tests
   - ExceptionMapper tests
   - response schema validation
  |
  v
6. Runtime policy tests
   - timeout
   - retry
   - idempotency
   - auth propagation
  |
  v
7. Publish
   - contract artifact
   - generated client artifact
   - changelog/release notes
```

Rule:

```text
Tidak ada OpenAPI change yang boleh merge tanpa:
  validate
  diff
  generate
  compile
  test
```

---

## 10. Artifact Promotion

Recommended artifact lifecycle:

```text
SNAPSHOT / PR build
  -> candidate artifact
  -> staging validation
  -> released contract/client artifact
  -> production deploy
```

Artifact types:

| Artifact | Example |
|---|---|
| Contract | `customer-api-contract-1.4.0.yaml` |
| Java client | `customer-api-java-client-1.4.0.jar` |
| Provider image | `customer-service:2026.07.13-abc123` |
| Changelog | `customer-api-1.4.0.md` |
| Diff report | `customer-api-1.3.0-to-1.4.0.html/json` |

Consumer rule:

```text
Consumer must pin generated client version.
Do not use latest/dynamic version.
```

Bad:

```xml
<version>LATEST</version>
```

Good:

```xml
<version>1.4.0</version>
```

---

## 11. Release Strategy: Backward-compatible First

Ideal internal microservice release:

```text
Provider release N:
  backward-compatible with existing consumers

Consumer migration:
  update generated client
  update adapter
  deploy gradually

Provider cleanup:
  only after usage of old contract is zero
```

Safe sequence:

```text
1. Provider adds backward-compatible behavior
2. Publish contract/client MINOR
3. Consumers upgrade one by one
4. Observe old/new usage
5. Deprecate old path/field
6. Sunset after migration window
7. Remove only in MAJOR/v2 cleanup
```

Avoid:

```text
Provider deploys breaking change first,
then asks consumers to catch up.
```

---

## 12. Expand-Contract Pattern

Untuk mengubah schema tanpa breaking:

```text
Expand phase:
  provider accepts old + new format
  provider returns backward-compatible response

Migrate phase:
  consumers switch to new field/endpoint/client

Contract phase:
  old field/endpoint deprecated
  old field/endpoint removed only after sunset
```

Example rename field:

```text
Bad:
  customerName -> displayName directly

Good:
  release 1.4:
    add displayName
    keep customerName deprecated

  consumers migrate:
    use displayName

  release 2.0:
    remove customerName
```

---

## 13. Deployment Strategy Governance

Kubernetes Deployments support rolling updates, and official Kubernetes docs describe rolling updates as incrementally replacing old Pods with new Pods while waiting for new Pods to start before removing old ones. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tutorials/kubernetes-basics/update/update-intro/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) The Deployment documentation also notes that RollingUpdate can run multiple application versions at the same time during rollout. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/deployment/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Implication:

```text
During Kubernetes rolling deployment,
old and new provider versions may serve traffic at the same time.
Therefore:
  new version must be compatible with old consumers,
  and old version may still receive new-style traffic if routing is not isolated.
```

Deployment strategies:

| Strategy | Use case |
|---|---|
| Rolling update | Default gradual replacement |
| Canary | Test small traffic percentage |
| Blue-green | Fast switch between full environments |
| Shadow traffic | Observe behavior without affecting user |
| Feature flag | Enable behavior gradually |
| Header/path routing | v1/v2 coexistence |
| Dark launch | Deploy code disabled first |

---

## 14. Kubernetes Readiness Governance

For HTTP provider, readiness should only pass when service is ready to serve real traffic.

Readiness should check:

```text
- app started
- Jersey resources registered
- required config loaded
- DB reachable if required for serving
- migrations compatible
- critical dependency strategy decided
- generated contract version exposed
```

Avoid readiness that only checks:

```text
JVM process is alive
```

Recommended endpoints:

| Endpoint | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `/health/live` | Process alive; restart if dead |
| `/health/ready` | Ready for traffic |
| `/health/startup` | Slow startup protection |
| `/actuator/info` equivalent | Version/build/contract info if platform supports |

Even in non-Spring Jersey apps, the concept applies: expose explicit health resources.

---

## 15. Contract Version Exposure

Provider should expose enough metadata for debugging.

Example:

```json
{
  "service": "customer-service",
  "buildVersion": "2026.07.13-abc123",
  "apiContracts": {
    "customer-api": "1.4.0"
  },
  "gitCommit": "abc123",
  "environment": "prod"
}
```

Why?

| Need | Benefit |
|---|---|
| Incident RCA | Know what version served traffic |
| Consumer mismatch | Compare client vs provider contract |
| Rollback | Identify bad deploy |
| Audit | Release traceability |
| Support | Faster debugging |

Do not expose secrets or internal topology.

---

## 16. Canary Governance

Canary is useful for risky changes.

Canary checklist:

```text
Before canary:
  - contract diff approved
  - generated client compiled
  - provider schema tests passed
  - dashboards ready
  - rollback command known

During canary:
  - route small traffic percentage
  - compare error/latency/contract violation
  - monitor 401/403/409/422/5xx separately
  - monitor retry/timeout/circuit

After canary:
  - expand traffic gradually
  - keep rollback window
```

Canary success criteria:

| Metric | Check |
|---|---|
| p95 latency | Not worse beyond threshold |
| 5xx/timeout | No spike |
| 4xx business | Expected only |
| contract violations | Zero |
| auth failures | No unexpected spike |
| retry ratio | No abnormal increase |
| circuit open | No |
| bulkhead rejection | No unexpected increase |

---

## 17. Rollback Governance

Rollback is not only redeploying previous image.

Rollback checklist:

| Area | Question |
|---|---|
| API compatibility | Can old provider handle current consumer traffic? |
| DB migration | Is migration backward-compatible? |
| Config | Did config change need rollback too? |
| Contract artifact | Was client/contract published? |
| Feature flag | Can new behavior be disabled? |
| Cache/state | Did new version write incompatible state? |
| Workflow | Did Camunda process version change? |
| Idempotency | Are command keys still valid? |
| Events | Did new event shape leak out? |

Rule:

```text
A deploy is rollback-safe only if state and contracts are backward-compatible.
```

---

## 18. Database Migration and API Release

Your stack uses MyBatis/PostgreSQL, so DB migration must align with API compatibility.

Safe DB/API change:

```text
1. DB expand:
   add nullable column / new table / new index

2. App release:
   write old + new if needed
   read old + new

3. Consumer migration:
   clients switch to new API field

4. DB contract:
   backfill
   enforce constraint
   remove old column later
```

Bad:

```text
Rename DB column
  -> update MyBatis
  -> API response field changes
  -> consumer breaks
```

Rule:

```text
DB schema migration must not directly leak into API contract.
API mapper protects consumers from DB refactor.
```

---

## 19. Camunda / Workflow Release Governance

Workflow release is special because process instances can live longer than service deployments.

Checklist:

| Area | Governance |
|---|---|
| Process definition version | Know which version instances run |
| External task endpoint/client | Keep old worker compatibility |
| Process variables | Stable schema, no generated DTO |
| HTTP commands | Idempotency key/business key |
| Retry | Avoid multiplying HTTP retry × Camunda retry |
| BPMN error codes | Treat as contract |
| Migration | Plan running instance migration |
| Rollback | Understand process version/state impact |
| Async operation | Prefer command/status model for long tasks |

Rule:

```text
API deprecation window for workflow endpoints must consider
maximum lifetime of process instances, not just sprint timeline.
```

---

## 20. Security Governance

Production HTTP communication must govern:

```text
- OpenAPI security scheme
- service token vs user token propagation
- token exchange/delegation
- mTLS strategy
- tenant propagation
- 401/403 handling
- secret management
- audit logging
- no-token logging
```

Security gate:

| Check | Block if |
|---|---|
| Security scheme missing | Protected endpoint has no scheme |
| Token propagation unclear | No decision: service/user/delegated |
| 401/403 not tested | Error mapping unknown |
| Tenant mismatch not tested | Data isolation risk |
| mTLS cert handling undocumented | Runtime outage/security risk |
| Secrets in repo/image/configmap | Critical security issue |
| Logs contain token | Critical security issue |
| Auth headers manually built in use case | Architecture smell |

---

## 21. Resilience Governance

Every outbound operation should have a documented policy.

Example registry:

| Downstream | Operation | Type | Timeout | Retry | Idempotency | Circuit | Bulkhead |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|---|
| customer-service | getCustomerById | Query | 400ms | 2 | N/A | Yes | 40 |
| customer-service | searchCustomers | Query | 1000ms | 1 | N/A | Yes | 15 |
| payment-service | createPayment | Command | 1500ms | 1 | Required | Yes | 20 |
| order-service | cancelOrder | Command | 1000ms | 2 | Required | Yes | 20 |
| report-service | startExport | Async command | 1000ms | 1 | Required | Yes | 10 |

Governance rule:

```text
No outbound HTTP call may be added without:
  timeout
  retry policy
  idempotency decision
  error mapping
  observability tags
```

---

## 22. Observability Governance

Production dashboard should exist before rollout for critical APIs.

Minimum dashboard:

```text
- request rate by downstream/operation
- p50/p95/p99 latency
- outcome/error classification
- timeout rate
- retry ratio
- circuit state
- bulkhead rejection
- fallback usage
- contract violation count
- auth failure count
```

Alert rules:

| Alert | Why |
|---|---|
| Contract violation > 0 | Provider/client mismatch |
| Timeout spike | Latency/dependency issue |
| Retry ratio spike | Early degradation |
| Circuit open | Dependency unhealthy |
| Bulkhead rejection spike | Saturation |
| Unknown command outcome | Business consistency risk |
| 401 spike | Token/auth config issue |
| 403 spike | Permission/tenant policy issue |
| Fallback spike | Degraded mode |

---

## 23. API Ownership Model

Every API needs owner metadata.

```yaml
info:
  title: Customer API
  version: 1.4.0
  x-owner-team: customer-platform
  x-slack-channel: "#customer-platform"
  x-support-tier: tier-1
```

Ownership table:

| Role | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| API owner | Contract approval, versioning |
| Provider team | Implementation, schema tests |
| Consumer team | Adapter, migration |
| Platform team | CI tooling, gateway, deployment |
| Security team | Auth/mTLS/token policy |
| SRE/on-call | Observability, incident response |
| Architecture reviewer | Breaking change governance |

Rule:

```text
Unowned API contract becomes tribal knowledge.
Tribal knowledge fails during incidents and onboarding.
```

---

## 24. Environment Governance

Different environments:

```text
local
dev
test
staging
pre-prod
prod
DR
```

Config that changes per environment:

| Config | Example |
|---|---|
| base URL | service DNS/gateway URL |
| timeout | sometimes same, sometimes lower in test |
| auth issuer | dev/prod IdP |
| client credentials | per environment |
| truststore | internal CA per env |
| mTLS cert | per service/env |
| feature flags | canary/migration |
| rate limits | lower in test |
| logging level | stricter in prod |
| mock/stub mode | local/test only |

Rule:

```text
Same artifact, different config.
Do not build a different binary per environment unless unavoidable.
```

---

## 25. On-prem / AWS / Azure Considerations

Governance concerns are similar, but implementation differs.

| Concern | On-prem | AWS/Azure/Kubernetes |
|---|---|---|
| DNS/service discovery | Internal DNS/LB | K8s Service, cloud DNS, gateway |
| TLS/mTLS | Corporate PKI | Mesh/gateway/cloud cert manager |
| Secrets | Vault/HSM/manual process | Secret manager/K8s Secret/workload identity |
| Network | Firewall/proxy | VPC/VNet, security groups, NetworkPolicy |
| Deployment | VM/bare metal/app server | Deployment/Helm/GitOps |
| Observability | Splunk/ELK/APM | Prometheus/Grafana/OTel/cloud monitors |
| Auth | Enterprise IdP | Enterprise/cloud IdP integration |
| Rollback | App server/VM strategy | Deployment rollout/blue-green/canary |
| Egress | Proxy often required | NAT/private endpoint/service mesh |

Rules:

```text
Do not hardcode platform-specific URL/security assumptions in code.
Keep them in config/factory/platform layer.
```

---

## 26. Kubernetes-specific Governance

Checklist:

| Area | Check |
|---|---|
| Deployment strategy | Rolling/canary/blue-green defined |
| Readiness probe | Only ready when traffic-safe |
| Liveness probe | Does not kill slow-but-healthy startup |
| Resource limits | CPU/memory requests/limits defined |
| HPA | Scaling based on meaningful metrics |
| ConfigMap/Secret | Config separated from image |
| Service DNS | Stable logical service names |
| NetworkPolicy | Only allowed service-to-service paths |
| Ingress/gateway timeout | Aligned with app timeout |
| PodDisruptionBudget | Avoid losing too many replicas |
| Graceful shutdown | Stop accepting traffic, finish in-flight |
| mTLS/mesh | Ownership clear |
| Observability sidecars/agents | OTel/log/metric path configured |

Important timeout alignment:

```text
client read timeout
  < app inbound timeout
  < gateway/ingress timeout
  < external caller timeout
```

---

## 27. Graceful Shutdown

During rolling deployment, old Pods receive termination signal.

Service should:

```text
1. fail readiness / stop accepting new traffic
2. finish in-flight requests within grace period
3. close Jersey/Feign clients if needed
4. flush logs/metrics/traces
5. stop workers gracefully
6. avoid starting new Camunda external tasks
```

Risk if not graceful:

| Failure | Impact |
|---|---|
| In-flight HTTP command killed | Unknown outcome |
| External task killed mid-call | Duplicate retry |
| Metrics not flushed | Incident blind spot |
| Client connection abruptly closed | Upstream error |
| Pod removed before readiness false | Traffic loss |

---

## 28. Gateway / Ingress Governance

API gateway or ingress can enforce:

```text
- TLS termination
- mTLS validation
- auth pre-check
- rate limit
- routing by path/header
- request size limit
- timeout
- access logs
```

But application still owns:

```text
- object-level authorization
- business validation
- error contract
- idempotency semantics
- domain mapping
- contract-compliant response
```

Anti-pattern:

```text
Gateway validates JWT,
therefore provider skips authorization.
```

Provider still needs resource/tenant/state checks.

---

## 29. Config Governance

Config should be typed, validated, and fail fast.

Example:

```yaml
downstream:
  customer-service:
    baseUrl: http://customer-service.default.svc.cluster.local
    connectTimeoutMs: 100
    readTimeoutMs: 400
    maxAttempts: 2
    authScope: customer-api.read
    circuitBreaker:
      enabled: true
    bulkhead:
      maxConcurrentCalls: 40
```

Validation:

| Config | Validation |
|---|---|
| baseUrl | non-empty, valid URI |
| timeout | positive, within platform max |
| maxAttempts | positive, command-safe |
| authScope | required for protected API |
| mTLS cert path | exists/readable if app-level |
| fallback | explicitly enabled/disabled |
| circuit threshold | sane range |
| bulkhead size | less than worker pool |

Rule:

```text
Invalid outbound client config should fail startup,
not fail first production request.
```

---

## 30. Secret Governance

Secrets must not be:

```text
- committed to Git
- baked into container image
- printed in startup logs
- stored in Camunda variables
- included in error response
- included in structured logs
```

Secrets include:

```text
client secret
API key
private key
keystore password
refresh token
database password
```

Production policy:

| Area | Rule |
|---|---|
| Storage | Secret manager / Kubernetes Secret / Vault / cloud secret store |
| Rotation | Documented and tested |
| Access | Least privilege |
| Logging | Redaction enforced |
| CI | Secret scanning |
| Runtime | Mount/read securely |
| Incident | Revocation process known |

---

## 31. Release Checklist

Before release:

| Area | Check |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI | Valid, linted |
| Version | Correct SemVer bump |
| Diff | Reviewed and approved |
| Changelog | Complete |
| Generated client | Generated and compiled |
| Adapter | Tests pass |
| Provider | Schema tests pass |
| Security | 401/403/token/mTLS checks pass |
| Resilience | Timeout/retry/idempotency tests pass |
| Observability | Dashboard/alerts ready |
| Deployment | Rollout strategy selected |
| Rollback | Known and tested |
| Consumers | Impact list prepared |
| Deprecation | Usage tracking ready if applicable |
| Config | Environment configs validated |
| Secrets | Present and rotated if needed |

During release:

```text
- monitor canary/rolling metrics
- check p95/p99 latency
- check 5xx/timeout
- check 401/403 spike
- check contract violation
- check retry/circuit/bulkhead
- verify no unexpected consumer errors
```

After release:

```text
- publish release note
- update API catalog
- watch deprecated usage
- track consumer migration
- document incident/rollback if any
```

---

## 32. Consumer Migration Checklist

When consuming new API/client version:

| Area | Check |
|---|---|
| Dependency | Generated client version pinned |
| Adapter | Mapper updated |
| Error mapper | New status/errorCode handled |
| Enum | Unknown/new value handling |
| Required fields | Request builder updated |
| Security | New scope/header/token model handled |
| Tests | WireMock fixtures updated |
| Timeout | Operation budget still valid |
| Observability | Operation tags unchanged or migrated |
| Rollback | Old client/adapter can be restored |
| Camunda | Process variables not tied to generated DTO |
| MyBatis | No generated DTO leak into persistence |

---

## 33. Provider Release Checklist

When changing provider API:

| Area | Check |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI | Updated before/with implementation |
| Jersey resource | Matches paths/methods/statuses |
| Request validation | `400` consistent |
| Error mapper | ErrorResponse/ProblemDetail consistent |
| Business errors | `409/422` documented |
| Security | Required scopes/permissions documented |
| Response schema | Schema validation tests pass |
| Backward compatibility | Old consumers still work |
| Idempotency | Command semantics tested |
| Observability | Metrics/logs added for new operation |
| Deprecation | If replacing old endpoint, mark deprecated |
| Deployment | v1/v2 coexist if breaking |

---

## 34. API Catalog Governance

API catalog entry should include:

```text
- API name
- owning team
- OpenAPI spec link/artifact
- current version
- generated client artifact
- base URL per environment
- auth model
- mTLS requirement
- SLO
- error contract
- deprecation status
- changelog
- dashboard link
- runbook link
```

This accelerates onboarding and incident response.

---

## 35. Runbook Template

For each downstream dependency:

```text
Service: customer-service
Operations:
  - getCustomerById
  - searchCustomers

Contract:
  customer-api-contract:1.4.0

Client:
  customer-api-java-client:1.4.0

Auth:
  service token scope customer-api.read

Timeout:
  getCustomerById readTimeout=400ms

Retry:
  getCustomerById maxAttempts=2
  no retry on 4xx

Dashboard:
  downstream/customer-service

Common failures:
  401 -> token config
  403 -> scope/tenant policy
  404 -> normal lookup miss
  503 -> provider unavailable
  timeout -> provider latency/network

Rollback:
  deployment/customer-service previous image
  contract remains backward-compatible
```

---

## 36. Senior Design Review Questions

Saat review PR/API baru, tanyakan:

```text
1. Apa contract-nya?
2. Siapa consumer-nya?
3. Apa breaking change-nya?
4. Bagaimana generated client berubah?
5. Apakah adapter boundary bersih?
6. Bagaimana error 400/401/403/404/409/422/5xx dimap?
7. Apakah command idempotent?
8. Timeout dan retry-nya apa?
9. Apakah token/service/user context benar?
10. Apa observability operation name-nya?
11. Bagaimana deploy dan rollback?
12. Bagaimana test contract/provider/consumer?
13. Bagaimana migration plan jika v2?
14. Bagaimana on-prem/cloud/Kubernetes config?
15. Bagaimana Camunda/MyBatis boundary tetap aman?
```

---

## 37. Final Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI dibuat setelah code | Contract-first jadi code-first disguised |
| No API diff | Breaking change lolos |
| Generated client tidak compile di CI | Spec tidak usable |
| Use case langsung pakai generated client | Architecture coupling |
| Retry semua HTTP calls | Duplicate command, retry storm |
| Timeout default | Thread exhaustion |
| No idempotency for POST command | Duplicate side effect |
| Token forwarding blindly | Security/audience leak |
| 401/403 disamakan dengan 503 | Wrong retry/alert |
| No downstream metrics | Incident blind spot |
| No contract violation alert | Schema drift tersembunyi |
| Rolling update with breaking provider | Consumer random failure |
| Rollback unsafe due to DB/API state | Incident makin parah |
| Store generated DTO in Camunda variable | Long-running process fragile |
| Generated DTO enters MyBatis | Persistence coupled to external API |
| Secrets in config/logs | Security incident |
| No owner/changelog | Tribal knowledge |

---

## 38. Production Readiness Scorecard

| Category | Minimum standard |
|---|---|
| Contract | OpenAPI valid, linted, versioned |
| Compatibility | API diff gate exists |
| Client | Generated Java client compiled |
| Architecture | Adapter/gateway boundary exists |
| Error | Standard error contract and mapper |
| Resilience | Timeout/retry/idempotency/circuit/bulkhead |
| Security | Auth propagation and mTLS/token model defined |
| Observability | Metrics/logs/traces/dashboard/alerts |
| Testing | Provider + consumer + mock + schema tests |
| Release | Rolling/canary/rollback strategy |
| Config | Typed, validated, env-driven |
| Secrets | Managed securely |
| Platform | Kubernetes/cloud/on-prem concerns handled |
| Migration | Deprecation/sunset/consumer tracking |
| Ownership | API owner and runbook defined |

---

## 39. Ringkasan Akhir Seri

Contract-first HTTP communication di microservice production bukan hanya:

```text
OpenAPI + generated client
```

Melainkan:

```text
OpenAPI contract
  + SemVer/versioning
  + API diff
  + generated client
  + adapter/gateway boundary
  + DTO/error mapping
  + timeout/retry/idempotency
  + security propagation/mTLS
  + contract testing
  + observability
  + deployment governance
  + rollback/migration plan
```

Golden rule:

```text
Provider boleh berubah cepat,
consumer boleh deploy independen,
production tetap aman,
hanya jika compatibility, resilience, security, dan observability dijadikan governance,
bukan keputusan ad-hoc per developer.
```

Kesimpulan senior-level:

> **Production governance adalah pagar agar contract-first tetap benar saat realitas production terjadi: rollout bertahap, mixed versions, network failure, token expiry, schema drift, dependency latency, retry storm, workflow retry, dan rollback.**
